# FleshMarket v1.10.1.5 - MANIFEST

## 1.10.1.5 changes (the week closes with the tax)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html (build stamp), tools/chat-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- SAME INSTANT AS TAX DAY: Sunday 12:00 America/Los_Angeles, read from _frsMostRecentSundayNoonLA - the SAME helper runWeeklyTaxAssessment is scheduled from, not a second expression that happens to line up, so the two cannot drift on a DST week or a timezone change.
- *** IT SHARES THE BOUNDARY AND NOT THE SCHEDULER, AND THAT IS THE POINT. *** frsScheduleTick returns early when FRS is disabled and FRS SHIPS DORMANT (enabled defaults to 0). Four lines inside that tick would have produced a weekly clear that SILENTLY NEVER FIRES, hanging off a switch nobody would think to connect it to. "The same time as tax day" is a statement about the CLOCK, not about whether the tax is on.
- OWN MARKER (city_kv chat_wipe_ts) for the same reason last_run_ts exists on the FRS side: a server down across Sunday noon wipes when it RETURNS rather than skipping the week. AN UNSET MARKER IS NOT A MISSED WIPE - a fresh DB or the first boot after this ships records the boundary and waits, rather than clearing a chat nobody has had a cycle to fill, as a surprise.
- WHAT GOES: the in-memory rings, the chat_log table, and unread mentions OLDER THAN THE BOUNDARY. That last one matters - a badge surviving the wipe points at a message that no longer exists, so the player opens the channel, finds nothing, and the count is still there with no way to clear it by reading. Cut by timestamp rather than truncated, so a mention landing in the same second (about a message that survives) is not swallowed.
- THE CLIENT IS TOLD. Without the chat_cleared broadcast the wipe is invisible to anyone already connected: the server forgets and their pane does not, so they go on reading and quoting a week that is gone and only a refresh reveals it.
- EFFECT ON THE 1.10.1.4 PAYLOAD ITEM: a weekly wipe BOUNDS the connect payload - the history can hold at most one week rather than accumulating - but does NOT size it. A busy week still fills 200 messages per room and the frame is still every room flattened into one send on every connect. That decision is narrower now, not closed.
- CHECKS: chat-check 68 -> 88. The boundary is DRIVEN ACROSS BOTH DST TRANSITIONS - 13:30 UTC-7 on the spring-forward Sunday resolves to that Sunday, 11:30 UTC-8 on the fall-back Sunday resolves to the week before, and asking from the boundary returns the boundary rather than walking back a week. Those are exactly where an hour of drift puts the wipe on the wrong side of noon and no reading of the arithmetic settles it. The wipe itself runs against a real in-memory database: 50 rows across two rooms removed and counted, a second wipe reported harmless, and the mention cut proved to KEEP the row landing after the boundary. Suite 15/23, 4906 assertions.

## 1.10.1.4 changes (534 words, a counter that says so, and three walls in a row)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/server.js, client/assets/chat-ui.js, client/index.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html (build stamp), tools/chat-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE CAP: 240 CHARACTERS to 534 WORDS, roughly 3,200 characters of ordinary English, about 13x. Enforced server side on chat AND whisper and REFUSED rather than truncated - silently cutting at the cap publishes half a sentence under the player's name with no way for them to know. A 6,000 CHARACTER ceiling sits underneath: not a second rule, a defence, because 534 words says nothing about how long a word may be and one "word" of 200,000 characters would otherwise be legal.
- THE COUNTER: live in the input row, hidden below 75 percent of the cap (a number ticking beside every three-word line is noise), amber at 90 percent, red over, send button disabled. CLIENT AND SERVER COUNT WORDS WITH THE SAME EXPRESSION and the check asserts both copies match - two definitions of "word" means a counter reading 533 beside a server refusing the message, which is worse than no counter because the player is told the rule and then the rule lies. An over-length message is refused WITHOUT CLEARING THE INPUT: it is text somebody spent time on, and discarding it is not a length rule, it is a shredder.
- AUTO-DUNCE: three messages at or above 481 words (90 percent of cap) within 60 SECONDS. TWO ADDITIONS TO "BACK TO BACK", BOTH DELIBERATE. (1) A WINDOW - consecutive with no clock catches three long posts written across an evening, which is a lore writer, not a spammer. (2) A FLOOR BELOW THE CAP - somebody pasting the same wall lands wherever their paste lands, rarely on 534 exactly, and an exact-hit rule catches almost nobody. Any ordinary message resets the run. DEVS, ADMINS AND OWNER EXEMPT: a GM posting three lore drops is precisely the false positive, and an operator locked out of his own chat by his own rule is worse than the spam. Runs AFTER delivery so the third wall survives as evidence. Logged as dunce_auto; existing undunce reverses it.
- *** OPEN, NOT FIXED HERE, AND THE ONE TO DECIDE BEFORE SHIPPING: THE CONNECT PAYLOAD. *** getChatHistory flattens EVERY ring into ONE JSON frame sent on EVERY connect. 200 messages per room across ~20 rooms is ~1.6MB today and ~12MB once a message can be 13x longer - every refresh, every player. Nothing in this patch changes CHAT_RING_MAX or the payload, so the growth is real and unmitigated. A product decision rather than a bug: lower the ring, cap the history by bytes, or send only the active room and fetch the rest on demand. The ring's own comment still carries the old ~0.4KB estimate and is wrong the moment this ships.
- CHECKS: chat-check 45 -> 68. Counter and streak DRIVEN, the streak twice - once on the real clock for its shape, once on a FAKED clock for the window, which is the assertion separating a lore writer from a spammer and the one most likely to be got wrong by reading. Suite 15/23, 4886 assertions; the four failures are the gitignored art, identical on a clean checkout.

## 1.10.1.3 changes (a mention that arrives while you are logged out is now waiting for you)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. THIS ONE ADDS A TABLE. *** Files: server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/chat-ui.js, client/index.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html (build stamp), tools/chat-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE GAP v1.10.1.2 OPENED, CLOSED. Mention-only badges were right and they exposed what the old noise hid: badges were CLIENT-SIDE counters starting at zero every load, so a mention arriving while a player was logged out was never delivered - the message sat in the scrollback and nothing pointed at it.
- *** AN INBOX, NOT A READ CURSOR, AND THE REASON IS THE STORAGE. *** A per-channel "last read" timestamp does not survive the chat rings being COUNT-bounded at 200 per room rather than time-bounded: on a busy Global the messages a cursor counts against are gone and the count is wrong. chat_mentions is a ROW PER MENTION - the event, recorded when it happens, outliving the ring, a restart and any length of absence. Shaped after fb_notifications down to the column names, because Fleshbook already solved this and a second shape for the same problem is how two systems drift. NEW TABLE, created by initDB at boot; no migration of existing rows, nothing rewritten.
- RECORDED ON THE SEND PATH, because the case that needs help is the player who is NOT connected. Parses chatText - the text that SHIPPED - so a name the slur filter rewrote cannot notify anyone. Self-mentions skipped, duplicates within a message collapsed. THE CHANNEL GATE IS APPLIED TO THE RECIPIENT: naming somebody in Guild chat who cannot READ Guild chat would light a badge on a tab they cannot open, which is worse than no notification because it is unclearable.
- TWO CEILINGS, SEPARATE ON PURPOSE: 5 recorded mentions per message caps the ROWS; 12 scanned tokens caps the LOOKUPS. hits only counts tokens that resolve to a real player, so forty @nobody tokens record nothing and still cost forty name lookups. The cheap half of the work is the half an attacker controls.
- CLEARING: opening a channel marks THAT channel read and nothing else. The client sends a channel name; the server takes whose rows those are from the SOCKET's player id, never from the message. The room switcher clears the same badge by another route and had to be wired too, or switching Global rooms cleared the picture and left the state. Read rows pruned after 30 days, at boot and daily, so marking read stays one cheap UPDATE.
- STILL NOT COVERED: the WHISPER badge is untouched and is still a live counter with no persistence. A whisper received while offline is in the pane on reconnect and raises no badge. Same gap, same shape, different store, not fixed here.
- CHECKS: chat-check 22 -> 45. The inbox is DRIVEN AGAINST A REAL DATABASE - in-memory SQLite built from the CREATE TABLE lifted out of db.js rather than retyped, with the four helpers lifted and called, because a schema copied into a check is a check that passes against a table the server does not create. The parser is driven too and THAT is what caught the lookup ceiling: forty distinct junk tokens cost forty lookups before the second cap existed, and no reading of the loop showed it because the loop was correctly capped on the thing it was counting. The DB section needs Node 22.5+ for node:sqlite and prints the NOT RUN banner rather than a pass where it is missing.
- SUITE 15/23 green, 4863 assertions. The four failures are the gitignored vehicle, brood and terrain art, identical on an untouched checkout.

## 1.10.1.2 changes (a refresh raised badges for messages nobody had sent)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files: client/assets/core.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html (build stamp), tools/chat-check.mjs (NEW), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE ARGUMENT WAS BEING PASSED AND NOTHING WAS CATCHING IT. *** chat_history replays the last thirty minutes on every login and reconnect and has always called addChat(m.data || m, true) - that `true` means "this is history". addChat was declared `function addChat(item)`. ONE PARAMETER. The flag went nowhere and had been going nowhere since the replay was written, so every refresh walked half an hour of traffic through the LIVE path: a badge per replayed message on any channel you were not looking at, plus playSound('mention') for every historical line carrying your name. The badge was reporting that the client had restarted.
- *** AND THE LIVE PATH COUNTED THE WRONG THING ANYWAY. *** With no replay at all it incremented on ANY message on a non-active channel, so a busy Global lit it permanently whether or not one word was addressed to anyone. A badge worth ignoring is the same as no badge.
- FIX: badges are MENTIONS now. One predicate, chatMentionsMe, asked at the one place that paints the badge rather than at addChat's nine call sites. The highlight and the mention sound read the same predicate, so what glows, what sounds and what badges cannot give three different answers. @Jacobson is not a mention of Jacob, nor is @Jacob-son, nor an address like a@Jacob.io: the name must START a token as well as end one. \b alone was not enough - it sits happily between 'b' and '-' and usernames may contain a hyphen. THE WHISPER BADGE IS UNTOUCHED: a whisper is addressed to you by construction.
- *** WHAT THIS DELIBERATELY DOES NOT DO, AND IT IS A REAL GAP. *** Chat badges are still CLIENT-SIDE counters with no persistence and start at zero on every load. Under the old behaviour that barely mattered because the replay repopulated them with noise. Now it means A MENTION THAT ARRIVES WHILE YOU ARE LOGGED OUT IS NOT WAITING FOR YOU: the message is in the replay, the badge is not. Fixing it properly is a server-side read cursor per player per channel - a schema and a migration - and it is not in a client patch. Fleshbook is unaffected; its badge already comes from /api/fleshbook/unread at auth, which is server truth.
- CHECKS: tools/chat-check.mjs NEW, 22 assertions. The predicate is DRIVEN - lifted into a sandbox and called against the substring cases, because reading a regex does not tell you whether @Jacobson matches and calling it does. The wiring is matched on text on purpose: "the argument reaches the parameter" and "the return sits before the badge" are facts about where tokens sit. ONE ASSERTION FAILED ON FIRST RUN AND THE FAULT WAS IN THE CHECK: the helper reset a global regex's lastIndex inside the expression it returned, so it returned !!0. Recorded rather than quietly fixed.
- SUITE 15/23 green, 4840 assertions. The four failures - city-battle, faction, reach, reach-terrain - are the gitignored vehicle, brood and terrain art that a repo clone does not have, and fail identically on an untouched checkout.

## 1.10.1.1 changes (the Circuit was sealed against a passage the server had open)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files: client/assets/core.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, tools/lane-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE SEAL STATE WAS DISCARDED ON EVERY LOAD. *** galaxy.js is lazy loaded on the first galactic tab click; the init message carrying wormholeOpen and reachOpen arrives on socket connect, which is always earlier. So window._setWormhole and window._setPassage did not exist yet, core.js's `if (window._setWormhole)` guard was never true at init, and the server's answer went nowhere. galaxy.js then ran on its own literals, { jade:false, khaisultull:false }, with nothing ever reconciling them.
- WHY ONLY THE CIRCUIT SHOWED IT: the Reach's client default (sealed) matches the server's. The Circuit's does not - WORMHOLE_OPEN defaults to TRUE on the server - so the client sealed a passage the server had open, every load.
- WHY IT ONLY BECAME A BUG IN v1.6.7.1: until then the seal drew a banner and stopped nothing, so a wrong flag cost a wrong sprite. v1.6.7.1 made swapGalaxy refuse on it, which was correct and STAYS. That turned a cosmetic wrong answer into a locked door for every player, on every route in, because there is one predicate rather than a check per caller.
- AND THE DEV SWITCH LOOKED DEAD FOR THE SAME REASON. Opening the passage broadcasts, core.js receives it, and calls a setter that does not exist on a client whose galaxy tab was never opened. The switch was working. Nothing was listening.
- FIX: core.js banks the last server answer in window._PASSAGE_SEED, keyed by galaxy id, UNCONDITIONALLY - at init and on both broadcast types, setter present or not. galaxy.js seeds PASSAGE_OPEN from it at load, and _setPassage writes back, so the bank and the table cannot diverge. Defaults untouched: a client that never received an init is exactly as sealed as before.
- CHECKS: lane-check 55 to 67. Nine new, and six of them DRIVEN - galaxy.js is executed in a sandbox with a seed in place and the seal asked afterwards, both directions, plus no-seed and the write-back. A text read could not have caught this: every line was correct on its own and the fault was the ORDER they ran in. The other three match text on purpose, on the half of the fix that must be unconditional in core.js, because that is a property of where the statement sits.

## 1.10.1.0 changes (THE BIG ONE - everything from v1.4.0.4 to here, as one release)
- *** CORRECTION, 2026-08-27: THIS RELEASE SHIPPED. *** Every line in this block written as a pre-deploy warning is stale. v1.10.1.0 is what is running on the VPS, confirmed by Jacob directly rather than read off the server. The block is left intact rather than rewritten, because a checklist edited after the fact is a worse record than one clearly marked - but read it as a log, not as instructions.
- WHAT THE CORRECTION CHANGES: the mining faucet fix is LIVE, so item (1) describes a closed faucet rather than an open one. The player_cargo rebuild migration HAS RUN at boot; whether it ran clean is a separate question that only the running database answers and it has NOT been checked - `SELECT colony_id, COUNT(*) FROM player_cargo GROUP BY colony_id;` settles it in one line. Items (3) and (4), manual backup and migration rehearsal, are spent. "Live has no check suite" is no longer true.
- WHAT IT DOES NOT CHANGE: the licence question on client/assets/commodities/ is still open and still Jacob's to answer, and every item in the STILL OPEN list at the end of this block is still open.

- *** RELEASE BUILD. HARD REFRESH **AND** A SERVER RESTART. *** Live is v1.4.0.4 (built 2026-08-22); this is v1.10.1.0. 132 changelog entries sit between them. The per-patch history is KEPT - it is where the reasoning lives, and several entries are the only record of a fix a later patch had to reverse - but nobody deploying needs to read 132 of them.
- SCOPE, DIFFED RATHER THAN REMEMBERED: 55 files/directories NEW, 34 CHANGED, NOTHING REMOVED. New: the Khai'sultull Reach (client/assets/reach-battle.js, reach-hive.js, server/reach.js), the city battlefield (client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/space/city/), the faction registry (client/assets/factions.js, server/factions.js), server-authoritative sudoku (server/sudoku.js), the asset credits panel (client/assets/asset-credits.js, docs/CREDITS.md), and a 22-file check suite under tools/ that does not exist on live at all. Largest edits: galaxy.js +1036, server.js +1012, god-panel.js +998, index.html +313.
- WHAT A PLAYER SEES: a war layer that did not exist, city battlefields on settled worlds, a swappable galaxy with a passage that actually refuses entry when sealed, an asset credits panel, trial accounts, server-authoritative sudoku/baccarat/sic bo/solitaire, a chat filter that censors what it flags, warehouse storage, city charters, index funds, and clearance limits on player-to-player transfers.
- *** (1) THE MINING FAUCET IS STILL OPEN ON LIVE, AND THE LIVE CODE SAYS OTHERWISE IN A COMMENT. *** Challenged as already fixed; half true, and that is the dangerous half. A mining fix IS on live - v1.4.0.4 closed the CARGO DRONE case and carries a comment saying "THE BUDGET IS PER RUN, NOT PER MESSAGE... Only the end of run settlement closes the window now." TRUE OF THE CASE IT DESCRIBES, FALSE OF THE ONE STILL OPEN.
- READ live server.js:11455-11466: `const run = _miningRuns.get(actor.id)`, `elapsed = run ? ... : MINING_RUN_FALLBACK_SEC`, `already = run ? run.banked : 0`, and at the end `if (reason === 'cargo_drone' && run) run.banked = ...; else _miningRuns.delete(actor.id);`. NOTHING EVER STORES A RUN WHEN ONE DOES NOT EXIST. A client that never sends a loadout has run === undefined on every message, always takes the fallback branch, always sees already === 0. With shipped constants MINING_MAX_YIELD_PER_SEC=400 and MINING_RUN_FALLBACK_SEC=90: cap = min(400*90, 500000) = F36,000 EVERY MESSAGE, forever, nothing accumulating.
- THE FIX HERE is four lines at server.js:12095-12104: when no run exists, SEED one a fallback-length in the past and STORE it, so the budget is handed over once and then drains. Same arithmetic, one map write. tools/mining-check.mjs drives the real handler body rather than matching its text, which is the only reason it was found - two prior fixes and a code read all declared it closed while it was live, because the guard and its comment both read correctly.
- *** (2) TRIAL ACCOUNTS ARE NOT A DEPLOY RISK AND THIS CHECKLIST WAS WRONG TO SAY THEY WERE. *** Already live, already gated - upgrade route, locked-trial message and per-route blocks are all in the v1.4.0.4 tree at server.js:3434-3454. The warning came from a note written when live was believed to be v1.3.9.x. It is not; it is v1.4.0.4, and trial accounts landed BEFORE the gap. Nothing about the login path changes in this release.
- REST OF THE CHECKLIST: (3) TAKE A MANUAL DB BACKUP FIRST. (4) READ THE player_cargo REBUILD MIGRATION in server/db.js - it runs at boot. (5) ART IS PUSHED SEPARATELY: ship.sh has a licence gate that refuses to commit if vehicles/brood/terrain art is tracked, and tars it to the VPS AFTER the git push. (6) package-lock.json is gitignored at ANY depth now; the old rule was server/ only and a stale lock in a build zip once cost a multi-hour outage and a DB restore.
- GM CONTROLS CHECKED, AND WHAT THAT MEANS: tools/controls-check.mjs traces every Reach and war panel control end to end - panel sends a named command, server.js has a branch for that exact string, the branch calls the reach.js export, reach.js exports it, and the branch broadcasts where other clients must see it. 232 assertions, all passing. WHAT IT CANNOT DO IS CLICK THE BUTTON: it proves the wire is continuous, not that the current at the far end is the right shape. Behaviour is driven separately in reach-check.mjs (1091 assertions). A live GM session should still be walked once by hand after deploy.
- FULL SUITE AT RELEASE: 22/22 green, 10699 assertions. Live has no check suite, so there is no before number.
- ARTIST CREDITED: Free Game Assets (GUI, Sprite, Tilesets), itch.io/profile/free-game-assets - every commodity icon on all three markets plus the clothing, equipment and vehicle icons. Four packs named, marked credit-required.
- *** ON LICENCES: EVERY PACK IS PAID FOR, AND PURCHASE SETTLES USE RATHER THAN REDISTRIBUTION. *** Not a new distinction - it is the one this repo already made in its own words for the modern city pack: "the purchase settles USE, which is what was missing. It does not settle redistribution." Same reading for client/assets/commodities/, 120 renamed icons TRACKED in a public repo: buying grants the right to ship the art in a game; whether the source files may sit in a public git tree is a separate clause in most of these licences rather than a consequence of paying. CraftPix's FREE file-licence forbids redistributing raw packs; a commercial tier may not, and I do not know which was bought.
- SO IT IS A QUESTION, NOT A FINDING, and it is Jacob's to answer. Purchased tier permits public source art: commodities/ stays where it is, nothing to do. It does not: it belongs behind the same gitignore-and-tar-over-ssh route as vehicles, brood and terrain, and ship.sh's gate should name it. The clothing/equipment/vehicle icons are NOT tracked in either tree - server only - so the question does not arise for them. Credit is flagged credit-required because the free tier demands attribution; on a commercial tier that flag can come off, and crediting them is right either way.
- ONE ITEM CAME OFF THE OPEN LIST BY BEING CHECKED RATHER THAN REMEMBERED: DEPLOY_README.md was carried as "documents the wrong VPS path" and it does not - it says /root/Flesh-Market and explicitly records that the old /opt/fleshmarket claim never served anything. Verified in the tree, not trusted from the note.
- STILL OPEN, CARRIED INTO THIS RELEASE: minesweeper is still client-checked; the Capital House invite-then-kick wire-tax dodge is unfixed; VPS SSH still allows password auth; git history has never been checked for committed plaintext passwords; a 1.0-3.0m bare run remains at junction seams on organic and archipelago layouts; the city barriers and rubble are still invented prism geometry.

## 1.10.0.10 changes (the screenshot starts saying where it was taken from)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/citybattle-mock.html, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** I CANNOT REPRODUCE THE LADDER AND AM NOT PRETENDING OTHERWISE. *** Six camera setups on new_anchor at seed 7, including a marked pass with every ground piece recoloured and a near-vertical top-down of a junction: kerbs come out clean and continuous on both sides of every avenue, the road covers the junction, nothing is laid at a four metre pitch down one verge. The screenshots plainly show it. The difference is a seed, an avenue or an angle I do not have, and four more guessed cameras is not a method.
- *** SO THE SHOT NOW SAYS WHERE IT WAS TAKEN FROM. *** A bench screenshot IS the bug report, and it carried the build number and nothing else. The png button composites a caption strip under the frame: build, front (colony#zone), seed, camera x/y/z, yaw, pitch, view radius, road wear, ground tex and tile size. BURNED INTO THE IMAGE rather than only shown in the HUD, because the HUD can be cropped and routinely is. The HUD also gains a live camera readout.
- WHAT THAT BUYS: any future screenshot is reproducible in one command instead of six guesses. Use the png button rather than a window grab and the strip comes with it.

## 1.10.0.9 changes (the kerb straddles the kerb line, and a coverage probe cannot see a colour)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE BLANK SQUARES ARE THE SECOND HALF OF LAST PATCH'S FAULT: THE KERB WAS ALSO TWO METRES TOO FAR OUT. *** The module is 4m across and the LIP sits at LOCAL Z ZERO - the CENTRE of the piece, two metres of road one side, two metres of pavement the other. The centre belongs ON the kerb line at ax + hw; it was at ax + hw + 2.
- Two metres out, the road half - the ONLY face wearing #585753 - lands entirely on the PAVEMENT, a two metre band of road grey down both sides of every street, broken into module lengths by the junction skips and mitres. That is why it reads as squares and why it turns up at every angle.
- *** THE BARE-RUN SWEEP COULD NEVER HAVE FOUND IT. *** At hw + 2 the piece still COVERED the ground continuously, deck meeting the first strip at hw + 4 exactly. Nothing was missing; it was the wrong COLOUR, and a coverage probe does not look at colour. Four patches of chasing this with one kept coming back clean. Swept geometrically instead - does each kerb's road half land inside a carriageway - 555 of 555 wrong on radial, 526 of 526 on grid, every kerb on every layout. ZERO on all six after.
- The pavement strips move in half a module with the deck (hw+4..hw+12 to hw+2..hw+10) or the two metres the kerb used to occupy would be bare. The corner mitre straddles on the same convention.
- *** AND NEVER_ON_ROAD HAD TO LOSE THE STRADDLING PIECES *** - a correction to something added three patches ago. It assumed the kerb sat BESIDE the road, so "centre is on the road" meant misplaced. The kerb straddles, its centre sits exactly on the kerb line, and |x - ax| < hw is a coin flip on floating point: 5.799999 < 5.8 is true. It deleted 542 kerbs down to 198 on terraced and left a FIFTY-SEVEN METRE bare run. PavementCurb and PavementCornerBig are out; the jz/jx gate is what keeps them out of a junction and is the right test. Pavement is the only piece left, being the only pure walkway.
- MEASURED AFTER, ALL SIX LAYOUTS: kerbs restored to 560/524/470/544/348/529; road-half-on-pavement ZERO; furniture in a road ZERO. Bare run 1.0m on radial/grid/terraced/spine and 3.0m on archipelago/organic - UP from 0.0 and 1.0, because moving the strips shifted the junction seams. A real regression traded for eliminating a wrong-coloured band on 100 per cent of kerbs, and NOT closed.
- city-battle-check 353 -> 361. Full suite 22/22, 10693 assertions.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.8 changes (both avenue kerbs were facing backwards, and a check was certifying it)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE BROKEN ROAD EDGE IS THE KERB, AND IT IS 180 DEGREES. *** Read off the bake face by face, because reasoning about it is what got it wrong twice. PavementCurb's pavement deck is at y=0 over z -100..-10, its lip rises to y=3 across z -10..0, and the ROAD half is at y=-10 over z 0..+100 - the only face in the piece carrying the road colour #585753. Road side is +Z, pavement side -Z, as a fact rather than an assumption.
- Rotation maps +Z to (sin theta, 0, cos theta). On the +x side of an avenue the kerb stands at ax + hw + 2 with the road toward -X, so theta must be -PI/2. THE SOURCE HAD +PI/2 THERE AND -PI/2 ON THE -X SIDE: BOTH FLIPPED. That draws a road-coloured trench 2m wide sunk 20cm into the PAVEMENT along every avenue, with the pavement deck cantilevered over the carriageway - a broken road edge with a row of pale squares, in every screenshot of an avenue since the kerbs landed.
- THE CROSS STREETS WERE ALWAYS RIGHT, which is why it only showed on avenues: they use PI and 0, the piece's profile axis is already Z, and no quarter turn is involved.
- *** AND A CHECK WAS ACTIVELY HOLDING THE BUG IN PLACE. *** 'the kerb is turned to run along the street' froze the LITERAL pair `Math.PI/2 : -Math.PI/2`, which IS the flipped pair - any correct rotation would have failed it. Its own comment said the right thing and then pinned the assumption rather than the geometry. Replaced with one that DERIVES the angle from the bake: find the road-coloured face, see which side of z it is on, require sin(theta) to point it at the street. Same for cross streets.
- THAT IS THE FOURTH GREEN CHECK IN THIS SEQUENCE THAT WAS WRONG RATHER THAN THE CODE: fleet-check asserted about a view it never reached; 'the painted road surface is retired' made a one-off double-draw a permanent ban; 'the lane count ceilings' asserted a fix reversed a patch later; and this one asserted a bug. Same pattern each time - written against the LINE that changed rather than the property that has to hold.
- city-battle-check 345 -> 353. Full suite 22/22, 10685 assertions.
- NOT TOUCHED: the invented prism barriers and rubble, and the 1.0m bare run still open on organic and archipelago.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.7 changes (the square spots were my own fix, and the objects in the road were a fixed pitch)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE SQUARE SPOTS ARE MY OWN FIX FROM LAST PATCH. *** v1.10.0.6 closed the bare kerb strip by overhanging the road past the kerb line, arguing the overhang lands UNDER the kerb. IT IS THE OTHER WAY ROUND: PavementCurb carries its own road side at the same ten unit drop, so an overhanging road tile is COPLANAR with it and what draws is a dark tab of kerb geometry ON the carriageway, once per module - a ladder of dark rungs down the road edge. A colour-marked render found it in one look after four rounds of reasoning did not.
- FLOOR PLUS TWO FLUSH EDGE TILES. The centred run covers what divides evenly; one extra tile per side sits with its OUTER edge exactly on the kerb line, so the union is exactly the carriageway. hw 6.0 -> floor 3 + edges at +/-4.0; hw 6.6 -> floor 3 + edges at +/-4.6 reaching +/-6.6; hw 8.4 -> floor 4 + edges at +/-6.4. Road over road is invisible, so the middle overlap costs nothing.
- *** THE RANDOM OBJECTS IN THE ROAD ARE NOT RANDOM. *** Verge furniture and lamps walk down the avenue at a FIXED PITCH - 9m for litter, 22m for lamps - and nothing asked whether the next stop was a junction. On the avenue verge, which is right; in the middle of the cross street, which is not. Counted on new_anchor before: two bins, two fences, two small fences, four lamp posts in the road. Every intersection where the pitch happens to land, which is why it looks arbitrary and is completely deterministic. Both loops test standsInRoad now; swept after, ZERO on all six layouts.
- THE MANHOLES STAY. Sewerage is the one piece that belongs on a carriageway - which is why it was moved out of the furniture pool - and it is placed by the lane loop.
- A THIRD, FROM THE SAME PROBE: the kerb's junction skip was hw + t0*1.6 = 13m while PavementCornerBig only reaches hw + t0 = 10.6m, so the straight kerb was skipped for 2.4m of approach the corner never covered - and that band is KERB-ONLY territory, so it showed bare ground, two rows per junction. Skip is hw + t0 now.
- MEASURED AFTER, ALL SIX LAYOUTS: widest bare run 0.0m on radial, grid, terraced and spine; 1.0m STILL on organic and archipelago, the two highest-jitter layouts (0.24 and 0.18) whose avenues wander off the ideal line while the junction geometry is laid as if they had not. Down from a full-length strip on every radial world, and NOT closed. Recorded rather than rounded up to zero.
- A CHECK SUPERSEDED ONE PATCH AFTER IT WAS WRITTEN: 'the lane count ceilings rather than rounds' asserted the fix rather than the invariant. The invariant is that the road covers exactly the carriageway and nothing crosses it. 'Does not round' survives.
- STILL THERE AND NOT TOUCHED: the invented prism props. 58 at one camera, heights 0.5-1.1, all correctly ON the pavement rather than in the road. The blue-grey boxes - intersection barriers and rubble - are the last invented box geometry in a scene built from a kit. The pack has Fence, FenceCorner, FenceSmall, Bin, UndergroundTrashBin, Sewerage, Steps1-3 and three trees; the barriers use none of them.

## 1.10.0.6 changes (the kerb strip was a rounding, and the horizon learns what the world does for a living)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE STRIP ALONG THE KERB WAS NOT FOG. IT WAS A ROUNDING. *** lanes = round(hw*2 / t0), laid from ax - hw, so the road covered lanes*t0 metres of a carriageway 2*hw wide and the remainder was never tiled. What shows through is the PLAIN - the tinted grass patch, bright green on a garden world - in a strip running the whole length of every avenue, hard against the kerb.
- INVISIBLE ON HALF THE WORLDS, which is why it survived. The gap is 2*hw - round(2*hw/t0)*t0: grid hw 6.0 divides exactly (NO gap), hollow 6.2 leaves 0.4m, radial 6.6 leaves 1.2m, spine 8.4 leaves 0.8m. Swept: lustandia clean, yujing and new_anchor both showing a 1m-plus bare run at ax+5.5..ax+6.5 over every near z row - both radial, which is the layout in the screenshot. Every earlier attempt to reproduce ran on lustandia, one of the two layouts where the arithmetic comes out even.
- CEILING AND CENTRED. The run is at least as wide as the carriageway and any overhang lands UNDER the kerb - Road and PavementCurb's road side share the same ten unit drop. One extra one-face tile per avenue where needed. Re-swept all six layouts: widest bare run 0.0m.
- *** A SKYLINE PER WORLD, FROM LORE THE REPO ALREADY HOLDS. *** It was the same city everywhere twice over: one silhouette recoloured by the holder, then density and height by POPULATION and Circuit membership - two dials across 37 colonies, so Nova Reach and Yujing come out the same shape at the same population.
- THE SECTOR IS ALREADY THE LORE AND ALREADY IN THE DATA. Every front carries a sectorName, and ROOF_STYLE and FURNITURE have keyed off it since the kit landed. A foundry is stacks and low sheds; a finance capital is crowned towers lit to the top; a bazaar is a jumble with no plan; an agricultural world is a handful of masts. 37 worlds distinguished with nothing invented. Mechanically a BAND into TOWER_KIND rather than the old two-way nudge, which could only say "taller than average or not". Same five kinds, no new geometry.
- *** THE PER-COLONY TABLE IS DELIBERATELY NEARLY EMPTY. *** Jacob is the GM and the author; the lore of a named world is his to state, and filling 37 rows inside a renderer would be writing canon where nobody would find it again. SKYLINE_WORLD is the hook; its four rows are ones the repo already asserts elsewhere. Everything else falls through to its sector.
- BACK ON, AS PRISM, WHICH IS A DIFFERENT DECISION FROM v1.10.0.1's. That verdict was about the MODEL style - geometry at 1400m is one flat silhouette - and it stands. It was never a verdict on having a horizon; the note left the condition for earning one back, which is something that VARIES ACROSS a far facade. Prism does three face values per mass, a lighter roof slab, a window grid and a beacon mast, and it is the only way a crowned tower and a slab differ at all. One flag either way.
- city-battle-check 324 -> 337. Full suite 22/22, 10669 assertions.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.5 changes (the industrial pack is the pack we have, and the Reach's ground comes back on a dial)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE INDUSTRIAL CITY PACK AND THE CITY KIT ARE THE SAME PACK. *** city-meshes.py --check on the uploaded IndustrialCityAsset passes BYTE FOR BYTE against the shipped kit.json - 526 meshes, 18990 faces, 252 with emissive. KIT_ATTRIBUTION.txt has said so since the kit landed. There is no second, better road set being held back.
- THE PACK'S ENTIRE ROAD VOCABULARY IS NINE PIECES, all already placed: Road (1 face), Pavement (1), PavementCurb (6), PavementCornerBig (17), PavementCornerSmall (19), Crossing (24), DashedLine (6), SolidLine (6), RoadSign (25), BigRoadSign (79). Whatever is wrong with the roads, a different pack is not the fix.
- *** THE BUG WORLDS' GROUND WAS NEVER REMOVED, ONLY ORPHANED. *** v1.9.9.0 stopped drawing it and deleted nothing: pats() still builds `asph` and `pave` from the same CodeSpree sheets the Reach draws, tinted per world through the same tintedPattern, and buildClips/tessellate/replay/roadClip/paveClip/eachCarriageway/eachPavement have sat here since with NO CALLER. One draw call away, not a rebuild.
- BACK AS A DIAL, like roadWear and for the recorded reason - settled by opinion, reversed, settled again. CB.opt.groundTex blends flat tiles against tiled ground; CB.opt.texScale is the smear dial (1 = 48m patch, 0.2 = 9.6m). Two bench sliders, patch size read out in metres. DEFAULT OFF: nothing that ships changes until it is looked at.
- THREE THINGS FOUND BY TURNING IT ON: (1) only Road and Pavement may stand down - one flat face each, no relief; the kerb, mitre, crossing and lines have a PROFILE, which a ground texture cannot draw. (2) THE LOTS ARE PAVED SURFACE AND THE CLIP DID NOT KNOW IT - eachPavement walks only the strips, so the yards showed green grass inside a city; eachPaved folds CELLS in. (3) The far flat fill must BE the pattern's average - the first attempt used the plain's ground/haze mix and everything past ten metres came out the colour of the field outside the city. Graded off the kit's #585753 and #332f2c.
- *** A BLOCKER THAT IS NOT TUNING: THE PASS DRAWS BEFORE THE HAZE, THE TILES DRAW AFTER IT. *** frame() runs ground, drawRoad, paintHaze, then the sorted queue - tiles sit ON TOP of haze, a band pass sits UNDER it. Swap the surface and the mid-ground dissolves into haze at about forty metres. A draw-order problem; no slider fixes it. Moving the pass after the haze is wrong - no depth test, it would paint over men and buildings. Real options are hazing the street clip separately or splitting the haze in two. Not attempted.
- A CHECK RETIRED FOR THE RIGHT REASON: 'the painted road surface is retired' asserted the pass was DELETED, making "never draw the ground the way the Reach draws it" permanent on the strength of one double-draw fault. The invariant is exclusivity, not absence.
- city-battle-check 311 -> 324. Full suite 22/22, 10656 assertions.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.3 changes (detail stops depending on its neighbours, and a red check that was lying)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-pop-check.mjs (new), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, tools/fleet-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE POPPING WAS TWO FAULTS AND ONLY ONE WAS A CULL. *** Buildings were culled on CENTRE distance with no reach term - alone among the culls here, queueProps has measured reach since it was written. A 40m block whose middle sits at 151m is entirely absent and arrives whole one step later. Culled on the half-diagonal now, exact because the footprint is axis aligned and the test is a circle.
- *** THE REST IS NOT A CULL AT ALL. *** `rich` was `i < 8` and the window budget was spent nearest-first down the sorted list, so a building's appearance depended on its NEIGHBOURS. Walk 4m, let a block leave the radius, and the ninth becomes the eighth and switches window pools while standing still at an unchanged distance. Keyed on distance now - rich at 62m, shopfronts at 40m - with the budget kept as a CEILING. Free, because 16000/32000/64000 were already measured rendering the identical frame.
- THE SAME FAULT IN A THIRD PLACE: flora ordered on VIEW Z, which changes when the camera TURNS, so turning on the spot reshuffled the budget order and tail trees blinked. World distance now.
- *** THE OBVIOUS PROBE WAS THE WRONG ONE. *** |delta faces| per frame is mostly faces crossing the NEAR PLANE - turning gives a large delta and no popping, and by that metric this patch made yaw WORSE (314 -> 423 mean) while fixing the complaint. The probe counts DETAIL STATE FLIPS and splits legitimate (entered/left the circle, or crossed a threshold) from SPURIOUS. Spurious per frame before -> after: dolly 0.19 -> 0.00, yaw 0.00 -> 0.00, strafe 0.27 -> 0.00.
- tools/city-pop-check.mjs holds that measurement. It RESTATES the range constants, so city-battle-check ties the literals together - a restated number drifts silently and the pop check would pass against its own stale copy.
- +1834 faces, 30523 -> 32357: buildings entering earlier, not more detail per building.
- *** AND THE fleet-check FAILURE WAS NOT WHAT I SAID IT WAS. *** Reported last patch as a real player-visible leak of Coalition hulls into Circuit space. IT IS NOT. Instrumented: activeGalaxy=coalition while the block asserts about phase=jade. V.swap('jade') is REFUSED when the passage is sealed, PASSAGE_OPEN starts { jade:false }, so the view never left coalition and the check asserted "the jade view shows nothing tagged coalition" against a COALITION VIEW. The two hulls were coalition hulls in the coalition view, drawn correctly.
- A CHECK THAT SILENTLY TESTS THE WRONG STATE IS WORSE THAN ONE THAT FAILS: the failure it produces names a bug that does not exist. Fixed by asserting the swap before asserting its consequences, opening the passage for the duration and restoring it, and adding the seal's own behaviour as assertions. One tautology removed: the hook returns undefined either way, so `!== undefined || true` would have passed on a broken seal.
- FULL SUITE GREEN FOR THE FIRST TIME IN THIS SEQUENCE: 22/22, 10627 assertions. city-battle 295 -> 301, fleet 61 -> 67, city-pop 12 new.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.2 changes (the arena gets edges and the camera stops wandering)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE CITY HAD NO EDGES AND NOW IT HAS THE REACH'S. *** Cells ran x -300..300 and z -600..900, the front was clamped to a literal +/-58 no other number agreed with, units spawned in a 350m band, the camera could walk out of all of it. Nothing threw - it meant the scene had no answer to "how big is this fight" and every system answered separately.
- THE REACH ANSWERS IT ONCE: FIELD_W x FIELD_D, normalized coords through wx()/wz(), the front one scalar with clamped travel, every offset measured off it, the camera anchored to the front rather than the map. Copied, with the front on WIDTH rather than DEPTH because that is the axis a single-facing pack can be drawn along.
- *** TWO EXTENTS, NOT ONE. *** The Reach fills five field-widths of ground past the field and says why: bound the DRAWING and the world reads as a rug on a floor. ARENA bounds the FIGHT; the built city runs past it as backdrop. Cover gated at addCover, not at its five call sites.
- *** THE DEPTH IS CAPPED. *** Derived off the last cross street it came out 326-663m by layout - swept over all 66 fronts: archipelago 452-468, grid 370, organic 372-391, radial 326-334, SPINE 658-663, terraced 448-452. On a spine world 500m of the fight is permanently fog and half the army spawns into it. Capped to 330, the Reach's number. Width still varies by layout (220-373) because the front travels across it.
- *** THE CAMERA IS FIXED. *** It rode four sine terms on four periods - what you write when the scene has no edges and you want one shot to feel like several. A camera drifting up the avenue re-frames the fight every few seconds so nothing settles, and moving height plus moving yaw walks off-axis toward the arc the pack has no drawings for. Locked: z0+62, height 6.0, fixed pitch, square down the street. Only x moves, tracking the front - the camera holding still while the battle moves under it.
- AND A CINE MODE, because locked and orbit are both geometric and neither knows where anything interesting is. Cuts to the densest knot still in contact, six second hold, framed on a FLANK with jitter - the Reach's rule for the Reach's reason.
- *** THE BURNT HAULER IS GONE *** - two untextured 'rust' prisms across the centre avenue, the last invented box geometry in a street built out of a kit. ITS COVER GOES WITH IT: keeping data and dropping geometry is right for ABSTRACT things (a camp is a claim over ground), wrong for a cover slot, which is a specific place a man crouches behind something. WHAT IS LOST: the only hard cover in the open, so the avenue is crossed rather than fought over. Getting it back needs a vehicle MESH - the modern pack has trucks the bake already produces and the shipping subset excludes.
- *** THE SKYVIEW IS NOT A TEXTURE PROBLEM. *** A contact shadow is a cheat that works from ONE place. From overhead it is a black rectangle offset from a building, and the city becomes acres of grey with black mats over it. Faded on pitch, floored at a tenth, rather than removed.
- MEASURED CONSEQUENCE, FLAGGED: cover counts drop and spine takes it worst, 25-51 slots for 52 men where it had 52-117. Degrades to a firing line rather than breaking. The fix is more slots per building, not a bigger arena, and it is not in this patch.
- Harness fix: the wide/wide_prism comparison stopped forcing the skyline on when the default went false, so both frames were the same empty sky and the identical-digest warning fired on a comparison that could not differ. Forced on for the shot; wide_model added.
- city-battle-check 273 -> 295. PRE-EXISTING: fleet-check still fails 'shows nothing from the other sector', confirmed failing in the untouched v1.10.0.0 zip.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.1 changes (the skyline comes back off, and the street gets the detail instead)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), package.json, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE MODEL SKYLINE IS OFF AGAIN AND THE PICTURE IS THE ARGUMENT. *** At 1400m a 280m tower is a SILHOUETTE - every face within a few luminance values of every other, haze closing the rest - so what reaches the screen is a flat coloured rectangle four hundred pixels across behind a street that has windows, kerbs, lamps and parapets on it. Wins on area, loses on everything else. A LOOK failure, not a bug: nothing is deleted, all three styles stay behind CB.opt.skylineStyle.
- TO EARN IT BACK it needs something that varies ACROSS a far facade - lit window rows, a vertical value break, a roof line that is not one silhouette. At that range variation is the only thing distance cannot flatten.
- *** THE BENCH HUD WAS HARDCODED AND THEREFORE LYING. *** bSky was born class=on and bStyle said "glyph" while the defaults were off and 'model'. The page opened misreporting the two things it exists to look at, and the first click on either moved it the wrong way. Both read CB.opt now, and 'model' is in the rotation - it was not, so the shipped default was the one style the bench could not get back to.
- *** A WINDOW HAS THREE STATES AND TWO WERE NAMED. *** The kit ships Halflit variants for Window1, 4, 5, 6 and 7 - fourteen pieces - unused since the kit landed. They are FREE: Window7Lit is 34 faces and Window7Halflit1 is 34, and every family's halflit is exactly half its lit sibling's emissive. Split 3 lit / 2 half / 3 unlit, which is the same lit-pane area over five windows instead of four, so brightness does not move. A family with no halflit SPLITS its fallback rather than defaulting to lit, or the cheap pool (Window10, 8, 9) comes out a quarter brighter than the rich one.
- *** HALF THE DOORS IN THE CITY WERE THE SAME DOOR. *** Door1 and Door2 have no Lit variant; Door3 has no plain variant. The old composition appended 'Lit' to whichever family it rolled, so Door1Lit, Door2Lit and Door3 all named nothing and the fallback turned all three into Door1 - half of every entrance in the city, while Door3Unlit was never drawn once. A fallback that lands on a real piece never warns.
- A MEASUREMENT THAT CORRECTS THE SOURCE'S OWN COMMENT: KIT_WINDOW_BUDGET is described there as the single biggest lever on detail and it is not the constraint at all. 16000, 32000 and 64000 all render 28144 faces. THE REAL LEVER IS viewRadius: 150 -> 28144, 220 -> 40803, 300 -> 56108. Detail is bought by drawing more city, not by spending more on the part already drawn.
- *** AND THE GROUND FLOOR IS NOT ANOTHER STOREY. *** Every module came from the same pool, so a ground floor was a row of office windows with a door punched in the middle - an elevation seen from four hundred metres up, not a street seen from the pavement this camera stands on. Window2 is what the pack has for it and it is the one piece the pools DELIBERATELY EXCLUDE, on the recorded grounds that at 146 faces no camera is close enough for a centrepiece. True of an upper storey, one cell in a grid of forty. NOT true of a shopfront directly in front of you: nine emissive faces, the widest glazing in the kit.
- SEPARATE BUDGET, not a share of KIT_WINDOW_BUDGET - out of that one, two near buildings' ground floors would starve every upper storey in the frame. Spent nearest-first, so it lands on the buildings the camera is among and stops. Five in eight lit rather than the facade's three (a shop at night is lit or shuttered), on a hash sharing no bits with the facade's so the ground floor does not light in lockstep with the storey above it.
- IT ALSO DOES THE HORIZON'S OLD JOB: a lit band down both sides of the avenue draws the eye along the street, which is where this scene should have been sending it.
- NOT DONE, ON A MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN A PRIORITY: PavementCornerSmall was listed as an unused junction piece and is not one. Off the bake, Big and Small are the SAME 20-unit L in opposite handedness - Big +X/+Z, Small +X/-Z - so four rotations of Big already cover every corner an orthogonal grid has. Placing it would be decoration invented to use a piece up. Kit trees left alone too: Tree1-3 are 627-835 faces and seven is a fifth of the frame for what the nature pack's urban flora row already spends on.
- PRE-EXISTING AND NOT FROM THIS PATCH: fleet-check fails 'shows nothing from the other sector' - the galaxy ship layer leaks Coalition hulls into the Circuit view after a swap. Confirmed failing in the untouched v1.10.0.0 zip.
- +2392 faces, 28144 -> 30536. city-battle-check 253 -> 273.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.10.0.0 changes (a skyline of real towers, and a pack that needed a different baker)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/space/city/modern.json (new, generated), tools/city-meshes.py, client/assets/city-battle.js, KIT_ATTRIBUTION.txt, client/assets/asset-credits.js, docs/CREDITS.md, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE PACK SHIPS NO TEXTURES AT ALL - 70 FBX AND NOT ONE IMAGE - so the palette-UV lookup the kit baker is built on does not exist for it. *** It carries a Material per surface with a DiffuseColor and a LayerElementMaterial assigning one ByPolygon: the same information by another route, ONE FLAT COLOUR PER FACE.
- *** THE FIRST BAKE CAME OUT WITH EVERY BUILDING EXACTLY 2x2 UNITS. *** The meshes are UNIT CUBES and all their shape is in the Model's Lcl Scaling, which the kit baker never had to read. Ignoring it turns a sixty storey tower and a corner shop into THE SAME BOX. The pack is also Z-UP - building_12 spans z -1..59 - so scale, translate and swap to Y-up in one pass.
- THE SKYLINE IS REAL BUILDINGS. THE CHEAP ONES ARE THE TALL ONES: building_12 is 30x90x30 units at FOUR HUNDRED AND TWENTY FACES, because a plain tower is a few extruded boxes while a detailed low-rise is a hundred mullions. A skyline wants the tall cheap ones - the opposite of what a street wants - so the two draw from different ends of one pack.
- TWO CORRECTIONS AFTER LOOKING: the ring distances were tuned for flat SPRITES, which read as distant whatever their size, and geometry does not - a 280m tower at 620m is a building at the end of the street. The near ring is skipped; the skyline starts where the city ends. And the materials are DAYLIGHT (0.8 grey, saturated blue glass), so they are graded to night or the far city glows brighter than the street in front of it - which the first attempt did.
- LICENCE: PURCHASED, BLOCK LIFTED FOR THE RIGHT REASON. The block was correct while the terms were unknown AND unbought; the purchase settles USE. It does NOT settle redistribution - the page still states no terms - so derived mesh data ships and the source FBX does not. 38491748 credited in the panel and in docs/CREDITS.md.
- NOT TAKEN: trains, helicopters, tanks, forklifts. Good models at 1,500-4,000 faces each; this is a city battlefield, not a rail yard. Already baked by the tool - only the shipping subset excludes them.
- city-battle-check 242 -> 253. Two assertions RETIRED for the right reasons: one asserted this pack was BLOCKED (the purchase overturned it), one asserted the skyline was OFF (it became a different thing to have on the horizon).
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

It is a 512px pattern covering forty metres of world being asked to hold at close range, and no band sizing helps with that.

- THE PLAIN OUTSIDE KEEPS THE BAND PASS, deliberately. Out there it is forty metres per tile of distant ground, which is exactly what the pattern is for.
- The audit gained the camera angle this was REPORTED from - on the pavement looking down at it. None of the nine existing angles were pointed there, which is why it took a screenshot to find.

## 1.9.8.1 changes (road wear behind a dial, defaulting to flat)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THIS SETTING HAS MOVED THREE TIMES IN THREE PATCHES: flat, jittered, wider, flat again. That is a TASTE call, not a correctness one, and one that keeps flipping belongs BEHIND A NUMBER rather than in the source.
- CB.opt.roadWear scales both terms from one value. 0 = exactly the palette colour, a poured uniform surface. ~0.35 = a hint that shows up close and vanishes at range. 1 = full spread with repair patches.
- Patches switch off at 0.5 rather than tapering: a half strength repair patch is not a subtler repair, it is a SMUDGE.
- AT ZERO THE PATCH PASS DOES NOT RUN AT ALL, so uniform is also the CHEAPEST setting - one face per tile and nothing else.
- Slider in the bench, so the question is settled by LOOKING rather than by another patch.
- Unchanged otherwise: ~26,000 faces at 150m radius, all 66 fronts render and fight, nine audit angles render. city-battle-check 235 -> 239.

## 1.9.8.0 changes (skyline off, and three ratios corrected against the author's own city)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE SKYLINE IS OFF AND IT WAS NEVER A PERFORMANCE COST. *** MEASURED FIRST: 35 sprites, 1.25 megapixels, 40 drawImage calls, 2.3ms of a 472ms frame - UNDER HALF A PERCENT. Five megabytes of texture is not a memory problem here. It is off because it was ASKED FOR - a legitimate reason and a different one. WHAT IT COSTS: the horizon goes FLAT, since the glyph towers were the only thing occupying the far half of a wide shot. Sprite cell 7x9 -> 5x7, so turning it back on costs a third of the memory.
- *** THE AUTHOR'S PAID DEMO CITY WAS USED AS A MEASUREMENT, NOT AS AN ASSET. *** Nothing from it is baked or shipped; that position is unchanged. But COUNTING what the pack's author built with their own kit is free and is better evidence than taste.
- 738 pavement tiles against 617 of road: THE WALKWAY IS WIDER THAN THE CARRIAGEWAY. Ours had one tile against three lanes, which is why the street read as a road with edges rather than a street.
- 55 of Lamp2 against 6 of Lamp1: the CHEAP 47-FACE POST IS THE STANDARD and the 142-face lamp the exception. We had it nearly backwards.
- Roughly two windows per plain wall - a coverage we are still below.
- IT ALSO SHOWED A PIECE WE WERE NOT USING: Window10 ships Bot/Mid/Top segments that stack into a CONTINUOUS vertical strip up a bay, and their scene uses 373 of the Mid alone - more than any other single piece. That is a curtain wall instead of punched holes. Glazing is a property of the BAY now, hashed on its index and held all the way up: a strip that stops halfway is a mistake, not a variation.
- THE ROAD IS A SURFACE, NOT A SLAB. Jitter 0.86-1.14 -> 0.66-1.28: on tarmac the old range was four or five values and still read as one poured sheet. And ONE TILE IN SEVEN IS A PATCH - tarmac is not one age, it is the original surface with repairs cut into it, and a repair is darker and squarer than weathering. One extra face on one tile in seven.
- ~25,000 faces at 150m radius. All 66 fronts render and fight; nine audit angles render. city-battle-check 227 -> 235.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.9.7.0 changes (three roof families, trees, steps, lamp LOD, and a pack that is not all one scale)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** NOT EVERY PIECE IN THIS PACK IS AUTHORED AT THE MODULE SCALE. *** Measured at the 200-unit module scale: lamp 5.8m, bin 1.3m, sign 4.9m, fence 2.0m - correct. A TREE IS 32 METRES. A rooftop barrel is 5.6m and the big one 8.5m. Steps are 6m TALL. The kit is modular at module scale and its SET PIECES ARE NOT - dropping them in at KIT_S lined the street with trees taller than the buildings, which is what the first attempt produced. Scale is a property of the piece now, measured once and recorded.
- THREE ROOF FAMILIES, AND THEY ARE THREE SILHOUETTES: Roof1 a low parapet (4 faces), Roof2 a TALL parapet with railings (26), Roof3 a cornice that PROJECTS past the wall (7). A bank, a plant room and a pre-war block - the difference reads at a hundred metres because it changes the OUTLINE, not the surface. Chosen by sector first, seed second.
- DOORS GET STEPS. A door opening onto bare pavement is a door in a wall; the kit's three step blocks make it an ENTRANCE.
- TWO LAMPS, AND THE CHEAP ONE IS THE POINT: Lamp1 142 faces, Lamp2 47 - three-fold, for something that is a silhouette and two glowing heads past thirty metres. Three times the lamps for the same faces, and the street stops being one repeated object. Lamp cap 14 -> 30, furniture 26 -> 46.
- SERVICE COVERS MOVED INTO THE ROAD, where a manhole is. They were in the furniture pool, which put them on the pavement.
- TREES ARE IN, capped at seven and nearest-first: at 627-835 faces EACH they are the costliest object in the scene, more than a whole building's walls.
- WINDOW BUDGET 7,200 -> 16,000 FACES. The single biggest lever on how detailed the city looks, set when the renderer was fighting for frames. Spent nearest-first on VISIBLE elevations only, so it extends detail OUTWARD rather than piling it on the front row.
- ~14,000 -> ~25,000 faces at 150m radius, ~37,000 straight down. THE VIEW RADIUS SLIDER IS STILL THE DIAL and it now moves considerably more than it did.
- All 66 fronts render and fight; nine audit angles render. city-battle-check 218 -> 227, and three of its assertions named literals the roof families and lamp LOD correctly displaced - rewritten to test BEHAVIOUR rather than the string.

## 1.9.6.0 changes (junctions, cross-street kerbs and edge lines)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE GRID WAS THREE PARALLEL AVENUES, NOT A STREET NETWORK. *** Kerbs ran STRAIGHT THROUGH every junction; cross streets had NO kerb and NO pavement, only carriageway. Same omission twice: nothing described what happens where two streets MEET, because each avenue was laid independently and a junction is where that assumption breaks.
- The straight kerb STOPS SHORT of a crossing now. Corners are MITRED with PavementCornerBig - measured off the bake as an OUTER corner carrying the carriageway in its (+X,+Z) quadrant, which fixes all four rotations by EVALUATION rather than by trying them.
- FOURTH PIECE IN THIS PACK PLACED FROM A MEASUREMENT INSTEAD OF AN ASSUMPTION, and the first three were all wrong the first time. The measurement is now the cheaper habit.
- Cross streets get the same kerb and pavement, and need NO quarter turn: a cross street runs along X, which is PavementCurb's own axis - only the mirror. A street with a kerb one way and a raw edge the other reads as a service cut through a real road.
- SolidLine is in as the road EDGE line against the kerb - the marking that makes a carriageway look bounded rather than merely dark.
- ~12,500 -> ~14,200 faces at 150m radius, ~22,000 looking straight down. The junctions are the most detailed thing in the scene AND where the eye goes, so that is where the faces belong.
- STILL UNUSED, EACH FOR A STATED REASON: trees are 627-835 faces EACH and the nature pack draws them for a fraction; PavementCornerSmall is the INNER corner and this grid has none, every block being rectangular; Roof2/Roof3 are pitched families - a look decision, not a fix; Lamp2 at 47 faces would make a good distance LOD against Lamp1's 142.
- All 66 fronts render and fight; all nine audit angles render. city-battle-check 213 -> 218.

## 1.9.5.0 changes (the window economy, and the roof pieces the kit had been shipping unused)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** 'ORBIT REMOVES THE WINDOWS' WAS THE WINDOW ECONOMY, NOT CULLING. THREE FAULTS STACKED. ***
- (1) WINDOW KINDS COST 18 TO 146 FACES and the picker sampled all ten UNIFORMLY - one window in ten cost as much as EIGHT. That emptied the budget partway down the sorted list; from orbit, twenty buildings in range instead of fourteen, so the cliff lands mid-frame. Window2 (146) is now in NEITHER pool: a building's whole allowance buys FOUR of them, which is how the NEAREST building became the blankest on screen.
- (2) *** THE BUDGET WAS SPENT ON ELEVATIONS FACING AWAY FROM THE CAMERA. *** Four sides walked in fixed order, two of them back-face culled - so a building seen from the front-right spent its allowance on the back and left, threw those windows away, and put PLAIN WALL on the two sides actually on screen. The more modules a building had, the faster it starved itself on invisible geometry. Visibility is tested once per side before anything is spent, and hidden sides are SKIPPED rather than pushed and culled - cheaper AND better.
- (3) A FLAT ALLOWANCE STARVES A TALL TOWER: nine storeys have three times the modules of three and got the same share, so the biggest thing in frame stayed the emptiest. Allowance scales with VISIBLE module count.
- A THIRD OF THE KIT WAS SHIPPED AND NEVER PLACED. Roofs matter most: a flat slab is what an orbit or overhead camera mostly sees. Roof1 is a 4-face edge, Roof1Corner a 10-face corner - a whole parapet costs less than three windows - and RoofBarrel adds 18 faces of clutter on near roofs, which is what stops a roof reading as a LID. Parapet direction MEASURED off the bake: third time that has been the difference between a piece landing right and backwards.
- NOT TAKEN, WITH A REASON: the kit's trees are 627-835 faces EACH. Three cost more than every window in the frame, and the nature pack draws trees for a fraction.
- STILL IMPERFECT: one front in sixty-six intermittently trips the sweep's engagement threshold, then passed three consecutive runs. A front that engages just after 45s, not a regression - recorded rather than tuned away.
- city-battle-check 204 -> 213.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.9.4.0 changes (road grain, sector litter, rooms instead of shells, and a sort that stops popping)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** ROAD GRAIN: THE VARIATION IS THE TILE. *** One flat colour per tile made a featureless slab at eye level. Each tile is jittered a few percent off its own palette colour - no texture, no second asset, CANNOT MISALIGN. Hashed on position, not rolled: a per-frame roll makes a road BOIL.
- *** A FOUNDRY DOES NOT LITTER LIKE A BANK. *** Bins, fences, signs, steps and sewerage shipped with the kit and were never placed. Weighted by SECTOR, and the RATE varies too - a bazaar verge is crowded, an agricultural one nearly bare. Capped and culled: Bin is 40 faces, RoadSign 25.
- *** A BUILDING WAS A SHELL YOU COULD SEE STRAIGHT OUT OF. *** Walls are single-sided with outward normals, so from inside every one is culled and the camera sees the whole street through its own building - about a second of free-cam away. I PROPOSED THIS FROM A SCREENSHOT THEN CHECKED IT PROPERLY: my first 'inside' audit camera was actually OUTSIDE a building, so the harness now asks the renderer for a real building's centre and stands there. Inverting the cull for that one building is what a room looks like; costs a boolean.
- *** THE PAINTER SORT USED EACH FACE'S NEAREST CORNER. *** A long wall seen obliquely has one corner close and the rest far, so it sorted as if the whole wall were at its nearest point and POPPED IN FRONT OF THINGS IT IS BEHIND - the orbit-camera flicker. The centroid costs the same and is where the face actually is. STILL AN APPROXIMATION: painter sorting cannot resolve interpenetrating or cyclic polygons at all, and that is the renderer's limit, not a knob. Applied to the nature meshes and prisms too.
- The audit is nine viewpoints. All 66 fronts render and fight. city-battle-check 194 -> 204.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.9.3.0 changes (a multi-angle audit, and the two faults it found immediately)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** A SCENE CORRECT FROM ONE ANGLE IS NOT A CORRECT SCENE, AND EVERY FAULT SO FAR HAS PROVED IT. *** Walls facing inward look fine head-on and hollow from a corner; a kerb laid across the street reads as texture from above and a ladder at eye level; a roof at the wrong height is invisible until you are over it. Eight fixed viewpoints now render together, each checked for a throw and for a frame that draws ALMOST NOTHING - which is what 'everything got culled' looks like from outside.
- *** THE KERB WAS RUNNING ACROSS THE STREET. *** PavementCurb carries its whole profile on Z - road at +Z ten units low, pavement at -Z flush, lip between - so its kerb LINE runs along X. An avenue runs along Z, so unrotated it stepped the kerb ACROSS the carriageway every four metres and tiled into a LADDER of light and dark rungs. That was 'the road looks like stripes'.
- SECOND TIME THIS PACK'S PIECES HAVE BEEN PLACED BY ASSUMING AN AXIS INSTEAD OF READING ONE, after the wall elevations. Same lesson both times: THE GEOMETRY SAYS WHICH WAY A PIECE FACES AND IT TAKES ONE MEASUREMENT TO ASK.
- *** BUILDINGS OVERHUNG THEIR LOTS: NOTHING RECONCILED METRES WITH MODULES. *** A 17m frontage became four 4m modules laid across 17m of lot - every building overhanging by up to half a module, walls standing slightly in the road. Footprints snap to whole modules now, and they snap BEFORE the collision box and cover slots are built: making the drawing agree with itself while the simulation kept the old footprint would be WORSE than the overhang, because men would take cover against a wall that is not where they think it is.
- All eight angles render; all 66 fronts render and fight. ~11,000 faces at 150m radius, ~16,000 straight down.
- STILL OPEN: the carriageway is one flat colour per tile because that is what the kit ships - no grain, no patching - so at eye level the near road is a large featureless area. That is the pack's look rather than a fault, and it is the next thing worth a decision. Bins, fences, signs, steps and sewerage remain unplaced.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.9.2.0 changes (buildings closed on all four sides, and the street laid from the kit)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** OPEN ON TWO SIDES, AND THE CAUSE WAS A MINUS SIGN I REASONED ABOUT INSTEAD OF EVALUATING. *** Rotating (0,0,1) about Y by theta gives (-sin theta, 0, cos theta). -PI/2 evaluates to (+1,0,0) - the RIGHT elevation - and I had assigned it to the LEFT. Both side walls faced INTO the building, were culled, and every block lost two of four elevations. It does not look like a rotation bug; it looks like a building you can SEE STRAIGHT THROUGH. Asserted as an exact array now.
- THE STREET IS THE KIT'S TOO: Road, PavementCurb, Pavement, Crossing, DashedLine and Lamp1 replace the tinted band pass, the kerb strips, the painted dashes and the grey prism gantries. Same 200-unit module as the buildings, so street and blocks line up BY CONSTRUCTION - most of why the old road never quite met the pavement.
- THE ROAD WAS BRIEFLY DRAWN TWICE and showed exactly that: noise under every road tile, a painted dash under every real one. The old surface is DELETED, not layered. The ground plain and lot fill survive - the kit has no piece for either.
- TILED OVER THE VIEW RADIUS, NOT THE MAP: a road tile is one face, cheap each and ruinous over a field - 400m square at 4m a tile is TEN THOUSAND. Same radius the props and flora follow.
- LAMPS ARE THE EXPENSIVE PIECE: Lamp1 is 142 faces, as much as five buildings' worth of walls. Spaced, culled, capped at fourteen. A lamp you cannot see is 142 faces of nothing.
- All 66 fronts render and fight; ~11,400 faces at a 150m radius.
- STILL ROUGH, SAID PLAINLY: the kerb reads as a repeating STEPPED profile rather than a continuous edge, and the pavement is darker than it should be beside the lit shopfronts. Both are PLACEMENT, not geometry. The kit's bins, fences, signs, steps, sewerage and trees are still unplaced.
