## 1.9.1.0 changes (the city is built from modules and the texture pipeline is deleted)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/space/city/ (facade sheets REMOVED), ATTRIBUTION.txt, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, tools/city-maps-check.mjs, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** ~450 LINES DELETED: atlas loader, night bake, composed cell sheets, affine texQuad, roof mapper. *** DELETED, NOT DISABLED - two ways to draw a wall is one for a future patch to pick wrongly.
- Every wall, window, door and roof is a 200-unit module whose faces carry ONE baked palette colour, which canvas fills EXACTLY at any angle and distance.
- *** FOUR MEASURED BUGS. *** (1) Pieces are authored normal +Z, origin at module CENTRE, so each elevation's rotation is FIXED - front PI, back 0, left -PI/2, right +PI/2. Wrong rotation does not draw a wall inside out, it CULLS it, and a building with half its elevations culled reads as LOOSE CARDS IN THE STREET. (2) A piece spans half a module either side of its origin, so s*MOD put the GROUND FLOOR HALF UNDERGROUND. (3) kitB accumulated across setZone and STACKED FIVE CITIES - it presented as a suspiciously detailed frame, and the face count HALVING after the fix IS the fix. (4) WallBlack is rgb(9,15,11): a daylight colour that at night is a HOLE IN THE STREET, excluded by name.
- FACE COST IS THE BUDGET AND THE PIECES ARE WILDLY UNEQUAL: wall ONE face, window TWENTY-TWO, door sixteen, roof centre one. Windows spent nearest-first; everything past wears plain wall - same silhouette at distance. The budget was MEASURED, not picked: only two of a window's 22 faces are glass, so 2600 gave the whole street TWENTY-NINE emissive faces and read as an unlit city with frames drawn on it.
- The facade pool retired into the kit's ten colourways: Circuit the colder half, Coalition the warmer, Gray Bazaar all ten. A building picks one so every piece matches.
- THE PAPPTIMUS SHEETS ARE DELETED AND papptimus STAYS CREDITED. CC BY attribution is owed for USE and they were used; dropping the credit because the files left would be READING THE LICENCE BACKWARDS.
- All 66 fronts render and fight. city-battle-check 208 -> 182, AND THE DROP IS THE POINT: 26 assertions guarded code that no longer exists, each removed with a note saying what replaced it rather than left to rot green.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.9.0.0 changes (canvas cannot map a texture in perspective, so the city stops using textures)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: tools/city-meshes.py (new), client/assets/space/city/kit.json (new, generated), KIT_ATTRIBUTION.txt (new), client/assets/asset-credits.js, docs/CREDITS.md, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** ONE ROOT CAUSE, AND IT IS NOT TUNABLE. *** Canvas 2D draws an image through an AFFINE transform ONLY. A rectangle in perspective is a general quad - a PROJECTIVE map - and no affine can express one. Subdivision shrinks the error and NEVER REACHES ZERO, and what is left IS the bowing and the hairline seams. Every patch to that pipeline was chasing an asymptote. The photo-texture-on-a-box approach was THE WRONG ASSET CLASS FOR THIS RENDERER from the day it went in.
- *** A PALETTE-TEXTURED MESH SIDESTEPS IT ENTIRELY. *** Voloshka's kit UVs every face at a flat swatch, so a face has exactly ONE colour - and a flat-filled polygon is drawn EXACTLY at any angle and distance. Same path the nature meshes have taken since they shipped; they have never bowed or gapped once.
- tools/city-meshes.py is a binary FBX 7.x reader that bakes the kit into the format this renderer already draws, resolving each polygon's UV against the palette AT BUILD TIME and shipping COLOURS INSTEAD OF TEXTURE COORDINATES. The runtime does no texturing at all. Sampled at the UV CENTROID, not a corner - the swatches are separated by dark gridlines and a corner sample lands on one often enough to put a BLACK FACE in the middle of a wall.
- 526 meshes, 18,990 faces, 252 emissive. THE KIT IS A STRICT MODULAR GRID: every wall, window, door, roof, road and pavement piece is the same 200-unit tile, so a building is ASSEMBLED FROM SQUARES rather than approximated by one.
- *** LICENCES DIFFER SHARPLY. *** Voloshka free edition: '100% free to download and use in your personal or commercial projects', tagged Royalty Free. Commercial use EXPLICITLY GRANTED; redistribution not addressed, so the FBX and palette PNGs are NOT in this repo and only the derived kit.json is. The PAID pre-assembled scene is deliberately unused - also the better engineering answer, since a fixed demo scene would be ONE CITY ON SIXTY-SIX WORLDS.
- *** 38491748's PACK IS BLOCKED: a $2 purchase with NO LICENCE TEXT ANYWHERE and no readme in the archive. *** That is the tile pack situation again and that one has blocked ship.sh for months. Nothing from it is baked or referenced, and the check asserts so BY NAME. It also ships no textures at all - 70 FBX and not one image.
- WHAT IS NOT DONE: the scene still draws the old textured facades. This patch delivers the PIPELINE, the ASSETS and the LICENCE POSITION, verified end to end. Wiring the kit into the block generator and retiring the facade path is the next patch, and pretending otherwise would be worse than saying so.
- city-battle-check 189 -> 208.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.8.4.0 changes (the sheets are atlases, and the walls tore up close)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** EACH SHEET IS A 2x2 ATLAS OF 128px CELLS, NOT A TILEABLE FACADE. *** Upper-floor windows top-left, shutters top-right, glazed SHOPFRONT bottom-left, DOUBLE DOORS bottom-right. Stretching the whole sheet per storey put a shopfront and a pair of doors ON EVERY FLOOR OF EVERY ELEVATION.
- THE TELL WAS THERE THE FIRST DAY AND I READ PAST IT: emissive mass measures 94 in the bottom-left quadrant and about zero in the other three. That is a sheet telling you where its street level is.
- COMPOSED CELL BY CELL NOW. Bottom row of the wall draws STREET cells, every row above draws UPPER cells, and the column each cell takes is a HASH OF ITS OWN POSITION - variation without a roll that would shimmer between frames or make two elevations of one corner disagree about what floor they are.
- BOUNDED BY CONSTRUCTION: cells composed at 64px (pixel art at distance), bays and storeys quantised, cache dropped on setZone. A few dozen canvases for the city you are looking at.
- *** THE WALLS TORE UP CLOSE BECAUSE THE SUBDIVISION CAP WAS A CONSTANT. *** The affine error is driven by DEPTH RATIO but only VISIBLE in proportion to SCREEN SIZE, and standing in the street both go up at once - a cap of six columns meant each column was two hundred pixels of pure shear. The cap scales with screen width now, and a wall tall on screen is cut vertically too. Far walls unchanged, still one quad. A subdivided wall samples the MATCHING slice, so it shows the same picture rather than repeating per piece.
- *** ONE THING FOUND AND NOT FIXED, SAID PLAINLY. *** Adding a street-level angle produced two frames BYTE FOR BYTE IDENTICAL from cameras 100m and 40 degrees apart, and every shot after matched too. A clean process renders those cameras correctly, and the ctx save/restore pairs balance - the obvious suspect. THE CAUSE IS NOT FOUND. What shipped is a DETECTOR: every frame is digested and a match with the previous is reported loudly, so it cannot pass silently again. The street shot is taken EARLY where the run is still honest, and that ordering is labelled a workaround in the file rather than left looking like a preference.
- city-battle-check 178 -> 189.

## 1.8.3.1 changes (a Path2D has no beginPath, and the harness could not see it)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE BENCH WAS BLACK BECAUSE A Path2D HAS NO beginPath. *** replay() is called with two KINDS of target: a context, which needs beginPath, and a Path2D, which IS a path and has no such method. It throws, drawRoad throws, frame throws, and requestAnimationFrame SWALLOWS THE EXCEPTION AND NEVER RE-QUEUES. The loop dies on frame one: canvas never draws, HUD frozen on dashes. It looks exactly like 'nothing loaded' and it was ONE LINE.
- *** THE HARNESS COULD NOT SEE IT, AND THAT IS THE SECOND TIME. *** node-canvas has no Path2D so the browser branch was never taken. The first was COLONY_VISUAL, which the harness supplied for itself. ONE SHAPE BOTH TIMES: THE CHECK AND THE PRODUCT DISAGREED ABOUT THE ENVIRONMENT, and the harness was the only consumer that could not hit the bug.
- The harness now SHIMS a Path2D: minimal, not a working path, and WITHOUT beginPath on purpose, so anything assuming one throws headlessly exactly as in Chrome. ctx.clip accepts and ignores it - a slightly wrong headless picture in exchange for the browser path being EXECUTED. CB_NO_PATH2D=1 tests the fallback. DRIVEN: reintroducing the bug now fails the harness with the same TypeError the browser gave.
- SECOND FIX, AND IT GENERALISES: a throw in the render loop was a black screen and nothing else - the stack was in the console and nowhere a person testing a RENDERER would look. The bench catches it, puts the message ON SCREEN, and KEEPS RUNNING, because a fault in one frame is not always a fault in every frame. Had this existed yesterday the report would have been one line instead of a screenshot of a black canvas.
- city-battle-check 172 -> 178.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.8.3.0 changes (the ten frames a second, measured; collision; view radius and fog)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** PROFILED FIRST. 49,357 CLIP QUADS AND 3,631 texQuad CALLS PER FRAME. *** Each texQuad is save + path + ctx.clip + transform + drawImage + restore, and ctx.clip is among the most expensive calls a canvas has.
- bandPass invokes its clip CALLBACK PER BAND, and the road/pavement callbacks re-tessellated the whole street network every time: six streets, 160 segments each, projected from scratch, 25 times a frame, twice over. THE CLIP IS IDENTICAL FOR EVERY BAND. Built once per frame into a coordinate array and replayed, keyed on the camera so a still view rebuilds nothing, Path2D where it exists. 1,486 tessellations -> 12.
- A facade was one quad per column PER STOREY PER LAYER. Both are bakeable: a facade repeated N times vertically is just a taller texture, and baking the shade lets the emissive go on top in the same bake. Cached on sheet, storeys and a five step shade bucket - a few dozen canvases for the whole city. COLUMNS ONLY now.
- A VIEW RADIUS WITH FOG. A city does not get cheaper by being SMALLER, it gets cheaper by being NEARER: the whole city still exists and only what is inside the radius is DRAWN. Men keep fighting in streets you cannot see, which is what a front line is. THE FOG IS NOT A DISGUISE FOR THE CUT, IT IS WHAT MAKES THE CUT LEGAL - its onset is COMPUTED from the radius, so wind one down and the other closes with it.
- Combined: 3,631 texQuad -> ~600, 2,819 polygon fills -> ~1,000. Roughly a SIX FOLD cut in the operations a browser is slow at.
- STATED PLAINLY: the harness runs on cairo, which is slow at pattern fills and fast at clips; a browser is the reverse. THE OPERATION COUNTS ARE THE HONEST METRIC for a Chrome frame rate. The cairo millisecond total barely moved and would have been the wrong number to quote.
- *** BUILDINGS HAVE COLLISION, AND THE FIRST VERSION TRAPPED EVERYONE. *** Cover sits outside a building so the line LOOKED right while the path to a far slot went through forty metres of concrete. A block grid makes collision a rectangle test; only masses above knee height block, because a kerb and a planter are cover, not walls.
- *** AXIS REJECTION ALONE IS NOT ENOUGH AND THE SWEEP PROVED IT. *** One front stopped engaging: 33 of 52 units still advancing after 45s, 32 of them more than three metres from the cover they had claimed. A wall across your path is slid along; a wall running the wrong way is slid along FOREVER, and a concave corner refuses both axes and stops a man for good. COLLISION THAT TRAPS UNITS IS WORSE THAN NO COLLISION. Progress is measured, and a unit with none for 1.5s commits to a perpendicular DETOUR on a side taken from its index and HELD - re-choosing every frame is how you get a man oscillating in a doorway.
- Sweep default 30s -> 45s: going ROUND a block is genuinely slower than going through it, and that is the change working rather than a threshold to hide behind.
- city-battle-check 154 -> 172.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.8.2.1 changes (the test runner was serving another folder's tree)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: test.bat, test.sh, tools/serve.mjs, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE ORDER OF TWO LINES WAS THE WHOLE BUG. *** test.bat opened the browser and THEN started the server. A serve.mjs left running from an OLDER FOLDER held port 8177, the new server exited on EADDRINUSE, and the tab connected to the OLD PROCESS - serving last week's tree happily. EVERY SYMPTOM POINTED AT A BROWSER CACHE AND NONE OF IT WAS: files current, bust correct, A DIFFERENT SERVER ANSWERED.
- REPRODUCED END TO END before fixing: old tree on 8177 serving 1.7.6.0, new tree's server exits 1, curl returns 1.7.6.0.
- server/start_server.bat has freed its port with netstat and taskkill SINCE IT SHIPPED. This ignored the repo's own established pattern and paid for it.
- test.bat: frees the port, starts the server, POLLS UNTIL IT ANSWERS, compares the answering build against client/version.json, and only then opens the browser - going red if they disagree.
- test.sh: the same, and it SELF HEALS. Not every machine has lsof or fuser, so the eviction can silently do nothing; it detects that the wrong tree answered and MOVES TO A FREE PORT rather than only complaining. Verified against a genuinely stale tree: detects 1.7.6.0 on 8177, serves 1.8.2.x on 8178.
- serve.mjs: a busy port is no longer a one line complaint. It ASKS THE OCCUPANT what build it serves and says so - 'something else is on this port' is not actionable, 'the thing on this port is serving 1.7.6.0 from another folder, and that is why your browser shows an old build' is. It also prints the FOLDER and BUILD on startup, so the first line of output answers the question. --auto steps to a free port.
- city-battle-check 145 -> 154, driving the startup ORDER in both scripts, the build verification, the self heal and the busy-port diagnostic. This bug was two lines in the wrong order and NOTHING would have caught it.

## 1.8.1.0 changes (the bench checks its own build, and climate reaches the sky, the planting and the skyline)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** NOT A CACHED FILE. AN OLD FOLDER. *** serve.mjs already sends Cache-Control: no-store, so a bench on :8177 CANNOT serve a stale script - a build of 1.7.6.0 in the corner means THE FILES ON DISK ARE 1.7.6.0. Unfixable from inside the renderer, very fixable from inside the page: the bench fetches version.json no-store and compares it to the BUILD the loaded scripts carry. Different, and the page gets a RED BAR naming both numbers and saying which folder to check. A bench serving an old tree looks IDENTICAL to one serving a current tree; the only tell was a five character string nobody reads.
- *** THE SKY WAS A CONSTANT. *** `PAL.sky = [22,34,52]` sat in buildPal with a comment saying it was safe because the ground never reads it - TRUE, AND STILL WRONG. Ice, lava and garden worlds all fought under identical skies, so the half of the frame that is sky carried NO information about where you were. planet-palette already ships a sky per body; it is a DAYLIGHT value, so it is GRADED toward night rather than replaced. Hue and relative saturation are the world's, value is the engagement's. Zenith is the same grade taken further.
- PLANTING WAS ONE MIX FOR EVERY COLONY - the same mistake as one sky. reach-battle.js already solved the shape: growth is decided by the TERRAIN KEY, which is already the climate statement the palette reads. Eight urban recipes, one per shipped terrain: cactus on dust, dead timber on veins, fungus on rift, snowbanks and winter timber on ice, rubble and almost nothing on a station deck, a real canopy ONLY on garden worlds. DENSITY VARIES TOO - a station at a garden world's density is a garden world growing different weeds.
- THE SKYLINE WAS ONE CITY ON EVERY WORLD IN THE ATTACKER'S COLOUR. Two errors: the far towers are the city being fought OVER, not the army's property, and a Circuit capital of eleven generations does not look like a thirty year old mining claim. Colour is the HOLDER's; density reads the colony's population string; Circuit worlds bias to stepped/tapered/crowned and frontier colonies to slabs - bought with a biased index, not new geometry.
- city-battle-check 123 -> 131.
- NOT IN THIS PATCH, DELIBERATELY: the GM war panel and council war approval. Both touch the LIVE ECONOMY rather than the renderer, and the questions they raise are listed in the handover rather than answered unilaterally.

The grass patches and the whole city/ directory are NEW, so a browser that opened the bench before they landed remembers the miss and never asks again.

The zone's SECTOR sets height, lot, setback and density - what a district DOES is the best predictor of its streets: finance is tall and narrow-lotted, a foundry is low and set back, a bazaar is dense and short. Circuit/Coalition space biases the facade pool.

A colony marked radial gets converging spacing, NOT A WHEEL - because the sprite pack needs the fighting to cross x and a true radial plan puts lanes at angles the art cannot be drawn along.

THEY ARE ORBITAL. A default would have produced a convincing city battle on a station with no surface - worse than none, because the picture would be LYING ABOUT THE WORLD confidently and nothing downstream could tell.
- tools/city-maps-check.mjs asserts the derivation is TOTAL over the other thirty-five, that the exclusions are exactly those two BY NAME, and that the maps are actually DIFFERENT: 52 distinct layout/terrain/sector/space shapes across 66 zones, all six layouts and all seven terrains in use. THAT LAST ONE MATTERS - if the inputs collapse, every zone becomes one city with a different tint and no single screenshot would show it.
- city-battle-check 103 -> 123. Two of its own assertions went stale and were REWRITTEN rather than deleted: one demanded an exact script src that now carries a ?v=, the other demanded a hardcoded 'grass_base' call that correctly stopped existing when the patch became derived.

## 1.7.9.1 changes (papptimus credited, the black roofs fixed, and a credit check that a wrong credit passed)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/space/city/ATTRIBUTION.txt, client/assets/asset-credits.js, docs/CREDITS.md, client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** CREDIT RESOLVED: papptimus, Cyber City, CC BY 4.0, https://papptimus.itch.io/cyber-city *** Verified against the STORE PAGE rather than taken on trust - the listing states the asset licence in its own metadata and the author confirms it in the comments. Credited in THREE places: the asset directory, docs/CREDITS.md, and the IN-CLIENT PANEL, which is the one that matters because CC BY asks for attribution in the medium the work appears in and a text file inside an asset folder is a credit nobody will read.
- FOURTH PACK TO ARRIVE WITH NO LICENCE FILE. The terms were on the STORE PAGE, not in the download. AN ARCHIVE IS NOT A LICENCE; THE PAGE IT CAME FROM IS. Record the URL at download and none of this costs anything later.
- *** THE ROOFS WERE BLACK HOLES. *** roof.png has a MEAN LUMINANCE OF 37/255 - it is drawn dark because roofing felt is dark. Running it through the WALL ambient multiply at 0.42 plus a shading fill took it to about 13, so from above the city was lit facades with VOIDS on top. The physics point is the one the shading comment ALREADY MADE and the code then ignored: a vertical surface sees half the sky, a horizontal one facing up sees ALL of it, so at night a flat roof is the BRIGHTEST large surface on a building. Roofs take their own ambient - a LIFT, not a multiply, because multiplying by more than one does nothing.
- The night cache is keyed on name AND ambient now. The roof sheets and facades are disjoint today so a name-only key HAPPENED to work, and would have silently served a wall-lit roof the first time anything shared a sheet.
- *** THE SECOND BUG WAS IN THE TESTS AND IT IS THE WORSE ONE. *** The new credit assertions checked that 'papptimus' appeared ANYWHERE in asset-credits.js and docs/CREDITS.md. Driven by tampering with each file, THE PANEL CHECK PASSED WITH THE NAME FIELD CHANGED TO SOMEBODY ELSE, because the url and note fields still carried the word. A CREDIT CHECK THAT A WRONG CREDIT SATISFIES IS NOT A CREDIT CHECK. Both match the name field now; all four tamper cases fail as they should.
- Dead code removed: an unused random alias, two void statements, a parameter nothing read.
- city-battle-check 98 -> 103.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.9.0 changes (real facades, and a CC BY pack we cannot yet credit)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/space/city/ (new), client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, docs/CREDITS.md, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** LICENCE FIRST: CC BY 4.0 CLAIMED, AUTHOR NOT SUPPLIED, city-battle-check FAILS ON IT. *** The most permissive licence this repo has received and the one we are closest to breaching. CC BY permits commercial use, modification and redistribution INCLUDING IN A PUBLIC REPO - more than the troop pack, the tile pack or the tank allow - and asks ONE thing back. The archive had no licence file, no readme, no author. ATTRIBUTION REQUIRED WITH NOBODY TO ATTRIBUTE IS THE FAILURE MODE OF THE CONDITION, NOT A WAY OF MEETING IT. Fourth time this shape has arrived; first time compliance is one line of text away.
- BUILDINGS ARE PHOTOGRAPHS OF BUILDINGS NOW. Nine albedo facades and nine matching EMISSIVE maps, black except lit windows, signage and keypads. Albedo at ambient, emissive ADDED on top, so a lit window is a SOURCE that survives the wall's shading and the haze - not bright paint.
- SCRAPPED: every glazing band and every warm shopfront band. Prisms wrapped round the shaft to SUGGEST storeys, which is what you do when a coloured box is your only tool. Keeping them would be painting stripes over a picture of a wall. Four prisms per building instead of ten; queue 2700 -> 2370 faces while looking enormously better. THE DETAIL MOVED FROM GEOMETRY INTO THE SURFACE.
- MAPPING A TEXTURE ON A WALL: canvas maps through an AFFINE only, and a rectangle under perspective is a general quad. WHICH AXIS TO SUBDIVIDE IS THE WHOLE TRICK, and it is the bandPass fact again - the error lives on the axis where DEPTH VARIES. A wall along z varies down its LENGTH and not up its height, so it is cut into columns, and the count comes from the depth RATIO: edge-on gets ten, face-on gets one.
- *** THE FIRST VERSION MADE THE WALLS TRANSPARENT. *** Albedo drawn at globalAlpha = shade; at 0.34 it read as smoked glass, plausible enough on a cyberpunk street that it took a second look to see THE SKYLINE SHOWING THROUGH THE BUILDINGS. ALPHA IS NOT BRIGHTNESS: darkening is a multiply against the pixels, alpha is a hole. Baked once per facade at load now - the third time this renderer has concluded that a recolour is paid for once, at load, keyed on what varies.
- ROOFS WERE THE BRIGHTEST THING IN THE SCENE AND BLANK. Once the walls were textured, a flat pale cap stopped reading as a roof and started reading as SNOW - the only large untextured surface left above street level. The two roof sheets go on the trim caps, subdivided both ways because a roof's depth runs front-to-back as well as across.
- *** THE HARNESS CAUGHT A CRY-WOLF BUG. *** Roofs correctly have NO emissive map, and the loader reported that absence as 'facade textures not found', putting a warning banner over a scene that was working perfectly. 'This file is absent' and 'this file SHOULD be absent' are different states, and a loader that cannot tell them apart will cry wolf.
- THE FBX MESHES WERE DELIBERATELY NOT TAKEN. Binary FBX needs an importer written first, and the meshes are the part of the pack we need LEAST: building shapes are GENERATED and must stay generated, because the block layout places them against streets and cover slots. What was missing was never the boxes - it was the SURFACES on them.
- city-battle-check 67 -> 98, one of which FAILS BY DESIGN until the author is named.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.8.0 changes (grass that is grass, things that touch the ground, and a glyph skyline)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: tools/terrain-synth.py (new), client/assets/space/terrain/grass_base.png (new), grass_rock.png (new), ATTRIBUTION.txt, client/assets/city-battle.js, client/citybattle-mock.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE GRASS WAS GRAVEL WEARING GREEN. *** There is NO GRASS IN THE TERRAIN PACK - the recipe is sand, gravel, basalt, snow, water, clay - so a garden world drew its fields with dust_base tinted with a forest palette. That is most of why the plain read flat however much was done to the palette over it.
- tools/terrain-synth.py generates grass_base and grass_rock out of ARITHMETIC: wrapping value-noise lattices for clumping, aligned blade strokes taking direction from a low-frequency lean field so neighbouring blades lie the same way, and a bare-scrape mask because turf is never total. NO SOURCE ART, NO PACK, NO LICENCE TO RESOLVE - the only file in that directory that can ship today without a decision first.
- SEAMLESS BY CONSTRUCTION, NOT BY BLENDING: every octave is a lattice whose period divides the image, so the wrap is exact. A blended seam is visible as a soft band once tiled at forty metres and looked at down a street. Authored WIDE: the tinted dust patch spans luminance 41-65, a 24-value range; these span roughly twice that.
- *** NOTHING IN THE SCENE TOUCHED THE GROUND. *** Every prism was a flat fill on another flat fill with a hard edge between - a forty metre tower and a one metre barrier sat on the plane identically, which is to say HOVERING. city.js has had the fix since it shipped: a dark quad on the plate, spread with height. Drawn ON THE GROUND PASS, not in the sorted queue, because a shadow is part of the surface and must go UNDER the men standing in front of it.
- FLAT FILLS ARE WHY EVERYTHING READ AS CARDBOARD. One value per face is right for a diffuse surface under one distant light and is not what a wall does: the base sits in bounce off the ground and the sky is a huge source overhead. Walls take a vertical ramp, GATED ON SCREEN SIZE at 22px so it lands on the couple of hundred faces that are walls and not the thousands of mesh faces where it would be under a value per pixel.
- A GLYPH SKYLINE, AS A SECOND STYLE AND NOT A REPLACEMENT. Switchable in the bench; neither is obviously right so neither is deleted. BAKED ONCE PER TOWER AND BILLBOARDED - twenty columns by forty rows is 800 fillText per building and thirty buildings is 24000 a frame, which is not a skyline but a text editor. At hundreds of metres the parallax a prism gives across its near faces is under a pixel, so a billboard loses nothing visible and costs one drawImage.
- ASCII RATHER THAN BOX-DRAWING CHARACTERS, DELIBERATELY. A missing block glyph renders as a TOFU BOX, which on a skyline is indistinguishable from a lit window - the failure would be invisible and wrong. The technique is decades old and the implementation is ours: nothing taken from anyone's art, palette or composition, which matters more than usual in a repo where three of four art packs already cannot sit in a public directory.
- city-battle-check 56 -> 67. One of the new assertions initially FAILED ON ITS OWN COMMENT - it forbade the string dust_base anywhere in the file, and the comment explaining why dust_base was dropped tripped it. A test that punishes you for writing down why you changed something is a bad test; it matches the CALL now.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.7.1 changes (the crawling road texture, measured and fixed)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- WITH ZERO CAMERA ROLL A SCANLINE MAPS TO THE GROUND EXACTLY - depth is constant along it - so bandPass has NO horizontal error. All of it is VERTICAL, and band edges are pinned to SCREEN space, so a moving camera slides the world through them and the seams crawl. That is why it never showed on the Reach's orbiting camera and shows immediately on a free cam at street level.
- MEASURED as texture slip against the true projection at 1440x810, 60 deg vfov: fixed 26px = 5.94px worst / 18 bands (SHIPPED); 12px = 2.78 / 38; 6px = 1.30 / 74; ADAPTIVE = 0.81 / 25.
- The error is CONCENTRATED JUST UNDER THE HORIZON, where depth changes fastest per pixel. Bands sized by distance from the horizon put the small bands where the error is: SEVEN TIMES LESS SLIP for forty percent more bands, and better than a uniform 6px band costing four times as many.
- reach-battle.js carries the same fixed-band code and the same latent error. NOT CHANGED HERE: it does not show on a camera that orbits above the plain, and a drive-by edit to a live renderer belongs in its own patch with reach-depth-check pointed at it.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.7.0 changes (a city block instead of a boulevard, a front that moves, and a skyline in the city view's own language)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, tools/citybattle-harness.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- A BOULEVARD WITH A LINE ON EACH VERGE IS A FIELD BATTLE WITH TARMAC DOWN THE MIDDLE. Nothing was ever between the two lines except distance, there was no flank that was not the whole flank, and no man ever lost sight of the enemy. Three avenues, three cross streets, blocks between them, and ALLEYS - the only way through a block that is not a street. THE AVENUES RUN ALONG Z ON PURPOSE: the pack is single facing, so the fighting has to cross x, and avenues along z make every street a firing lane pointing the right way. Engagement 130m -> 62m.
- THE FRONT MOVES. frontX drifts toward whoever is winning; cover is claimable only on your own side of it, so a gain opens a rank of corners and the line BOUNDS forward, and a loss drops slots that are now behind the enemy.
- *** THE FIRST VERSION OF THAT PARKED THE ENTIRE ARMY IN THE BACK ROW. *** Cover scored by DISTANCE, and the nearest free slot to a man who just spawned is the one BEHIND him. Fifty-two units took the rearmost corners, none came within sixty metres of an enemy, ZERO CASUALTIES AFTER THIRTY SECONDS - and the harness's own guard is the ONLY reason it was caught, because the frame looked completely fine. Front first, distance second.
- SKYLINE REBUILT IN THE CITY VIEW'S LANGUAGE. city.js draws districts as TRANSLUCENT FILLED PRISMS - three face values per mass, a lighter roof slab so the silhouette edge reads, band lines, WINDOWS ON A GRID, mast and glowing beacon. The grid is load-bearing: a scatter reads as noise, a grid reads as a building, and it is the single thing that made the old skyline look like a wireframe test.
- *** A REAL TESSELLATION BUG, FOUND BY STANDING IN THE STREET. *** stripPath took a segment COUNT, not a LENGTH. 34 segments over a 1500m avenue is a 44m segment, and a segment with any corner behind the near plane is dropped WHOLE - so standing in the middle of one punched a 44m HOLE IN THE ROAD CLIP CENTRED ON THE CAMERA, and the carriageway drew as grass. It read as a shading bug. Fixed world length now, walked only over the span the camera can see.
- HAZE IS AIR, NOT GROUND. reach-battle hazes toward `horizon` and is right to, because on a brood world the lit ground value and the sky are within a few degrees. On a garden world `horizon` is a SATURATED GREEN, so the middle distance of a city at dusk was green fog and read as a park.
- CLEANER SURFACES, AND CONTRAST IS THE DIAL RATHER THAN TILE SIZE. Enlarging the tile to kill speckle just turns speckle into SMEARS - the same noise at a scale where each blob is a metre across, which on a pavement reads as damp patches.
- A CITY BLOCK IS NOT A LAWN. One quad per cell floors the lots; cheapest correction available and it does most of the work of making this read as a city rather than a park with offices in it.
- Building height RISES AWAY FROM THE CONTESTED AVENUE - a forty metre wall both sides of the fight is a canyon that swallows it, and stepping back is how a real city is built anyway, so the constraint costs nothing. Street trees pruned to 55%: the same timber that reads as scenery on the outskirts stands taller than the shopfront behind it and blocks the lane it is planted beside.
- city-battle-check 48 -> 56, driving the grid, the moving front, the cover score, bounding, the haze split and the strip tessellation.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.6.0 changes (no shield troopers in a city fight, and a detail pass on everything behind it)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** NO SHIELD TROOPERS ON A CITY LINE, AND THE REASON IS HIS OWN JOB. *** v1.7.4.0 settled it: a shield is for stopping the thing that CLOSES, which is why a charging class is weighted at half distance. A city fight between two polities has NO CHARGING CLASS - both lines are riflemen behind cover across a street - so he arrives with nothing to bash and falls into the hold branch, THE STATE THAT PRODUCED THE SIDEWAYS DRIFT. Not banned: ABSENT, because nothing here is his target. The class table is where he returns the day a melee class stands on a city line.
- His share goes to the ENGINEER, not the riflemen. Without the shield the line is ONE SILHOUETTE, and the engineer is the only other shape on the field and the only thing on it that is not a man.
- TOWERS: five KINDS - slab, step, taper, twin with a skybridge, crown. SILHOUETTE is the only thing that survives at two hundred pixels and a tenth of an alpha, and thirty copies of one recipe reads as one building repeated BECAUSE IT IS. Setback ledges, floor bands, roof plant, masts with guy lines. MULLIONS ON THE NEAR RING ONLY: vertical bays are what make a face read as glazing rather than a box with stripes, and they are one segment per bay per face - the far ring would pay thousands of segments for lines under a pixel apart.
- BLOCKS: floors counted FROM HEIGHT rather than fixed at two fat bands. A 19m block and a 74m block do not have the same number of storeys and drawing them as if they did is what made the rank look like ONE MODEL AT TWO SCALES. Warm SHOPFRONT at street level - the only warm value down there and the thing that says somebody is inside. Cornice WIDER than the shaft, so the roof is an edge rather than a cut. A second offset mass turning every plan into an L: a rectangle from any angle is a rectangle, a notch is a silhouette.
- STREET: a walkable PAVEMENT instead of a one-metre kerb line; expansion JOINTS across it, the single cheapest thing that turns a grey ribbon into concrete because A SLAB HAS EDGES AND A RIBBON DOES NOT; a worn VERGE because real ground does not change material along a ruled line; deterministic PATCHING on the carriageway, off a fixed seed because a road that re-patches every frame shimmers; stop bars at the junction.
- GROUND: rubble from the pack's scatter rocks on the CONCRETE ramp rather than the vegetation one, and the low growth weighted up because A FIELD OF EVENLY SPACED TREES IS AN ORCHARD.
- *** ONE THING WAS BUILT, MEASURED AND CUT. *** A third full-plane pass at a hundred-metre tile, for broad light and shade under the grain, on the sound argument that one frequency never looks like ground. MEASURED: 60ms of a 240ms ground stage under cairo, for an effect at a FIFTH ALPHA underneath a haze already at THREE QUARTERS. Worst value of the three by a wide margin. Gone, with the argument kept in a comment rather than deleted silently - somebody will have it again, and it has to come from something that is not a third tiled fill.
- THE PAVEMENT CAUGHT A REAL BUG ON THE WAY IN: it borrowed the SCRUB pattern, which is tinted with PAL.rock - the VEGETATION colour on this world - so the concrete came out STREAKED GREEN. It looked like a shading fault and was a material wearing ANOTHER MATERIAL'S TINT. Its own pattern now.
- Detail passes are DEPTH BOUNDED: grain and pavement stop where the haze eats them; the base pass still runs to the horizon BECAUSE THAT ONE IS THE GROUND.
- city-battle-check 46 -> 48, driving the class table. Re-adding the shield trooper takes it to 47/1.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.5.0 changes (the city war had no picture, and the two faction id namespaces had never been mapped)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/city-battle.js (new), client/index.html, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/factions.js, client/citybattle-mock.html (new), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/city-battle-check.mjs (new), tools/citybattle-harness.mjs (new), tools/serve.mjs (new), test.bat (new), test.sh (new), package.json, .gitignore, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt. NO SERVER CHANGE AT ALL.
- THE MECHANICS ALREADY EXISTED AND WERE NOT TOUCHED. server/city.js prices control at WAR_FUND_BASE_PER_PCT + warRate(cityBook); war_fund_pool accumulates it; onColonyCaptured vacates every seat; maybeStripOccupied salvages development and leaves monuments. city-battle.js is a VIEW over that and adds no second combat model - a fork there would have forked the thing the economy is built on.
- *** TWO FACTION ID NAMESPACES SHIPPED AND NOTHING MAPPED BETWEEN THEM. *** galaxy.js: coalition/syndicate/void/guild/jade/fleshstation. factions.js: coal/synd/void/guild/jade/khai. THREE OF SIX COLLIDE BY LUCK, which is what hid it. `gid` is on the registry row now and FM_FAC_API.fromGalaxy/toGalaxy is the one map; a private table at the crossing would have been the SIXTH copy of a faction's identity. fleshstation maps to NULL deliberately - a station with no kit, and a fallback to coal would put Coalition uniforms on a faction that has never had any.
- NEW IN THE RENDERER, each a real gap in reach-battle.js: A MATERIAL PER FACE (paintFace has ONE ramp, far -> rock - right where every surface is the same dead mineral, wrong the moment a kerb stands next to a hedge); genTowers, a wireframe skyline in the genSpires slot; the street as bandPass-with-a-clip plus kerb and lane quads; urban cover; and FLORA urban, which finally spends the nature pack's trees - baked since the nature pass, never named by any world because the only battlefield was a brood world.
- FOUR BUGS THE HEADLESS DRIVER CAUGHT THAT A BROWSER HIDES. (1) *** pats() CACHES AN OBJECT OF NULLS *** when the sheets have not decoded - terSheet invalidates PATS from inside its own onload, so a build starting before the sheets land finishes AFTER the invalidation. THE SAME SHAPE IS IN reach-battle.js TODAY and survives only because the load order happens to be kind. (2) the band one pixel under the horizon covers 172000 x 56000 world units - FOUR MILLION TILES for a 26px strip; canvas clips it, cairo dies. Most expensive band, least visible: filled flat past a threshold, a saving in the browser too. (3) `horizon` is the planet's LIT GROUND value, not sky - using it as sky put a BLUE FIELD on the screen. (4) the tinted base patch spans luminance 41 to 65, a 24-value range, which is why a plain reads as a flat mat - buildPatterns has ALWAYS built a rock pattern NOTHING DREW, and drawing it at a second tile size and a third alpha puts the variation back with no new texture, load or licence.
- ONE-CLICK TESTING. test.bat / test.sh: suites, headless frames if node-canvas built, then client/ served over http and the benches opened. HTTP IS REQUIRED, NOT PREFERENCE - a canvas that has drawn a file:// image is TAINTED, getImageData throws, and every faction silently comes out in the sprite pack's own colours. `--no-serve` is the CI shape and exits on the suite's status.
- canvas is an OPTIONAL dependency, not a devDependency: a native build that fails on plenty of machines, needed only by the headless frame writer, imported by nothing in client/ or server/. A failed optional install leaves that ONE tool SKIPPED and every other check running.
- city-battle-check: 46 assertions, ZERO dependencies, so run-all picks it up everywhere. It guards REACHABILITY, which a rendering harness is blindest to - a viewer can be pixel perfect and completely unreachable and every way that happens is silent. Driven: breaking the lazyLoad path takes it to 44/2; unmapping syndicate's gid fails the map AND the roster.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.4.0 changes (the shield line did not engage humans, and it was also the "joined the attacking line" bug)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/reach-depth-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt. BOTH REPORTS WERE THE SAME BLOCK.
- IT LOOKED FOR MELEE_CLS AND NOTHING ELSE, AND MELEE_CLS IS BROOD-ONLY: rush, brute, leap, grub, maw. A shield trooper facing enemy INFANTRY or an enemy SHIELD TROOPER found NOTHING, kept u.mel at -1, and fell into the hold branch - DRIFTING SIDEWAYS ALONG THE LINE PAST MEN IT SHOULD HAVE BEEN BASHING.
- *** AND THE HOLD BRANCH IS WHERE THE EARLIER REPORT CAME FROM. *** It marched to `front+0.055`, a HARDCODED HOME-SIDE OFFSET, so an away shield trooper walked ACROSS the front and stood IN THE HOME LINE. "Shield enemies seem to join the attacking line" was NEVER A TARGETING FAULT - he was doing exactly what he was told. Reported two patches ago, unexplained because THE DIAGNOSTIC COULD NOT SEE IT. `front + dirH*0.055` now.
- NO SIDE FILTER EITHER. `!MELEE_CLS[v.cls]` was the ONLY test, so the nearest rusher was a target WHOEVER OWNED IT - safe only while every melee class was on one side BY DEFINITION. Put the brood on a home roster and a Coalition shield trooper bashes his own escort. The retained target is re-validated for SIDE as well as death, so a roster change mid-engagement cannot leave a man locked onto a unit that has BECOME friendly.
- THE SCAN WAS THIRTY-FOUR RANDOM DRAWS out of seven hundred - THE THIRD INSTANCE OF THAT PATTERN in this file after the flyers and the turret. Even with the class filter fixed it would have MISSED THE MAN STANDING NEXT TO IT most of the time. Driven: the one enemy in a crowd of 700 is found 200/200.
- A PREFERENCE, NOT A FILTER, AND THAT DISTINCTION IS THE WHOLE LESSON - A FILTER IS WHAT CAUSED THIS BUG. A shield is for stopping the thing that closes, so a charging class is weighted at HALF DISTANCE and wins out to twice the range; a rifleman at arm's length is STILL BASHED. Measured at the boundary: rusher at 1.3x wins, 2.0x ties, 2.7x loses.
- flash() was also passing a LITERAL 1 for side, so an away shield trooper struck in HOME COLOURS.
- reach-depth-check 4106 -> 4118, driving the search against adjacent enemy shield troopers, friendly units CLOSER than the enemy, the charging-class boundary, out-of-reach targets, aircraft overhead, and a 700-unit crowd. Suite green.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.3.0 changes (the huddle: two causes, and moving the line was the smaller one)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/reach-depth-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- MEASURED BEFORE CHANGING ANYTHING. Front mapped hive percent onto 0.05-0.95 and at the top of that range THERE IS NO FIELD LEFT TO STAND IN: a zone opens at hive 100, front opened at 0.95, roomK had 0.03 of depth to scale 0.455 of offsets into, and THE ENTIRE HOME LINE OCCUPIED 0.011 OF THE FIELD - seven hundred men in a stripe ONE PERCENT DEEP.
- *** BUT BELOW ABOUT HIVE 50 THE FRONT ALREADY HAD ALL THE ROOM IT NEEDED AND THE LINE STILL STOOD IN 0.165 OF DEPTH *** - that is simply how wide the band was: infantry ran front+0.015 to front+0.18 and NO FURTHER. Relocating the front fixes the CRUSHED case and does NOTHING for the ORDINARY one: a line pinned to midfield would have given 0.165 against the 0.138 already visible at hive 60. THE BAND WIDTH WAS THE LEVER THAT MATTERED.
- BOTH CHANGED. Range 0.20-0.80; infantry to +0.32, hive line to -0.30. hive100 0.011 -> 0.121 (ELEVEN-FOLD), hive60 0.138 -> 0.282, ordinary 0.165 -> 0.305.
- THE BAND WIDENING COSTS NOTHING IN THE SCALING BUDGET. DEPTH_NEED_HOME is the widest offset on the side - the ENGINEER's 0.40 plus the jade posture - so infantry at 0.32 is still inside it, roomK is unchanged and NO OTHER BAND MOVES. On the hive side 0.30 is EXACTLY DEPTH_NEED_HIVE (the struck tank standoff), so that one is AT ITS CEILING.
- NOT PINNED TO MIDFIELD, DELIBERATELY. The mapping stays a PURE FUNCTION of z.hive and stays MONOTONIC, because every camp, every ownership test and the whole advance rule read it. The line still travels 0.60 of the field across a war. A FRONT THAT NEVER MOVES IS A PICTURE THAT HAS STOPPED REPORTING ANYTHING.
- *** THERE WERE TWO COPIES OF THE MAPPING AND ONLY ONE WOULD HAVE BEEN FIXED. *** reachWatch set the front when an engagement opened and the TWO SECOND TICK set it again from live state, each with its own clamp - so widening reachWatch alone would have LOOKED LIKE IT WORKED FOR TWO SECONDS and then been silently overwritten, EVERY TICK, FOREVER.
- THE DUPLICATE HAD ALSO BEEN CARRYING `(Z.hive||50)` SINCE AFTER THAT BUG WAS FIXED AT THE OTHER SITE IN v1.6.3.1. A zone at hive 0 fell through to the 50 default and drew its line AT MIDFIELD ON EVERY TICK. FIXING ONE OF TWO COPIES IS NOT FIXING ANYTHING, and this is what that looks like three patches later. One frontFor() now, both sites, typeof not a falsy check, default that moves WITH the range.
- reach-depth-check used to lift the clamp EXPRESSION, which worked only while the mapping was ONE INLINE LINE - so the expression it tested was NOT NECESSARILY THE ONE THAT RAN. It lifts and drives the FUNCTION now and asserts there is EXACTLY ONE MAPPING, plus monotonicity and minimum travel. 4101 -> 4106.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

- THEY LOOKED FOR cls === 'heli' AND NOTHING ELSE. HELI_SHARE_BENCHED has been true since the airstrike replaced gunships, so that class DOES NOT SPAWN, so the search NEVER FOUND ANYTHING, so every flyer fell into the idle branch and drifted along front-0.16 BOUNCING OFF THE WALLS FOR THE ENTIRE ENGAGEMENT. Not misbehaving: looking for something that is not there.

Tanks and turrets are that thing now. Weighted heli 3, tank 2, turret 1, so a flyer CROSSES THE FIELD FOR A TANK rather than settling on the turret it happens to be over.
- IT ALSO HAD NO SIDE FILTER AT ALL - any heli within thirty random samples, FRIENDLY OR NOT, harmless only because there was never more than one air unit in play. Enemy-only now.

It reads the rebuildTargets index now, widened across the WHOLE FIELD because armour is rare and a flyer is fast.

- THE TURRET WAS SCANNING THE SAME BROKEN WAY (twenty random draws for a "flyer on approach") - little consequence while flyers never attacked, real consequence now that turrets are PREY. Same index.
- It also asked pickTarget for SIDE 1'S ENEMIES WHOEVER OWNED IT, a hardcoded literal harmless only because awayClass never returns 'turret'. A bug waiting on a content change, and THE FOURTH OF THIS EXACT SHAPE found in this file.
- *** THE SHIELD REPORT IS NOT FIXED AND IS NOT DISMISSED. *** Nothing found accounts for it, and the instruments COULD NOT TELL a faction on the wrong half of the field from one on the right half - facMix counts factions and says NOTHING about sides.
- New _fmReachDebug.sides(): per-side faction counts plus CROSSED, the number of men whose faction is NOT on the roster for the side they stand on. Should fall to zero as the pre-change seed dies off; anything that PERSISTS is the reinforcement path putting a unit on the wrong side - the mechanism worth suspecting. Wired into the bench beside the geometry readouts.
