## 1.7.1.1 changes (Coalition: one blue, green on every class)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/factions.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/faction-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- ONE BLUE, AND IT IS THE ONE IT ALWAYS HAD. The multi-kit pass gave the Coalition five dye lots on the reasoning that a treaty quartermaster issues one coat across nine colonies and gets nine batches. THE REASONING HELD AND THE PICTURE DID NOT: at field size five values of one hue read as a line that is UNEVENLY LIT rather than one with history, and the Coalition is the faction whose whole identity is BEING ORGANISED. Variation is the Syndicate's trait, and spending it on two factions SPENDS IT.
- THE SHADE IS MATCHED, NOT GUESSED. The reference was SAMPLED (top uniform colours 52,70,111 / 31,41,65 / 38,51,81) and compared against all five kits: kit1 at mean error 1.4, the others 12.5-14.8. kit1 IS the standard-issue tint the Coalition wore BEFORE the kits existed, so this RESTORES a value rather than inventing one, and the graded output reproduces the reference EXACTLY.
- KEPT AS `tint`, NOT A ONE-ENTRY `kits` ARRAY. A faction that issues a uniform and one that does not are DIFFERENT KINDS OF THING; "one has a list of length one" LOSES THE STATEMENT. The Syndicate is the only row with kits again - the distinction working.
- GREEN GLASS ON EVERY CLASS. opticOn 'augmented' -> 'all', so the assault trooper gets the visor. The old gate said his goggles must stay as drawn because HE HAS A FACE - the right argument WHILE THE OPTIC WAS RED AND MEANT AUGMENTATION. It is GREEN now and means OPTICS: a sensor package, issued kit, NO CLAIM ABOUT THE MAN. THE ARGUMENT RETIRED WITH THE COLOUR IT WAS ABOUT.
- It also matters at field size: the assault trooper is THE MOST NUMEROUS UNIT ON ANY LINE, so an optic he does not wear is an optic MOST OF THE ARMY DOES NOT HAVE. Verified on pixels: assault 9 green, shielded enforcer 23 (faceplate AND shield panel), unshielded enforcer 8.
- THREE ASSERTIONS UPDATED, ONE WRONG IN KIND: 'both kitted factions have enough kits' asserted a COUNT, which is NOT THE PROPERTY - what matters is which faction issues a uniform and which does not. It asserts the KIND now, and would fail correctly on a category change rather than THROWING on a missing array.
- KNOWN LIMIT, STATED RATHER THAN PAPERED OVER: faction-sheet.py does NOT transcribe burnEye, the per-cell face-finding routine that paints the engineer's single eye pixel. That pixel follows the faction optic correctly IN THE GAME and shows unpainted on the sheet. NOT TRANSCRIBED ON PURPOSE - one pixel against a real risk of a DIVERGENT SECOND IMPLEMENTATION, the failure this tool is most exposed to.
- faction-check 3911 -> 3900 (removed kit assertions). Suite green.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.1.0 changes (Coalition: multi-shaded blue and a green visor)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/factions.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, tools/faction-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- BLUE MASS, GREEN GLASS. At twenty pixels a faction is a COLOUR MASS and A COUPLE OF LIT PIXELS, and those two should not be doing the same job. Nothing else on the field pairs blue with green.
- THE KITS ARE ONE NAVY IN SEVERAL DYE LOTS, WHICH IS NOT WHAT THE SYNDICATE'S ARE - and that distinction is the whole reason the Coalition can have kits at all WITHOUT BECOMING A SECOND MERCENARY COMPANY. Syndicate varies by HUE (men who turned up in what they owned); Coalition varies by VALUE INSIDE ONE HUE (one coat, nine colonies, nine batches).
- MEASURED: closest Coalition pair 18 apart against the Syndicate's 20-40. DELIBERATELY TIGHTER, so a Coalition line reads as ONE ARMY WITH WEAR ON IT rather than as a crowd. Every kit is blue (blue channel highest in all five) and the check asserts it - ONE KIT DRIFTING OFF HUE TURNS THE ARMY INTO A CROWD.
- GREEN, NOT RED, AND THE RED WAS NOT WASTED. The old red said AUGMENTATION - something lit behind the glass on helmeted classes only. Green says OPTICS, a sensor package rather than a modified man, which is more accurate for a treaty army fielding engineers and turrets - AND IT FREES RED ENTIRELY FOR THE SYNDICATE. Red is now dominant in EXACTLY ONE faction's optic.
- opticOn STAYS 'augmented'. The assault trooper has a FACE and burning his goggles would claim otherwise; that gate was never about the colour. It also keeps a SECOND AXIS of distinction from the Syndicate beyond hue: Syndicate marks EVERY class, Coalition only those behind a helmet, so they differ on THE MOST NUMEROUS UNIT ON THE FIELD as well as on the mass.
- *** THE BUG THIS TURNED UP: A HARDCODED RED EYE UNDER A GREEN FACEPLATE. *** The engineer's painted eye was EYE_RED [255,78,60] - right for exactly as long as the only faction painting an eye burned red. With a green visor the Coalition engineer would have had a RED EYE UNDER GREEN GLASS: ONE FIGURE, TWO ANSWERS TO THE SAME QUESTION. It takes the faction's own optic now, constant kept as a fallback.
- THREE CHECKS FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE. (1) One asserted the Syndicate was the ONLY row with kits - true, and NEVER THE PROPERTY WORTH DEFENDING; what matters is that the two vary along DIFFERENT AXES, so it measures cluster tightness and hue span now.
- (2) One used `r > g + 40` for "red" and FLAGGED THE VOID COLLECTIVE, whose purple is 186,104,246: red beats green by 82 and the HIGHEST CHANNEL IS BLUE. A DISCRIMINATOR THAT CANNOT TELL PURPLE FROM RED IS NOT TESTING WHAT IT SAYS - it asks which channel is DOMINANT now.
- (3) A THIRD FIXED-WIDTH WINDOW BROKE: reach-terrain matched `coal:` then `skin: 'range'` within 400 CHARACTERS, and the five-entry kits array pushed them apart. A window measured in CHARACTERS goes red whenever the thing it guards GETS MORE DETAILED, and the pressure is to raise the number until it stops complaining. It EVALUATES the registry now.
- faction-sheet.py emits a squad sheet for ANY faction with kits, so the Coalition gets one automatically.
- faction-check 3891 -> 3911. Suite green.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.7.0.0 changes (shields match visors, the Syndicate becomes a company)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/factions.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/battle-test.html, tools/faction-sheet.py, tools/faction-check.mjs, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE SHIELD PANEL IS AN OPTIC, NOT AN ACCENT. The shielded enforcer's faceplate and the panel on the shield he holds up IN FRONT OF IT are the SAME EQUIPMENT DOING THE SAME JOB. Split across two channels, a Coalition trooper burned his faceplate red and kept a FACTORY BLUE PANEL A HAND'S WIDTH IN FRONT OF IT. Moved to OPTIC_SRC; accent is now exactly what it says - markings on kit, one source, the engineer's wrist device.
- MERCENARIES DO NOT HAVE A UNIFORM AND THE FIRST PASS GAVE THEM ONE. A single brown tint made the Syndicate A THIRD ARMY IN A DIFFERENT COLOUR, next to two others that already say that better.
- `kits`: an array drawn PER SOLDIER off his own index. FIVE - three reads as a pattern at forty men, more than five stops reading as one faction. All LOW-CHROMA WORKING COLOURS separated by HUE AT SIMILAR VALUE, so they read as different men in different surplus rather than ONE MAN LIT FIVE WAYS.
- SKIN POLICY `merc`: the whole human range PLUS the steel casing in ONE POOL. Roughly one in seven is an android - one draw, seven outcomes, one of which is not a person.
- RED VISORS, THE ONLY THING THE COMPANY ISSUES. No optic at all read as FIVE UNRELATED MEN rather than one faction, because NOTHING IN THE FRAME SAID THEY WERE TOGETHER. A mercenary company does not issue coats; it issues the thing that identifies you as being ON THE CONTRACT.
- opticOn:'all' NOT 'augmented' IS THE LOAD-BEARING HALF. The Coalition already burns red and TWO REDS AT TWENTY PIXELS ARE TWO REDS. What separates them is WHICH CLASSES WEAR IT: the Coalition burns only helmeted classes and its assault trooper keeps teal goggles, so the Syndicate differs on THE MOST NUMEROUS UNIT ON THE FIELD.
- Shade on MEASUREMENT: crimson 244,46,74 - luminance 108 against a kit ceiling of 88 so it clears every coat, and 34 from the Coalition's red in RGB, about the most a red can differ from another red while still clearing the coats underneath.
- *** THE BUG THIS TURNED UP: A STRIDE IS NOT A SPREAD. *** skinFor used (i*7+3) % pool.length - fine while no pool is 7 long, DEGENERATE the moment one is: 7 mod 7 is 0, every soldier gets index 3. The merc pool is 7. TWO HUNDRED MEN, ONE FACE.
- A bigger prime only MOVES the collision to a different pool length, so it is a HASH now (two xorshift rounds and a Knuth multiply) with NO relationship to the modulus - it spreads a pool of ANY length, including lengths nobody has written yet. All 35 tone-by-kit combos reachable and stable per index.
- AND THE CHECK THAT SHOULD HAVE CAUGHT IT WAS MEASURING THE OLD MECHANISM. "does not band" asserted under 40 adjacent repeats in 200 - but A STRIDE NEVER REPEATS CONSECUTIVELY while the multiplier is coprime, so it was really asserting "THIS IS A STRIDE" and any hash fails it. A hash gives random-like adjacency: 1-in-4 for the Guild is ~50 in 200, correct and not banding. BANDING IS A RUN, so the run is measured now, plus per-tone evenness - a hash that spread badly would pass a run test BY BEING UNIFORMLY WRONG.
- ENGINE: `kit` threaded through tinted/drawFrame/drawAnchored and into the CACHE KEY, so a Syndicate sheet holds five tinted copies and everyone else holds one. The key only grows where the faction HAS kits. kitOf(u) mirrors skinOf(u): same index, DIFFERENT SALT, fixed for the soldier's life so he does not change coats when he takes cover.
- faction-sheet.py also emits a SQUAD SHEET for any faction with kits: SIXTEEN CONSECUTIVE soldier indices exactly as seedField hands them out - the line as it will ACTUALLY STAND, not a curated selection of the nicest pairs. Both Python implementations verified BYTE-EQUAL against the JS on grade output AND index selection, including the SIGNED-32-BIT MODULO the hash depends on.
- faction-check 3865 -> 3891, reach-check 1086 -> 1088, reach-terrain 306 -> 308. Suite green.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.9.2 changes (the portrait did not load because there was nothing to press)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/server.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/core.js, client/index.html, client/assets/portrait-manifest.js (comment), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/faction-sheet.py (NEW), tools/faction-check.mjs, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/controls-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- v1.6.9.0 shipped the assignment as an HTTP ROUTE with NO CONTROL ANYWHERE IN THE PANEL and a changelog note saying to POST to it. A FEATURE THAT EXISTS AND CANNOT BE USED - the exact failure controls-check was written to catch. It sailed past because that file walks SOCKET commands and an HTTP route has no command string to walk. A ROUTE NOBODY CAN REACH IS WORSE THAN NO ROUTE: IT READS AS DONE.
- IT ALSO HAD TO PUSH, AND DID NOT. setPlayerPortrait writes the row, which changes what the NEXT LOGIN sends and nothing else - so even a SUCCESSFUL call left the target with the old face until they reloaded. From outside that is INDISTINGUISHABLE FROM THE WRITE FAILING, which is what "did not load to the account" looked like.
- broadcastToPlayer tells the target and they repaint on the spot; a broadcast tells everyone else so the next chat line carries the new face. Scrollback keeps the old one - each message froze a portrait at post time, deliberate elsewhere and unchanged.
- NOW A SOCKET COMMAND on the same godCmd path as every other dev control, with a REAL CONTROL in War Controls: player name, portrait id defaulting to 'hive', ASSIGN. The HTTP route is DELETED rather than kept alongside - TWO PATHS FOR ONE ACTION is what the DEV_PORTRAITS comment already argues against. 'hive' resolves SERVER SIDE, so the panel needs no edit the day new art lands.
- tools/faction-sheet.py: EVERY FACTION AGAINST EVERY CLASS ON ONE IMAGE, so colour can be ARGUED ABOUT rather than described. The recolour was judged three ways and NONE OF THEM WAS LOOKING AT IT: faction-check drives the grade and asserts a luminance spread, the bench parades five figures behind a running server, and the battlefield draws them at TWENTY PIXELS THROUGH HAZE - where a mistake is LEAST visible.
- 4978x3690, six factions by eight cells at 3x. Cells chosen to carry the channels: the shielded enforcer has BOTH the faceplate the optic burns AND the shield panel the accent marks, the engineer has the wrist device, the assault has skin and goggles at two skin indices, the Hound is the only thing the hull grade touches.
- *** ITS ONE REAL RISK IS BEING A SECOND RECOLOUR ENGINE. *** coalition-sprites.js owns tinted(); a Python copy that drifted would show A GAME THAT DOES NOT EXIST - exactly what made the battle bench worthless for THREE PATCHES RUNNING.
- So EVERY NUMBER IS EXTRACTED from factions.js at run time by brace matching (no colour can be CHOSEN in the tool), and the arithmetic is a transcription in the SAME ORDER OF OPERATIONS: accent remap runs BEFORE the grade and SKIPS THE PIXEL; camo split decided on RAW luminance BEFORE the lift; keep reads WHICHEVER GRADE IS IN FORCE. Get any of the three wrong and the sheet is PLAUSIBLE AND UNTRUE.
- VERIFIED BY RUNNING BOTH IMPLEMENTATIONS against the pack's six real uniform colours: BYTE-EQUAL ON ALL SIX FACTIONS. faction-check asserts the extraction and all three ordering properties.
- One reach-check assertion named the deleted route and is UPDATED to the command, plus a new one that the target is TOLD IMMEDIATELY rather than on next login - the half of the bug that would have survived the other half being fixed.
- controls-check 215 -> 228, reach-check 1085 -> 1086, faction-check 3857 -> 3865. Suite green.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.9.1 changes (Zharkofin has his face)
- HARD REFRESH plus one dev call. No server restart for the art itself, but PORTRAIT_SET is built from the directory AT BOOT, so the server must come up after the file is in place. Files: client/assets/portraits/prawn_commander.png (NEW), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- The resolver shipped AHEAD of the art in v1.6.9.0. The art has landed, so it now returns it.
- CONFORMED TO THE PACK, NOT JUST DROPPED IN. All 258 existing portraits are EXACTLY 393x397 with the subject run to the BOTTOM EDGE. The source was 111x168 at a different aspect, so a straight copy would have been THE ONE PORTRAIT IN THE SET THAT FRAMED DIFFERENTLY FROM ALL THE OTHERS.
- THE FRAMING IS NOT DECORATION: FMPortraitFrame renders anything without a measured head box 1:1 AND NO CROP, so THE CANVAS IS THE COMPOSITION.
- INTEGER NEAREST, because the art is authored at its own pixel pitch - 36 colours, NO 2x OR 3x BLOCK STRUCTURE, measured rather than assumed - and a fractional resample would make some source pixels two screen pixels wide and others three, which on pixel art READS AS DAMAGE. 2x is the largest whole factor that fits: 168*2=336 <= 397, 168*3=504 does not.
- BOTTOM-ANCHORED AND CENTRED ON THE SILHOUETTE, not on the canvas: the figure is NOT centred in its own source file - the antennae lean and the alpha bbox is asymmetric.
- MEASURED AGAINST THE PACK'S OWN NUMBERS. Subject top 0.154 (pack 0.020-0.244), bottom 396 (pack 379-396), head width 0.28 (pack 0.29-0.55, prawn1 0.29). EVERY MEASURE INSIDE THE RANGE and within 0.01 of the drone it replaces - the comparison that matters, same subject family.
- INSTALLING ART DID NOT ADD A COSTUME. prawn_commander is on DEV_PORTRAITS beside prawn1: refused by /api/portrait for EVERYONE INCLUDING DEVS, never in the picker. That is the whole point of the mechanism and exactly what a carelessly regenerated manifest would undo, so the check asserts it EXPLICITLY now that the art exists.
- reach-check 1079 -> 1085, DRIVEN AGAINST THE REAL DIRECTORY: PORTRAIT_SET is a fact about the FILESYSTEM, not about any source file, so the resolver is LIFTED AND RUN against the actual listing rather than pattern-matched. Also asserts the canvas matches prawn1's by reading the PNG IHDR, and that the picker still offers ZERO prawn entries.
- TO USE: POST /api/dev/portrait {player:'Zharkofin', portrait:'hive'}. The 'hive' alias resolves through hiveLordPortrait(), so it is THE SAME CALL that would have given him the drone yesterday.
- NOTED, NOT FIXED: tools/sudoku-check.mjs failed ONCE on a clue-target assertion then passed 14 consecutive runs including a deliberate 12-run sample. Randomised generation, unrelated to this change - but IT IS A FLAKE IN THE SUITE YOU ARE ABOUT TO SHIP BEHIND, so it is written down rather than smoothed over.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.9.0 changes (force any faction into the war, dev seals, the hive is not a costume)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/reach.js, server/factions.js, server/server.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/portrait-manifest.js, client/index.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/controls-check.mjs, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, tools/lane-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- WHO IS IN THIS WAR: war-wide belligerent bar above the per-world cards, one button per faction.
- A DECLARATION THAT CHANGES NOTHING IS A FLAG, NOT AN EVENT - the mistake setCoalitionEntry ALREADY MADE ONCE, where declaring an interstellar power left every world entirely Jade until ten were dialled by hand. Declaring puts the faction on EVERY UNCOMPOSED LINE through rosterFromReach, which is also why the roster had to be the thing the field reads before this was worth building.
- THEY SHARE THE GROUND JADE IS NOT HOLDING, NOT JADE'S FLOOR. Jade opened this war and its floor is a rule; the Coalition and everyone after it divide the REMAINDER. A third power VISIBLY COSTS THE COALITION rather than quietly costing Jade: Jade 60% stays 60%, Coalition 40% -> 20% when Void joins. DRIVEN in the check, not read.
- COMPOSED LINES ARE NEVER REWRITTEN. rosterOf returns a hand-composed roster BEFORE the derivation runs, so a statement about ONE battlefield always beats a statement about the war - the same guarantee jadeSet gives on the scalar model, through a different mechanism because a composed line is a stronger statement than a dial.
- THE COALITION IS ROUTED, NOT DUPLICATED: entry gate, jadeSet interaction, effJade floor. A second field would be TWO AUTHORITIES on whether it is at war. JADE REFUSED - cannot be declared into its own war. BROOD REFUSED - it is what is being fought, not a party that declares; compose it onto a line in War Controls instead.
- belligerents IS ON THE RESTORE LIST and was VERY NEARLY LEFT OFF. Off it, a restart withdraws every declared faction WHILE LEAVING THE COALITION DECLARED - a war that half-forgot itself, HARDER TO DIAGNOSE than one that forgot itself entirely.
- DEV ACCOUNTS PASS BOTH SEALS. A seal is a rule for players and an OBSTACLE FOR THE PERSON RUNNING THE GAME: a GM has to stand in a galaxy before opening it to anyone, and that was impossible without unsealing first - the one thing he is not ready to do. ONE PREDICATE PER SEAL, TAKING THE ACTOR, absent meaning NO exception, so a caller that forgets to pass one keeps EXACTLY the behaviour it had.
- THE BANNER ASKS THE SEAL, NOT THE PERMISSION. passageSealed is new and separate from passageOpen because a dev in a sealed Reach was being told it was OPEN - THE ONE READER WHO MOST NEEDS TO KNOW IT IS NOT. Same for the faction panel line and the colony funding note.
- EACH SERVER SITE DECIDES EXPLICITLY: six Jade routes and three Reach routes pass the actor; the SHARED ARBITRAGE BOARD DELIBERATELY DOES NOT, because publishing sealed prices to everyone listening is not the same act as a GM acting on a colony. lane-check asserts the price board is the ONLY actorless call.
- THE HIVE IS NOT A COSTUME. prawn1 sat in the ordinary selectable set, so ANY ACCOUNT could put on the face of the thing the entire war layer is about. The Reach reads as a standing threat because EXACTLY ONE VOICE speaks with that face; a station full of them is a joke about the enemy rather than the enemy.
- Removed from the picker and refused by /api/portrait FOR EVERYONE INCLUDING DEVS - two paths for one action is how a costume ends up on an account with NO RECORD of who put it there. New dev-gated, admin-logged /api/dev/portrait assigns it.
- THE PNG STAYS ON DISK and in PORTRAIT_SET: the boot sweep clears any stored id that no longer names a file, so deleting the art would STRIP THE PORTRAIT OFF THE ACCOUNT ALREADY WEARING IT.
- *** THE COMMANDER ART IS NOT IN THE REPO. *** portraits/ has exactly ONE Khai'sultull portrait: prawn1.png, the base drone. No commander variant exists anywhere in the tree. SHIPPED THE MECHANISM: hiveLordPortrait() resolves to prawn_commander if the file exists and prawn1 if not; /api/dev/portrait accepts portrait:'hive' which resolves through it, so the caller need not know whether the art has landed and NOTHING NEEDS CHANGING WHEN IT DOES.
- TO INSTALL: drop prawn_commander.png into client/assets/portraits/ and RESTART (PORTRAIT_SET is built from the directory at boot), then POST /api/dev/portrait {player:'Zharkofin', portrait:'hive'}.
- controls-check 193 -> 215, including the share arithmetic DRIVEN through the real module.
- FOUR CHECKS FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE, AND THE PATTERN IS WORTH NAMING. TWO pinned the restore list BY ITS LAST ENTRY, so appending belligerents broke them - a check that goes red when the list it guards is CORRECTLY EXTENDED ARGUES AGAINST EXTENDING IT. One grepped for Khai'sultull and matched THE COMMENT EXPLAINING WHY IT IS GONE (fifth time in this codebase); it PARSES THE MANIFEST OBJECT now, which also tests the thing that matters. One text-evaluated factions.js with `export` stripped, turning a hoisted const into a TEMPORAL DEAD ZONE ERROR; it IMPORTS THE MODULE now, which also means it drives exactly what the server imports.
- Suite green: controls 215, reach 1079, faction 3857, terrain 306, lane 58, depth 4101, plus city/lore/mathtest/mining/mobile/sudoku/tutorial/filter.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.8.0 changes (the advance was running backwards, and one control talked only to the presser)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/reach.js, server/server.js, server/city.js (comment), client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/god-panel.css, tools/controls-check.mjs (NEW), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- REACH_WORLDS IS NOT A LIST OF WORLDS, IT IS THE ROUTE. Index 0 is where the war starts, frontAllowed unlocks each entry off the one before it, and the panel renders in this sequence - so an order that does not match the map IS NOT COSMETIC, IT INVERTS THE CAMPAIGN. It was id order; the map was drawn later.
- MEASURED, NOT EYEBALLED. Return gate at (990,250). Nearest world VESSKANOTH (880,300) = 121 units. Sahn'tekk - THE WORLD WHOSE ID SAYS `gate` - at (120,120) = 880 units, THE FARTHEST BODY ON THE MAP. The advance began at the far side and crept BACK TOWARD THE DOOR IT CAME THROUGH, and the panel refused to open Vesskanoth until Thennsur, most of a galaxy away, was 40% taken.
- THE ID ks_gate_reach IS NOW A LIE AND IT STAYS: primary key in saved state, the KV store and COLONY_META. Renaming is a migration across THREE STORES to fix a name nobody sees.
- REACH_CITY_IDS DELIBERATELY NOT REORDERED. It is a Set asked "is this a Reach world" - sequence means nothing - and making it match would imply the two lists carry the same fact, leaving the next reorder with TWO lists to keep in step for no reason. The check asserts same MEMBERS.
- THE ROUTE SHIPS ON THE PAYLOAD (order, openAt). The panel took its sequence from OBJECT KEY ORDER and its 40% from a LITERAL; both are server facts and a client copy is a second authority that silently disagrees the next time the route moves - WHICH IT JUST DID. The card STATES THE UNLOCK RULE instead of answering a press with an error naming two world ids, and shows each world's position on the advance.
- PANELS REFORMATTED. Every row carried its own inline cssText - label column 52px here, 42px there, NOTHING LINED UP - which is why eight controls read as a wall. Grid now: fixed 74px label column, fixed right-aligned TABULAR value column so numbers compare DOWN the card, only the middle stretches (where the sliders are).
- FOUR SECTIONS: GROUND / THE WAR / WHAT IT COSTS / WHAT IS STANDING. These cards mix four unrelated jobs and a GM scanning for the fund should not read past the posture toggle. Engagements indented onto their own grid. Disabled controls SAY WHY on the card.
- *** THE BUG THE NEW CHECK EARNED ITSELF ON: reach_garrison replied with ws.send TO THE SOCKET THAT SENT IT AND NEVER BROADCAST. *** Garrison is what forcesFor reads to decide how hard the brood pushes back, so a GM raising it mid-session CHANGED THE FIGHT FOR HIMSELF AND NOBODY ELSE - unnoticed for exactly the reason it is hard to catch: the panel it is pressed from is the one surface that DID update. Every other reach_* write broadcasts; this was the single exception.
- tools/controls-check.mjs: 193 assertions, ALL FIFTEEN CONTROLS END TO END - panel function exists, sends the named command, server has a branch for that exact string, the branch calls the reach.js export, reach.js exports it, and it BROADCASTS rather than echoing.
- PLUS THE OTHER DIRECTION: no command on the wire without a sender, no inline onclick naming a function that does not exist. A handler with no button is a feature that cannot be used - same waste as a button with no handler and MUCH HARDER TO NOTICE.
- IT CANNOT CLICK THE BUTTON. A wiring check, not a behavioural one: it proves the wire is CONTINUOUS, not that the current at the far end is the right shape. The model underneath is DRIVEN in reach-check. Both are needed and neither is the other.
- FOUR OF ITS OWN ASSERTIONS WERE WRONG AND WERE FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE: guessed the export name from the command name (setHive, actually setControl); guessed the import alias by convention (reachFlipWorld, actually reachFlip); sliced on a SINGLE-SPACE literal against column-aligned assignments; looked for reach_disarm only in god-panel.js when it is sent by an inline onclick in index.html. A check that DICTATES NAMING or REPORTS FORMATTING AS BREAKAGE is worse than no check - the pressure is to change the code to suit it.
- *** NOTHING SHIPPED SINCE v1.3.9.x: the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION, and this is the patch before a VPS deploy. ***

## 1.6.7.1 changes (sealed means sealed, quiet means quiet)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/reach.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/lane-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THREE HOLES, ALL THE SAME SHAPE: THE FLAG EXISTS AND NOTHING CONSULTS IT.
- (1) THE SEAL DREW A BANNER AND STOPPED NOTHING. passageOpen was consulted in THREE places - portal sprite, lane to the gate, PASSAGE SEALED banner - and in NONE of them by swapGalaxy, THE FUNCTION THAT ACTUALLY MOVES THE PLAYER. The map said sealed with the player standing on it, and every route in walked straight through.
- Refused in swapGalaxy rather than at each caller, BECAUSE THE CALLERS ARE THE THING THAT KEEPS GROWING. The Coalition stays ALWAYS REACHABLE: a player who cannot get ANYWHERE is worse than one who got somewhere he should not have.
- AND IT EJECTS. Sealing while somebody is inside would strand them with NO LANE AND NO PORTAL TO LEAVE BY - worse than the bug being fixed. Checked against the galaxy the player is LOOKING AT, not the one being sealed, so a future third galaxy cannot slip through.
- (2) NO ZONE IS BORN LIVE. blankZones seeded `live: i === 0 ? 1 : 0` - THE FIRST ZONE OF EVERY WORLD CAME UP LIVE AT SEED. Even after v1.6.7.0 stopped the gate world opening its front, TEN WORLDS EACH HAD A RUNNING ENGAGEMENT UNDERNEATH, because `live` was never a consequence of the war having started: IT WAS A PROPERTY THE GROUND WAS BORN WITH. That is the Jade-versus-brood fight visible on a war nobody declared.
- THE FRONT IS WHAT MAKES GROUND LIVE NOW. Opening lights the first unfinished zone; closing quiets every zone on the world. `cleared` - the banked progress - is UNTOUCHED by both, so a front closed and reopened RESUMES rather than restarting the world.
- setWaves had THE SAME HOLE THROUGH A DIFFERENT COMMAND: raising the wave count re-opened cleared ground whether or not a front stood over it. Gated on w.front too.
- (3) A CURSOR IS A HINT; THE HANDLER IS THE GATE. The engagement row set its cursor from z.live and then bound the click handler UNCONDITIONALLY, so a row labelled QUIET opened a battle anyway. The other watch entry point already did this correctly, which is how it survived.
- reachWatch now also refuses a world with NO FRONT - a stronger statement than "no live zone" and the one the GM actually makes - so a future path that lights a zone without opening a front cannot put a battle on screen behind his back.
- reach-check 1051 -> 1068, with the fresh seed DRIVEN: blankState lifted and run, asserting ZERO fronts, ZERO live zones, every world quiet and the Coalition undeclared ALL AT ONCE. That conjunction is the property; three separate literals that each look right are not.
- ONE ASSERTION IN lane-check FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE: it sliced A FIXED 1200 CHARACTERS from window._setWormhole and went red when _setPassage grew the eject branch, with the calls it wanted STILL PRESENT three lines further down. A window measured in BYTES fails on any unrelated edit above the thing it wants, and the pressure is then to WIDEN THE NUMBER UNTIL IT STOPS COMPLAINING. Brace matched now.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.7.0 changes (the Reach takes the banner, not the wire, and a fresh Reach is quiet)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/server.js, server/reach.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE PRAWN WAR WAS COMPETING WITH MARKET NEWS AS IF IT WERE MARKET NEWS. The feed is a WIRE - rotations, fills, cargo, blockades, dozens of lines an hour - and a headline's whole life is the seconds before the next one pushes it up. A Khai'sultull transmission or a front being overrun scrolled past AT THE SAME WEIGHT AS A FUND BUYING TWO HUNDRED SHARES.
- NOT ONE OF THE EIGHT CALL SITES CHANGED. They already carried cat:'reach', which is the only reason this is a SEAM and not a rewrite.
- TWO LAYERS, NOT ONE. The GM's own banner is a DELIBERATE ACT WITH NO TIMER ON IT, and a war that silently ate it would make the panel unreliable IN EXACTLY THE MOMENT A GM IS LEANING ON IT. A Reach line sits ON TOP for 45s then STANDS DOWN to whatever the GM had set, untouched underneath.
- LAST ONE WINS rather than a queue: a queue would hold a push-window line behind four others, and A BANNER IS A STATEMENT ABOUT NOW.
- Precedence lives in ONE function (breakingPayload) so the init payload, the broadcast and the stand-down CANNOT DISAGREE - the sort of thing that only shows up when both layers are set. The stand-down is SCHEDULED, not computed at read time: the banner is PUSHED, so nothing would notice the expiry until the next unrelated broadcast.
- THE ARRAY IS THE ARCHIVE AND THE FEED IS NOT. A Reach line is still FILED (/state and the snapshot stay complete) but wireHeadlines filters cat:'reach' out of BOTH feed handouts. Without it, a player joining an hour later gets a war headline pushed into a market scroll: THE SAME BUG DEFERRED TO RECONNECT. Sliced AFTER the filter, or a burst of Reach traffic empties the thirty a new client gets.
- CLIENT: same element, same geometry, same tone colours - ONLY WHAT IT CALLS ITSELF. A Khai'sultull transmission carrying the word BREAKING reads as a market event, which is the confusion that put these lines in the wire to begin with. Falls through to the old label on a server that does not send src.
- A FRESH REACH IS QUIET. blankState opened the gate world AT SEED, on the reasoning that ten quiet worlds read as a war that has not started. THE CAUSALITY WAS BACKWARDS: that reading is CORRECT when the war has not started, and what was wrong was A SERVER COMING UP FOR THE FIRST TIME ALREADY FIGHTING SOMEBODY - no moment left for a GM to declare anything.
- Every world stays front 0 / status quiet; the gate opens through reach_front like every other front, so the first fight is A DECISION ON THE RECORD rather than a side effect of seeding. frontAllowed already returns true at index 0, so NO OVERRIDE IS NEEDED to start it.
- SCOPED TO A FRESH SEED. blankState runs when there is no saved Reach; an existing save KEEPS ITS FRONTS, because rewriting live war state to enforce a new default IS A MIGRATION and this is not one. A GM wanting an existing Reach quiet closes its fronts from the panel.
- reach-check 1031 -> 1051. One assertion asserted the OPPOSITE seed behaviour and is INVERTED WITH ITS REASONING RECORDED, not deleted. The routing is DRIVEN - pushHeadline lifted and run - confirming reach never broadcasts news, stays out of the wire, stays in the archive, and outranks the GM banner while live.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

- AND THE WIREFRAME PATH HAD EXACTLY TWO FACTION COLOURS. pInf/pTank/pHeli/pKnife (Coalition) and jInf/jTank/jHeli/jKnife (Jade), picked by `const jd = u.fac === 'jade'` - A TWO-WAY SWITCH ON A SIX-WAY FACT.
- So a Void rifleman that fell to wireframe DREW IN COALITION BLUE. That is the blue stick figures in the shot, and it is precisely the "same unit changes faction when it gets far enough away" failure the FAC comment warns about, arriving through the FALLBACK instead of through the tables. ONE ROOT CAUSE AND ONE CONSEQUENCE THAT WOULD HAVE OUTLIVED THE FIX.
- pathsFor(fac) allocates ON DEMAND: one faction a side pays for TWO sets, not six. The stroke loop WALKS WHAT WAS ALLOCATED rather than a hand-written list - two named blocks meant a THIRD faction drew in the first one's colour.
- THE NAMED COALITION SET IS DELETED, NOT KEPT. A named set for one faction beside a per-faction allocator is a SECOND AUTHORITY on where that faction's geometry goes. What remains is genuinely not per-faction: brood classes (one faction by definition) and the dead (none that matters at 40% alpha).
- reach-check 1027 -> 1031. Two assertions pinned the two-faction path layout, UPDATED NOT DELETED: the property they defend - a path strokes in ONE colour, so a faction needs its own - is unchanged and now holds for SIX instead of two. A new assertion pins the animation picker to the faction SO THIS CANNOT COME BACK A FOURTH TIME.

## 1.6.6.0 changes (the terrain, which was three silent fallbacks stacked)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/battle-test.html, tools/bench-manifests.mjs (NEW), client/assets/space/bench-manifests.js (NEW, GENERATED), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/faction-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- REPORTED FIVE TIMES. Something else was fixed four of those times. THREE SEPARATE SILENT FALLBACKS, none of which errors and all of which DRAW SOMETHING PLAUSIBLE.
- (1) THE BENCH NEVER LOADED THE PLANET TABLES, and this was WRONG OVER HTTP TOO. paletteFor reads window.PLANET_PALETTE and window.COLONY_PLANET; terrainKey reads window.REACH_TERRAIN. All three come from planet-palette.js and galaxy.js and the bench loaded NEITHER, so every lookup missed and the field fell to TERRAIN_COL - the legacy table whose own comment records four of ten Reach worlds fighting on a colour their planet is not.
- MEASURED: ALL TEN WORLDS RESOLVED TO dust / rock (150,124,86). ONE TAN FOR THE ENTIRE REACH. After: ks_02 (255,138,76) red desert, ks_04 (249,137,99) lava, ks_05 (91,178,157) teal rift, ks_07 (41,183,255) ocean, ks_08 (156,157,171) ice.
- AND THE TERRAIN KEY WAS WRONG, WHICH IS WORSE THAN THE COLOUR. The key decides what cover is SHAPED like. Collapsed to dust, ks_04 generated boulders and craters where the game generates SEAMS, ks_05 instead of CHASMS, ks_07 instead of pressure terraces. SEVEN OF TEN WORLDS GENERATING THE WRONG COVER GEOMETRY - so every judgement made on this bench about bounding, cover claiming or collision was made on GROUND THE GAME DOES NOT HAVE.
- (2) NO ROCK MESHES. loadMeshes FETCHES nature/meshes.json and FETCH IS BLOCKED ON file://; the catch sets _meshPend=2, "wireframe keeps the field". Every rock, ridge and block drew as an UNTEXTURED PRISM. That is the flat tan slab terrain.
- (3) NO BROOD GEOMETRY. loadBrood fetches brood/geometry.json, same block, same silence. THOSE ARE THE WIREFRAME ENEMIES.
- FIX: tools/bench-manifests.mjs generates client/assets/space/bench-manifests.js with FOUR things - COLONY_PLANET and REACH_TERRAIN LIFTED BY BRACE MATCHING from galaxy.js (7215 lines and a map renderer), plus the two manifests as objects. Both modules ALREADY HAD the override hooks FM_NATURE_SRC and FM_BROOD_GEOM for exactly this.
- GENERATED, NOT HAND-COPIED, and asserted current: brood geometry has been regenerated TWICE and a stale frame index would STROBE or walk into the next creature's attack row rather than fail visibly.
- THE BANNER SHIPPED LAST PATCH SAID "terrain and pricing are all unaffected by this". FLATLY WRONG. The same origin rules that block getImageData block fetch; terrain and the brood are hit HARDER by file:// than the uniforms are. A banner that names what is fine HAS TO BE RIGHT ABOUT IT or it is worse than no banner.
- THREE NEW STATUS ROWS - PLANET PALETTE, ROCK MESHES, BROOD SHEETS - one per fallback, so the next one NAMES ITSELF instead of being reported five times as "the terrain is wrong".
- faction-check 3783 -> 3857, including the palette DRIVEN through the real paletteFor and terrainKey against the bench's globals: ten worlds, SIX distinct rock colours, FIVE terrain keys, NONE left on the fallback tan.
- ONE ASSERTION FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE, FOR THE FOURTH TIME IN THIS FILE: it compared filenames with indexOf and matched the HEADER COMMENT above every script tag, reporting a loading order THAT WAS NOT THE LOADING ORDER. Compared as script tags now.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.5.3 changes (the bench was not outdated, it was running from a file:// URL)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/battle-test.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/faction-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- REPORTED OUTDATED THREE TIMES. The screenshot settled it: TURQUOISE HOUNDS and FIVE IDENTICAL UNIFORMS in the parade - exactly what the game looked like BEFORE the faction pass, which is why it kept reading as stale.
- tinted() reads the sheet back with getImageData to remap skin, optics and accents BY EXACT PALETTE VALUE. A canvas holding an image loaded from file:// is TAINTED by the browser's origin rules, getImageData throws SecurityError, and the catch returns the RAW IMAGE.
- THAT CATCH IS CORRECT FOR THE GAME - a tint that throws must not lose the figure - and it is INDISTINGUISHABLE ON SCREEN from a build with no factions in it.
- tintedPattern's brightness probe throws for the same reason and grades the ground BLIND, which is the washed-out flat terrain in the same shot. ONE ROOT CAUSE, WHOLE PICTURE.
- THERE IS NO CLIENT-SIDE FIX. fetch and XHR are blocked on file:// in Chrome too, so the pixels cannot be reached by any route. The client already serves client/: open http://localhost:<port>/battle-test.html instead of the file on disk. WHAT IS FIXABLE IS THE SILENCE.
- PROBED FUNCTIONALLY, NOT BY SNIFFING THE PROTOCOL. location.protocol would be a proxy for the thing that matters and WRONG IN BOTH DIRECTIONS: local file access can be enabled, and a misconfigured host can taint over http.
- tinted() returns a NEW CANVAS per faction when the remap ran and the SAME UNDERLYING <img> for every faction when it threw, so the bench compares OBJECT IDENTITY for Void against Guild. Two factions whose kit could not look more different handing back ONE IDENTICAL OBJECT is the failure, stated exactly. Both chosen because neither can take tinted's early "nothing to do" return.
- A BANNER, NOT A STATUS ROW. THIRD SILENT DEGRADATION IN THIS BENCH IN THREE PATCHES: missing script tag (1.6.5.1, three factions instead of six), mis-spelled hook key (1.6.5.2, a dash reading as "nothing happening"), tainted canvas (now, the pre-faction build). ALL THREE RENDERED SOMETHING PLAUSIBLE INSTEAD OF AN ERROR, and the first two were missed because the warning was a small row in a scrolling panel.
- It shouts across the top, NAMES THE FIX rather than only the fault, and SCOPES THE DAMAGE HONESTLY: geometry, AI counters, roster and pricing are UNAFFECTED, and a banner that overstates gets dismissed.
- faction-check 3763 -> 3779. ONE ASSERTION FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE, FOR THE THIRD TIME IN THIS FILE: it scanned for location.protocol and matched THE COMMENT EXPLAINING WHY IT IS NOT USED. A check that cannot tell a branch from a description of one FORCES THE HISTORY OUT OF THE FILE to go green. Comments stripped; a second assertion now requires the explanation to STAY.
- Suite green: 4101 depth, 1027 reach, 3779 faction, 305 terrain, plus city/lane/lore/mathtest/mining/mobile/sudoku/tutorial.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.5.2 changes (the AI half of the bench, which the last patch left out)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/battle-test.html, client/assets/reach-battle.js (7 new hooks, pause+step in the loop), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/faction-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- v1.6.5.1 fixed the bench's FACTION rendering and shipped it AS IF THE JOB WERE DONE. Every instrument for judging BEHAVIOUR was still missing.
- THE LEVER IT NEVER HAD: TEMPO, PAUSE, SINGLE STEP. Bounding overwatch, cover claiming, suppression and target acquisition all resolve FASTER THAN A PERSON CAN FOLLOW at the shipped 0.24 tempo, so what gets reported is an IMPRESSION OF THE AGGREGATE - and those impressions have been WRONG HERE IN BOTH DIRECTIONS (heading churn was measured and did not separate two implementations at all).
- At a fifth speed a bound is A MAN WALKING TO A ROCK, and either he arrives on his own side of it or he does not.
- THE CAMERA KEEPS RUNNING WHILE HELD, deliberately: a frozen field you cannot orbit is a SCREENSHOT. Nothing in the client can set the hold flag - reachWatch never touches it and the god panel has no path to it.
- SEVEN COUNTERS NEVER READABLE FROM OUTSIDE THE FILE. stepField writes bound/cover/supp/melee/air/shield/kills EVERY FRAME and EXACTLY ONE reached a human: coverCount, through the fps line, behind a debug flag.
- bound IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS. The comment on that block claims a THIRD of the line is moving; measured it was ELEVEN PERCENT, and the fix was tuning a phase window AGAINST A NUMBER NOBODY COULD SEE.
- aimQuality, states, nades, built and probe were ALL ALREADY THERE and none was shown. aimQuality is what "men turn around at random" is actually about: whether a man shoots at the enemy who is GENUINELY nearest. states is PER CLASS, because AN AGGREGATE HIDES THE CASE THAT MATTERS - a class that never bounds and one that never holds average out to a line that looks fine.
- NEW HOOKS: tempo(v), pause(on), step(n), field(), slots(), terrain(), forceAwayClass(cls).
- slots() is cover SUPPLY against DEMAND, the pressure the whole bounding model sits under: SIXTY POSITIONS CONTESTED BY TWO HUNDRED AND THIRTY UNITS is what collapsed the line onto a few rocks and is why terCount went up.
- terrain() reports features BY KIND and how many block, which is how you CHECK the claim that collision is keyed on kind and covers all seven terrains, rather than trusting it.
- forceAwayClass is the mirror of forceCoalClass, which only ever had the home side. Fine while the away side was always creatures; NOT FINE NOW.
- TWO BUGS IN THE BENCH CODE ITSELF, both caught by READING rather than running. (1) The AI panel guessed `exactlyNearest` against a hook returning `exactNearest`, WITH A FALLBACK CHAIN, so it would have shown A DASH FOREVER and read as "nothing is happening" rather than as a typo. (2) kills is CUMULATIVE and sits beside six PER-FRAME counters; read as a rate it would have shown a number that only ever climbs AND CALLED IT ATTRITION. Differenced.
- faction-check 3718 -> 3763. It asserts EVERY KEY THE BENCH READS against the hook's own return literal - same class of check as the pricing mirror against the server - plus a DUPLICATE-KEY check on the debug object: two keys of the same name is not an error in any mode, and that silently killed a richer counts() for six versions.
- ONE ASSERTION HAD TO BE FIXED RATHER THAN THE CODE: it scanned for `f.` and matched the FOB local three functions away, demanding field() return `type` and `length`. A check that cannot tell two scopes apart FAILS ON CORRECT CODE, which is the worst kind - the pressure is then to delete it.
- Suite green: 4101 depth, 1027 reach, 3763 faction, 305 terrain, plus city/lane/lore/mathtest/mining/mobile/sudoku/tutorial.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

A missing script tag is a QUIETER VERSION of the bench embedding its own renderer, which is the one thing that file's header has warned against since it shipped.

- THE PARADE: one figure per faction at 2.6x through FMTroops.drawFrame, the SAME CALL the muster strip and the battlefield make. A recolour CANNOT BE JUDGED AT FIELD SIZE - a soldier is twenty pixels, the black and the brown armour only separate at 4x, and the optic is TWO PIXELS.
- The shielded enforcer is the figure ON PURPOSE: the only class carrying BOTH accent channels (the faceplate the optic burns, the shield panel the accent marks), so one silhouette shows whether either landed. Engineer alternates in for the wrist device. Skin pinned to index 0 so it is a UNIFORM comparison, not a skin one.
- TWO HOOKS FOR TWO INVISIBLE BUGS. _fmReachDebug.depth(): front, deepest/shallowest live unit per side, count off the field. A TANK THAT REVERSED OFF THE MAP IS OFF THE MAP, which is why nobody saw it. _fmReachDebug.stuck(): live units inside a solid hull - a body half inside a rock LOOKS LIKE a body beside a rock.
- Both should read ZERO. Drag HIVE HOLDS to 95 then to 5; OFF FIELD must stay 0 at BOTH ends.
- THE FIELD READOUT WAS LYING BY OMISSION. Its label table named NINE classes; broodClass alone returns EIGHT, four of them missing - so a field full of brutes and leapers read as NO CONTACT in the one readout that exists to say what is on it. All fourteen named, plus a per-faction mix, which is the readout the roster editor is USELESS WITHOUT.
- THE PRICING MIRROR IS PRESERVED VERBATIM. reach-check LIFTS that block out of the HTML and evaluates it against the server; it caught a two-release drift once already.

## 1.6.5.0 changes (War Controls: any faction, any battlefield, either side)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** Files: server/reach.js, server/server.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/index.html, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/faction-check.mjs, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- v1.6.4.0 shipped the roster primitive WITH NOTHING READING IT and no tab, deliberately. This is the other half: THE FIELD READS A ROSTER, so the buttons move men. pickFac samples it for EVERY unit at both spawn sites.
- NOTHING WAS MIGRATED AND PRESENCE IS THE SWITCH. w.jade/jadeSet/jadeFwd are persisted state on a DB SEVENTY VERSIONS BEHIND A DEPLOY, and a bad migration there is a RESTORE FROM CRON BACKUP, not a patch.
- NO w.roster = a Reach world, roster DERIVED on read, nothing stored, every existing save identical. w.roster PRESENT = a GM composed a line and it is the authority, effJade deriving FROM it. Same pattern jadeSet already established. ONE AUTHORITY PER WORLD EITHER WAY.
- REVERT DELETES THE ROSTER AND THE DERIVED ANSWER COMES BACK EXACTLY, because nothing was overwritten to produce it. That is what makes composing safe to poke at live on stream.
- SIDE -1 MEANT BROOD BY COINCIDENCE, the deepest thing in the pass. The away side had ALWAYS been the brood, so the class table, the sprite gate and the animation router all asked `side === -1` and got the right answer BY ACCIDENT. Put the Syndicate on the away line and EVERY SOLDIER WOULD HAVE BEEN A CRAWLING HORROR.
- awayClass routes on the FACTION. A brood faction draws creatures; a polity draws INFANTRY - riflemen, shield troopers, the odd engineer - because there is no funding on the away side and nobody buys the enemy gunships. That is what a garrison is.
- FACTION IS DRAWN BEFORE CLASS AT BOTH SPAWN SITES. The class table depends on it, so the other order gives a Syndicate soldier a crawling horror's class AND THEN a uniform: the same bug arriving one line later. The sprite gate names the faction too, not the side.
- POSTURE GENERALISED. The forward offset read `u.fac==='jade'` on one branch and `jadeFrac>0` on the other - TWO SPELLINGS OF THE SAME JADE CASE, neither of which survives a line with no Jade on it. The roster names the forward faction. ZERO when a side has one faction, because a posture is a RELATIONSHIP between two parts of a line.
- COMMANDS: war_roster (world, side, entries), war_roster_clear, war_forward. Sanitised at the SOCKET BOUNDARY as well as in setRoster, because that is the untrusted edge. All three BROADCAST: open battlefields restock from the mix as men die.
- AN EMPTY SIDE IS REFUSED. A battlefield with nobody on one half is not a state the renderer has a reading of: the band clamp would have a front with no army behind it and reinforcement would restock from a mix with no members.
- THE BROOD IS NOT SPECIAL-CASED OUT OF THE HOME SIDE. The layer's own intended end state has the hive lord keeping 1/3 to 2/3 of its worlds and PERSISTING AS A STANDING POWER, and a power that persists is one that can be allied with. Refusing it would be this file deciding a STORY question that belongs to the GM.
- WEIGHTS, NOT PERCENTAGES. 3 and 1 means three quarters and one quarter; the server normalises ON READ AND NEVER ON WRITE. Faction labels wear their OWN LINE COLOUR in the panel, because composing a line IS choosing what the field will look like.
- SEPARATE TAB FROM PRAWN WAR, DELIBERATELY. The Reach is ONE WAR with a named enemy and a story; this is the GENERAL MACHINERY. Filing it under the shrimp would have made the faction war a feature of the bug war forever. Both tabs render from ONE PAYLOAD - a second fetch is a second thing that can be stale by a different amount.
- faction-check 3660 -> 3683, asserting EVERY LINK END TO END (button, pane, command, handler, model, field), because "a control wired to nothing" is the specific failure this tab was held back a patch to avoid.
- reach-check 1025 -> 1027 and reach-terrain both carried assertions pinning the old two-faction spellings. UPDATED, NOT DELETED: the properties they defend are unchanged.
- Suite green: 4101 depth, 1027 reach, 3683 faction, 305 terrain, plus city/lane/lore/mathtest/mining/mobile/sudoku/tutorial.

- The battlefield already said the right thing and then did not do it: "side stops meaning Coalition and starts meaning belligerent A and B." True of side. NOT true of anything else.
- ONE ROW PER FACTION. VOID COLLECTIVE black armour, steel casing, purple on BOTH channels. SYNDICATE brown, multiracial, visor as drawn - the one row that remaps nothing. MERCHANT GUILD desert camo, the DARKER HALF of the skin range, blue visor. Coalition and Jade unchanged.
- THE ENGINEER'S BLUE PIXELS ARE ON HIS WRIST, NOT HIS CHEST, and the shipped comment says otherwise. It reasoned from the ROW number alone ("rows 30-32, mid-torso") and NEVER CHECKED THE COLUMN. On engineer_idle cell 0 the torso at row 30 spans x 9-20; the blue is at x 30-32, at the forward edge of the arm he is holding out. A GAUNTLET DEVICE. The torso at that row has no blue on it at all.
- NOT PEDANTRY ABOUT A COMMENT. That reading is WHY paintsEye EXISTS: the first pass recoloured it, got "a red light on his sternum", and built a per-cell face-finding routine instead. The eye routine WORKS AND STAYS; the premise is corrected and the wrist is now the secondary channel.
- TWO ACCENT CHANNELS, NOT ONE, because they are two different sentences. OPTIC is what is behind the glass: enforcer faceplate (194,134,42)/(143,96,26), and the ASSAULT GOGGLES (128,157,160)/(86,125,121) WHICH NOTHING HAS EVER TOUCHED. ACCENT is equipment marking: engineer wrist (100,138,194)/(61,96,147) and shield panel (51,81,111)/(70,98,103).
- opticOn:'augmented' PRESERVES THE SHIPPED COALITION EXACTLY. The assault trooper has a FACE, so his goggles are not a place to claim he is not a person. A faction of machines has no such distinction and takes 'all'.
- TWO BUGS THE DRIVEN CHECK CAUGHT THAT A CODE READ DID NOT, both in tables that described themselves correctly.
- (1) THE VOID WAS DARK AND ALSO FLAT. 0.44/0.46/0.55 lift 0.17 gave a luminance spread of 23 against Jade's 61 on the pack's six real uniform colours. A HEAVY LIFT ON A SMALL MULTIPLIER COMPRESSES THE RANGE RATHER THAN MOVING IT, and a figure spanning 23 values has no readable limbs at twenty pixels. Dark is a MULTIPLIER problem, flat is a LIFT problem, and lift was solving both. Now 0.66/0.68/0.84 lift 0.04: spread 40, darkest at 25.
- (2) THE GUILD'S CAMO BREAK WAS OUTSIDE THE ART. at:96 reads like a midpoint of 0-255; the pack's uniforms span 28-89, so EVERY pixel took the split grade and THE BASE GRADE NEVER RAN ONCE. A flat brown the table called camouflage. Moved to 58, between the coat's shadow cluster (28-42) and its lit cluster (67-89).
- SERVER: A LINE IS A LIST OF SHARES, NOT A NUMBER. w.jade=0.4 only parses because there are exactly two candidates and the reader knows which.
- SIDE STAYS +1/-1 AND STAYS ARITHMETIC. u.side is a NUMBER in the damage path (-p.side) and two dozen band expressions; a third value breaks combat in ways that do not show until somebody watches a specific blast. A roster has TWO SIDES FOREVER; a three-way war is an ALIGNMENT on those two.
- rosterFromReach reproduces effJade EXACTLY across its whole input range, asserted against the LIFTED LIVE FUNCTION, including the JADE_MIN floor WHICH THE CHECK CAUGHT ME DROPPING. The floor is applied in the BRIDGE, not the primitive: "Jade never leaves its own war" is a fact about the Khai'sultull war, not about rosters.
- *** NOTHING IS MIGRATED ONTO IT YET AND THERE IS NO WAR CONTROLS TAB YET, DELIBERATELY. *** effJade, jade_commit and coalition_enter are UNTOUCHED and remain the live path. The god panel's own Reach comment names the reason: "a switch wired to nothing rots untested. That is exactly how the trial gate sat broken across 120 routes for four patches while static checks passed." The tab is the NEXT patch, once a roster is what the field actually reads.
- tools/faction-check.mjs: 3660 assertions, including the grade DRIVEN on the pack's real uniform colours and a PAIRWISE test that no two factions wear the same uniform at distance.
- 14 assertions in reach-check and reach-terrain pinned the old table shapes and are UPDATED, NOT DELETED: the properties they defend are unchanged. reach-terrain 300 -> 305.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.3.1 changes (armour reversing off the map, ground that was scenery to the pathing)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js (build stamp), tools/reach-depth-check.mjs (NEW), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- TWO REPORTS, ONE PATCH, NOT RELATED TO EACH OTHER. And the tanks are NOT a sprite fault this time: v1.6.3.0 fixed a MIRRORED HOUND, this is the GEOMETRY UNDERNEATH IT.
- CL.front IS z.hive/100 AND NOTHING ELSE. server/reach.js seeds every zone at hive:100 and relights a cleared one at hive:100, so THE FIRST THING ANYONE EVER SEES on a new front is front=0.95, the top of the clamp. Not an edge case: the DEFAULT STATE of every zone that has just opened.
- EVERY DEPTH OFFSET WAS A FLAT CONSTANT ADDED TO front. Tank standoff front+0.20, front+0.30 struck, engineer band ceiling front+0.40+fwd, infantry ceiling front+0.18. At 0.95 those are 1.15, 1.25, 1.41 and 1.13.
- wz(y)=(1-y)*FIELD_D MAPS ANYTHING PAST 1 TO NEGATIVE Z: behind our own baseline, on top of the camera. NOTHING CLAMPED IT.
- SO THE TANK DID NOT DISAPPEAR. It drove out of the world and KEPT FIGHTING: still alive, still in units, still acquiring, still firing, still counted in the reinforcement mix AGAINST FUNDING A PLAYER HAD JUST COMMITTED. The armour was bought and then parked off screen.
- THE MIRROR IS EQUALLY REAL and only looks rarer because a zone has to be NEARLY WON to reach it: at front=0.05 a spitter's band floor is -0.17 and the brood walks off the far edge.
- PINNING THE LINE TO MIDFIELD WOULD HAVE HIDDEN THIS, NOT FIXED IT. front is not a starting position, it is a PURE FUNCTION OF SERVER CONTROL - the one property the camp code exists to preserve. A midfield default stops the picture reporting who holds the zone AND leaves the same units leaving the field from the other edge as soon as a push lands.
- SCALED, NOT CLAMPED. Offsets multiply by the room that ACTUALLY EXISTS behind the line. A clamp puts eng, tank and rifleman all on 0.98 and the line loses its layering EXACTLY WHEN IT IS MOST PINNED; scaling collapses them proportionally, so an army with its back to the baseline reads as STACKED UP WITH NO DEPTH. That is what pinned looks like.
- A HARD RAIL AT Y_LO/Y_HI SITS UNDERNEATH, so the next depth-offset mistake is a unit pressed against the baseline rather than one driving off the map still shooting.
- GROUND IS SOLID NOW, ON ALL SEVEN TERRAINS. The COVER half has worked for a long time (two firing positions per metre of frontage per feature, fireteams taking a rock weighted toward their objective, COVER_STOP eating frontal fire) and NONE OF IT WAS REBUILT. What was missing: a unit WITHOUT a claimed slot WALKED THROUGH THE ROCK THE SLOT WAS ON. Riflemen crossed station decking; tanks drove through chasms the generator's own comment calls IMPASSABLE.
- BLOCKING IS KEYED ON KIND, NOT ON MAP. TERRAIN_KIND already gives all seven terrains their vocabulary out of one shared set of kinds, so a rule on kind covers dust, veins, rift, ice, station, tether and ocean at once, WITH NO PER-WORLD TABLE TO DRIFT.
- CRATERS STAY PASSABLE ON PURPOSE: shallow, and the ONLY feature you fight from INSIDE rather than behind. Solid, it becomes a wall men walk around. Chasms (5-9 deep) DO block.
- THE HULL IS THE GENERATING ELLIPSE, DELIBERATELY INSIDE THE JITTERED MESH. The outline reaches w*0.5*1.22 on its widest lobe; slots sit at cy +/- h*0.52. A hull matching the lobes would leave men UNABLE TO REACH the cover the same rock provides. Asserted: all 52 features, all 7 terrains.
- ONE RESOLUTION PASS, AFTER THE WHOLE UNIT LOOP. Four places in stepField write a position and the next branch somebody adds is the fifth; a pass after all of them CANNOT BE FORGOTTEN BY CODE THAT DOES NOT EXIST YET.
- ONE LOOK-AHEAD so a column ROUNDS a ridge instead of SCRAPING ALONG IT. Not a path search: 700 units cannot afford one, and the line already has objective-directed lateral motion to ride on. Air unaffected. PROJECTILES UNAFFECTED - terrain occlusion on rounds changes the whole fire economy and is a separate job.
- THREE FAULTS THE NUMERIC HARNESS FOUND THAT A CODE READ DID NOT. tools/reach-depth-check.mjs LIFTS THE REAL EXPRESSIONS OUT OF THE SOURCE AND EVALUATES THEM rather than matching text around them, which is the whole reason it exists: two previous fixes here READ CORRECTLY AND WERE LIVE.
- (1) A hive tank scaled against DEPTH_NEED_HIVE=0.22, taken off the SPITTER band, still crossed the far edge from hive 8 through 31. The struck-tank standoff is 0.30. The constant was wrong THE MOMENT the standoff was made side-aware; it is the max over every offset measured off front on that side now, and the drift is asserted.
- (2) OVERLAPPING FEATURES left a unit STUCK after a single push pass. genTerrain scatters 52 hulls with NO separation rule and a seam on veins runs to 0.30 wide, so clearing the rock a man is in routinely put him inside the next. Four sweeps with an early bail, MEASURED not guessed.
- (3) (z.hive||50) CANNOT TELL A CLEARED ZONE FROM A MISSING FIELD. A zone at hive 0 - every hive-held metre taken, the whole point of the layer - fell through to the 50 default and drew its line AT MIDFIELD. The picture said the fight was even on ground that had just been won. Same for intensity 0.
- 4101 assertions in reach-depth. reach-terrain 300/300 and reach-check 1025/1025 unchanged.
- LEFT ALONE DELIBERATELY: jade-forward posture is -0.055 against an infantry band floor of 0.015. It is LARGER THAN THE FLOOR IT IS ADDED TO, so jade-forward already stands home infantry ~0.04 PAST THE FRONT, on ground campOwner says the hive holds, AT EVERY FRONT POSITION and not only the extremes. The band clamp HIDES it now, which is why it is written down. Posture design question, not an edge-of-map one. JACOB'S CALL.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.3.0 changes (blue bullets, backwards tanks, a gun that fired every ten seconds)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THREE SEPARATE CAUSES BEHIND THREE SYMPTOMS.
- THE BLUE BULLETS WERE THE MUZZLE FLASH, NOT THE TRACER. Friendly flashes and impacts go through a path stroked PALE CYAN - the colour of an ENERGY WEAPON, on an army whose sheets draw BRASS AND POWDER. The TRACERS were made warm two patches ago and this was MISSED, so the shot was amber and the flash AT BOTH ENDS OF IT was blue.
- THE HOUND IS DRAWN FACING LEFT. The battlefield mirrors everything on one rule that assumed art drawn facing RIGHT - true of the troop pack, false of the tank. EVERY TANK ON THE FIELD WAS REVERSED: driving forward and shooting over its own engine deck.
- It read as plausible because a tank is near enough SYMMETRICAL in silhouette at field size and the gun is thin.
- WHICH WAY A SHEET'S ART FACES IS A PROPERTY OF THE SHEET. Same shape as the anchor and the pixel density before it - ONE PROPERTY OF ONE SHEET TAKEN AS A PROPERTY OF THE RENDERER - and the THIRD TIME this pack has produced that mistake. faceLeft is declared per sheet; the mirror asks.
- THE FIRING ANIMATION WAS EFFECTIVELY NEVER SEEN, a second fault under the same complaint. The sheet was chosen off u.fire, a RECOIL TERM that decays in ~100ms, while hound_fire is 20 frames at 55ms = 1100ms of flash and clearing smoke. CUT OFF TWO FRAMES IN; the tank snapped back to walking mid-flash.
- Driven off fireAt now - the same stamp the frame picker already used - with the duration NAMED ONCE instead of implied in two places that disagreed.
- AND THE CADENCE WAS SET FOR A DIFFERENT GAME: 8-14 seconds, chosen when armour was a RARE WIREFRAME SILHOUETTE and each shell was meant to land as an EVENT. A funded push now puts a dozen Hounds on the field WITH a firing animation, and at that rate most are idle most of the time.
- 4.5s: still far slower than a rifle at 3, so it reads as a gun being RELOADED rather than one being PARKED. Bench: hound_fire is 8.7% of Hound frames, from effectively zero.
- Two assertions pinned the old behaviour (the flip expression, the sheet choice) and are updated; the properties they defend are UNCHANGED.
- reach-terrain 293 -> 300. Suite 15/15, 1977.

- MEASURED ON THE BENCH: jade 265 / coal 0 before. jade 265 / coal 0 after.
- *** THE SAME TRAP WAS IN THE GAME, AND WORSE THERE *** A GM could declare an interstellar power into the war and watch NOTHING HAPPEN on any of TEN WORLDS until they dialled each one BY HAND. The declaration is the event; it should look like one.
- UNTOUCHED WORLDS NOW TAKE A DEFAULT MIX ON ENTRY: 60% Jade, because it is still THEIR war and they opened it, and the Coalition arrived late. Worlds the GM EXPLICITLY dialled keep their value.
- THAT IS WHAT jadeSet EXISTS FOR: there is NO value of `jade` that can mean "unset", so "has anyone touched this" had to be a FLAG rather than a comparison against 1. The move is LOGGED so the GM knows what shifted rather than discovering it.
- WITHDRAWAL DOES NOT PUT THEM BACK, deliberately: leaving the dials where they are means RE-DECLARING RESTORES THE WAR AS IT WAS rather than resetting it, and effJade already returns 1 while coalIn is 0 whatever is stored.

- NEW facMix() on the debug hook: the Coalition toggle is exactly the kind of control whose effect is EASY TO ASSERT AND HARD TO SEE, so it gets a number rather than only a screenshot.

## 1.6.2.0 changes (the whole pack, and the bench was lying again)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/space/nature/meshes.json (18 NEW meshes), tools/nature-meshes.py, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- "DEAD AND BARE ONLY" WAS TOO NARROW. Written to stop a pine forest on Ussaleth - still the right instinct - but it ALSO BANNED THE CACTUS, and a cactus is exactly what a desert world wants and says NOTHING about pine forests. The rule banned a CATEGORY when the thing it cared about was CLIMATE.
- GROWTH IS PER TERRAIN KEY NOW, which is already the climate statement the palette and ground patches read:
-   dust    cacti, dead palms, dry scrub, tufts
-   veins   dead pines, snapped trunks, bare bushes  (NO cacti)
-   rift    fungus, scrub, a dead willow
-   tether  dead broadleaf, snapped trunks, scrub
-   ocean   fungus, tide-line scrub, a dead palm
-   ice     snow-loaded bushes, winter trees          (NO timber, NO cacti)
-   station almost nothing
- Nothing can appear on a world whose recipe does not name it. STRONGER than a blanket ban and MORE USEFUL, because it can say YES.
- 14 meshes -> 32: five dead timbers, four bare simple trees, two winter forms, the cactus, four bushes, two snow bushes, grass, two mushrooms, two snow rocks, a third cliff. Willows and oaks are ~1000 faces each and OUT ON COST ALONE.
- HEIGHT IS A PROPERTY OF THE MESH, NOT A SHARED ROLL. Every prop took the same 1-5m range, which put MUSHROOMS THE SIZE OF TREES next to BUSHES THE SIZE OF MUSHROOMS. Cactus 1.8-3.4m, dead pine 5-9m, grass knee high - facts about the OBJECT, not about where it stands.
- BUDGET IS IN FACES, NOT PROPS. Cacti 436 faces, dead pines 247, bush 22. "How many props" stopped being a useful cap the moment the pack was used properly: 60 bushes and 60 cacti are the SAME COUNT and 20x the cost.
- Sorted by depth, drawn until spent: expensive ones near camera always draw, the first dropped are the FURTHEST - which is also the right order visually. Per world 34-64 generated, 16-36 drawn.
- *** THE BENCH WAS UNDER-REPORTING AGAIN. SECOND TIME. ***
- TWO `counts:` KEYS IN ONE OBJECT LITERAL. Two keys of the same name is NOT AN ERROR IN ANY MODE - the later silently replaces the earlier - so the rich counts() written for the bench in 1.5.6.0 HAS BEEN DEAD EVER SINCE. Every reading came from a four-line one reporting class tallies.
- CLASS TALLIES LOOK ENTIRELY PLAUSIBLE. NOTHING EVER LOOKED WRONG.
- First time was _animSeen missing anything bypassing queueSprite (egg clutches reported not-drawing while drawing). SAME PATTERN, WORTH NAMING: AN INSTRUMENT THAT RETURNS A PLAUSIBLE SUBSET OF THE TRUTH IS MORE DANGEROUS THAN ONE THAT RETURNS NOTHING - nothing prompts a question, a plausible subset does not. A check now asserts EXACTLY ONE counts key.
- ONE OF MY OWN NEW ASSERTIONS FAILED CORRECTLY: it sliced FLORA_MIX with an UNANCHORED indexOf, and `dust:`/`veins:` also appear in the terrain colour fallback and KIND_MESH - so it read the WRONG TABLE and truthfully found no cacti. Anchored, with the reason written where the next person slicing a table by key name will hit it.
- reach-terrain 282 -> 293. Suite 15/15, 1955.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.1.0 changes (the men stopped turning round, and the war starts at the gate)
- *** SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. *** server/reach.js gains frontAllowed; setFront takes a force flag. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, server/reach.js, server/server.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** WHY THE MEN TURNED ROUND AT RANDOM - TWO FAULTS, NEITHER IN THE FACING CODE ***
- (1) pickTarget WAS A RANDOM SAMPLE: 26 units drawn out of ~700, nearest of THOSE. The odds a sample of 26 holds the actual nearest enemy are SMALL, so every shot picked a different, usually distant enemy AND THE SHOOTER TURNED TO FACE IT. Not a facing bug - a facing instruction handed a new random direction every few seconds.
- (2) FACING WAS A SIDE EFFECT OF SHOOTING. u.aim was written ONLY when a round fired, so: face front, snap to a random enemy, snap back. The snapping BACK was as visible as the snapping out.
- FIX IS ONE THING: know who is nearest, cheaply, all the time. 32 columns across the field, rebuilt once per step; a query reads its own column and WIDENS OUTWARD until a further ring could not beat what it has. Facing follows the ENEMY, not the trigger, on a JITTERED timer so a line does not re-acquire in lockstep. A man shoots at what he is already looking at.
- MEASURED WITH A NEGATIVE CONTROL, AND MY FIRST METRIC WAS WRONG. Heading churn does NOT separate the two - most units are bounding and facing follows movement either way; both read 0 deg median. What separates them is AIM QUALITY:
-   exact-nearest target    28.6% -> 53.8%
-   mean distance ratio      1.19 -> 1.04
-   units holding a target      7 -> 39
- Small samples (single-frame counts) and an INDIRECT metric. It measures the mechanism, and the mechanism is what changed.
- *** THE WAR ADVANCES FROM THE GATE ***
- Everything in the Reach arrives through ks_gate_reach, so a front on Vesskanoth with the gate untouched is an army that SKIPPED NINE WORLDS. The map already told that story and nothing enforced it.
- A world may open a front once the world BEFORE it in REACH_WORLDS is >=40% taken. AN ADVANCE, NOT A WHITELIST - a whitelist needs editing every time the war moves; this needs nothing. "Broken into" not "taken": waiting for full capture means one world at a time forever, and the point is a front that CREEPS.
- A fresh state opens the GATE WORLD ONLY.
- GM OVERRIDE IS DELIBERATE: shift-click Open Front forces it, and forcing is LOGGED so an out-of-order front shows in the digest rather than looking like the rule failed. A rule that cannot be broken from the panel WILL be in the way during a live session.
- *** THE HOUND NOW WEARS ITS FACTION ***
- It matched NEITHER army. Coalition got the flat tint (full luminance recolour) so its TRACKS AND ROAD WHEELS turned blue - a tank painted like a toy. Jade got the art untouched, which is TURQUOISE, while Jade infantry are OLIVE: armour and men in different colours.
- The hull separates like skin did, because the sheet is a 16-COLOUR PALETTE: hull is a saturated teal family, running gear is neutral grey at ~zero saturation. Recolour anything SATURATED, leave anything NEUTRAL. TRACKS STAY TRACKS.
- Orange marker and blue lamp survive BY HUE, deliberately: they are the only pixels whose hue is not the hull's, which makes them the parts that read as EQUIPMENT rather than paint. Repainting them with the hull is how a vehicle stops having any.
- AND JADE COULD NOT REACH THE TINT PATH AT ALL: tinted() returned the raw image when a faction had no FAC_TINT entry, and Jade has none BY DESIGN. That is why the Jade Hound stayed turquoise. The gate is now "does this faction need anything doing to THIS SHEET", not "does it have a tint". An assertion PINNED that bug; corrected with the reason attached.
- reach-check 999 -> 1009, reach-terrain 271 -> 282. Suite 15/15, 1944.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.6.0.0 changes (the Coalition is people, the Circuit is a nation)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE PACK IS A 12-COLOUR INDEXED PALETTE and that is what makes this PRECISE rather than a guess. Skin, faceplate and optics are EXACT RGB values appearing NOWHERE ELSE, so they are remapped BY EQUALITY, not by a hue/sat test that would catch webbing on the wrong frame.
- MEASURED across every troop sheet: skin (238,188,154)+(217,160,102) on assault and engineer ONLY; faceplate (194,134,42)+(143,96,26) on enforcer only. THE ENFORCER HAS NO SKIN ON ANY FRAME - fully helmeted - so the faceplate IS his face, and remapping it IS his red eyes.
- SIX TONES FOR THE COALITION, ONE FOR THE CIRCUIT. Setting, not decoration: the Coalition is a treaty of COLONIES, the Jade Circuit is ONE NATION. "Everyone looks the same" is a STATEMENT ABOUT THE CIRCUIT, not an omission - and it is why its branch returns a CONSTANT rather than a narrower range.
- A tone is FIXED FOR A SOLDIER'S LIFE, off his index. A per-spawn roll would change his face when he takes cover, dies, or is reinforced back on - which reads as A DIFFERENT MAN ARRIVING. Prime-stepped so a fireteam does not walk the list in order and BAND.
- SKIN IS REMAPPED BEFORE THE TINT AND SKIPS IT. The tint is a LUMINANCE RECOLOUR, so running it over a face paints the man BLUE ALONG WITH HIS COAT - exactly why this could not be another FAC_TINT entry.
- Cache key gains the skin variant ONLY WHERE IT CAN CHANGE ANYTHING: keying every sheet would hold SIX IDENTICAL COPIES of the enforcer.
- AUGMENTATION IS THE OTHER HALF: Coalition shield troopers and engineers are augmented, Circuit soldiers are not. One faction's soldiers have something behind the glass; the other's have EYES. Kept HOT not merely red - 2 to 5 pixels on a 20px figure, and a dark red there is indistinguishable from RUST ON THE HELMET.
- *** THE ENGINEER'S BLUE PIXELS ARE NOT AN EYE AND MY FIRST PASS SHIPPED THAT ASSUMPTION ***
- MEASURED: figure spans rows 16-47, FACE at rows 21-23, BLUE at rows 30-32. It is a CHEST DEVICE. Recolouring it gave a man with a red light on his STERNUM - a different sentence.
- He has NO dedicated eye colour to remap: his whole face is SEVEN PIXELS of the two skin values, in a palette that never spent one on an iris.
- SO THE EYE IS PAINTED, per cell, from HIS OWN FACE'S BOUNDING BOX: one row below the top of the head (the top row is brow and hairline; a red pixel there reads as a HAT), forward-most skin pixel. Lands correctly in EVERY frame of EVERY animation with NO per-animation offset table to keep in step. Verified across idle and walk.
- Chest device LEFT ALONE so the one red thing on him is the eye.
- MUSTER STRIP wears the same faces as the field. Left at the default it mustered six identical pale soldiers next to a mixed line - a preview of a DIFFERENT ARMY than the one funding buys.
- CAUGHT IN PASSING: a second `var g2` in the eye block was THE SAME VARIABLE as the pixel loop's green channel, because var hoists to the function. Harmless in that order and exactly the thing that stops being harmless WHEN SOMEONE MOVES A BLOCK. Renamed.
- FIVE reach-check assertions pinned the old signatures. The property they defend is UNCHANGED - a cache key must name everything that varies - and skin is simply a THIRD thing that varies.
- reach-check 993 -> 999, reach-terrain 255 -> 271. Suite 15/15, 1916.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***
