- HELIANTHUS GAMES made the spinning planets: every world on the galaxy map and in system view. (25 Spinning Planets, helianthus-games.itch.io)
- THAT CREDIT MATTERS MORE THAN ITS SIZE SUGGESTS: tools/planet-palette.mjs SAMPLES those sprites to generate every world's ground, sky and rock colour. THE ENTIRE BATTLEFIELD PALETTE IS A DERIVED WORK OF THIS ART. If the terms ever forbid derivatives, planet-palette.js is the derived work and the battlefield colour scheme goes with it. Recorded in ATTRIBUTION.txt where it will be FOUND, not only in a changelog.
- Their pixel fonts (Pixelosopher, Pix Romana) are NOT used here and are NOT claimed.
- WRITING THE CREDIT EXPOSED THREE MORE GAPS: the black hole, the Flesh Station megastructure, the Circuit dyson and quasar bodies, the suns, and the 16px system icons all sit in the SAME directory and are NOT part of that pack. They were HIDDEN BEHIND THE PLANETS - a directory that mostly has one author reads as if it entirely does.
- BACK ON THE UNATTRIBUTED LIST rather than folded into someone else's credit. Attributing a whole directory because most of it has one author is how the WRONG person gets credited and the RIGHT one does not.
- *** A CHECK I WROTE LAST PATCH FIRED AND IT WAS ASSERTING THE WRONG THING ***
- It asserted the unattributed list was EMPTY. So it FAILED THE MOMENT THE LIST CORRECTLY STOPPED BEING EMPTY - it was telling me not to admit something I had just found out.
- EMPTY IS A STATE, NOT A REQUIREMENT. What has to hold is that the list is HONEST: whatever the client admits, docs/CREDITS.md admits too; the panel shows the heading when there is something under it and hides it when there is not.
- Suite 15/15, 1900.
- UNCHANGED: client/assets/space/planets/ is already tracked and has been public for months. Same position as troops/ - gitignoring now would not undo it, so it is not being moved. Decision, not cleanup.

## 1.5.9.0 changes (the assets 404'd, and the bench could not have caught it)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/planet-palette.js (REGENERATED), client/assets/space/nature/meshes.json (6 NEW meshes), tools/planet-palette.mjs, tools/nature-meshes.py, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** WHY NOTHING RENDERED IN GAME ***
- srcFor turns a name into a URL through geom(name); sheet() resolves an animation to its SHEET first. THE SHEET KEY HAD NO GEOM ENTRY. It fell through to the default base, so every brood sheet was requested out of assets/space/troops/, 404'd, and every creature SILENTLY fell back to wireframe.
- THE BENCH COULD NOT HAVE CAUGHT THIS, STRUCTURALLY. It inlines every sheet into FM_TROOP_SRC keyed by filename, and srcFor consults that map BEFORE it touches geom. The one path a self-contained bench can NEVER exercise is the one that turns a name into a URL - EXACTLY the path that broke. That is a limit of the tool, not a misuse of it.
- FIX + A TEST THAT NEEDS NO BROWSER: reach-check resolves every declared animation through the REAL srcFor and checks the file is on disk. NEGATIVE CONTROL: reintroducing the fault fails 3 assertions. Also verified against a real HTTP server - every sheet 200.
- *** EVERY PLANET HAD DARK GROUND AND IT WAS BAKED IN AT GENERATION ***
- planet-palette normalizes every entry to a FIXED target luminance. That is deliberate and it is what keeps hues stable across bodies - but it means ground and horizon carry COLOUR and NO information about BRIGHTNESS. All ten worlds clamped to the same value; hue was the only thing separating them.
- The unnormalized art mean was ALREADY COMPUTED and was going into a COMMENT. It ships as a field now:
-   ice 0.47 | teal rift 0.46 | tan dust 0.37 | ocean 0.33 | red desert 0.22 | tether 0.16
- Hue STILL comes from the normalized entry: a mean drifts toward whichever hemisphere was lit, and the normalized values exist to be stable against that. Value from one, colour from the other.
- VALUE-CRUSHING RETIRED. It existed in 1.5.2.0 so a ONE-PIXEL WIREFRAME stayed legible on the plain. Every unit is an opaque sprite now. THE REASON EXPIRED AND THE CODE OUTLIVED IT. Ground ~doubled; mesh shading widened to match or the crags read as BLACK CUT-OUTS on a lit desert.
- MORE OF THE TERRAIN PACK: dead snags + dry scrub, 6 new meshes, 22-247 faces.
- DEAD AND BARE ONLY, a SETTING decision not a budget one: a pine forest on Ussaleth is a different game; dead trunks on a world the brood has lived on for years is the setting.
- Authored per terrain key: ice gets almost nothing and NO trees (a snag on an ice sheet is a question the setting does not answer); ocean gets scrub and no snags. Takes the world palette like the rocks; one tone term keeps growth a shade UNDER rock, because dead matter is not stone.
- SHARES the cover mesh path rather than copying it: two back-face culls is two places for it to be wrong in a way nobody sees.
- *** BROOD GRADED PER CREATURE INSTEAD OF FLATLY ORANGE ***
- A pure luminance recolour threw away hue ENTIRELY, so a crawler, a hopclops and a grub were ONE COLOUR AT THREE BRIGHTNESSES and a hundred of them read as one substance.
- `keep` lets some of the pack's OWN chroma back through - a yellow eye stays yellower than the carapace around it. That is variation the artist put there and the tint was discarding.
- Tint is PER SHEET: crawlers red-orange, hoppers sour yellow, flies deep red, grubs brown. RANGE STAYS INSIDE AMBER on purpose - they must read as ONE faction against Jade green and Coalition blue at 20px. Variety inside a hue, not a second palette.
- GUNSHIPS BENCHED behind one flag, HELI_SHARE_BENCHED. Model, flight code, brood air arm that hunts them, and the funding curve ALL KEPT and all still correct. Waiting on ART, not a removal; restoring it is a NUMBER not a rewrite.
- JET AIRSTRIKES UNTOUCHED, which is the part that matters: funding must buy something VISIBLE, and jets crossing the field with bombs answer "what did my credits do" more loudly than four loitering gunships did.
- ONE ASSERTION MOVED WITH IT: "armour and air are what money buys" cannot be tested through a share pinned at zero. Asserted through ARMOUR now, with the gunship curve checked BELOW the clamp - so restoring the flag restores a TESTED feature, not an untested one.
- reach-check 986 -> 993, reach-terrain 238 -> 253. Suite 15/15, 1897.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.5.8.0 changes (the wireframe scenery is gone, the bugs are the right size)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/asset-credits.js, client/assets/space/brood/ (6 NEW sheets), client/assets/space/terrain/ATTRIBUTION.txt, docs/CREDITS.md, tools/brood-sprites.py, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** WHY THE FLYERS LOOKED UNREPLACED, AND IT WAS ONE NUMBER ***
- The whole brood pack shared 32 px per world unit. A space fly is 16px tall, so it was HALF A METRE TALL, and the size cutoff then dropped it to WIREFRAME past about a third of the field. Tiny where you could see it, wireframe where you could not - which reads exactly like art that was never wired in.
- The PROJECTILE hit the same wall a patch earlier and I gave it a SPECIAL CASE. THE SPECIAL CASE WAS THE SYMPTOM. A pixel density is a property of a SHEET, not of a pack.
- Sized by what the creature IS vs a 2m rifleman: horror 1.6/3.2m, hopclops 1.5/2.8m, fly 1.4/2.9m, grub 1.0m, egg 2.4m. Large and small SHARE a density so "large" is exactly 2x and means the same thing everywhere. Creatures ~2.7x their old size.
- Cutoff 3px -> 2px: it is a SCREEN size and that is right, but it was tuned against 64px troop cells. A brood cell is 12-48, so it retired a creature at a THIRD of the distance it retired a rifleman.
- WIREFRAME SCENERY REMOVED: camps, works, hive settlements, mound domes. Line drawings on a field where everything else is art do not read as stylisation, they read as a PLACEHOLDER SOMEBODY FORGOT. Removed rather than restyled: there is no art for any of them and inventing some is four more assets to licence.
- THEIR DATA IS UNTOUCHED and the check GUARDS THAT INSTEAD of guarding the drawing. works/mounds/camps/hiveCities still generated, still stepped, AI still reads them - rushers still path to hive settlements, works still bonus the push. ONLY THE DRAWING STOPPED.
- gCamp/gWork/gMound/gHiveCity left DEFINED AND UNREFERENCED on purpose: they are the only record of what those things looked like. One function brings it all back the moment there is art.
- A mound still shows - as its EGG CLUTCH, which is art, and the only part of a spawning mound anyone needed to see.
- *** CAMPS WERE THE ONLY PICTURE OF ZONE CONTROL AND THEY WERE THE WRONG PICTURE ***
- A strip of props flipping as the front passes is a CAPTURE-POINT GAME. It says the fight is about a fixed number of discrete things. The Reach is a world taken over WEEKS by grinding waves.
- CONTROL IS DRAWN ON THE GROUND, which is already a band pass:
-   HELD BAND: everything short of the front tinted toward the holder, fading as it nears the line. "How much of this is ours", CONTINUOUS, moving when the war moves rather than in six steps.
-   PRESSURE PATCHES: one per objective. Colour = who is winning. Intensity = how EVEN it is. Twenty vs twenty is a battle; twenty vs nobody is a CAR PARK and should be quiet even though it is busy. A camp has two states; a fight has a TEMPERATURE.
- No art, no flags, still pure decoration - control arrives over the wire and this draws it.
- MORE OF THE PACK: large grub as 'maw' (rarest and toughest thing on the ground; having both sizes makes the small grub read as its YOUNG rather than the only kind). Small and large egg. FIVE splatter variants instead of one, picked off the unit's own index so a corpse does not change shape while you look at it, big variants to big creatures - a brute should not leave a rifleman's mark. 11 sheets -> 17.
- CREDITS COMPLETE. CodeSpree = the 114 Free Seamless 64px RPG Tiles the ground textures derive from. RGS_Dev = the nature pack (corrected from the shortened form).
- THE UNATTRIBUTED LIST IS EMPTY. The SECTION STAYS IN THE CODE: the next pack without a name needs somewhere to go, and a heading rebuilt from scratch is a heading that gets SKIPPED. Panel hides it while empty; a check fires if it stops being empty unnoticed.
- reach-check 979 -> 986, reach-terrain 230 -> 238. Suite 15/15, 1878.
- *** STILL WIREFRAME AND I HAVE NO ART FOR IT: the Coalition GUNSHIP. Last wireframe unit on the field. It is also WHAT FUNDING VISIBLY BUYS - an unfunded push is infantry, a funded one lands tanks and gunships - so removing it to make a screenshot cleaner deletes a payoff the whole funding model exists to show. Needs a side-on animated gunship/attack-helicopter sprite; nothing in any pack here has one. ***
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.5.7.0 changes (every brood class is a creature, and the credits name everyone but one)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/asset-credits.js, client/assets/space/brood/ (2 NEW sheets + geometry), client/assets/space/terrain/ATTRIBUTION.txt, docs/CREDITS.md, tools/brood-sprites.py, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** THE SPITTER GETS A BODY AND MY EARLIER DECISION WAS WRONG ON THE NUMBERS ***
- The 1.5.5.0 reasoning held ON ITS OWN TERMS: nothing in the pack fires anything, the projectile art is a round with NO shooter, and a creature firing from an idle pose looks worse than a wireframe.
- I APPLIED IT TO A CLASS CARRYING 299 OF ~380 BROOD UNITS. Four in five of the enemy stayed wireframe, so the field read as "the bugs are still wireframes" HOWEVER MANY creature types were wired in behind it.
- A PRINCIPLE RIGHT ABOUT ONE UNIT AND WRONG ABOUT THE OTHER 79% OF THE ARMY IS WRONG.
- AND THE READING WAS AVAILABLE: the hopclops IS a ranged creature - one huge eye, an open mouth, NO strike animation - the shape of something that hits you from over there. Projectile art with no shooter attached is the other half of the same sentence.
- SMALL hopclops = spitter (hops between firing positions). LARGE hopclops = leaper. Same creature, two sizes, two jobs, which is what the pack drew.
- THEY HOP FOR DIFFERENT REASONS and the code says which: leaper's gate is "am I going somewhere", spitter's is "have I just given away where I am". Gating both on MOVEMENT left the spitters - which mostly HOLD position - sitting still, never using the frames the creature is built around.
- BROOD ROUND IS ART, claw is the fallback. The claw existed because a straight segment read as an ENERGY BOLT from a species with no energy weapons; the pack ships an actual organic round.
- QUEUED INTO THE SORTED SPRITE LIST, not stroked: a round passing behind a rock is BEHIND it. The claw never was - a batched stroke has no depth. Claw survives for a sheet that has not decoded, so a slow load is a moment of wireframe, not a brood that stopped shooting.
- IT NEEDED ITS OWN PIXEL DENSITY AND FINDING OUT TOOK A RUN. A round is 8px because it is a small SPRITE, not because it is a quarter-metre object. At the brood's 32 px/unit that is EXACTLY what it became: the size cutoff threw nearly every round away and the wireframe kept quietly drawing them. SIX rendered as art in a 9-second run. Projectiles are 10 px/unit now: 219 in ten seconds.
- CREDITS - EVERY AUTHOR FROM THE LIBRARY IS IN. Two close real gaps:
-   AL_Core (Sci-Fi Animated Army Squad) = the Coalition and Jade INFANTRY.
-   Aralepixel = the Turquoise Hound TANK.
-   Both were on the "no recorded author" list since the art landed.
- Also NickyBHobbying (Merchants and Scoundrels AND Verbattan Shipyards Fleet - the ambient defence fleet was theirs too), Webtential, subotai, Hanker, almostApixel, Free Game Assets, heondu, Poly Haven, AntonXCM (Anti Kvak face).
- ONE ASSET REMAINS UNATTRIBUTED, still LISTED rather than omitted: the 64px seamless tile set the ground textures derive from. No licence, no readme, no author.
- KNOWING WHO MADE SOMETHING IS NOT KNOWING WHAT THEY PERMIT. ATTRIBUTION.txt records Aralepixel and AL_Core and says PLAINLY that their terms are still unread. Redistribution position UNCHANGED; the gitignore stays.
- reach-check 970 -> 979, reach-terrain 221 -> 230. Suite 15/15, 1861.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. ***

## 1.5.6.0 changes (Asset Credits, and the mobs finished)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/asset-credits.js (NEW), client/assets/eod-timer.css, client/index.html, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, docs/CREDITS.md, tools/battle-bench.py, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- CREDITS MOVED AND RENAMED: out of the tab row, into an ASSET CREDITS button beside the end-of-day clock. Styled off the timer's own vocabulary, not the generic .btn - a button in a different visual language inside that wrap reads as something that fell out of the toolbar.
- WHY THERE: the tab row is where you go to DO something; this is a standing statement about the game. It is also the one surface a licence OBLIGES to exist, so it belongs where the next tab cannot crowd it out.
- ONE TABLE, NOT MARKUP. Every credit is a row in asset-credits.js; the panel is generated from it. Adding an asset is one object and CANNOT HALF-LAND - no second place to forget.
- LOADED EAGERLY. No lazy loader, no fetch. A credit that depends on a loader firing is a credit that can SILENTLY NOT BE THERE.
- reach-check asserts every name in the table also appears in docs/CREDITS.md. Hand-maintained lists drift in the direction of the client having FEWER names, which is the direction nobody notices.
- Will Tice marked REQUIRED-BY-LICENCE; RgsDev marked NOT-REQUIRED and credited anyway.
- UNATTRIBUTED ART IS LISTED, NOT OMITTED, with an invitation to come forward. A panel that quietly leaves people out READS AS COMPLETE and a reader cannot tell that from "nobody else contributed".
- Names escaped before reaching innerHTML.
- MOBS FINISHED - EGG CLUTCHES ON SPAWNING MOUNDS. The data model always called them spawning mounds and the wireframe drew a CLOSED DOME, which is a BUILDING. The pack ships an egg, so what a spawning mound looks like is EGGS.
- Seeded off the MOUND'S OWN SEED so a mound is the same mound every time the zone opens. Set AROUND the dome, not on it: a clutch at the foot reads as something the mound PRODUCED; on the crown it reads as decoration.
- THE HATCH ANIMATION EXISTS AND IS DELIBERATELY UNUSED. A mound that hatches on a loop is a mound perpetually about to do something. Kept for the moment a wave actually spawns off a node - which is what it is for.
- A DEAD BROOD UNIT SPLATTERS. No creature has a death animation, and the generic wireframe X reads as a casualty MARKER rather than a thing that just burst. One-shot off deadAt, held on its last frame: the mark stays as long as the corpse counts toward attrition, and a LOOPING splatter is a puddle that keeps exploding.
- *** THE BENCH WAS LYING TO ME, AND THAT IS THE MORE IMPORTANT FINDING ***
- _animSeen is incremented inside queueSprite. The egg clutches push STRAIGHT into _sprites, so they reported as NOT DRAWING across three runs WHILE DRAWING CORRECTLY THE WHOLE TIME. I chased a bug that did not exist.
- A readout that silently UNDER-reports is WORSE THAN NO READOUT: it says "broken" about something that works, and it is trusted precisely because it is the tool you reach for when you cannot tell by eye. Anything bypassing queueSprite counts itself now.
- SAME SHAPE: the bench defaulted to nodes:[] and fobs:[]. A LEGAL state, and also the ONE state where several systems draw nothing - so it quietly tested LESS than it looked like it did. The clutches never appeared once and NOTHING WAS WRONG. Both seeded now; MOUNDS/WORKS are toggles.
- counts() reports mounds, works, objectives and dead, so an empty one is VISIBLE rather than inferred.
- reach-check 958 -> 970, reach-terrain 211 -> 221. Suite 15/15, 1843.
- *** UNCHANGED AND STILL URGENT: nothing shipped since v1.3.9.x, so the mining_bank_delta faucet is very likely OPEN IN PRODUCTION. Read the deployed handler. ***

## 1.5.5.1 changes (the art leaves git, and the game has credits)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: .gitignore, ship.sh, docs/CREDITS.md (NEW), client/index.html, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/battle-bench.html (REGENERATED), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- TWO BLOCKERS, NOT TWO FEATURES. Together they are the difference between a build that can ship and one that cannot.
- THE LICENCE PROBLEM WAS NEVER ABOUT ANY ONE PACK. 3 of 4 art assets forbid redistributing the art; the repo is PUBLIC. "Use it in your game, do not redistribute the source art" is what nearly every commercial art licence says. Pack-by-pack fixes would have kept failing forever.
- ART LEAVES GIT: client/assets/space/vehicles/, client/assets/space/brood/, client/assets/space/terrain/*.png are GITIGNORED. ship.sh pushes them to the VPS over ssh AFTER the git push. Live game has the art; the public repo never does.
- A CLONE WITHOUT THEM STILL RUNS. The wireframe fallback built in 1.5.3.0 as a LOAD-FAILURE path is what makes this workable - written so a slow asset could not blank the field, and it happens to be exactly what a checkout with no art needs.
- CC0 meshes and ATTRIBUTION.txt STAY TRACKED. A settled licence means the art belongs in the repo. Ignoring the whole terrain dir would have taken the licence position out with the art.
- THE GATE RUNS BEFORE THE COMMIT, BECAUSE THERE IS NO UNDO. Deleting art after it is pushed does NOT remove it - it stays in history and stays fetchable. ship.sh refuses to commit if any restricted dir is tracked, checked before `git add -A`.
- VERIFIED BY RUNNING IT: against a repo with tracked art it STOPS with the untrack command in the message; it CLEARS once untracked.
- tar over ssh, NOT rsync: tar and ssh are the only two things this deploy has ever assumed are on the box. A step that works until someone rebuilds the VPS without rsync is a worse step.
- NEVER --delete. Only adds and overwrites. A bug in the local file list should cost a STALE asset, never a WIPED one.
- RESTART IS LAST, after both the pull and the art. Restarting between the two serves a client asking for sheets still in flight.
- CREDITS: the creature pack's licence requires attribution to WILL TICE IN THE CREDITS. That applies to USE, independently of redistribution. FleshMarket CREDITED NOBODY ANYWHERE. The obligation was simply unmet.
- NEW docs/CREDITS.md + a Credits tab. STATIC MARKUP, no loader, no lazy script: it is a legal obligation and the one thing on the page that must not depend on a fetch succeeding.
- THE FILE ADMITS WHAT IT DOES NOT KNOW: troop sprites, tank sheets and the tile pack have NO recorded author and may require attribution too. A credits list that quietly omits people READS AS COMPLETE, which is worse than one that says it is not.
- reach-check asserts every name in the file appears in the client. A credit survives one refactor and quietly does not survive the next; nothing breaks when it goes and NOTHING WOULD EVER TELL YOU.
- THREE OF MY OWN NEW ASSERTIONS FAILED ON MY OWN PROSE: searching for 'rsync' and 'space/nature' found the COMMENTS explaining they are deliberately NOT used. THIRD TIME this exact mistake has landed in this suite. Comments stripped before matching now, and the reason is written where the next person will hit it.
- *** UNRESOLVED - DECISIONS, NOT WORK ***
- (1) IF ANY OF 1.5.3.0-1.5.5.0 WAS ALREADY COMMITTED AND PUSHED, gitignoring now does NOT remove that art from history. Needs git filter-repo or a fresh repo; until then it is STILL FETCHABLE from the public remote. CHECK: git log --stat -- client/assets/space/vehicles
- (2) client/assets/space/troops/ has an UNKNOWN licence and has been public for MONTHS. Deliberately NOT gitignored here: it would not undo the history, would not tell us the licence, and would quietly change the status of an asset shipping since long before any of this. That is a call to make deliberately, not a cleanup to slip into a patch.
- reach-check 937 -> 958. Suite 15/15.

## 1.5.5.0 changes (the brood has bodies, and the bench is in the repo)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/space/brood/ (NEW, 9 sheets + geometry.json), client/assets/space/terrain/ATTRIBUTION.txt, tools/brood-sprites.py (NEW), tools/battle-bench.py (NEW), tools/battle-bench.html (NEW, GENERATED), tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** LICENCE: creature pack forbids public redistribution AND requires credits. THIRD OF FOUR ASSETS. SEE BOTTOM. ***
- ROSTER COMES OFF WHAT EACH CREATURE HAS FRAMES FOR, not off a wishlist:
-   crawling_horror  idle/move/ATTACK  -> melee. small = rush, large = BRUTE (same job, slower, more to chew through)
-   space_fly  attack_start/LOOP/end   -> that is a STRAFING RUN, not a peck. Replaces the flyer wireframe, plays 3 phases IN ORDER. small = flyer, large = WING (rarer, 2x hp, 2x damage)
-   hopclops   jump/fall, NO strike    -> LEAPER. Closes in bounds, hits on arrival. REAL vertical motion: the anim is picked off sign(u.vy), so a cosmetic arc would show the jump frame coming down.
-   grub       idle/move ONLY          -> a BODY. Slow, tough, hurts you by arriving. Honest reading of 2 animations vs inventing an attack it cannot show.
- SPITTER STAYS WIREFRAME. Nothing in the pack FIRES anything (the projectile art is a round with no shooter). A hopclops shooting from an idle pose looks worse than the wireframe.
- AN ANIMATION AND A FILE STOPPED BEING THE SAME THING. The pack ships ONE SHEET PER CREATURE, ONE ANIMATION PER ROW. Keying the image on the animation = 9 creatures become 30-odd re-cropped files. Geometry gained `sheet`; the loader keys the IMAGE on that, everything else still keys on the animation.
- FRAME COUNTS FROM THE PACK'S OWN INDEX, NEVER THE IMAGE. Rows are NOT equal length (crawling_horror attack=19, move=8, sheet as wide as the longest). Dividing width counts trailing blank cells as frames - THE EXACT BUG THAT MADE THE HOUND STROBE, four times worse here.
- THREE MORE OF THAT SHAPE, FOUND AND FIXED: (1) ready() not resolving through imgKey reports every brood anim not-ready FOREVER and queueSprite drops the unit off BOTH paths; (2) a row-pinned anim wrapping downward walks into the NEXT creature's attack; (3) the tint cached per animation holds 4 identical copies of one sheet.
- THE BENCH CAUGHT THE REAL ONE IN ONE GLANCE: the sprite gate asked the COALITION's table about a brood unit. 31 brutes + 38 leapers ALIVE, sheets decoded, and NOT ONE brood animation in the seen list. Looks completely fine on screen - they were drawing, as wireframes, exactly as always.
- MELEE WAS GATED ON THE STRING 'rush' IN SIX PLACES. Three new creatures walked to contact and STOOD THERE. It is a SET now; a new creature needs one edit, not six.
- COLOUR IS A HEX VALUE, NOT AN ART PASS. Pack ships green/blue/violet; brood is amber; same luminance recolour as the Coalition's blue.
- THE COST IS REAL AND STATED, NOT HIDDEN: luminance recolour keeps shading and throws away HUE, so the hopclops loses its yellow eye and the horror its pink carapace. Brood wireframes were already monochrome amber so the field stays coherent. If a creature ever needs two hues, REVISIT rather than special-case.
- FIRST TINT PASS WAS TOO BRIGHT: a warm lift put creatures at the SAME VALUE as the tinted ground, and 100 of them read as scribble on sand. AT FIELD SIZE, VALUE SEPARATION *IS* THE SILHOUETTE. Dark and saturated now, BELOW the plain.
- BENCH: tools/battle-bench.py -> tools/battle-bench.html. It was already the tool answering every "does this look right" since 1.5.2.0 and lived OUTSIDE THE TREE where only its author could run it.
- SELF-CONTAINED: every sheet/patch/mesh inlined as base64, opens over file:// with no server and no asset dir. The failure it must survive is a MISSING ASSET PATH, and a bench that silently falls back to wireframe while you look at a sprite is worse than no bench.
- IT RUNS THE SHIPPED CODE: reach-battle.js and coalition-sprites.js inlined VERBATIM, and it binds the REAL panel markup from client/index.html. A bench with its own renderer tests the bench; a copied panel drifts until a button the renderer writes to is gone.
- CONTROLS: world picker, Coalition entry, Jade share, live sliders for push funding / zone control / intensity, force-line-to-one-class, live per-class counts, ANIMATIONS-SEEN readout (the panel that caught the gate bug).
- *** LICENCE ***
- Creature pack: unTied Games / Will Tice. Commercial + non-commercial OK. ATTRIBUTION IN CREDITS REQUIRED. Public distribution of contents in whole or in part NOT PERMITTED except as part of a game/application product.
- A PUBLIC GITHUB REPO IS NOT A GAME PRODUCT. It is distribution of the contents in part, to anyone, in a form they can take the art out of. Baking/renaming/re-tinting does not change that.
- THAT IS 3 OF 4 ART ASSETS THAT CANNOT LEGALLY SIT IN A PUBLIC REPO. The one that can is CC0. This is NOT bad luck with three packs - "use it in your game, do not redistribute the art" is what nearly every commercial art licence says.
- FIXING IT ASSET BY ASSET WILL KEEP FAILING. ATTRIBUTION.txt records three structural options; the one keeping BOTH the public repo and the art is to gitignore the asset dirs and rsync them separately. The fallback that makes a clone-without-art still run IS ALREADY BUILT.
- SEPARATE AND INDEPENDENT: the CREDITS requirement applies to USE, not just distribution, and FleshMarket currently credits NOBODY ANYWHERE.
- reach-terrain-check 175 -> 211. Four reach-check assertions inverted where they pinned the old roster. Suite 15/15, 1800.
- STILL OPEN: strafing run, leaper bound and brood composition are STRUCTURALLY IN PLACE AND NOT TUNED against a real engagement. Hop cadence, strike timing, how rare a brute should feel - all set by argument rather than by watching. That is what the bench is for.

## 1.5.4.0 changes (cover with a silhouette, hive on the horizon, a line with width)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/space/nature/meshes.json (NEW, GENERATED), client/assets/space/terrain/ATTRIBUTION.txt, tools/nature-meshes.py (NEW), tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** LICENCE: nature pack is CC0 and SETTLED. Terrain patches + Hound STILL BLOCK ship.sh. ***
- THE PRISMS LOOKED LIKE BRICK LOAVES AND THAT WAS INHERENT, not a shading problem. A prism is a footprint pushed straight up: flat top, vertical walls, ONE SILHOUETTE FROM EVERY ANGLE.
- Cover is LOW-POLY MESHES now, flat-shaded per face from the same planet palette the ground uses, so no world needs its own art.
- GENERATOR UNTOUCHED: a feature still has kind, footprint, height, cover slots; the AI still reads all four. Only what is DRAWN there changed.
- FACE COUNT IS THE WHOLE BUDGET (canvas fills one polygon per call): rocks 7-40 = scatter; mountain LODs 103-205 = major cover; 350+ cliffs baked but reserved for distance. Back-face culled in WORLD SPACE before any projection work.
- SIZING MISTAKE 1: horizontal scale off the FOOTPRINT made a boulder 15m across and 4m tall. A slab that dominated the field. The footprint is a GAMEPLAY extent (what cover slots lay along) and was NEVER a silhouette. Height leads; footprint stretches within bounds.
- SIZING MISTAKE 2: the prism side shading range turned a mesh into HOT METAL. A mesh presents faces at every angle, so all the top faces hit maximum at once. Narrower and darker.
- Faces stroked in their OWN fill colour: canvas antialiases polygon edges, so abutting faces leave a HAIRLINE of background along every shared edge and the mesh looks CRACKED.
- SPIRES: six brood mounds well beyond the field. Too far to reach or shoot. They say the hive goes on past the clearing being contested.
- FIRST VERSION DREW PYRAMIDS. A base ring + straight lines to a tip is a CONE, and a cone on a skyline is EGYPT, NOT A HIVE. Now a grown profile sampled up the height: flared foot, pinched waist, leaning tip, per-spire noise phase so six mounds are SIX SHAPES.
- AND THEY WERE INVISIBLE: amber line work at 1/3 alpha against Ussaleth's AMBER SKY. Drawn every frame, could not be seen. A distant mass is a DARK SHAPE AGAINST SKY before it is detail - body filled, ribs over it.
- Deliberately the HIVE'S OWN LANGUAGE, not scenery mountains. A nature-pack peak would have been cheaper and said the wrong thing: that the far country is empty.
- OBJECTIVES: THE LINE HAD DEPTH AND NO WIDTH. The band clamp always decided how far UP the field a man may stand. NOTHING decided where he stood ACROSS it.
- A unit with no cover slot took u.x += u.vx and bounced off the field edges. THAT IS A RANDOM WALK: the army diffused into an even wash flank to flank. Correct depth, NO SHAPE. Reads as a crowd milling in a strip.
- Objectives give CONCENTRATION (gaps are actually empty, the eye finds the fight), CONTEST (a front is places under pressure, not a gradient), PERSISTENCE (somebody has to arrive and push the holders off).
- DERIVED from the terrain the generator already made: a rift world contests chasm mouths, a dust world contests ridges, no per-world data.
- BUCKETED ACROSS THE FRONTAGE, not top-N by size: size alone clustered every objective wherever the wide features landed and the rest of the field went back to empty for a DIFFERENT reason. An EMPTY bucket still gets one - open ground between strongpoints is exactly where an attack goes.
- Cover choice biased toward the objective, weighted BELOW distance. That makes a fireteam bound cover to cover in a DIRECTION instead of digging in behind the first rock it passed. A man does not walk past three rocks to reach the fourth because it is nearer the flag.
- Orders REVIEWED on a long JITTERED timer. Fixed for life = a unit walks to its objective and stays while the front moves past (the other half of the complaint). Unjittered = a line re-tasks in unison and visibly swaps flanks.
- Loads RECOUNTED each step, not bookkept. A bookkept count drifts on every death/reinforcement, and a crowding term off a drifted count PUSHES MEN AWAY FROM GROUND THAT IS ACTUALLY EMPTY.
- LICENCE - NATURE PACK IS SETTLED: CC0 1.0 Universal by RgsDev, stated in the pack's own License.txt. Redistribution + commercial permitted, attribution NOT required. CREDITED ANYWAY.
- Only the derived meshes.json ships. NOT the pack, .blend, FBX or OBJ trees. Trees/flowers/mushrooms/grass deliberately UNUSED: a pine forest on Ussaleth is a different game. Revisit for Coalition/Circuit worlds; the table is in tools/nature-meshes.py.
- TERRAIN PATCHES AND THE HOUND UNCHANGED AND STILL BLOCKING.
- STILL OPEN: the gunship wireframe reads as a tangle of lines at close range. Predates all of this, untouched, and is now the least finished thing on the field.
- reach-terrain-check 149 -> 175. Suite 15/15.

## 1.5.3.1 changes (the Hound, and the colours the other way round)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/space/vehicles/ (NEW, 2 sheets), client/assets/space/terrain/ATTRIBUTION.txt, tools/reach-check.mjs, tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** LICENCE STILL UNRESOLVED, NOW TWO ASSETS. DO NOT ship.sh. SEE BOTTOM. ***
- HOUND replaces the wireframe tank. walk 250x200 4 frames 1 row; fire 400x200 8 cols x 3 rows.
- FIRING DECLARES 20 FRAMES, NOT THE 24 THE GRID HOLDS. Last 4 cells are EMPTY; cols*rows plays 4 blank cels at the end of every shot = the tank VANISHES between rounds.
- Firing is a ONE-SHOT off a new u.fireAt stamp, holding the last frame. As a free-running cycle at troop frame rates it re-fires ~3x/sec and the tank STROBES.
- NO DEATH SHEET. A dead Hound holds its last firing frame rather than naming a missing sheet - sprAnim naming one takes the unit OFF wireframe without putting it ON sprites, and it disappears from the field.
- SHEET GEOMETRY IS NO LONGER GLOBAL. CELL_W/CELL_H/PAD were constants because every sheet was a man (80x64, 16 pad, 1 row, feet at a known spot). A vehicle is none of those. Pitch/grid/anchor/px-per-world-unit are per sheet via FMTroops.geom. The 3 constants stay exported: still exactly right for every troop sheet.
- PX PER WORLD UNIT IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS. Troops 16 (2m man in 32px). Hound 20 (201px nose-to-tail for a ~10m hull). Force one number on both and whichever lost is permanently the wrong size. A tank the size of a man is the more embarrassing failure.
- THE MIRROR MOVED INTO THE SHEET LAYER. Battlefield translated by CELL WIDTH and flipped the context - correct ONLY while every figure is centred in an 80px cell.
- THE HOUND IS CENTRED IN NEITHER CELL AND NOT IN THE SAME PLACE IN THE TWO: tracks at x 64-230 walking, 214-380 firing. Cell-centred mirror SLID THE TANK SIDEWAYS on every turn, BY A DIFFERENT AMOUNT per animation.
- New drawAnchored mirrors about the GROUND CONTACT POINT. Anchors MEASURED off the tracks: 147,159 walk / 297,159 fire, both bottoming row 159.
- FACTION COLOURS INVERTED. Art ships GREEN (troops mean 48,55,46; Hound 43,73,73). That is JADE, whose war this is and whose line is the only one on the ground until the Coalition declares. JADE IS UNTINTED and pays nothing.
- STEEL RETIRED. Written when the Coalition was the default force and Jade the guest in a recolour of its kit. Roles inverted, so tinting the MAJORITY faction every frame to look like the pack with its colour removed is work done to make the art WORSE.
- COALITION IS BLUE, NOT CYAN. Old FAC.coal teal sat a few degrees from the Hound's own turquoise: a Coalition tank and a Jade tank would have been ONE VEHICLE IN TWO SHADES OF ONE HUE at field size. Blue/green/amber survives haze and 20px.
- WIREFRAME FAC MOVED WITH THE TINTS. Infantry are sprites near the camera and wireframe past the cutoff; if the two disagree a unit CHANGES FACTION when it walks far enough away.
- ONE REAL TRAP: tinted() read `fac === 'coal' ||` BEFORE consulting FAC_TINT. Adding a coal tint would have been SILENTLY IGNORED - a hex value that never reaches a pixel, no error, nothing to debug but a tank that stayed green. No faction is hardcoded as the identity now; untinted = absent from the table.
- FOUR ASSERTIONS IN reach-check.mjs WERE PINNING THE OLD MODEL: the steel tint, the coal short-circuit, the cell-width mirror, and a sheet check assuming 80x64/16/one-row for EVERYTHING. All four faithful descriptions of the model this patch inverts.
- THE SHEET CHECK CAME BACK STRONGER: measures every sheet against ITS OWN declared geometry, BOTH WAYS (sheet big enough for the frame count; frame count not past what the grid holds), plus anchor-inside-cell bounds. That is what silently rots when art is replaced.
- One assertion of mine failed on its own PROSE: it searched for the old guard's text anywhere in the file and fired on the comment explaining why the old guard was wrong. Anchored on `if (` now.
- VERIFIED ON A SPRITE BENCH, not by reading: every frame of both sheets in both factions; a FLIP TEST with the anchor drawn as a line that must not move between the pair OR between the two animations; a SCALE AGREEMENT row putting tank and infantry at the same px-per-world-unit from their own densities. Then in the real renderer at close range.
- reach-terrain-check 133 -> 149. Suite 15/15.
- *** LICENCE ***
- Terrain patches: unchanged from 1.5.3.0. UNKNOWN, not permissive. Repo is PUBLIC.
- Hound: arrived NAMED with editable Aseprite sources. MORE PROVENANCE THAN THE TILE PACK HAD AND STILL NOT A LICENCE. Most pixel-art vehicle packs permit use in a game but NOT redistribution of source art, and a public repo IS redistribution. .aseprite/.resprite deliberately NOT shipped.
- ATTRIBUTION.txt now covers BOTH assets.
- ASYMMETRY WORTH KNOWING: deleting the terrain patches degrades cleanly to the 1.5.2.0 wireframe ground. Deleting the Hound leaves tanks on the old wireframe model - it still works, but the sprite line and the wireframe line will not match while the sheets are gone.

## 1.5.3.0 changes (ground under the war)
- NO SERVER CHANGE, NO RESTART. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/assets/space/terrain/ (NEW: 14 patches + ATTRIBUTION.txt), tools/terrain-patches.py (NEW), tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- *** LICENCE UNRESOLVED. DO NOT RUN ship.sh UNTIL SETTLED. SEE BOTTOM. ***
- 1.5.2.0 gave the plain the world's COLOUR. It was still a flat fill with wireframe outlines on it. This gives it a SURFACE and gives cover a BODY.
- GROUND: 14 x 512px GREYSCALE patches, 2 per COLONY_VISUAL terrain key, baked by tools/terrain-patches.py from a 64px seamless tile pack. THE 114-FILE SOURCE PACK DOES NOT SHIP.
- GREYSCALE IS THE POINT, NOT A SIZE TRICK. Runtime 'color' composite takes hue+sat from the fill and luminosity from underneath, so a patch with no hue takes ALL colour from planet-palette.js. One patch set = red desert + teal rift + ice world, no repainting. station and tether baked ahead of any world using them, for the faction war.
- MODE 7, EXACT NOT APPROXIMATE. No camera roll -> R[1]=0 -> a ray's vertical component depends only on screen ROW -> every pixel on a scanline meets the plane at the same depth -> screen-x to world is exactly affine. 250 transformed fillRects/frame vs 600,000 per-pixel samples.
- THE MOMENT ANYTHING ROLLS THE CAMERA THIS IS A SILENT SHEAR. Nothing rolls today. If something ever does this must become quad subdivision; there is no cheap patch.
- TILE_M = 4 METRES, A COMPROMISE, ARGUED IN THE SOURCE so nobody "corrects" it:
-   1.5m  59,640 tiles  honest scale, grain invisible past 30m
-   4m     8,400 tiles  grain survives to mid field, tile = squad frontage  <-- SHIPPED
-   12m      933 tiles  tile bigger than a tank, reads as aerial photography
-   30m      154 tiles  one tile = a city block
- TILE COUNT IS A NON-ISSUE: as a repeating canvas pattern 60,000 tiles cost what 64 do. Repetition costs BANDING, hence 8x8 patch + per-cell flips + low-alpha sprinkle.
- COVER: wireframe prisms -> solid geometry. Split by SURFACE, not by object.
- TOPS are horizontal planes -> same band pass at their own height, quantised into 5 height BUCKETS. 5 passes not 34; a top lands <=1m off, invisible on a surface with its own relief.
- SIDES are vertical, cannot be a plane pass. Back-face culled, shaded by facing, vertical gradient for free AO. They go into the SPRITE QUEUE, the only depth-sorted list here. THAT is what buys back occlusion: what hides a man behind a ridge is the ridge's NEAR FACE, and that face now sorts against him.
- HOLES filled with the SKY colour at depth-scaled opacity, not black. A chasm on Zhaal'un is a teal shadow, on Ussaleth a red one. Black reads as a hole cut out of the world.
- KNOWN AND STATED, NOT DISCOVERED LATER: wireframe units draw in 3 BATCHED depth bands, not painter-sorted (batching is what keeps 400 units at 12 stroke calls). A tank or rusher behind a ridge STILL draws over it. Infantry are the numerous ones, so this is most of the value for none of the rewrite.
- THREE BUGS, ALL FOUND BY LOOKING AT IT. All passed syntax. NONE would have been caught by a code read.
-   (1) SIDES HOTTER THAN TOPS. Shading ran 0.42-1.0 toward the ROCK value; on rock (255,138,76) every wall was brighter than the sunlit surface above it. 34 features of glowing orange cardboard. A vertical face does not catch the sky. Range is 0.10-0.40 now, anchored on GROUND so a shadowed wall is the same material as its plain.
-   (2) BRIGHTNESS WAS A MAGIC 1.9, tuned by eye on one patch on one world. The patches do not share a mean (sand ash vs basalt), so it washed out one world and blacked another. MEASURED by a 1x1 downscale now, plus a SCREEN pass so a patch darker than its world can come UP, not only down.
-   (3) PAL CLEARED, PATS NOT. Nikkathaal opened after Ussaleth = an ice world with grey cliffs standing on RED DESERT, because sides read the palette live and ground+tops held a cached tint. Both cleared in one place; the check COUNTS the clear sites rather than matching one.
- WIREFRAME IS THE FALLBACK, NOT THE LOOK. Missing/failed/absent patches drop straight back to the 1.5.2.0 appearance and take the solid sides with them, so the field is never quads floating on a flat colour. VERIFIED BY RUNNING WITH THE ART REMOVED, not by reading the guard.
- BAND_PX rename: the band height was BAND while draw() declares a local BAND array for unit depth banding. Same name, different type, one scope apart. Asserted.
- window.FM_BUILD published from coalition-sprites.js so the terrain cache bust has no SECOND hand-bumped version literal to rot. Absent -> query dropped, which is worse than a bust and much better than a stale wrong one.
- tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs 98 -> 133 assertions. Suite 15/15.
- *** LICENCE ***
- The tile pack arrived with NO licence file, NO readme, NO author. The licence of these derived patches is UNKNOWN, NOT PERMISSIVE. THIS REPO IS PUBLIC.
- Baking/greyscaling/recombining does NOT change the copyright position. A derived work of an unlicensed asset is still an unlicensed asset.
- client/assets/space/terrain/ATTRIBUTION.txt records the position and the three ways out. The check asserts the word UNRESOLVED is still in that file. That is a REMINDER and explicitly NOT a licence check.
- UNTIL SETTLED: delete client/assets/space/terrain/ before shipping. It costs the look and nothing else.
- NEXT: feature detail passes, and the HEIGHTFIELD, which is still the thing that should land before troop AI.

## 1.5.2.0 changes (the planet under the war, and whose war it is)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Files: server/reach.js, server/server.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/reach-hive.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/planet-palette.js (NEW, GENERATED), client/index.html, tools/planet-palette.mjs (NEW), tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs (NEW), tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- TWO DEFECTS, both CERTAIN not probable (fired on every world every time), both cosmetic+narrative severity. NO MONEY PATH TOUCHED.
- DEFECT 1: the battlefield had no ground and no sky. #04050a container, wireframes on black, IDENTICAL ON EVERY WORLD.
- Under that: terrain colour came from a 7-entry table keyed on terrain SHAPE, authored separately from the planet sprite. The two had DRIFTED ON FIVE OF TEN WORLDS. Sampled off shipped frames, hue degrees out:
-   ks_05 barren_2 TEAL art -> drew VIOLET, 109 deg
-   ks_08 ice            -> drew CYAN,    36 deg
-   ks_02 desert_2 RED   -> drew TAN,     24 deg
-   ks_06 desert_2 RED   -> drew TAN,     24 deg
-   ks_04 lava_2 ORANGE  -> drew GOLD,    21 deg
- REPORTED AS FOUR IN SESSION, IT IS FIVE. ks_08 was missed because ice reads plausible next to cyan BY EYE. The measurement caught it; the eye did not.
- FIX: colour is GENERATED FROM THE ART. tools/planet-palette.mjs decodes PNG via zlib (no deps), samples the LIT DISC across 6 frames per body, emits client/assets/planet-palette.js. Battlefield looks a world up by the SAME COLONY_PLANET folder the galaxy map uses. No second authoring left to drift.
- SHAPE STAYS HAND-AUTHORED. terrain (dust/veins/rift/ice/ocean/station/tether) is a COVER VOCABULARY and a real design decision. Nothing generated touches it.
- VALUE IS CRUSHED ON PURPOSE. A sky at the sprite's own luminance leaves stroked wireframes unreadable. Hue full fidelity, value forced into a legible band. Worlds ARE darker than orbit implies; that is the trade and it is stated in the tool header, not buried in a constant.
- MY OWN FIRST PASS HAD THE SAME CLASS OF BUG: naive luminance scaling CLIPPED channels, which IS a hue shift. barren_4 came out (255,64,66), red pinned. Fixed by pinning at the ceiling and DESATURATING the rest of the way.
- NEW RENDER PASS: sky + ground gradients split at a horizon computed from camera pitch (H/2 + tan(pitch)*focal, where any dy=0 ray lands against the existing basis), horizon line, distance haze OVER terrain and UNDER units.
- reach-hive.js takes the SAME palette, so a world cannot change colour between being surveyed and being fought over.
- DEFECT 2: blankWorld seeded jade:0 and the renderer defaulted jadeFrac=0, so EVERY UNTOUCHED WORLD DREW A FULL COALITION LINE in a war the Coalition has not joined. facOf defaulted to 'coal'. Strike feed, funding strip, works and camp colours were HARDCODED COALITION.
- The Reach war is JADE CIRCUIT'S: their FTL programme made contact, the brood came back down the line at them. The Coalition is a bystander until it declares.
- FIX: coalIn at the STATE ROOT, one switch for the whole Reach. A polity cannot be at war on Ussaleth and at peace on Khai'ru. Per-world jade stays as the commitment dial (how much did they send HERE).
- EVERYTHING READS effJade(s,w), which returns 1 while coalIn is 0 regardless of the stored dial. A dial set before entry cannot leak a Coalition uniform onto the field.
- JADE_MAX 0.75 -> JADE_MIN 0.25. Same constant ("neither faction carries the whole line alone"), roles inverted.
- setJade REFUSES rather than clamps before entry. Clamping would write a dead value and report success: set three worlds to 40%, see nothing change, no way to find out why.
- A CARRIED support vote enters the Coalition. The preset already asked the question and answering yes moved a depth offset and nothing else. CARRIED ONLY, NEVER DEFAULTED: an empty room must not walk a polity into an interstellar war. GM switch authoritative both directions; withdrawing KEEPS stored dials.
- reviveAsCoalition -> reviveAsHome. Its first line already assigned u.fac from jadeFrac, so the name asserted a uniform the body did not give it. That naming is what let the wrong default read as correct.
- god-panel: DECLARE/WITHDRAW COALITION button with confirm both ways; Jade dial min 25 max 100 and DISABLED (not hidden) before entry, with the reason attached; its private REACH_TERRAIN copy demoted to _FALLBACK behind window.REACH_TERRAIN.
- NEW tools/reach-terrain-check.mjs, 98 assertions. effJade is LIFTED FROM SOURCE AND DRIVEN, not text-matched: 9 cases including absent dial, zero dial, garbage dial, null state.
- HUE ASSERTION compares battlefield rock stroke against sampled art IN DEGREES, tol 20. NEGATIVE CONTROL runs the retired table against the same art and requires >=3 worlds to fail; it fails 5. Without the control the assertion measures nothing.
- THREE ASSERTIONS IN reach-check.mjs WERE PINNING THE BUG (jade:0 in blankWorld, the 0.75 ceiling, the raw-dial payload clamp). All three were faithful descriptions of the wrong model. Inverted, with a note, so their previous green is not read as evidence the default was considered.
- planet-palette.mjs --check fails on a stale committed palette; the terrain check shells out to it.
- Suite 15/15, 1582 assertions.
- NOT VERIFIED IN A BROWSER. The horizon maths and the haze band are the parts most likely to read wrong in motion, and no assertion covers "does it look like a planet".

## 1.5.1.4 changes (the mining faucet was never closed)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Files: server/server.js, tools/mining-check.mjs (NEW), client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- DECLARED CLOSED TWICE, OPEN BOTH TIMES, and I reported it closed a third time last session off a code read without running it.
- Fix 1 retired the client-reported TOTAL. Correct. Fix 2 kept the window open for cargo drones, and closed EXACTLY ONE PATH: the window was still deleted on any non-drone settlement, and a client that never sent a loadout never had a window to delete.
- LIVE EXPLOIT: positive mining_bank_delta with no open run took FALLBACK_SEC elapsed and 0 already-banked = a full Ƒ36,000 budget, EVERY MESSAGE. Nothing decremented.
- TRACED: Ƒ36,000/msg -> Ƒ1.08m/sec -> Ƒ64.8m/min -> Ƒ3,888,000,000/HOUR at the 30 msg/sec socket limit. The war layer's whole daily burn on a contested world is Ƒ240m.
- The honest shape leaked too: loadout, settle, then every message after the settlement found no window and took the fallback again.
- FIX: the window is a TOKEN BUCKET and is NEVER closed on a credit. Budget accrues at MINING_MAX_YIELD_PER_SEC from run open; every credit for EVERY reason deducts; only a loadout resets, and it resets to ZERO elapsed hence zero budget, not full.
- No open run still gets ONE grant, seeded a fallback-length in the past (a restart clears the map mid-run, which is real) then leaves it spent.
- AFTER: msg1 36,000, msg2 4, msg3 4. Hour flat out = 500,000 (the per-run ceiling). 20 loadout resets + 600 messages = 8,000. Optimal cycling = 396 Ƒ/sec vs a 400 Ƒ/sec design rate.
- HONEST PLAY UNTOUCHED, traced: 5-minute run claiming Ƒ42,000 pays in full; 4 drone banks + settlement all pay.
- NEW tools/mining-check.mjs, 20 assertions, ALL DRIVING the real handler body lifted by span. Matching its TEXT is what missed this twice: the guard is present and the comment above it is right.
- 5 negative controls, all 5 bite. Restoring the shipped code prints 3,888,000,000 vs ceiling 500,000 in the failure detail.
- ONE BAD ASSERTION: the rate check ran 9 cycles of 21 minutes (3.15h) and divided by 3, reporting a breach that was really 396 vs 400 Ƒ/sec. A rate assertion divides by the clock that advanced.
- WORTH REVISITING: MINING_MAX_YIELD_PER_SEC=400 was justified as 2.5x headroom for a PER MESSAGE clamp. It is a SUSTAINED RATE CEILING now: Ƒ1.44m/hr for a forger vs ~Ƒ576k/hr for a flawless honest run. env-tunable.
- Suite 14/14, 1581 total.

## 1.5.1.3 changes (sudoku server-authoritative, deploy paths)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Files: server/sudoku.js (NEW), server/server.js, client/assets/casino-sudoku.js, tools/sudoku-check.mjs (NEW), tools/mathtest-check.mjs, deploy/update.sh, deploy/setup.sh, deploy/setup_github.sh, deploy/DEPLOY_README.md, deploy/GITHUB_DEPLOY.md, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- SECURITY: browser generated the puzzle, held the solution, graded itself, named the payout. Bounded by a Ƒ4,200 cap, 20s duration floor, localStorage cooldown. Two console calls = Insane reward every 20s.
- CORRECTNESS (this one hid the first): random clue removal, NO uniqueness check, client graded by equality against ITS stored solution. Measured unique-solution rate: Easy 53%, Medium 10%, Hard/Expert/Insane 0% (median 50+ completions). ABOVE EASY A CORRECT SOLVE WAS TOLD IT WAS WRONG. Forging was close to the only way to be paid for Hard.
- IT ALSO INVERTED THE LADDER: a 17-clue board with 50+ completions is EASIER than a 46-clue unique one. Insane paid Ƒ4,000 for the softest puzzle on the menu.
- THEY HAD TO BE FIXED TOGETHER. Validity grading on ambiguous boards turns nobody-wins-Hard into everybody-wins-Insane.
- Removal preserves uniqueness (solution counter, stops at 2). Ladder = what greedy unique removal reaches: 46/39/33/29/26. 25 puzzles per tier, 25/25 hit target, all unique, worst 274ms vs a 250ms budget. Budget overrun leaves MORE clues: easier, never invalid.
- REWARDS UNCHANGED (50/200/750/2500/4000). COOLDOWNS UNCHANGED (30 min) - they just moved to the server.
- HINTS CAPPED AT 4 (where the penalty already floored). Unbounded hints at a floored penalty = free auto-solve for 20% of the prize. HINT_MAX = HINT_STEPS-1, so the last hint still leaves something.
- A REVEALED CELL CANNOT BE WRITTEN OUT OF THE SUBMISSION, or the hint is free.
- ONE ROUND KEY PER TIER. Single 'sudoku' key = one cooldown + one cap for all five: Easy's backstop was the INSANE prize (Ƒ4,200 for a Ƒ50 puzzle). All tiers in SERVER_SETTLED_GAMES; retired 'sudoku' key refuses new rounds like 'mathgame'.
- FLOAT BUG: 1 - used*0.2 gave 0.19999999999999996, so 4 hints on Ƒ4,000 paid Ƒ799 not Ƒ800. Fraction arithmetic now. Found by an assertion expecting the round number.
- DEPLOY: APP_DIR was /opt/fleshmarket in update.sh, setup.sh, setup_github.sh; the server runs from /root/Flesh-Market. backup.sh + pull_backup.sh were already right.
- THE FAILURE MODE IS NOT AN ERROR: rsync CREATES a missing destination, so update.sh built a dead copy at a path nothing serves and THEN pm2-reloaded the real process. Now refuses without client/version.json, refuses to sync a checkout onto itself, takes ownership from the install rather than a baked-in 'fm'.
- NEGATIVE CONTROLS: 11 mutations, 3 WALKED THROUGH, ALL THREE BAD TESTS. (a) 'overwriting a given' altered a given inside the solution, which row/col rules reject anyway - passed with the givens check DELETED; now submits an unrelated valid grid. (b) equality-grading control used a mutation that could never fire. (c) the ambiguous board was hand-built from a swappable rectangle with the band condition BACKWARDS, so the search found nothing and the test ran against a 1-completion board; it strips until the counter says 2 now.
- mathtest-check pinned the whole SERVER_SETTLED_GAMES literal on ONE LINE and failed on a reformat that did not touch the property. Restated whitespace-insensitively, not deleted.
- sudoku-check.mjs: 56 assertions. Suite 13/13, 1561 total.

## 1.4.9.8 changes (war fund)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Files: server/reach.js, server/server.js, client/assets/reach-battle.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/coalition-sprites.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- FUND per world: balance + fundAt. FUND_TAX_PER_DAY fixed; fundBurnPerDay(w) scales on garrison and hive, the SAME TERMS as pushTarget. Quiet world burns 0.
- TAXES CANNOT COVER CONTESTED GROUND BY CONSTRUCTION. Fresh: 288m/day burn vs 96m tax (33%). Hard: 403m. Nearly held: 180m. Coalition holds what it owns; players pay past the border.
- DRAIN IS THE MECHANISM: a dry fund makes a world slip, with a cause a player can name. REPLACES the flat decay constant proposed in design. Same behaviour, visible reason, one fewer invented number.
- SLIP CANNOT EAT A BANKED WAVE: bounded per day, skips zones with a live window and finished zones, writes only z.hive. worldHive floors on cleared. Neglect costs the live wave, never the campaign.
- forcesFor now takes MAX(window ratio, fund cover). Field no longer collapses to a skeleton between pushes, which read as the war stopping.
- cover is replicated onto each zone in the payload: forcesFor takes a zone and the suite lifts it out headless, so its signature is load bearing.
- NOTHING PAYS OUT. reach.js moves no cash; suite asserts it. Same invariant as Chamber treasuries.
- Tick rides the WINDOW SWEEP, not its own timer: PM2 forgets setTimeout, and a fund that stops ticking freezes the war silently.

## 1.4.8.3 changes (stutter fixed; gunship rebuilt)
- HARD REFRESH, no server restart. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- STUTTER = THE FRAME INDEX. sprFrame offset the counter by u.ph, but u.ph is ADVANCED every step (it drives the idle bob + rotor). A 5fps idle cycled at ~27fps, unevenly. Offset is now u.i (array index: fixed for life, unique per unit). Measured 27.4 -> 4.8 fps against intended 5.
- IDLE BOB REMOVED: sin(u.ph)*0.0004 nudged stationary units every frame; sub-cm in world terms but the sprite layer rounds to whole pixels, so it was a 1px twitch.
- SECOND SUSPECT MEASURED AND LEFT: unrounded sprite scale gives ~0.4 size changes/sec from perspective. Quantising trades a rare sub-pixel change for a rarer larger jump. Worse.
- FIRST SUSPECT WAS WRONG: modelled the mirror-flip flapping near an end-on camera. Zero flips. Not the cause.
- GUNSHIP STAYS WIREFRAME: pixiv link is auth-walled and rebuilding a named artist's design is out regardless. Technically a gunship is the WORST case for single-facing art: it reverses at the field edge (u.vx*=-1) so it flies both ways while the camera orbits, and no side-view pack has a banked frame. A still rotor reads as a crash.
- REBUILT: 12 segments -> 50, on the same 3 LOD tiers as infantry. Faceted hull + canopy, recoiling chin turret, stub wings with pods, tapering boom to fin, tail rotor on its own axis, 4-blade main disc with mast/hub, skids on struts. Far tier keeps the old cheap silhouette.
- reach-check.mjs: 543 assertions. Suite 11/11, 1086.

## 1.4.5.5 changes (one command for the suite; it admits what it skipped)
- NO CLIENT OR SERVER CODE CHANGED. No restart, no refresh. Files: package.json (NEW, root, tooling only), tools/run-all.mjs (NEW), tools/mobile-check.mjs, .gitignore, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- `npm run check` runs every tools/*-check.mjs and classifies each PASSED / FAILED / SKIPPED. SKIPPED is loud and first-class: a suite that quietly shrinks still prints green, which is how the swap bug survived with a correct assertion on top of it.
- Sims and audits deliberately excluded (no pass condition to invent).
- jsdom declared as a root devDependency instead of installed by hand. FLOORED AT 30: fleet-check builds a PointerEvent that jsdom 24 does not implement.
- Root package.json is TOOLING ONLY. Server manifest is still server/package.json. ship.sh git-pulls and pm2-restarts; it never runs npm at the root.
- BUG FOUND IMMEDIATELY: mobile-check called process.exit ONLY on failure. pretendToBeVisual keeps a rAF timer alive, so a FAILING run exited and a PASSING run hung forever. Hand runs were piped through tail, which prints the result and hides the hang. Now closes the window and exits both ways: 3s instead of never.
- TWO CHECKS WERE SMALLER THAN THEY LOOKED: with jsdom present lore-check goes 38 -> 48, and mobile-check's 105 were never counted.
- .gitignore: `server/package-lock.json` -> bare `package-lock.json` (any dir). A stale lock in a zip once cost a multi-hour outage + DB restore; the narrow rule stopped covering the case the moment a second package.json existed.
- NOT CHANGED, DELIBERATELY: Zharkofin's title colour stays a GENERAL rule (equipped gifted title outranks dev default), not scoped to the account. No other operator is seeded with a gifted title, so scoping buys nothing today and costs the next GM character a patch.
- Suite: 11/11 green, 959 assertions.

## 1.4.5.4 changes (the script conforms to the sheet)
- HARD REFRESH. No server restart for this patch alone. Files: client/assets/khai-script.js, docs/lore/khaisultull-alphabet.svg (NEW), tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- The v1.4.5.0 script was a RECONSTRUCTION (the original sheet had been generated to a scratch dir that did not survive). It was internally consistent, rendered every canon name, passed every check, and was wrong in almost every particular.
- WRONG: glottal stop was a MARK (it is SIGN ZERO); invented a bare z; missed aa as a mark; all 16 values misassigned.
- RIGHT: the arithmetic. value = 4 x right hooks + left hooks. Confirmed by measuring hook geometry of all 16 signs out of the SVG, not by reading the caption.
- CORRECT TABLE: iss, sath, ruur, thaal, lull, nekk, tekk, kar, vor, mekh, zhaal, khai, hoss, fin, shuur, nguth. Marks: ah, aah, eh, ih, oh, uh, eye.
- The glottal stop draws as a closed ring (zero has no hooks) and STILL occupies a digit position.
- WORKED EXAMPLE VERIFIED: Zharkofin = zh r k f n = A 2 7 D 5 = 665557. Module computes 665557. Asserted.
- OVERFLOW RULE SETTLED: a doubled sign must be PRECEDED BY A VOWEL; "no word opens on one" falls out of that. Ossuveth/Ussaleth are legal. NO WORLD NEEDS RENAMING. Code enforces the preceding unit; test covers the mid-word-after-consonant case neither earlier reading did.
- THE SHEET SHIPS AT docs/lore/khaisultull-alphabet.svg. The suite parses it and compares sign-for-sign to the module so they cannot drift.
- Verified by transposing two values (3 assertions fail), then restoring.
- reach-check.mjs: 416 assertions.

## 1.4.5.3 changes (NPC freight comes back after a galaxy swap)
- HARD REFRESH. No server restart for this patch alone. Files: client/assets/galaxy.js, tools/fleet-check.mjs, tools/mobile-check.mjs, tools/city-shortcut-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- A galaxy swap PERMANENTLY killed the NPC fleet until page reload, in BOTH sectors.
- CAUSE IS SCOPE: gShipList/gServerShips are var-scoped inside the ship IIFE (2144-2972); swapGalaxy (3653) is outside it and assigned them directly, creating two unread globals. The real gServerShips kept every npc id, and spawnServerShip's `if (gServerShips[npc.id]) return;` then refused every reconcile forever.
- The try/catch around it caught nothing: var assignment from an outer scope does not throw, it targets the wrong binding silently.
- Adjacent `if(typeof clearAmbient==='function')` was also false outside the IIFE, so ambient never cleared and every swap leaked animated detached nodes.
- FIX: export _fmFleetReset from inside the IIFE and call that; resync immediately rather than waiting up to 8s.
- ROOT CAUSE WORTH MORE THAN THE FIX: tools/fleet-check.mjs already asserted this and had been FAILING since the clear landed. It needs jsdom (not a project dep), so it crashed with ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND, which reads as a broken tool. 3 of 13 checks were unrunnable.
- All three jsdom checks now print a loud NOT RUN banner naming the fix command instead of crashing.
- The swap assertion was rewritten: it counted ships spawned BEFORE the swap, which the clear removes by design. Now respawns per phase. New behavioural section: same npc id must respawn after a reset and after a swap round trip.
- Verified by reintroducing the bug (3 assertions fail), then restoring.
- TWO reach-check ASSERTIONS WERE PINNING THE DEFECT: they required the literal `gShipList = []; gServerShips = {}` line, i.e. the bug written down as a requirement, so the correct fix FAILED the check. Replaced with the guarantee (clear via the owning module, do not reach into its internals). reach-check 402.
- First-ever run: fleet 62/62, mobile 105/105, city-shortcut 20/20.

## 1.4.5.0 changes (the push window, and their script)
- SERVER RESTART + HARD REFRESH. Files: server/reach.js, server/server.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/khai-script.js (NEW), client/index.html, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- PUSH WINDOWS. GM opens a window on a live engagement; players commit; the sweep resolves it. Load-bearing rule is the FUNDER MINIMUM (4 separate people), enforced by a per-funder cap of F2,500,000. Target scales on garrison and remaining zone control.
- OUTCOMES: carried (line moves 12-24 pts, pool burns), repelled (brood regains ground, pool burns), unanswered (funder minimum never met, FULL REFUND).
- ESCROW CANNOT BE ORPHANED: flipWorld, signAccord and resetReach all harvest open windows and return credits before rewriting the ground. Asserted per function.
- reach.js STILL CANNOT TOUCH A WALLET. One import (the KV store), no safeAddCash/savePlayer/getPlayer. server.js is the only file where credits move.
- GUESTS BLOCKED from funding: a guest is the cheapest way to fake a distinct funder, and the funder minimum is the mechanic.
- FUNDER NAMES WITHHELD while a window is open; count and own total only. Names ship once resolved.
- BUG FOUND, PRE-EXISTING: reach_zone and reach_envoy called reachSetZone/reachSetEnvoy which server.js NEVER IMPORTED. Both threw ReferenceError on every use since those controls shipped. Now imported; suite asserts every called reach function is an imported one.
- KHAI'SULTULL SCRIPT (client/assets/khai-script.js): 16 signs, 7 marks, base sixteen. Hooks right = 4, hooks left = 1. Vowels carry no value. Doubled sign doubles its value, drawn as a struck stem, may never open a line. Renders world names and zone names; title attribute gives the figure.
- OVERFLOW RULE held against a real conflict: four canon names open on a doubled s but open on a bare MARK, so the line is legal. Check enforces on the first cell and still rejects a true violation.
- sh and ng are UNATTESTED extrapolations, stated rather than hidden.
- reach-check.mjs: 371 assertions.

- ONE ASSERTION RETIRED, NOT FIXED: it asserted the standalone strip was removed. True when the banners were merged; deliberately false now.

## 1.4.2.0 changes (the war is in the game)
- SERVER RESTART + HARD REFRESH. Files: client/assets/reach-battle.js (NEW), client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/city.js, client/index.html, server/reach.js, server/city.js, server/server.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- BATTLEFIELD IS NOW A CLIENT MODULE: reach-battle.js, the mockup's renderer ported, lazy loaded behind the galaxy bundle. DECORATION ONLY: zone + world control come over the wire from reach.js; anonymous-wireframe attrition resolves locally (no economic consequence, same rule as ambient patrols). Seeded from colonyId+zoneIdx so all viewers see the same ground. Re-reads live state every 2s. Closes itself if the zone goes quiet.
- ZONES SCALE WITH POPULATION: <15 pop = 1, <25 = 2, >=25 = 3. Capped at 3 (what the surface panel can show). WORLD CONTROL = MEAN OF ITS ZONES, so the front is what the ground adds up to, and clearing every zone is what converts a world to a city. Old saves get zones built; changed population corrects the count.
- PRAWN WORLDS NO LONGER SHOW A MARKET. LIVE MARKET + FACTION CONTROL (four zeroes) + FUND FACTION is not a neutral default: it tells a player there is a control track to buy into and there is not. Reach surfaces show the front, the engagements (watch button on each live one), and the settlement state.
- KS-07 IS AN OCEAN WORLD. Was rendering as a station because I gave it station terrain + static/tech art. Terrain is now 'ocean', a REAL terrain the city renderer draws (open water, swell, shelf line), not ice tinted blue. Lore rewritten: the ministry is under the water.
- DETAIL BANNERS: wide banner with world name + region, replacing the 64px thumbnail floating over the lore. Reach banners use the brood colour; true name only once surveyed.
- THREE BUGS CAUGHT PRE-SHIP, ALL MINE, ALL IN THE PORT:
  1. Reach HUD overwrites whole sections INCLUDING LABELS; standard updaters write into inner divs by id. One Reach visit would leave EVERY Coalition world afterwards headerless with no price list. Sections rebuilt before the standard updaters.
  2. Module bound pointer handlers to its canvas AT PARSE TIME. Safe in the mockup (canvas already in the document), not here (lazy loaded, overlay may not exist). Threw on null canvas and took the module down. Binding deferred to open; a load-safety check boots the module with NO DOM.
  3. setCam called .classList on the mockup harness's 4 camera buttons, none of which exist in the viewer. Every lookup is now present or guarded, asserted by sweeping the source.
- SUITE: city 84/84, lane 54/54, lore 38/38, mathtest 77/77, mobile-136 71/71, reach 232/232, tutorial 22/22, selector 28/28.

## 1.4.1.5 changes (login out of a trial left the client in trial mode)
- SERVER RESTART + HARD REFRESH (fm-auth.js changed). Files: client/assets/fm-auth.js, server/server.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE BUG: boot puts EVERY visitor on a trial and calls setGuestState, which sets window.FM_GUEST.active, renders the guest bar and unhides Claim Account. FM_GUEST is documented IN THAT FILE as the single source of truth every other module reads. THE LOGIN AND REGISTER HANDLERS NEVER TOUCHED IT. Sign into a dev account from a trial session and the bar stays, Claim Account stays, FM_Auth.isGuest() keeps returning true for the whole session.
- The UPGRADE path already solved this: resets FM_GUEST, removes bar + lock screen, reloads, with a comment saying every panel that cached a guest flag on boot is now wrong. Login/register had the same problem and NONE of the handling.
- SECOND HALF: /api/login and /api/register did not return is_guest AT ALL. Code that wanted to check read undefined. Both emits omitted is_guest and guest_locked while whoami included them. Both responses now state is_guest:false explicitly. Login already 401s guests, so a success is by definition permanent - say it on the wire rather than hoping readers coerce undefined correctly.
- FIX: one shared leaveTrial() called by both handlers. Resets FM_GUEST from the response, removes guest bar and lock screen, defaults to non-trial if the server omits the field rather than inheriting old state. Reloads when coming FROM a trial (same reason as upgrade). Direct login with no trial does not reload.
- REPRODUCED then verified by replaying the sequence against the real handler shape: old flow reproduces the bug; new one clears flag, bar, lock screen and isGuest(), including from a LOCKED trial, and falls back safely against a server that omits the field.
- SUITE: city 84/84, lane 54/54, lore 38/38, mathtest 77/77, mobile-136 71/71, reach 153/153, tutorial 22/22, selector 28/28.

## 1.4.1.4 changes (dev flag stripped at boot)
- ORDER MATTERS: deploy, then node server/seed_devaccounts.mjs, THEN restart. Files: server/db.js, server/seed_devaccounts.mjs, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE BUG: seed_devaccounts.mjs sets is_dev/is_admin. syncDevAccounts() ALSO sets them AND RUNS AT BOOT. It blanket-resets is_dev=0,is_admin=0 on every non-owner, then re-flags ONLY names in the DEV_ACCOUNTS env var. Zharkofin is not in that var, so EVERY RESTART silently stripped the flag the seeder had just written. Nothing logged.
- REPRODUCED vs stub DB: seeder writes dev=1 for all three; replaying syncDevAccounts(['MrFlesh','DEV-SMASHER']) writes dev=0 for Zharkofin.
- ALWAYS was the behaviour; would hit ANY future seeded account. Surfaced now because this deployment evidently DOES define DEV_ACCOUNTS. THE STANDING ASSUMPTION THAT seed_devaccounts.mjs IS THE SOLE AUTHORITY ON PRIVILEGE FLAGS IS RETIRED.
- FIX IS A UNION, NOT A PRECEDENCE RULE. Picking a winner just moves the trap to whoever edits the loser next. Seeder records its names in city_kv key seeded_dev_accounts; syncDevAccounts unions that with the env list BEFORE the reset. An account is stripped only if it is in NEITHER.
- Adding a seeded account no longer requires remembering to edit .env on the VPS. Setting DEV_ACCOUNTS no longer silently deletes seeded privileges.
- If the seeder cannot write the KV row it PRINTS THE NAMES and tells you to add them to DEV_ACCOUNTS by hand instead of failing quietly. Boot logs the union.
- VERIFIED end to end vs stub DB with a deliberately stale env list: Zharkofin keeps dev through boot. Union rule asserted for duplicates, case differences, empty list.
- SUITE: city 84/84, lane 54/54, lore 38/38, mathtest 77/77, mobile-136 71/71, reach 144/144, tutorial 22/22, selector 28/28.

## 1.4.1.1 changes (Prawn War dev controls)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Files: server/reach.js (NEW), server/server.js, client/index.html, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/core.js, tools/reach-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- WAR STATE = one JSON blob in city_kv under key 'reach_state'. Not a table: 10 worlds + a few scalars is not a schema, and a table means a live-DB migration for data rewritten wholesale every change. Loader MERGES against REACH_WORLDS so a newly added world cannot come back undefined.
- TWO FRONTS MAX, enforced server-side, REFUSED with a message not silently clamped. Rule not preference: the push window needs separate funders converging on ONE contest. Ten live windows = nobody converges anywhere.
- ANNOUNCE THEN EXECUTE on flip / accord / reset. First call arms, second within 20s commits. Arming is action-specific AND world-specific: an armed Take on KS-03 cannot be discharged by clicking Lose on KS-07. No undo on a live stream.
- TAKING A WORLD REVEALS ITS NAME. Capture is how you learn what they call it. Manual reveal/conceal still available.
- ACCORD IS NOT A WIN BUTTON: closes all fronts, freezes the map as it stands. Held stays held, lost stays lost, nothing handed back, terms on record. Breaking costs 40 peace.
- PAYLOAD SEPARATION: garrison, armed slot and GM log never leave the dev socket.
- NO CASH PATH IN reach.js. No .cash, no savePlayer, no payout. Asserted by reach-check. Capture rewards, when they exist, go through cities/charters which already have provenance checks.
- WIRED: passage open/seal, per-world control, garrison, reveal/conceal, open/close front, take/lose, Khai'sultull voice (live broadcast + news crawl), demands (kind + deadline + accepted/refused/ignored), peace track, accord sign/break, reset, disarm, action log.
- DELIBERATELY ABSENT: wave composition, tempo, blast tuning. No war layer in the build yet. A switch wired to nothing rots untested - exactly how the trial gate sat broken across 120 routes for 4 patches.
- BUG FOUND WHILE BUILDING: called injectHeadline() for the voice. NO SUCH FUNCTION. Real one is pushHeadline(text,tone,symbol,category,meta). It was inside try/catch, so transmissions would have broadcast fine and silently posted NOTHING to the crawl forever, with no error. reach-check now asserts the real function is used and the invented one is absent.
- SUITE: city 84/84, lane 52/52, lore 38/38, mathtest 77/77, mobile-136 71/71, reach 87/87, tutorial 22/22, selector 28/28. jsdom checks (fleet, mobile, city-shortcut) not run here: no jsdom, and they fail identically on pristine.
