## 1.4.0.4 changes (pre-deploy sanity pass; SERVER ONLY)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. NO CLIENT CHANGE. Files touched: server/server.js, tools/guest-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE BUG: trial REST gate was inside requirePlayer. requirePlayer covers 22 of 142 ROUTES. All /api/funds, /api/fund, /api/warehouses, /api/council, /api/patreon resolve tokens inline via tokenFrom, so THE GATE NEVER RAN ON THEM.
- IMPACT: a trial could create/join/deposit into a Capital House, buy a council seat, contribute to a faction treasury, lease a warehouse, link a Patreon email. All create rows that outlive the account, which is the rule the block list exists to enforce.
- HOW IT WAS FOUND: booted the server, POSTed /api/funds/create with a live guest token, got insufficient_funds instead of guest_blocked. FOUR PATCHES OF STATIC PARITY CHECKS PASSED WHILE THIS WAS OPEN. A check that reads the block list cannot tell you the code holding it never executes.
- FIX: gate moved to global app.use middleware, mounted right after express.json() and before any route declaration. Sees every request regardless of that route's auth style. Fails open to next() on throw rather than 500ing the whole API; WS gates and canSendValue hold independently. requirePlayer copy DELETED with a do-not-restore comment.
- ALSO FIXED: GET is no longer blocked. Prefix matching stopped trials BROWSING Capital Houses, the Fbay shelf, warehouse quotes. Rule is "row outlives the account or obligates another player"; a read does neither. Audited all 10 GETs under blocked prefixes: all reads. A future mutating GET must go in GUEST_BLOCKED_EXACT.
- tools/guest-check.mjs asserts the gate is global AND fails if it moves back inside requirePlayer.
- VERIFIED AGAINST A RUNNING SERVER (the point of this patch):
  - REST: 12 blocked (funds/create, fund/deposit, funds/:id/join, warehouses/set, council/seat/buy, council/treasury/contribute, patreon/link, portrait, fleshbook post/reply/edit, items/market/list); 7 open (funds, funds/:id, warehouses, items/market, fleshbook/feed, items/inventory, whoami).
  - WS: transfer, share_buy, council_post -> guest_blocked. Chat unlock message pre-15min. Empty frame dropped without charging cooldown. Normal order reaches its handler.
  - UPGRADE: succeeds; whoami flips permanent; login works after; RETURNED TOKEN BYTE-IDENTICAL to the guest's, confirming nothing migrated. Not repeatable (not_a_guest).
  - UPGRADE FAILURES: name_reserved / name_inappropriate / password_too_short / name_invalid each with own message; session survives 4 consecutive failures; then upgrades cleanly.
  - EXPIRY: backdated guest settles + locks on socket contact; guest_locked reaches client; writes refused; reads OK; upgrade still opens it.
  - FLESHBOOK FILTER on real accounts: clean stored verbatim; n1gg3r and n1gg3rking refused inappropriate; nigger refused empty after censoring.
  - REGRESSION: normal account gets none of the six guest_blocked responses.
- NOTED, NOT CHANGED: guest IP limit is 3 per 24h and is per-IP. Behind carrier CGNAT or a shared campus/office NAT that is 3 trials for everyone on that address. Raise GUEST_IP_LIMIT if trials look suppressed.

- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. NO CLIENT CHANGE. Files touched: server/server.js, tools/guest-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THE GAP: Fleshbook was the last player-authored surface other players read with NO slur filtering. chat + bio ran filterChat, names ran isTextClean, Fleshbook ran neither. Invisible because a post looks the same either way. Open across several patches.
- NEW fbCleanBody(raw, maxLen) -> {ok, body, flagged, error}. Called on ALL THREE write routes: /api/fleshbook/post (1000), /reply (500), /edit (1000, re-sliced per branch so reply edits still cap at 500).
- EDIT IS THE ROUTE THAT MATTERED. post+reply filtered with edit open = publish clean, substitute after. Whole filter beaten by one extra request. ADMINS FILTERED ON EDIT TOO; exempting them puts the hole back behind a privilege check.
- NEEDED BOTH FILTERS. THIS WAS A TEST RESULT, NOT A PREFERENCE. filterChat FLAGS leet but does NOT CENSOR it: leet branch sets flagged, then working.replace(re) runs against the ORIGINAL string which does not match. "n1gg3r" -> flagged:true, clean UNCHANGED. "n1gg3rking" -> not caught at all. filterChat alone would have stored the slur verbatim AND reported filtered:true, worse than no filter because it reports success.
- isTextClean (3 passes: raw substring, leet-normalised substring, containsSlur) catches both. Order in fbCleanBody: trim/slice -> filterChat (censor what it handles) -> asterisk-only check -> isTextClean (reject the rest).
- REJECT not censor for the remainder. A post is a deliberate compose action against a form; author can edit and resend. Chat censors because bouncing a live line mid-conversation is the worse trade. Asterisk-only result refused rather than stored.
- filterChat's LEET HOLE IS NOT FIXED HERE and STILL AFFECTS CHAT AND BIO for every player. Shared filter every message passes through; needs its own patch and its own testing, not a ride-along in a Fleshbook change. OPEN ITEM.
- GM authoring path (isGm) untouched. That copy is written by the GM, not a player.
- tools/guest-check.mjs asserts fbCleanBody is called on every Fleshbook write route. A fourth write path added without the gate restores the hole silently.
- VERIFIED BY EXECUTION against the real function extracted from source. 9 assertions: clean post unchanged; plain slur refused; LEET slur refused via the isTextClean pass specifically; padded leet refused; slur+content refused; whitespace-only refused; no false positive on ordinary text incl. Scunthorpe; 1000 char cap applied before filtering; emoji preserved.

## 1.4.0.1 changes (trial gates tightened; SERVER ONLY)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. NO CLIENT CHANGE, no hard refresh needed. Files touched: server/guest.js, server/server.js, tools/guest-check.mjs, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- THREE OF SIX ASKS WERE ALREADY SHIPPED IN 1.4.0.0, re-verified against live handlers not the changelog: wire = transfer (blocked), Fbay = /api/items/market/* (blocked by prefix), council chambers = council_post (blocked).
- NEW: 90s per message chat cooldown for trials. GUEST_CHAT_COOLDOWN_MS in guest.js. Does NOT replace the 15 min unlock. Unlock stops a throwaway made FOR one message; cooldown stops a cleared account flooding. Global chatAllowed() is 500ms/6-in-3s, a typing guard, never a spam guard.
- WHISPERS SHARE THE SAME COUNTER. Independent timers would halve the real interval for anyone alternating surfaces, and DM spam is worse than channel spam.
- COOLDOWN CHARGED AFTER AN EMPTY TEXT CHECK. Empty text is dropped silently at `if(!rawText)return` far downstream; charging first let a blank frame burn the full 90s. That was a real bug in the first draft of this patch.
- COOLDOWN CHARGED BEFORE MUTE AND SLUR CHECKS, deliberately the opposite call. A rejected message still costs the interval; free retries are what make a filter probe-able.
- NEW: /api/portrait added to GUEST_BLOCKED_PATHS. Identity on a throwaway account is impersonation, and a portrait is the most identity-carrying thing a trial could set.
- NEW: Fleshbook WRITES blocked, READS open. Needed GUEST_BLOCKED_EXACT (new, exact match) rather than a prefix, because /api/fleshbook as a prefix takes the feed with it and reading Fleshbook is most of the trial's value. Blocked: /api/fleshbook/post, /reply, /edit, /pin, /delete. Open: reading, /seen, /vote.
- VOTE LEFT OPEN, JUDGEMENT CALL NOT OVERSIGHT. Anonymous accounts voting is the same brigading vector the chat unlock blunts. Not in the ask, so not in the patch. One line if trial votes start swinging Fleshbook.
- guestBlocksPath() now checks GUEST_BLOCKED_EXACT first, then the prefix list.
- tools/guest-check.mjs EXTENDED: also asserts every GUEST_BLOCKED_EXACT entry resolves to a real app.post/get/delete/put route. Same reason the type check exists: the first draft of the block list was written from memory and was fiction. Now 5 exact paths + 34 types + 25 locked-allow, against 98 message types and 140 REST routes.
- VERIFIED BY EXECUTION, 21 assertions: cooldown at 0s/1s/89s/90s/91s, per-player isolation, countdown rounding, prune cannot evict a live cooldown, portrait + all 5 fleshbook write paths gate, feed/seen/vote do not, fbay list+buy gate, inventory+equip do not, and the three already-blocked items really are blocked.
- STILL OPEN, PRE-EXISTING, NOT TOUCHED: /api/fleshbook/post does not run filterChat, unlike the chat and bio endpoints. Guests cannot reach it now, so this patch does not widen it, but it remains open for every permanent account.

## 1.4.0.0 changes (trial accounts; SERVER + CLIENT)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files touched: server/guest.js (NEW), server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/fm-auth.js, tools/guest-check.mjs (NEW), client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- WHAT IT DOES: first time visitor gets a playable account with no signup form. Runs 7 days from creation, then LOCKS. Never deleted. Upgrading to a permanent account keeps everything.
- LOCK NOT DELETE, AND THIS IS THE LOAD BEARING CALL. 69 tables, ~40 carry a player id, no deletePlayer has ever existed here. A cascade is 40 hand written statements running unattended against prod; one wrong WHERE takes real accounts with no undo but the cron backup. Probability moderate, severity unrecoverable. Same shape as the portrait sweep floor. A dead row is a few KB.
- UPGRADE IS ONE UPDATE ON ONE ROW. token IS player.id (see /api/login), so upgradeGuestSync sets name/hash/salt and clears is_guest. Nothing migrates, nothing half migrates. Carries AND is_guest=1 so a replay cannot rewrite a permanent account.
- UPGRADE NEVER DEAD ENDS. It is the only exit from a locked account. name_taken / name_reserved / name_inappropriate / password_too_short / server_error all return a specific message and re-prompt in place. No path clears the token.
- SCHEMA: players gains is_guest, guest_created_at, guest_locked, guest_settled_at via the existing _migrations list. Additive, no backfill, no table.
- CLOCK IS guest_created_at, NOT last_seen. Sliding on last_seen means a guest never has to convert, which removes the only pressure the feature creates.
- guest_locked IS THE AUTHORITY, NOT THE CLOCK. Set only AFTER settlement returns. If settlement throws the row stays unlocked and the next sweep retries rather than sealing an unsettled account.
- TRANSFERS: flat block inside canSendValue (server.js), the single chokepoint all four routes already pass. Clearance alone was NOT enough: allowance rises with peak net worth, mining_bank is still client reported, and free accounts remove friction from that existing hole.
- BLOCKED FOR GUESTS, by one rule (anything whose row outlives the account or obligates another player): transfer; share_buy/sell/swap; tcg_list_card, tcg_cancel_card_listing, tcg_buy_card; list_title, cancel_title_listing, buy_title_listing; all city_* mutations; council_post; blockade_fund, counter_blockade, private_army, buy_president; auto_accum_*; god_cmd, admin_cmd. REST prefixes: /api/fund, /api/funds, /api/items/market, /api/warehouse, /api/council, /api/patreon.
- ALLOWED: stocks, shorts, commodities, cargo, casino, mining, math exams, solitaire, shipping, smuggling, quests, tutorial, inventory, codec, chat. The trial is the real game.
- FIRST DRAFT OF THE BLOCK LIST WAS WRITTEN FROM MEMORY AND WAS FICTION. It said wire, buy_share, fund_join. The dispatcher uses transfer and share_buy and has no fund_join. Every one would have been a gate that silently gated nothing. Rebuilt from a grep of the dispatcher.
- tools/guest-check.mjs (NEW) asserts all 34 blocked and 25 locked-allow types resolve to live msg.type handlers and that no type is in both sets. 98 handled types total. Run it after any handler rename. Same job lane-check.mjs does for lanes.
- LOCKED STATE IS DENY BY DEFAULT with a 25 entry read allowlist. An unlisted read makes a panel look empty; an unlisted write would let a locked account act. Wrong-direction failure is the safe one.
- NAMES: reserved Drifter-XXXX namespace, refused by /api/register AND /api/rename. Trials cannot squat real names, nothing needs freeing at lock, and picking a name becomes part of the upgrade. Guest rows hold random bytes as a password AND are refused at /api/login by name, so the namespace cannot be probed.
- CREATION DEFERRED TO FIRST REAL INTERACTION (pointer/key/touch/scroll) or 2.5s of visible tab. NOT page load: that mints a permanent row for every crawler, link preview and 3 second bounce. IP limited 3 per 24h in-memory (friction, not security).
- CHAT AND WHISPER OPEN AT 15 MINUTES of session time, measured from row creation. Anonymous free accounts plus open chat is a raid vector; blocking chat outright would gut the trial since chat is the social core.
- SETTLEMENT ONLY TOUCHES WHAT CAN LOSE VALUE UNATTENDED. Cargo, shipping contracts and mining runs are LEFT ALONE deliberately: they resolve on server timers into the account and the money is there at upgrade. Shorts are the reason the pass exists (open short + nobody home = margin call wipes the portfolio the upgrade is promising back). Limit orders cancelled with escrow returned, mirroring cancel_limit exactly. Cash floors at 0.
- SWEEP: hourly interval, once at boot (downtime over an expiry must not hand back a live account), plus lazy settle on socket contact for a guest returning between sweeps. Idempotent, re-reads guest_locked.
- LEADERBOARD EXCLUDES GUESTS (is_guest=0 added to getLeaderboard). A trial that tops the board and then locks leaves a name nobody can play against.
- VERIFIED BY EXECUTION, NOT BY READING. Real SQLite DB. Migration on existing schema; creation; reserved name match; 7 day clock; leaderboard excluded before and included after upgrade; chat gate false at 0 min, true at 16. Settlement run 4 ways: (1) short 20% against a F150 collateral position with a F40 buy order open landed on F5069.70 with F0.30 tax, matching the hand computed figure exactly; (2) catastrophic short floored at 0 rather than going negative; (3) delisted symbol with no price left in place rather than guessed; (4) locked row never re-picked by the sweep.
- CLIENT: fm-auth.js boot no longer opens a login form when there is no token. It shows a boot veil carrying a permanent LOG IN affordance, arms deferred guest creation, then emits fm:authed as normal. Adds window.FM_GUEST state, a bottom status bar (days left + Create account + Log in), a lock screen, and the upgrade modal. Listens on fm_ws_msg for guest_locked and guest_blocked.
- LOG IN STAYS VISIBLE FOR THE WHOLE TRIAL. Not decoration: a returning player whose browser was cleared gets auto-guested and this is their only route back to their real account.
- KNOWN, PRE-EXISTING, NOT FIXED HERE: client/assets/account-auth.js, auth-ui.js and account-persist.js are dead (index.html loads only fm-auth.js), 875 lines of auth that looks live. The WS dispatcher also still lets an unauthenticated socket broadcast chat as "Guest" with no identity beyond the socket.

## 1.3.9.3 changes (cat_ear_headband -> Waow's Band, common -> legendary; SERVER + CLIENT)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files touched: server/db.js, client/assets/inventory.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- WHICH ITEM: cat_ear_headband. TWO existed. cat_ear_beanie (uncommon/35, in crimson_wave set) is UNTOUCHED. Picked the headband on the sprite (it is a band) and because it is standalone, so no set bonus is disturbed. If wrong, same two fields in the beanie entry plus a crimson_wave decision.
- ID UNCHANGED. player_inventory stores item_id, so existing rows become legendary IN PLACE. No migration, no reissue, NO UNDO short of hand-editing the table. Anyone who rolled it as a common owns a legendary now. Alternative considered and rejected: new waows_band id leaving old headbands as commons; the ask said rename.
- name: 'Cat-Ear Headband' -> "Waow's Band" (double-quoted for the apostrophe). rarity: common -> legendary. passive: 15 -> 555.
- PASSIVE HAD TO MOVE. getEquippedPassiveBonus sums item.passive off the catalog; it does NOT derive from rarity. RARITY_CONFIG.legendary.passiveBonus is display metadata, not payout. 555 = existing legendary hat rate (warlord_helm). Verified by parsing both catalogs: name/rarity/passive/slot identical across server and client, and passive matches the benchmark.
- DROP ODDS: rollItemDrop picks rarity by weight then uniform within pool. Before: 550/1000 over 42 commons = 1.3095%/drop. After: 5/1000 over 23 legendaries = 0.0217%/drop. 60x rarer. COLLATERAL: legendary pool 22 -> 23, so every OTHER legendary went 0.0227% -> 0.0217% each.
- APOSTROPHE AUDITED, NOT ASSUMED. First catalog name containing one. Every render site checked: inventory.js 533/557/574/576/764/784/813 and player-profile.js 213/237/410 all put the name in HTML text or a double-quoted attribute. inventory.js:574 onclick passes invId+slot, not name. No escaping needed.
- ENTRY MOVED to the end of the new-pack hat block (ascending rarity). Inert: only consumer of catalog order is Object.values in rollItemDrop, which filters by rarity then picks at random. No store or UI sorts by insertion order.

- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files touched: server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/player-profile.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

Same source (playerSockets), same cadence (60s), different columns. New column started at zero -> dossier 521h 39m, profile a few minutes, same account.

- CLIENT fmtPlaytime REWRITTEN to be output-identical to _frsTime in god-panel.js:705. Verified: 1877940s -> "521h 39m" in both. Old version printed seconds under 60 and dropped minutes over 100h.

ACCEPTED DELIBERATELY: applying the gate to a column with a long history silently redefines every number already in it and puts a discontinuity in a dev-panel analysis stat. Counting idle time is the smaller failure.
- CONSTRAINT UNCHANGED AND NOW WIDER: NOTHING MAY BE GATED ON PLAYTIME. No payout, unlock, title, or prized leaderboard. Applies to play_seconds, which predates 1.3.9.0.

- ORPHAN COLUMN LEFT IN PLACE. Databases that took 1.3.9.0 keep an unread playtime_sec. Additive, near-empty, and dropping a column risks a players table rewrite for zero gain.
- SWEEP CLEAN: grep for playtime_sec / addPlaytimeSeconds / _fmVisible / PLAYTIME_TICK_MS / _playtimeLastRun across server/ and client/ returns nothing.

## 1.3.9.0 changes (player profiles: bio, playtime, for-sale; popup replaced by modal; SERVER + CLIENT)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files touched: server/db.js, server/server.js, client/index.html, client/assets/player-profile.js, client/assets/player-profile.css, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- ONE SURFACE. #playerProfilePopup is now a card inside a new #ppOverlay backdrop, not a cursor-positioned box. Chat name click, chat avatar click and header name click all route to openPlayerProfile. openModPanel still forwards here (unchanged). Legacy x,y args accepted and ignored, so no caller had to change.
- EDIT CONTROL IS IN THE MODAL, NOT THE HEADER. mobile.js paintTitle() mirrors #fm-header-name.textContent into the mobile top bar; a nested button leaks its label into the mobile title. #fm-header-name got an onclick instead -> window.openMyProfile. It carries [onclick] so mobile.js wireDrawerAutoClose already dismisses the drawer on tap, no new code.
- BIO RENDERS THROUGH textContent ONLY. The rest of the panel is innerHTML built from server ITEM_CATALOG strings, which is why that path was safe; player text through it is stored XSS on every viewer. No auto-linking: a clickable stranger-controlled URL is a phishing surface and the server does not vet destinations.
- CAP + FILTER ARE CONTENT POLICY, NOT THE INJECTION DEFENCE. 2000 chars, filterChat on write (same pass as chat), 20s cooldown (admin exempt), isDunced blocked. Admin POST /api/profile/bio/clear wipes the field only.
- NEW COLUMNS via the existing players migration block: bio TEXT, bio_updated_at INTEGER, playtime_sec INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0. Additive, no rewrite, no backfill.
- TWO TIME STATS. Joined = created_at (already present, cannot be manufactured). Playtime = playtime_sec. The ask conflated "track myself" with "flex on account age"; they are different facts and both are shown.
- PLAYTIME IS SERVER-ACCRUED FROM playerSockets, one tick/min, skipping sockets whose ws._fmVisible === false. Client contributes only a {type:'visibility'} frame. Forging it buys exactly what an open tab already buys.
- IDLE TIME COUNTS. DELIBERATE. Session odometer, NOT an engagement score. NOTHING MAY BE GATED ON playtime_sec: no payout, no unlock, no title, no prized leaderboard. Constraint is written above addPlaytimeSeconds AND above the tick, in both places on purpose.
- ELAPSED IS MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED (_playtimeLastRun delta), so a stalled loop under-credits instead of drifting. addPlaytimeSeconds clamps to 3600s so a suspended process cannot dump a day in on resume. Single db.transaction per tick.
- OLD CLIENTS COUNT AS VISIBLE (ws._fmVisible !== false), or the stat freezes at zero for everyone until they hard refresh.
- VISIBILITY FRAME IS HANDLED BEFORE THE RATE LIMITER. It fires on tab switch; rapid alt-tabbing would otherwise spend the player's chat budget.
- FOR SALE IS A SEPARATE QUERY. getInventory already excludes rows with an open item_market listing, so reusing it would have shown a shelf with the for-sale stock missing. New getPlayerItemListings + idx_item_market_seller(seller_id,sold) so profile opens are not a table scan.
- STALE-FETCH GUARD: the profile response is discarded if _ppTarget moved on, or a slow load paints one player's bio under another player's name.
- ESCAPE + BACKDROP CLOSE. The old handler was document-level and closed the profile when the portrait picker overlay was clicked; the listener is now on #ppOverlay itself and Escape peels one layer (picker first).
- ppShowItemDetail NO LONGER FORCES position:fixed on the card. It did that to anchor against a fixed popup; inside a flex overlay it would drop the card out of centering.
- OPEN, NOT FIXED HERE: /api/fleshbook/post does not run filterChat. Chat does, bio now does. Pre-existing gap in a shipped UGC surface, same class, unrelated to this patch. Decide it deliberately.

FM_PORTRAITS.credits keyed by GROUP LABEL; a group with no entry renders NO credit rather than inheriting. Colonist + Synthetic = gatlingart, https://gatlingart.itch.io/.
- SIX FACES: mchallan -> scan99, rahtan -> scan80, xen -> droid38 (codec-data.js); jaquet UNCHANGED on hacker1; Ostrow -> scan10, Vasari -> scan31 (SEAT_META, server.js).
- CLOSED AN UNFILED COLLISION: Rahtan and Vasari were BOTH corpo7. Guild factor and Syndicate proxy wore the same face, one in the codec and one in the chamber. Comment above SEAT_META rewritten and given a cross-check note against codec-data.js.
- FRAMING WAS ONE CONSTANT (1.55x at 37% down) applied to every face ring. Calibrated against art that sits INSET in frame. The scan set runs to the frame edges and the same zoom cropped through the face: Ostrow rendered as hair and goggles, Vasari as a jaw.
- REPLACEMENT: frame:{id:[headWidth, headCentreX, subjectTop]} measured off the PNGs at build time for the SCAN SET ONLY (97 entries). window.FMPortraitFrame(id) returns {scale, ox, oy} as multipliers of the CONTAINER so SVG callers multiply by diameter and CSS callers write percentages. TARGET 0.78, HEADROOM 0.16, scale clamped [1, 3.2]. EVERYTHING ELSE RENDERS 1:1.
- OFFSETS CLAMPED FOR COVERAGE: max(1-scale, min(v, 0)) on both axes, or art drawn hard against the top of frame leaves a bare crescent inside the circle. NOT HYPOTHETICAL: fired on five portraits the first time the test ran.
- EXCLUDED FROM MEASUREMENT, ON EVIDENCE: all 100 droids (a machine bust is head all the way down; the rule reads narrow crowns as small heads, worst case droid16 at 2.79x framed on blank helmet with the robot out of shot) and scan31 by name (narrow hood over a wide beard, measures like a machine, was cutting Vasari's crown). Found by RENDERING all 40 portraits the rule zoomed past 1.25x and looking at them.
- THE ORIGINAL SIXTY AND ALL item:/data: PORTRAITS RENDER 1:1. For the sixty this is an ART CALL, not a revert: they always shipped at 1.55 and only read as too tight once looser heads sat beside them. jarred_brain is 32x32 and 1.55 was removing the glass entirely, leaving brain matter with no jar; Mr. Flesh renders whole now.
- CONSUMERS: council chair discs (SVG), council seat cards (52px), council room avatars, main chat avatars. All read the same helper, nothing to hand-sync.
- CHAT AVATAR was 40px INLINE while the text scaled with --chat-font-scale. Now .chat-avatar-w in style.css, base --fm-avatar:52px, scaled by the same variable. Council room avatars .croom-av base 34px. The img keeps class chat-avatar and its data attributes because _refillChatAvatars() and the profile click handler query it by those. onerror hides the WRAPPER, not just the img.
- TESTS: jsdom picker suite. Tile count, every src resolving to a real file, no duplicate ids, loading=lazy retained, one selection highlight on the right tile, 7 group credits with 2 gatlingart at the correct URL, frame keys equal exactly the measured set, no ring with an uncovered gap, legacy + droid + item ids all return exactly {1,0,0}. Control run against unmodified files fails 11.

### Council chamber redesign (same release)
- Files touched: client/assets/council.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/sound.js.
- PROBLEM: the chamber was ONE ~2500px column (ring, seat cards, composer, treasury, chat, accords, log). Nothing sat next to what it related to; you could not see who held a chair and what was being said at once.
- LAYOUT COPIES THE MAIN CLIENT. style.css .grid is centre + chat rail; the chamber now matches instead of inventing its own arrangement. .cnbar (title + exit) across the top, then .cnmain as grid 2fr / minmax(320px,1fr): .cnstage (centre) and .cnside (right rail).
- .cnstage = .cnstagehead (ring + four chairs, flex:0 0 auto, DOES NOT SCROLL) + .cnfloor#cnfloor (composer, accords, log, own overflow-y). Chairs stay on screen while the floor scrolls, which is the pairing the old single column made impossible.
- .cnside#cnroom mirrors #rightPanel: #ctreasWrap flex:0 0 auto, #croomWrap flex:1 min-height:0, #croomBody max-height:none so the chat FILLS the column instead of the arbitrary 300px it was pinned to.
- FIRST ATTEMPT PUT THE RING IN A FULL-WIDTH BAND. A 900x260 SVG with preserveAspectRatio=meet under a height cap letterboxed into a wide strip with dead gutters either side. Inside the centre column the column width sets the height and the cap (min(300px,32vh)) only catches tall windows.
- FULLSCREEN reuses the cities mechanism exactly: galaxy.js toggles .fmfull on #gCouncilPane and sets body overflow. NOT a second implementation. Unlike cities this covers the tab bar, so the band carries a Leave chamber control which CLICKS [data-gstab="map"] rather than hand-unsetting display.
- NOT-FULLSCREEN IS A REAL STATE, not dead code: #gCouncilPane:not(.fmfull) collapses .cncols to a flowing block, which is the old layout. Nothing assumes a fixed viewport.
- TEXT SCALE: all 82 inline font-size literals in council.js converted to calc(Xrem * var(--cn,1)); --cn = calc(var(--chat-font-scale,1) * 1.15). Driven by the EXISTING A+/A- control; sound.js TARGETS gains 'gCouncilPane'. Set on the PANE not the inner, because the inner is rebuilt by innerHTML and would drop the variable with it.
- MOBILE (<=900px, the SAME breakpoint style.css uses for .grid rather than a second number): .cnmain goes display:block with one scroll, .cnring hidden, #croomBody back to a 340px cap. A 900x260 SVG at phone width is a smear and the cards say the same thing in text.
- SEAT CARDS condensed to name/status/timer; title + note + the title-market signpost move into .cnmore, opened by clicking the card. Open state held in st._openSeats so it SURVIVES a rebuild. The signpost calls event.stopPropagation() so buying does not also toggle the card.
- RING CAPPED at min(200px,26vh). At its natural 262px it took a third of a laptop viewport before any accord was visible.
- FOUND BY TEST: THE ACCORD COMPOSER NEVER RESTORED ITS CARET. 1.3.8.2 gave croomInput text+caret+focus across rebuilds and left cpTitle/cpRider with text only. Same bug class, one element over, opposite direction. captureDraft now records the focused composer field and its selection; restoreDraft puts both back, and ONLY takes focus if the person actually had it, so it cannot steal the cursor out of the room chat below.
- BOTH COLUMN SCROLL POSITIONS preserved across the rebuild (captureScrolls/restoreScrolls). A reader thrown to the top of the accord list because an unrelated chair moved has lost their place the same way a half-typed clause used to be lost.
- window.__councilDebug added as a harness hook (setState/render/state), same precedent as city.js's __cityFrame. Read/write only, no behaviour.
- TESTS: 32 jsdom assertions on the shell, region placement, seat detail toggle, and everything that must survive a rebuild. NO CONTROL RUN for this suite (it drives a hook the old file lacks); the caret assertion did fail against unfixed code during development.

## 1.3.8.2 changes (Council tab was eating typed text; CLIENT ONLY)
- CLIENT ONLY. NO RESTART. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files touched: client/assets/council.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- SYMPTOM: Council tab appeared to refresh every ~10s, clearing anything half typed. NOTHING in the codebase runs on a 10s cadence. Cause was event driven and entirely in council.js.
- ROOT CAUSE: renderRooms() rebuilds croomWrap with innerHTML and THE ROOM CHAT INPUT WAS NEVER CAPTURED. The Accord composer got captureDraft/restoreDraft when it shipped; the chat box directly beneath it got none of it. Same mistake, one element over.
- TRIGGERS (none matching 10s, they just add up): anyone posting in the room; council_dirty on any accord/seat movement; a BLIND 30s tick calling a FULL render whether or not anything changed. Two people in a room = every few seconds.
- FIX 1: room input preserves TEXT + CARET + FOCUS across every render. Restoring text but dropping the cursor to the end is its own insult mid-line. Focus only taken back if they ACTUALLY had it: unconditional focus() steals the caret out of the composer or the main chat.
- FIX 2: an incoming post APPENDS one line instead of rebuilding the pane. Most frequent trigger by a distance; not touching the input at all beats restoring it.
- FIX 3: the 30s tick (which exists ONLY to age relative timestamps) skips entirely while anything in the pane has focus.
- FIX 4: council_dirty DEFERS + COALESCES while typing, so a burst of activity is one reload after you stop, not one per event on top of your sentence.
- Scroll position preserved; follows new messages down ONLY if already at the bottom.
- VERIFIED IN A REAL DOM under jsdom, not by reading: 11 assertions (text, caret, focus, message rendered, send still clears). CONTROL RUN against the unfixed code REPRODUCES the symptom, failing on text/caret/focus, which is what makes the passing run mean anything.

The chamber resolver only understood bare stems, stripped the colon, and built assets/portraits/itemjarred_brain.png. ## 1.3.8.0 changes (The Council Chamber; SERVER + CLIENT)
- SERVER SIDE. RESTART REQUIRED. HARD REFRESH for the Store and Galactic tabs. Files touched: server/db_council.js (NEW), server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/council.js (NEW), client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/market-state.js, client/assets/sound.js, client/index.html, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
- NO .env CHANGE. No new dependency. New tables council_seats, accords, accord_clauses, council_log, council_posts, faction_treasury, treasury_ledger. All additive CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plus four ALTER TABLE columns on accords. Safe against the live database.
- VERSION NOTE: drafted across seven patches numbered 1.4.0.0 to 1.5.0.0, consolidated here as 1.3.8.0 (straight patch increment on 1.3.7.6). THE OLD NUMBERING COLLIDED WITH REAL HISTORY: v1.5.0.0 and v1.5.1.x already exist from 2026-07-24. Nothing compares version strings (apply.sh/ship.sh read it only to name a commit), so renumbering downward breaks nothing.
- WHY: players were ALREADY doing territorial diplomacy in global chat and every part of the Void/President swap was ALREADY executable, because /api/galaxy/fund never required faction membership. What was missing was ENFORCEMENT, not a room.
- THE ACCORD: typed obligation lists the server escrows and executes atomically + a free-text RIDER it stores and NEVER acts on. Composer, ledger and signature confirm all state which is which IN WORDS, not only colour. A player signing a rider they believed was bonded kills the guarantee permanently.
- NO CASH CLAUSE, DELIBERATELY. Without the wire's 2% + 90%-above-10,000 surcharge it is a strictly better wire than the wire with no 12h cooldown (the Fbay hole from 1.2.x wearing a treaty). WITH the surcharge a 500,000,000 political payment costs 450,000,000 in tax. One clause kind: fund_colony, which BURNS. No transfer = no clearance surface = no laundering route.
- applyColonyFunding EXTRACTED VERBATIM from the /api/galaxy/fund route body; the route is now a thin auth wrapper. alreadyDebited=true skips the debit because an Accord escrows hours earlier.
- FOUR CHAIRS. coalition = the EXISTING Presidency (7 DAY term, bought in the Title Market). guild = NEVER purchasable (notary + house; it holds real territory via control_guild, so a Guild outside the room could conquer and not be bargained with). syndicate + void = Legendary titles at 500,000,000 (72h terms), in the SAME Title Market rack.
- THE TITLE IS THE OFFICE: granted on acquisition, auto-equipped, stripped on overthrow, drives chat colour. council_seats stays authoritative FOR ONE REASON: it carries acquired_at and a title has no timestamp, so a protected term cannot live in ownedTitles. buildAvailableTitles re-derives BOTH directions so drift self-corrects.
- CHAIR COLOURS PICKED AGAINST THE CHAT CHAIN, NOT THE FACTION PALETTE. #e74c3c and #9b59b6 are ALREADY the escaped-cyborg and plain-cyborg colours. syndicate #ff2e63, void #c77dff.
- ALLEGIANCE GATE IS LOAD BEARING: you must ALREADY hold the faction. Otherwise the chair is a SECOND ROUTE INTO A FACTION (Void leadership without the permanent conversion, and a bypass of the 30 day switch lock).
- ESCROW FOLLOWS THE PERSON, RIGHT TO SIGN FOLLOWS THE CHAIR. Ousted delegate refunded IN FULL incl. notary; Accords addressed TO the chair STAY OPEN for the incoming holder.
- THREE SURFACES: floor (delegates only, NEVER PRUNED, excluded in the prune query not by convention), gallery (anybody, bounded 200), faction:<id> (members only, a non-member cannot even READ it). Reading is deliberately laxer than writing.
- GM REGENT VOICE: dev/admin/owner addresses the floor as any REGENT HELD chair; room sees the regent's name, portrait, colour and a live typing indicator. COSTUME IS ON THE DISPLAY ONLY: author_id always holds the real account, speaking_as the persona, author_id NEVER sent to clients. A GM cannot speak for a player-held chair.
- SPEAKER IDENTITY RESOLVES LIVE, not frozen on the row. The row records WHAT WAS SAID; who the speaker IS NOW is a property of the speaker. Old posts pick up a later portrait and recolour on faction change.
- TREASURIES. Before this a faction OWNED NOTHING. THE INVARIANT, a property of the code: treasury credits can become control or Accord escrow and can NEVER become personal cash, BY ANY PATH. NO WITHDRAW ENDPOINT. FOUR refund routes go through ONE router reading payer_proposer/payer_counter. A bad leader can WASTE, never STEAL. Voluntary funding, NOT taxed. NOT clearance-gated (clearance guards player-to-player; a contribution reaches no player ever).
- ANNOUNCE THEN EXECUTE. The brake applies to exactly one pair: PAID FROM A TREASURY *AND* FUNDING A FACTION THAT IS NOT THAT TREASURY'S OWN. Self-paid ceding and treasury-funding-your-own-ground both still execute immediately. A 12h WINDOW, NOT A VOTE: quorum in a dozen-player faction dies quietly and needs a UI nobody reaches. THE RIGHT TO PULL FOLLOWS THE CHAIR, so whoever takes the chair off the leader who committed can cancel it. The RECEIVING chair deliberately CANNOT pull it: they agreed, and the window protects a faction from its own leader.
- THE WINDOW COVERS BOTH ROUTES. THE FIRST CUT DID NOT, and this is the important one. It was gated on the ACCORD SHAPE, not the condition, so a leader wanting to hand ground away DID NOT NEED AN ACCORD: /api/council/treasury/fund pointed at a rival landed INSTANTLY, no counterparty, nobody able to pull it. Found by inventorying what a chair actually grants BEFORE shipping, and CONFIRMED against a running server: one call moved lustandia void control 28% -> 32%. Predicate now applied AT THE SOURCE. Constant renamed ACCORD_CEDE_DELAY_MS -> CEDE_DELAY_MS, named for the CONDITION not the Accord, because naming it after the shape is what caused the gap.
- NEW TABLE treasury_pending. A direct spend has no Accord to hang a status on. Credits leave the balance AT COMMIT and are held there exactly as Accord escrow is, so the figure members read is honest about what is already spoken for. Sweep every 2 min + boot pass at 10s. If the committer no longer exists the credits are RETURNED rather than attributed to a guess.
- TWO BUGS IN THAT FIX, BOTH CAUGHT BY THE SUITE: (1) NAME COLLISION. applyColonyFunding ALREADY returns a field called `pending` (the war-fund carry-forward remainder, a NUMBER). Spreading its result beside a boolean `pending` made an INSTANT spend read as held. Flag renamed to `held`. (2) DOUBLE-COUNTED OUTFLOW. Credits are debited ONCE at commit and logged there as spend_pending; logging the execution as another negative made the ledger stop summing to the balance, which is the one property that makes the ledger worth reading. Execution entry is now amount 0, kind spend_executed.
- BUGS FOUND, RECORDED BECAUSE THEY REPEAT: (1) STALE OBJECT OVERWRITE, caught by the escrow assertion and would have shipped. refundProposerAccords saves its OWN row; anything read BEFORE it is stale by exactly the refund and saving it afterwards SILENTLY REVERSES THE CREDIT. Latent in the President path too. (2) savePlayer INSIDE a conditional left ousted Presidents still holding the title across a reload. (3) GLOW ON AN IMAGE IS A BLUR: the portrait filter smeared the face; it belongs on the ring. (4) TWO CONSTRUCTIONS OF ONE WIRE SHAPE: the broadcast hand-built its payload while history used councilPostView.
- VERIFIED: 199 assertions across eleven suites against a live server, plus i18n-check, selector-check, scopecheck, mobile-audit.
- CURRENCY GLYPH FIXED: 23 PRE-EXISTING instances of U+0192 (small f with hook) where U+0191 (capital F with hook) is the credit sign. server/server.js (13), client/assets/galaxy.js (5), client/assets/core.js (5). Warehouse rent + shortfall messages, faction bonusSummary strings, and THE CHINESE TRANSLATIONS, where the English read '+Ƒ500 per income cycle' and the zh string beside it read '+ƒ500'. Ƒbay was ALREADY correct in all 17 uses, which is what confirmed the intended character. Every occurrence inventoried and classified BEFORE the swap; there is no legitimate use of U+0192 in the tree. docs/CHANGELOG.md historical prose LEFT ALONE (4 instances): it records what was written at the time, it is not shipped copy.
- STILL OPEN: regent names and the two chair title names are PLACEHOLDERS, not GM-authored. MINING CASH IS STILL CLIENT-AUTHORITATIVE via mining_bank and it matters MORE now: a fabricated balance can flow into a treasury and out as territory, laundering into world state instead of staying isolated to one account.

## 1.3.7.6 changes (Dev account decommission; SERVER SIDE)
- SERVER SIDE. Requires a .env edit on the VPS and a restart. Files touched: server/seed_devaccounts.mjs, server/.env.example, server/server.js (comment only), docs/README_LOCAL.md, deploy/DEPLOY_README.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt, client/version.json.
- RETIRED: DEV-FIXER, DEV-SLUT, DEV-GURU, DEV-PEAK. KEPT: MrFlesh (owner), DEV-SMASHER. Both kept accounts get new passwords and new salts.
- ROWS ARE KEPT, NOT DELETED. PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON is set in db.js and three references to players(id) have NO ON DELETE CASCADE: fund_proposals.proposer_id, fund_votes.player_id, funds.owner_id. DELETE FROM players would hard-fail for any retired dev who ever filed or voted on a fund proposal and succeed for the others, leaving a partial purge that looks complete. It would also orphan trade history and cascade away chat.
- LOGIN SEALED, NOT ROTATED. The four retired accounts get 64 bytes of RANDOM DATA in password_hash. That is not the hash of any string, so no plaintext can ever satisfy verifyPassword. No plaintext was generated for them at all. Reviving one requires re-issuing a real hash by hand.
- CRITICAL: DEMOTION ALONE WOULD HAVE *PROMOTED* THEM. seed sets patreon_tier=3 on every dev. revokeExpiredPatreon() skips tier 3 entirely (lifetime CEO), so the tier never lapses. syncFundMembership() auto-enrols patreon_tier>=2 into MERCHANTS_GUILD. They are currently held out ONLY by the sweep deleting memberships where (is_dev=1 OR is_admin=1) AND is_prime=0. Clear is_dev without clearing the tier and that sweep stops matching, the patron sweep enrols them next tick, and they GAIN deposit/withdraw/propose on the guild hedge fund. Removing names from DEV_ACCOUNTS and stopping there widens access.
- RETIREMENT CLEARS: is_dev, is_admin, is_prime -> 0. patreon_tier -> 0 with patreon_member_id and patreon_expires_at nulled. faction 'fleshstation' -> NULL (syncDevAccounts sets it on promote, never removes it on demote, and /api/faction/join rejects fleshstation as dev-only). fund_memberships rows for FLSH and MERCHANTS_GUILD deleted.
- ORDER MATTERS: patreon_tier is zeroed BEFORE membership is pruned, or the patron sweep re-adds what the prune removed.
- BOTH HALVES REQUIRED. DEV_ACCOUNTS in .env governs role flags at boot (syncDevAccounts resets is_dev/is_admin for all non-prime, then re-flags listed names). It does NOT touch passwords, tiers or fund membership; the RETIRED_ACCOUNTS block does. Env-only leaves four working logins. Script-only means the next boot is clean but nothing enforces it after.
- NEW AUDIT LINE. Seed script prints every account still carrying is_dev/is_admin/is_prime, and a [WARN] if any is not in DEV_ACCOUNTS. Read it after running.
- IDEMPOTENT. Safe to re-run; converges on the same state.
- VPS .env MUST BE EDITED BY HAND: DEV_ACCOUNTS=MrFlesh,DEV-SMASHER. It is not in the repo, so apply.sh/ship.sh will not change it.

McHallan carried COLD OPEN in quests[]; Rahtan and Jaquet used the legacy rep.quest shape, which the engine WRAPS INTO A ONE ITEM QLIST at call time. Quest pitch wins, tree never plays.

## 1.3.7.4 changes (_godTok is not defined; CLIENT ONLY)
- CLIENT ONLY. No restart. HARD REFRESH REQUIRED. Files touched: client/assets/god-panel.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/version.json.
- REPORTED FROM CONSOLE: `Uncaught ReferenceError: _godTok is not defined` at window.godPatreonHolders and window.godPatreonAudit. EVERY Patreon dev panel button failed on click.
- ROOT CAUSE: the 1.3.7.3 code was APPENDED TO THE END OF THE FILE. god-panel.js is ONE IIFE, line 2 to line 856. `_godTok` is declared at line 803, INSIDE it. The new functions landed at 858+, OUTSIDE it. AN INNER DECLARATION IS NOT VISIBLE TO AN OUTER REFERENCE.
- `node --check` PASSES ON THIS. Scope error, not syntax error; the file parses perfectly. Nothing in the build validates identifier resolution. Only clicking the button or a scope checker catches this class. SECOND TIME THIS PATTERN HAS SHIPPED (see comZ, 1.6.2.1), which is what tools/scopecheck.py exists for AND IT WAS NOT RUN.
- FIX: block moved inside the IIFE where every other god function lives. `window.godX = ...` still exports fine from in there.
- SCOPECHECK THEN FLAGGED galaxy.js: 1.3.7.2 warehouse helpers appended after the last IIFE closed, with renderWarehousePanel() called from inside one at line 2644. FALSE POSITIVE: a top level function declaration hoists to global and an inner reference resolves OUTWARD. Confirmed with a direct repro of both shapes. THE ASYMMETRY IS THE POINT: outer-decl/inner-ref works, inner-decl/outer-ref does not.
- Helpers moved inside the calling IIFE anyway. A checker that reports a known-benign hit every run trains you to skip its output, which is how the REAL one gets missed next time.
- FIRST RELOCATION ATTEMPT WAS WRONG, CHECKER CAUGHT IT: targeted the LAST `})();` instead of the one closing the IIFE containing the call site, putting the helpers in a SIBLING IIFE and genuinely breaking renderWarehousePanel, gToast and renderMarketsTab. Re-run reported 5 cross-scope risks where there had been 1. Corrected to the first top level close after renderMarketsTab. Now clean: 171 changed lines across 3 IIFEs.
- Also removes an em dash that survived into dev panel output.
- TESTED: 117 assertions, 6 suites, 0 failures, clean boot 0 errors. Server behaviour unchanged by this patch; suites confirm no regression.
- PROCESS NOTE: run tools/scopecheck.py on ANY client change that adds functions, and never append to a file without checking whether the tail is inside an IIFE.

## 1.3.7.3 changes (Patreon membership audit; SERVER)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. One column migration (players.patreon_exempt). Hard refresh for the dev panel. Files touched: server/db.js, server/server.js, server/.env.example, client/index.html, client/assets/god-panel.js, client/version.json.
- THE CODEBASE COULD NOT ASK PATREON ANYTHING. Only PATREON_WEBHOOK_SECRET existed, which verifies INBOUND signatures. No access token, no campaign id, so nothing could ever query the member list. Every tier rested on webhooks arriving AND matching, with no way to check.
- WHY THE QUITTER KEPT THEIR TIER: members:pledge:delete resolves by patreon_member_id first, patreon_email second. Webhook never arrives, or arrives with an email Patreon reports differently from the linked one, and NOTHING HAPPENS. revokeExpiredPatreon is the backstop and ALREADY RAN HOURLY, so cadence was never the problem: every members:update pushes patreon_expires_at +40 days, so a missed cancellation sits up to 40 days past the last event.
- THIS ADDS VERIFICATION, NOT FREQUENCY. auditPatreonMemberships pulls the campaign member list from the Patreon v2 API (paginated, capped at 25 pages) and reconciles against every paid-tier holder. Keyed by BOTH member id AND lowercased email, because the webhook matches on either and the audit must resolve the same players. Tier derived through parseTierFromPatreon so audit and webhook cannot drift on the tier lines.
- Outcomes: verified (expiry renewed +40d), adjusted to the entitled tier, or revoked. A tier-3 upgrade found by the audit routes through grantPatreonTier so CEO_MAX still binds.
- THREE STANDING EXEMPTIONS, applied in revokeExpiredPatreon TOO so the hourly sweep and the monthly audit cannot disagree: (1) tier 3, CEO is lifetime; (2) patreon_member_id LIKE 'dev_grant_%', which set_patreon ALREADY writes for GM grants, so custom tiers were self-identifying before this patch and needed no new marker; (3) patreon_exempt, new column, toggled from the dev panel.
- CEO LIFETIME HAS A CONSEQUENCE: CEO_MAX is 10 and a lifetime CEO holds the slot whether or not they keep paying. The ten slots are now a ONE-TIME allocation, not a rolling one. Deliberate, but revisit if they fill with lapsed patrons.
- CIRCUIT BREAKER IS THE IMPORTANT PART. A bad campaign id or a token missing campaigns.members returns a VALID 200 WITH AN EMPTY LIST, which reads as "nobody is subscribed" and would strip every paying account in one pass. The audit now DECIDES in pass one and APPLIES in pass two, and refuses to commit when Patreon reports zero members while accounts hold paid tiers, or when a run would revoke more than PATREON_AUDIT_MAX_REVOKE_FRAC (default 0.5) of non-exempt tiers. Blocked runs return the full plan; explicit force overrides.
- BUG CAUGHT IN TEST: the first breaker condition was `(wipe || force !== true) && (wipe || mass)`, so wipe short-circuited the force check and the override COULD NEVER FIRE. Now `force !== true && (wipe || mass)`.
- DEV PANEL: Preview (dry run), List holders, Commit (confirm-gated), plus exempt/un-exempt by player name. Holders list flags whether the API is configured AT ALL, so a silently inert audit is visible rather than assumed working.
- NEW ENV, documented in .env.example: PATREON_ACCESS_TOKEN (needs campaigns.members and campaigns.members[email] scopes), PATREON_CAMPAIGN_ID, PATREON_AUDIT_MAX_REVOKE_FRAC, PATREON_API_BASE (test/mock override). Without the first two the audit reports not_configured and CHANGES NOTHING.
- TESTED: 117 assertions, 6 suites, 0 failures, clean boot 0 errors. New Patreon suite (22) runs against a MOCK Patreon v2 API on a local port via PATREON_API_BASE: quitter absent from campaign loses tier (the motivating case), former_patron still listed loses tier, active patron keeps tier, downgrade adjusts not revokes, all three exemption classes survive, dry run touches nothing, breaker blocks an empty campaign, force overrides, non-admin 403. Clearance (24+15), mining (8) and warehouse (24+24) suites unchanged.
- CANNOT BE TESTED HERE: the live Patreon endpoint. Container cannot reach patreon.com and there is no real token, so request shape, scopes and pagination are written from the v2 API contract and verified ONLY against a mock. FIRST REAL RUN MUST BE A PREVIEW.

## 1.3.7.2 changes (Warehouses; SERVER)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Schema migration on boot. Hard refresh for the Markets tab. Files touched: server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/version.json.
- STORAGE WAS FREE AND UNBOUNDED. addCargo just incremented. Ship capacity (2500/10000/35000/70000) is checked ONLY in /api/cargo/ship, so it gated per-run movement and NEVER gated buying or holding. Holding was not a decision; cornering a colony's supply cost nothing to sustain.
- WHAT WAS PROPOSED ALREADY EXISTED: player_cargo has been keyed (player_id, commodity_id, colony_id) since a migration off a global table, sell clamps to the passed colony, ship checks origin holdings. Cargo already lived at a colony and already had to be shipped to sell elsewhere. THE GAP WAS THAT STORAGE COST NOTHING.
- CAPACITY IN F, NOT CRATES. Per-unit pricing charged the same to shelve frayed wiring (210) as nano filament (4100) at 20x the value. New `store_unit_val` on player_cargo, blended on merge exactly like avg_cost so a partial sale releases shelf at the blend with no per-lot bookkeeping. Backfilled from avg_cost.
- VALUATION LOCKS AT ENTRY, NEVER MARKED TO MARKET. A crash must not silently free shelf; a spike must not blow a lease set in good faith. Shipments lock theirs AT SHIP TIME off the DESTINATION price (persisted on cargo_shipments.store_unit_val): origin cost would let a player buy cheap on a poor colony and shelve it on a rich one at the cheap valuation (arbitrage on rent), and locking it makes the berth reserved EXACTLY the berth consumed, so in-flight drift can never overflow the destination.
- DAILY, NOT PER TICK. tickCommodityPrices reverts 0.25 toward target per 5min = ~12min half-life, so waiting never beats reversion. Durable holding value comes only from a change in the TARGET (faction control, tension, civic demand), which moves over days. Per-tick rent would tax ordinary buy-ship-sell flow and barely touch hoarding. 0.8%/day = ~5.6% for a week held, vs ~30-60% for calling a control flip (COMMODITY_FACTION_MOD spans 0.82 to 1.30 on med).
- RENT PRICED BY COLONY CONTROL, which previously drove ONLY price. Syndicate 0.70 / contested 0.75 cheap and unbonded; Coalition 1.30 / Guild 1.15 dear and safe; tension adds up to 35%. Cheap-goods colonies now carry a downside and a control flip is something inventory holders must react to.
- RESERVE AT SHIP TIME, NOT DELIVERY. /api/cargo/ship escrows units out of the origin via removeCargo, so a delivery-time capacity check has NO STATE TO FALL BACK ON and a full destination orphans the cargo. Booking up front turns it into a clean pre-launch rejection. Released on delivery AND on interception.
- ARREARS REUSE THE MARGIN CALL MODEL. Unpayable rent rolls into arrears rather than pushing cash negative (nothing here handles a negative balance). Call at 2 days owed, 36h grace. Sweep every 60s for ALL open calls online or not, re-reading live state at the deadline so anyone who paid or sold down is never touched. Settlement: cash first, then stock at that colony's price DEAREST FIRST so the fewest units go, priced through nudgeAndBroadcast so a forced sale eats its own slippage. Overshoot REFUNDED: the player loses price control, not value.
- CONTAINMENT IS THE POINT. A shed in arrears settles from ITS OWN stock at ITS OWN colony. Never reaches cash held for anything else, another colony's shed, equities, or fund stake (same principle as settleMarginCall never touching fund stake). Lease enforcement, not confiscation.
- EXPLOIT 1, THE DODGE: the first design DELETED the shed when settlement found no cash and no stock. A player could let arrears build, sell stock VOLUNTARILY at a price of their choosing, move proceeds to equities before the deadline, and settlement would find nothing and tear up the lease WITH the debt. Free storage on a loop, arbitrage profit kept. Now the lease is cut to its reserved berth, arrears stay on the row, colony closed 30 days.
- EXPLOIT 2, THE UNIT MISMATCH: a patch script hit an assertion and aborted before writing; the set route never got its F conversion, so the slider floor compared a F CAPACITY AGAINST A UNIT COUNT. F366,630 of goods in 1,100 units could sit in a F1,100 lease at 0.3% of the rent owed. THE PRE-EXISTING "cannot shrink below stored" ASSERTION PASSED THE WHOLE TIME because units happened to exceed the value under test. True and useless.
- EXPLOIT 3, THE UNLIFTED LOCKOUT: the settlement comment claimed a player could buy their way out early, but nothing cleared locked_until, so paying cleared the debt and left the 30-day bar standing. Settling in full now lifts it immediately; an expired lockout writes off residual debt.
- DEFAULT IS 30 DAYS, NOT FOREVER. Arrears stay so a player can settle early; if they do not, the sweep writes the debt off when the lockout lapses. It always ends, and it never follows them to another colony or any other asset.
- 7 DAY GRANDFATHER WINDOW anchored in warehouse_meta on first boot: rent AND liquidation both suspended so nobody is called for inventory bought under free storage.
- CLIENT: warehouse panel in the Markets tab (per-shed usage bars, capacity, daily rent, arrears and lockout countdowns, live rent quote as the slider moves, PAY). Buy and ship failures surface the server's written message instead of a raw error code.
- TESTED: 95 assertions, 5 suites, 0 failures, clean boot 0 errors. 1.3.7.0 clearance suites (24+15) and 1.3.7.1 mining suite (8) unchanged. New warehouse suites (24+24): F cap creep via many small buys, per-commodity cap splitting, unit-count floor bypass, shrinking under stored/in-flight value, buying into reserved shelf, negative payments and capacities, leasing for another player, unauthenticated calls, containment vs other colonies and equities, the sell-out dodge, lockout expiry.
- KNOWN GAP: rent is charged on CAPACITY, so a player who over-leases pays for empty shelf. Intended (you pay for the berth) but it means the slider needs active management, and there is no auto-shrink.

## 1.3.7.1 changes (The mining budget was per message; SERVER)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. No schema change beyond 1.3.7.0. Files touched: server/server.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/drone-mining/index.html, client/version.json.
- STARTED AS TUNING, FOUND TWO BUGS. 1.3.7.0 flagged MINING_MAX_YIELD_PER_SEC=5000 as needing a check against real yields. Reading the code to set it properly exposed the cargo drone path.
- CARGO DRONES DO NOT ADD CAPACITY. They FERRY from the hold (`state.cargoValue -= takeVal`). CARGO_DRONE_CAP 300 is value per trip, not storage. So the honest per-run ceiling is a RATE, which is the shape the existing bound already had.
- BUG ONE, DOUBLE CREDIT: on dock the drone ran `state.runBanked += cd.carryingValue` AND sent its own bank_delta for the same value; the end-of-run settlement then sent `delta: state.runBanked`, which still contained it. EVERY DRONE HAUL WAS PAID TWICE. `dockDrone()` is the correct comparison: accumulate only, settle once. Fixed with `state.runFerried` for stats plus a direct `state.bank` bump; ferried value never rides in runBanked.
- BUG TWO, PER MESSAGE CAP: `_miningRuns.delete()` fired on the FIRST positive delta. Cargo drones bank MID RUN, many per run, so every later message found no open run, fell to the MINING_RUN_FALLBACK_SEC branch, and got A FRESH FULL CAP. The ceiling was never 450k per run, it was 450k x however many bank messages the client sent. Unbounded in count. Budget is now cumulative on the run window (`run.banked`) and only reason='banked' closes it. MEASURED: five forged 1,000,000 claims credit 897 total, was ~2.25M.
- THE CEILING WAS 31x THE PHYSICALLY IMPOSSIBLE MAX. Derived, not picked: LASER_MINE_RATE 1.6 vs threshold 100, best drillMul 1.75 = 35.7 frames = 0.60s per ORE UNIT (one unit per completion). Richest mineral musgravite 250. Heat governs: +0.9/frame firing, -0.55/frame cooling = 38% sustained duty. ~160 F/sec AND ONLY IF every rock is musgravite with zero travel and perfect aim, which the flat 30% depleted rate rules out.
- MINING_MAX_YIELD_PER_SEC 5000 -> 400 (2.5x headroom over an unreachable ceiling, never clamps real play). MINING_MAX_RUN_BANK 10,000,000 -> 500,000. Forged orphan claim 450,000 -> 36,000.
- NEAR CAP NOW LOGS. Old signal fired only ABOVE the cap, so a forgery tuned to sit under the ceiling was SILENT. >75% of remaining budget raises `mining_near_cap` alongside `mining_clamped`.
- WHY THIS GOT WORSE AFTER 1.3.7.0: recordNetWorth is called inside the mining handler, so banked mining raises peak_net_worth. FORGED MINING WAS THE CHEAPEST WAY TO MANUFACTURE GUILD CLEARANCE. Clearance made this vector more attractive, not less. Does NOT close client authority over mining; server-derived yield is still the top backlog item.
- CLEARANCE PROMPT REWORDED: the zero-clearance line pointed new players at Capital Houses, which they will not touch for a long time. Now "Trade, gamble, mine, complete tests." on BOTH the client readout (core.js renderClearance) and the server denial (clearanceDenialMsg), so the two cannot drift.
- TESTED: 47 assertions, 3 suites, 0 failures, clean boot with 0 errors. 1.3.7.0 clearance suites unchanged (24 + 15). New mining suite (8): loadout opens the run, five forged ferry claims SHARE one budget, end-of-run claim bounded by what is left, orphan claim capped at the new fallback, honest 900 yield credited in full.
- VERIFIED NON-VACUOUS BY MUTATION: re-run with MINING_MAX_YIELD_PER_SEC=5000 fires four assertions and the orphan claim credits EXACTLY 450,000.

## 1.3.7.0 changes (Guild Clearance; SERVER)
- SERVER RESTART REQUIRED. Schema migration on boot. Files touched: server/db.js, server/server.js, server/tcg/tcg-db.js, client/index.html, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/inventory.js, client/assets/funds.js, client/version.json.
- THE EXPLOIT: `/api/register` is unthrottled (ratelimit.js is WS only, express-rate-limit is not a dependency), `createPlayerSync` hardcodes 1000 cash, and the wire handler had no provenance check. At TAX_RATE 0.02 that is exactly 980 extractable per registration, scriptable in minutes. NOT theoretical.
- REJECTED, IP GATE: CGNAT puts thousands of mobile users on one address, households and offices share one, and a proxy pool costs pennies. High false positive against real players, near zero true block against a scripted attacker. IP is a SIGNAL, not a GATE. nginx already forwards X-Real-IP and X-Forwarded-For (deploy/setup.sh:233-249); nothing recorded it. Logging it is still open work.
- REJECTED, AGE TIMER: registration is free and instant, so a 30 day lock is a DELAY not a COST. Pre-register tonight, harvest on day 31. A delay only defends if somebody is watching the window.
- THE RULE: allowance = peak_net_worth - seed_grant - lifetime_received; remaining = allowance - lifetime_sent. Fresh account allowance is zero FOREVER, so there is no timer to outwait. An earned player never sees the ceiling.
- SUBTRACTING lifetime_received IS THE ANTI CHAIN TERM. Received value raises peak and is subtracted straight back out, so A -> B -> C terminates at B.
- ALL FOUR ROUTES GATED, NOT JUST THE WIRE. Gating one would have been decorative.
  - `transfer` (server.js): checked on the DELIVERED amount, not amount+fee, because the fee is a sink and never reaches another account.
  - `tcg_buy_card` (tcg/tcg-db.js:128): full price to seller, NO fee, NO cooldown, ceiling 1e15. A junk common listed at 980 moved credits at 100% efficiency against the wire's 98%. Ƒbay WAS A BETTER WIRE THAN THE WIRE. New `tcgGetCardListing()` prices the buy before the transaction, because a value movement cannot be un-run.
  - `/api/items/market/buy` (db.js:2904): identical shape, ceiling 999,999,999. New `getMarketListing()` for the same reason.
  - `/api/funds/:id/deposit`: model B withdrawals are capped by FUND CASH, not by the withdrawer's own stake (db.js:1419, deliberate, unchanged), so a deposit reaches a pool an owner or treasurer can drain. NO OWNER EXEMPTION: an alt owning its own house and appointing the main as treasurer is the same route wearing a hat.
- fund_out IS TRACKED BUT NOT SUBTRACTED FROM THE ALLOWANCE. playerNetWorth already includes playerFundStake, so a deposit is not a loss of value. fund_out exists ONLY so a withdrawal can separate return-of-own-capital from a drain of other members' deposits; the excess books as received. Without it a whale round tripping through their own house would burn allowance permanently.
- PEAK RIDES ON THE EXISTING HISTORY WRITE. recordNetWorthFn raises peak_net_worth in the same transaction, so all 11 existing recordNetWorth call sites maintain it untouched. Monotone by the WHERE clause: a drawdown never destroys earned clearance. Live net worth is re-maxed at check time and the raise persisted, covering routes that never write history.
- BACKFILL, ONE TIME, guarded by clearance_meta key `backfill_v1`: peak seeded from MAX(net_worth) in net_worth_history, floored at cash. Nobody established locks out. Past transfers AMNESTIED, no record exists to reconstruct them from.
- value_flow_log IS THE FORENSIC TRAIL THAT DID NOT EXIST. Every player to player movement with both counterparties, kind and note, indexed on from/to/ts. Before this a farm could not be sized, attributed or unwound. Four admin routes: /api/admin/clearance/:name, /flows/recent, /farms, POST /exempt. REPORTING ONLY, nothing acts automatically on a heuristic.
- ROUTE ORDER BUG CAUGHT IN TEST: `/api/admin/clearance/farms` was swallowed by `/:name` because Express matches a single segment. Literal paths now registered FIRST. This is the class of bug that only shows when you actually call the endpoint.
- WIRE COOLDOWN WAS SILENTLY DEAD. `global._lastWire` was a plain in-memory Map: every PM2 restart handed everyone a fresh wire. Moved to `last_wire_at` on the players row, max'd against the in-memory value so a live process does not forget one mid flight. Broken since written, independent of the alt question.
- KNOWN COSTS, ACCEPTED: gifted credits are spendable but NOT forwardable (recipient can trade, gamble, mine, spend at NPC sinks; cannot use Ƒbay or deposit into a house until they earn their own clearance). Card and item sales mint no allowance since proceeds raise peak and received equally, which is also what stops wash trading. Escape hatches: clearance_exempt per player, CLEARANCE_ENABLED=0 globally, CLEARANCE_GRANT to retune.
- STILL OPEN: signup and login IP logging. The gate closes the exploit; the log is what tells you whether it is working.
- TESTED AGAINST A LIVE BOOTED SERVER, 39 assertions, 0 failures. Seed cannot leave a fresh account; earned account wires normally; gifted value cannot be forwarded; all four routes reject and record NO ledger entry when they do; own capital round trip books nothing; drain books excess as received; peak survives a wipeout; cooldown persists; farm report fires on a synthetic three-alt farm; non-admin gets 403.

## 1.3.6.2 changes (The drawer painted under the scrim; CLIENT ONLY)
- CLIENT ONLY. No restart. Hard refresh required. Files touched: client/assets/mobile.js, client/assets/mobile.css, client/mobile-mockup.html, tools/mobile-136-check.mjs.
- REPORTED BY SCREENSHOT: "pops but no button works". Drawer renders, every row in it dimmed to EXACTLY the same degree as the grid behind it, while the close button and menu button are not. That is the tell.
- ROOT CAUSE: THE DRAWER IS z-index 9995 AND THE SCRIM IS 9994 AND THOSE TWO NUMBERS WERE NEVER COMPARED. `#fmTop`, `#fmNav`, `#fmScrim` and `#fmDrawerClose` are created with `body.appendChild` and sit in the ROOT stacking context. The drawer does not: `#fm-header-user` lives at `.wrap > .row:first-child > .row > span`, and `body.fm-mobile .wrap` is `position:fixed`. A FIXED POSITION ELEMENT ALWAYS CREATES A STACKING CONTEXT, with or without a z-index. So 9995 resolved INSIDE .wrap, and what competed with the scrim was .wrap itself at z-index auto, which loses to 9994.
- TRUE BEFORE 1.3.6.1 TOO. Invisible then because the drawer was also display:none and generated no box. Fixing the first bug exposed the second. Three layers on one element: no box, then a box under the scrim, then usable.
- FIX RETIRES THE PREVIOUS FIX: `hoistDrawer()` moves `#fm-header-user` to `body` on enter; `returnDrawer()` restores it to the recorded parent AND next sibling on leave. Same stacking context as the rest of the chrome, so every z-index in mobile.css finally means what it says, and the ancestor display:none becomes irrelevant. The 1.3.6.1 zero height chain is DELETED and the header row is plain `display:none` again.
- MOVING IS NOT WHAT DESIGN RULE 1 FORBIDS. The rule exists so the drawer's listeners, ids and data-i18n attributes survive. `appendChild` relocates the same node and preserves all three; REBUILDING it is what would break them. Verified nothing else depends on the drawer's parent: the only external reference is `document.getElementById('fm-header-user')` in funds.js, an id lookup.
- WHY NEITHER SUITE CAUGHT IT: a stacking context is not a property of any one rule. It is a consequence of .wrap being fixed AND the drawer being nested. No grep over CSS computes it and jsdom does not lay out. The 1.3.6.1 assertions were TRUE AND USELESS: they confirmed the drawer was rendered, which was never the question.
- THE REAL CHECK IS NOW IN THE HARNESS. client/mobile-mockup.html calls `document.elementFromPoint` at the drawer's centre while open and reports `tappable` or `BLOCKED` plus whatever is actually on top. Asking the browser what is on top is the only thing that settles this. It also reports whether the drawer is hoisted. This is the assertion the static suite structurally cannot make, and the pattern to reach for whenever the question is "is it reachable" rather than "is it declared".
- STATIC SUITE COVERS WHAT IT CAN, 71 assertions: hoist exists, wired into enter and leave, records next sibling not just parent, idempotent, observable. Four assertions that the OTHER chrome elements are still body children, since the drawer being the one exception is the whole bug. Two ANCHORs: drawer still nested in the markup, `.wrap` still `position:fixed`.
- VERIFIED NON-VACUOUS: un-wiring the hoist fires two assertions; also changing .wrap off position:fixed fires the anchor.

## 1.3.6.1 changes (The drawer never rendered; CLIENT ONLY)
- CLIENT ONLY. No restart. Hard refresh required. Files touched: client/assets/mobile.css, client/mobile-mockup.html, tools/mobile-136-check.mjs.
- REPORTED BY SCREENSHOT: menu opens, scrim dims, close button appears, NO MENU. 1.3.6 made the state escapable. It did not make the drawer exist.
- ROOT CAUSE: `#fm-header-user` sits at `.wrap > .row:first-child > .row > span`, and `body.fm-mobile .wrap > .row:first-child` was `display:none !important`. A `position:fixed` descendant of a `display:none` ancestor GENERATES NO BOX. display:none removes the whole subtree from the box tree; nothing a descendant declares brings it back, not position:fixed, not !important, not z-index 9995.
- SO EVERY DRAWER RULE IN mobile.css WAS DECORATING BOXES THAT WERE NEVER GENERATED: the 238px width, the translateX(100%) to translateX(0) slide, the border-left, overflow-y:auto, and the whole `> *` child block. Since 1.3.0.
- THIS IS THE FIRST CAUSE OF THE REPORTED FREEZE. The four gaps closed in 1.3.6 (untappable scrim on iOS, open-only button, button buried under the scrim, no close control) are what made the state UNRECOVERABLE. This is what made it EMPTY. Both were needed to produce "tap the menu and the game is gone". Neither alone explains the report.
- FIX: keep the CHAIN in the box tree at zero height (`display:block; height:0; margin/padding/border 0; overflow:visible` on the row AND the nested row), hide the row's other contents instead via `> div:not(.row)`. `#name` and `#helloBtn` are already inline display:none, so the wordmark and EOD block is the only sibling group needing it.
- `mirror()` reads `#eod-timer.textContent`, which display:none does not affect, so the top bar clock is unchanged by either the old rule or the new one. Verified rather than assumed.
- CONTAINING BLOCK VERIFIED: nothing on the chain declares transform, filter or contain, so the drawer's containing block is the viewport. `.wrap` having `overflow:hidden` does not clip it, because overflow only clips descendants whose containing block is inside the scroller.
- `#fm-header-user` now carries `display:flex !important` on mobile. index.html ships it inline `display:none`; funds.js sets flex on the auth event. On mobile it IS the drawer and must exist regardless, or a guest gets an empty menu, which is the bug this release exists to fix.
- WHY THE 1.3.6 SUITE MISSED IT: it asserted the drawer rules were present and correctly shaped. They were. It never asserted the element those rules target can RENDER, because that is a computed style question and the suite is static. The gap was real and the check was passing honestly.
- SIX NEW ASSERTIONS, 61 total: two ANCHORs on the DOM position (drawer still nested inside the first header row, still inside a nested .row rather than a direct child), a guard that no rule sets display:none on a selector ending at `.row:first-child`, three on the shape of the replacement, and one that no rule hides the drawer itself.
- NEGATIVE ASSERTIONS NOW RUN AGAINST A COMMENT-STRIPPED COPY OF mobile.css. Same trap mathtest-check hit: the comments explaining this bug quote the exact patterns the guards grep for, and the first draft failed two of its own checks.
- VERIFIED NON-VACUOUS: restoring the exact 1.3.6 rule fires five of the six.
- client/mobile-mockup.html now ships `#fm-header-user` as inline `display:none`, matching production, so the harness exercises the override. THE HARNESS REPRODUCED THIS DEFECT FAITHFULLY AND WOULD HAVE SHOWN IT ON FIRST OPEN. It was not opened before 1.3.6 shipped. That is the process lesson, not the CSS.

## 1.3.6 changes (Mobile: drawer lockup, touch incompatible systems, casino widths; CLIENT ONLY)
- CLIENT ONLY. No restart. Hard refresh required. Files touched: client/assets/mobile.js, client/assets/mobile.css. Files added: client/mobile-mockup.html, tools/mobile-136-check.mjs.
- REPORTED SYMPTOM: the top right lined menu freezes the game on mobile. FOUR independent gaps, all pointing the same way.
- GAP 1, THE LIKELY PRIMARY ON iOS: `#fmScrim` was a bare div with a `click` listener. Safari only synthesizes a click on a non-interactive element when it has decided the element is clickable, and one signal is `cursor:pointer`. The `pointer:coarse` block at mobile.css:17 sets `cursor:auto !important` on EVERY element on the page, stripping that signal from the scrim. Tap goes nowhere.
- GAP 2: `.fmt-menu` called `drawer(true)` unconditionally. It never toggled, so a second tap could not have closed it even if reachable.
- GAP 3: scrim is z-index 9994, `#fmTop` is 9992. The menu button was UNDERNEATH the thing it opened.
- GAP 4: the drawer had no close control of any kind. With gaps 1 through 3, reload was the only exit.
- FIXES: scrim binds `pointerdown` + `touchstart` + `click`, debounced 400ms, `role="button"`. Menu toggles. `#fmTop` lifted to 9996 while `.fm-drawer` is on, with portrait/back/id/eod made `pointer-events:none` so a stray tap cannot fire the picker under an open menu. `#fmDrawerClose` added (inside the drawer under 340px, where a 238px drawer leaves no room beside it). Escape and `popstate` both dismiss.
- CONFIDENCE NOTE: gap 1 is iOS specific. Android Chrome fires click on any div with a listener and would not have locked. Gaps 2 through 4 are platform independent and are enough to read as a freeze on their own.
- SECOND DEAD END, SAME SYMPTOM, DIFFERENT CAUSE: the drawer Bugs button runs `document.getElementById('bugsTabBtnHidden').click()`, a real `.tab` node. core.js switches the center panel; mobile.js never hears about it because `bridgeShowTab` only patches `window.showTab`. From any non-center view `#fmCenter` still carried `.fm-off`, so the tap changed NOTHING on screen. Fixed with `bridgeTabClicks`, a capture phase document listener that resyncs the shell after any `.tab` click the shell did not initiate, guarded by the existing `syncing` flag. Root fix, not per button.
- TOUCH GATE: `TOUCH_ONLY` is `(pointer: coarse)` AND NOT `(any-pointer: fine)`. Deliberately not a width check: a 1024px tablet has no mouse and a 380px desktop window does. Sets `body.fm-touchonly`, cleared on `leave()`.
- DRONE MINING LOCKED ON TOUCH: `LOCKED` table drives it, one line per system. Tile gets `.fm-locked` (red border, greyed icon, DESKTOP ONLY tag). `#fmMiningLock` notice injected directly above LAUNCH EXPEDITION, which is disabled in both JS and CSS. TAB DELIBERATELY STILL REACHABLE: the brief screen carries the bank balance and the leaderboard, and those are worth reading on a phone. Blocking it outright is a two line change if that is preferred.
- NO TOAST FOR THE LOCK, ON PURPOSE: `gToast` is inline styled `bottom:24px`, which is UNDERNEATH the 56px nav. It would have been invisible. Anything else calling gToast on mobile is also invisible; not addressed here.
- CASINO WIDTHS, WHY THE 1.3.4 NET WAS NOT ENOUGH: `#casinoContent{overflow-x:auto}` said in its own comment that it did not reflow anything. It guaranteed nothing was unreachable, which is not the same as fitting.
- `#wheelCanvas` is `width="400" height="400"` with NO CSS width, so roulette had a 400px floor before padding, on the pane that opens by default. Single largest offender. Capped at 300px; the wheel is drawn into the backing store and is display only.
- `.rl-num-cell` was a hard 22px across 13 `1fr` tracks: 310px inside a table already spending 32px on padding. The hard width was the ONLY thing stopping the 1fr tracks reflowing.
- THREE BOARDS SCALED, NOT REFLOWED: `#chessBoard` (360px, squares absolutely positioned at `x*45` from JS), `.sol-board` (`repeat(7,minmax(56px,1fr))`, 440px floor, cards drawn from a sprite sheet whose `background-position` is DERIVED FROM CARD WIDTH so a CSS width override tears the sprite), `#ms-board` (JS writes `repeat(cols,28px)`; Expert is 30 cols, 898px). CSS cannot resize any of them without breaking hit testing or the sprite math.
- WHY SCALING IS SAFE: all three use real child elements with their own listeners, or `document.elementFromPoint` in the solitaire drag case, and browsers map pointer coordinates through transforms for both. Verified by reading each input path, not assumed.
- WHY THE NEGATIVE MARGINS: `transform` does not shrink the layout box, so a scaled board still forces the pane wide. `marginRight: -(nat*(1-k))` and the same on height pull the box in to match. That is what actually kills the horizontal overflow; the transform alone would only have changed the paint.
- MINESWEEPER FLOOR 0.58: below it a cell is under 17px and no thumb can hit one. Past the floor `#ms-wrap` scrolls. EXPERT ON A PHONE REMAINS THUMB HOSTILE and is a live candidate for the LOCKED table; flagged, not decided.
- REFIT TRIGGERS: `requestAnimationFrame` after `render()` (the pane was display:none a frame ago, so measuring during render reads zero), a `MutationObserver` per fitted pane on `childList+subtree` (every board rebuilds its own innerHTML per move and drops the inline transform), the 1Hz mirror tick as a backstop, `resize` and `orientationchange`. The observer watches childList only, and fitBoard writes only style attributes, so it cannot re-trigger itself.
- `leave()` now clears the fit transforms and `--fm-sol-k` as well as `fm-touchonly`. Widening past the breakpoint would otherwise strand a permanently shrunk chess board on the desktop layout. Same class of bug as the existing `.fm-off` cleanup two lines below it.
- SOLITAIRE DRAG GHOST is `position:fixed` and therefore OUTSIDE the scaled board. mobile.js writes `--fm-sol-k` on body and the ghost matches it, or the card jumps size the instant it is picked up.
- tools/mobile-136-check.mjs NEW, 52 assertions. FIX assertions cover the change; ANCHOR assertions cover the measurements the change depends on. THE ANCHORS ARE THE POINT: `fitBoard` uses hardcoded natural widths (362 = 360 + 1px border each side; 7*56 + 6*8) because none of the three can be measured from CSS. Asserts `const S = 45`, `repeat(7,minmax(56px,1fr))`, `gap:8px`, `repeat(${m.cols},28px)`, `cols:30`, `width="400"`. Move any of them and the scale goes silently wrong with nothing throwing.
- CHECK VERIFIED NON-VACUOUS BY MUTATION: reverting the menu toggle, moving chess S from 45 to 44 and lifting the wheel cap produced exactly three failures and no others.
- client/mobile-mockup.html NEW. Skeleton satisfying everything mobile.js reaches for (three .panel children, the .tab strip including the hidden bugs tab, #casinoTabs subtabs, #miningLaunchBtn, the right panel ids tagGroups maps), loading the REAL mobile.css and mobile.js. Boards stubbed at exact natural sizes. Readout shows viewport, touchOnly, view/tab/game/drawer state, document overflow and the live k for each board. No server, socket or login. Safe to delete before a public release.
- NEW I18N KEYS, ENGLISH ONLY, NEED ZH: `mob.lock.tag`, `mob.lock.mining`, `mob.lock.miningLong`. Same hazard class as the JS-array surfaces: a grid built from a JS array is exactly the shape that silently stays English in Jade mode.
- SUITE TOTAL 560 across eleven tools (i18n, i18n-scope, selector, mobile, mobile-136, city, city-shortcut, fleet, lore, tutorial, lane, mathtest).
