- NOT ADDRESSED, STILL OPEN: drone mining `mining_bank` is still client reported; blackjack `minDurMs` fast hand voids; sudoku and minesweeper solutions still client checked; sudoku and mathgame cooldowns still in localStorage.

## 1.3.5 changes (Guild Numeracy Exams; two payout holes closed; SERVER + CLIENT)
- REPORTED SYMPTOM: math quiz not paying out. FIVE causes, and the one generating the reports is not the interesting one.
- CAUSE 1, THE REPORTS: the client opened a casino round at Start and closed it at Test Complete. A refresh, dropped socket or tab-away left it `open`. `casino_bet` refuses a second open round per game. The client handled that refusal with `.then(r=>{ if(r&&r.ok) mqRoundId=r.roundId })` and NO ELSE BRANCH, so `mqRoundId` stayed null, ten questions were answered, the score row read "Earned: 800", and `endTest` ran `if(mqRoundId)` and sent nothing. NOTHING ON SCREEN WAS WRONG. The number was never real. Persisted until the 15 minute sweep, so every test started in that window paid zero.
- CAUSE 2: error branches of `casino_result` sent no roundId. casino-net.js correlates result acks on `m.data.roundId===roundId`. So EVERY failed settle in EVERY casino game resolved as a 12 second timeout rather than its real reason. Cross-cutting, fixed for all games.
- CAUSE 3: the math client discarded the settle promise. No failure could surface at all.
- CAUSE 4: the nominal 1 stake locked out any player at zero cash, silently. That is precisely the player grinding a quiz for money.
- CAUSE 5: a backgrounded tab throttles setInterval to ~1/s, stretching a test past its round timeout so the sweep expired it mid-play.
- CAUSE 6, UNREPORTED, THE LARGEST: payout was client-declared. `CasinoNet.result(roundId, totalEarned+1)`, cap flat 900, minDurMs 5000, cooldown in localStorage. A console one-liner was worth 900 per 5 seconds, ~648k/hr against START_CASH 1000, with no arithmetic involved. Larger than the drone mining hole.
- server/mathtest.js NEW. Generates the paper, holds the answer key, times each question on the SERVER clock, grades every submission, settles the round itself. `publicQuestion` is the ONLY path a question takes to the wire and it strips `answer` and `tol`. Same architecture as solitaire replay.
- TEN TOPICS x FIVE TIERS: arith, order, powers, percent, fractions, algebra, sequence, ratio, interest, wordpnl. Tier drives SHAPE not just size (tier 4 algebra puts x on both sides; tier 4 interest compounds; tier 4 sequences use second differences), so tier 5 is different work from tier 1 rather than the same sum with bigger numbers.
- FIVE PAPERS, one CASINO_CFG entry each, generated from the exam table so a retune moves the cap with it: drill (free, topic-selectable, 4min cd), ledger (150, 8min), speed (250, 9s clocks, streak bonus every 5, 10min), broker (700, 15min), quant (2500, 30min, GATED on a passing broker result).
- PAYOUT IS NOW A WAGER. Earnings accrue visibly per correct answer, then multiply by the grade band at settlement. Under 60 percent the multiplier is 0 and the entry is gone. Break even 65-75 percent on every paid paper. Verified by assertion, not by eye: the CURVE group asserts net(0.5) <= -entry, net(0.6) < 0, net(0.8) > 0 for each.
- NO MIGRATION. Cooldown reads `getLastCasinoRoundTs(player, game)` from casino_rounds; the certification gate reads `getBestCasinoResult` and passes on `payout > wager`, which under this grade curve is reachable only at C and above, so "has ever profited" and "has ever passed" are the same fact.
- COOLDOWN COUNTS FROM opened_ts, NOT resolved_ts, so abandoning a paper does not reset the clock.
- RESUME: the lobby reports any open paper. The question live when the connection went is scored WRONG and the paper moves on. Re-issuing it with a fresh clock makes refresh a free re-roll on anything hard, which is a worse bug than the one it fixes. One question is the price of the interruption.
- STALE ROUND CANNOT LOCK ANYONE OUT: `math_start` auto-forfeits a prior open round for that paper (solitaire's pattern). That is cause 1 closed structurally rather than by handling the error message better.
- WORST FIND, AND IT PREDATES THIS PATCH: `casino_result` resolved a round by id and player and paid what the client asked, capped at that game's flat, WITHOUT CHECKING WHICH GAME OPENED IT. Any server-authoritative round could therefore be settled through the client-declared path, skipping the code meant to decide the payout. `solitaire_start` then `casino_result{payout:999999}` paid 1848 on a 250 buy-in WITH NO CARDS PLAYED, and solitaire minDurMs is 0 so nothing throttled the loop. LIVE SINCE SOLITAIRE SHIPPED. The exams would have inherited it on day one.
- FIX: `SERVER_SETTLED_GAMES` names solitaire plus all five papers. Both `casino_result` and `casino_bet_addon` refuse them and log to the admin panel. Addon is included because raising the wager raises the cap, which is the same lever by another name.
- FOUND BY PLAYING IT, NOT READING IT. Every static check passed with that hole open and was right to: each handler in isolation is correct. The hole only appears if you ask what ELSE can reach a round after one of them opens it, and no file answers that. A live socket run against a booted server hit it on the first pass. This is the argument for the live harness existing at all.
- CAP FALLBACK WRONG IN FOUR PLACES: `(cfg.mult||1)` reads 1 for every game declaring 0, because 0 is falsy, so sudoku, minesweeper, solitaire and the exams capped at wager+flat instead of flat. Inert today. A backstop that is not the number you wrote is not a backstop. Two found by reading, the THIRD (casino_play) found only because the check asserts the COUNT of guarded sites rather than the absence of the old pattern, the fourth is the new settle. Check asserts 4.
- THREE BUGS THE SUITE FOUND IN THIS PATCH'S OWN CODE:
  1. `maxGross` computed the cap from the unrounded product while `buildPaper` rounds each reward, and rounding UP is the common case (perQ 8 x weight 0.85 = 6.8, paid as 7). Drill cap 136 vs flawless paper 140. Would have clamped the best player in the game and logged them to the admin panel as a cheater.
  2. Topic resolution tested `TOPIC_IDS.has('mixed')`, false, so every "mixed" drill fell through to arithmetic only. Reads as a run of bad luck rather than a bug, which is the worst possible failure mode.
  3. Compound interest carried tol 1.2 on an answer specified to the nearest 1, so an answer wrong by exactly 1 was paid.
- I18N: old keys deleted with the game that used them (five difficulty labels, one payout line, none of it still true). Word problems ship `tk` (template key) + `tv` (vars) beside the rendered English so Jade mode renders the QUESTION, not the generator's output; symbol-only questions ship text alone because there is nothing in them to translate. 10 topic names, 16 question templates, 45 UI strings, all with zh. Subtab renamed to Numeracy Exams. i18n-check clean.
- tools/mathtest-check.mjs NEW, 77 assertions in seven groups. PAPER fuzzes 41,000 questions and asserts every answer is finite, typeable at 3dp, graded correct by its own tolerance, and NOT graded correct one off. KEY asserts publicQuestion strips the key. CURVE asserts a failing paper actually loses money. CAP asserts the backstop clears a flawless paper and is not slack past 1.6x. WIRE asserts the client declares no payout, holds no answers, calls no Math.random and keeps no localStorage cooldown. SERVER asserts the handlers, the guards and the four cap sites. SOURCE ASSERTIONS STRIP COMMENTS FIRST: the first draft failed two of its own checks because the comments explaining each bug quote the exact pattern the grep looks for.
- SUITE TOTAL 508 across ten tools (i18n, i18n-scope, selector, mobile, city, city-shortcut, fleet, lore, tutorial, lane, mathtest). Plus a 37 assertion live socket run against a booted server, NOT SHIPPED because it needs a running instance and a throwaway account. It covers what static analysis cannot: cash moving by exactly the amount the result claims, the cooldown holding against a real second attempt, out-of-order and forged roundIds refused, resume and abandon accounting, the gate opening only after a genuine pass, and the two settle-path holes above.
- CSS REWRITTEN TO 1.3.4 MOBILE RULES: every width is a max-width, the card grid floor is `minmax(min(260px,100%),1fr)` so it cannot exceed a 320px screen, the question sizes with `clamp(1.3rem,6vw,2.1rem)`, and the answer input is `max-width:100%;min-width:0`. The old file was a verbatim copy of the inline <style> block; the inline block is gone.
- STILL NOT FIXED, STATED PLAINLY: this is not bot-resistant and cannot be made so by server authority. The server asks a question a computer answers instantly and correctly, and no latency floor separates a script from a fast human without punishing the fast human, so none was added. Authority buys that earning REQUIRES ANSWERING, which pins the drain to entry x curve x cooldown. Botting is now a number to price. Bot-hard means questions that stop being machine-solvable, a different feature.
- ECONOMICS ARE A PROPOSAL, NOT A FINDING. No data on live mining or trading income. Perfect-play ceilings: drill ~1.7k/hr, quant ~11k/hr. `perQ` in the exam table is the single tuning number; cap and UI both derive from it.
- Changed: server/mathtest.js (new), server/server.js, server/db.js, client/assets/casino-mathgame.js (rewritten), client/assets/casino-mathgame.css (rewritten), client/assets/core.js, client/index.html, client/version.json, tools/mathtest-check.mjs (new), docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.3.4 changes (Mobile panel audit; CLIENT, CSS + one new tool)
- WHY A TOOL AND NOT A CHECKLIST: the Cities bug rendered, populated and silently truncated. Nothing looked wrong; content below the fold simply did not exist and there was no scrollbar to suggest otherwise. That is not findable by tabbing through panels on a phone, because the failure mode is the absence of something you were not looking for.
- tools/mobile-audit.mjs NEW. Reads index.html inline styles, all client .css, and JS files that ship CSS as strings (40 sources, 28 surfaces). Seven hazard classes: H1 CLIP (overflow:hidden + height cap), H2 WIDE (fixed px grid tracks), H3 MINW, H4 NEST (auto-fit minmax floor), H5 CANVAS (height:100% under a non-definite parent), H6 FIXED (position:fixed outside the scroll region), H7 BASIS (hard width or flex basis on a sidebar). Plus reachability: every .tab in markup must be openable from the shell. STATIC, exits 0, reports SUSPECTS. `--unowned` filters to surfaces with no body.fm-mobile rule naming them.
- "owned" IS A WEAK SIGNAL AND THE TOOL SAYS SO. It means a mobile rule names the selector, not that the rule works. The sell modal was reported "ok" for four patches while being broken, because mobile.css did name it. Read the rule, do not trust the label.
- WORST FIND, SELF INFLICTED: sell-modal.css:3 and short-modal.css:3 declare `min-width:420px;max-width:520px`. mobile.css (from 1.3.0) set `width:95vw!important;max-width:95vw!important`. MIN-WIDTH BEATS MAX-WIDTH. The rule did nothing. Card stayed 420px on a 360px viewport, confirm button off the right edge, and body overflow:hidden means no sideways scroll to reach it. Sell and Cover were unreachable on a phone from 1.3.0 through 1.3.3. FIX: `min-width:0!important` on both.
- SAME TRAP, LOGIN: fm-auth.js:44 `#fm-auth-card{min-width:340px;max-width:400px;width:92%}`. Overflows a 320px device. `min-width:0!important`.
- H1 CLOSED STRUCTURALLY, NOT PANEL BY PANEL: core.js shows bugsTab / fleshbookTab / galacticTab / miningTab as `flex`. All carry `flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:hidden`, which requires an ancestor with a definite height. In the shell that is ONLY fill mode (body[data-fmfill=1]), currently galactic and chat. Elsewhere .grid is block flow, flex:1 is inert, height resolves to content, the clip clips nothing. So fleshbook/bugs are fine TODAY. The rule `body.fm-mobile[data-fmfill="0"] #fmCenter > [id$="Tab"]{flex:0 0 auto;height:auto;min-height:0;overflow:visible}` states the contract so the class cannot re-arm when a future surface is added to fill mode. SIDE EFFECT ACCEPTED: fleshbookTab and arenaTab have border-radius:6px and lose corner clipping.
- H7 SUDOKU: casino-sudoku.css:3 `#sdk-board{...width:369px}` is the ONLY genuinely hard pinned width in the casino. Grid is `repeat(9,1fr)` so `width:100%;max-width:369px` scales it with no cell math. Baccarat 520, sicbo 560, mathgame 500, chess 360 and the sudoku wrapper 520 are all MAX-width and already reflow.
- CASINO NET: `#casinoContent{overflow-x:auto}`. Games are still laid out for a ~700px pane and this patch does not reflow them. The net is the difference between a cramped screen and an unreachable one, and it costs one rule.
- H7 LORE: lore-book.css:71 `#loreLeft{width:270px;flex:0 0 270px}` inside `#loreBook{width:min(980px,94vw)}`. At 320px the book is 300px and the sidebar takes 270 of it. Now stacks: #loreInner column, #loreLeft full width capped at 32% height with its own scroll, #loreSpine becomes a 1px horizontal rule, #loreCover drops its scale(1.6) which overflowed the book during the open animation.
- H6 GOD BUTTON: index.html `#godModeBtn{position:fixed;bottom:22px;right:22px;z-index:9998;48px}`. #fmNav is z-index 9992, so the circle sits ON the More slot. Admin only (display:none otherwise) but the admin is the one testing on a phone. Lifted to `bottom:calc(var(--fm-nav) + 12px)`, shrunk to 40px.
- VERIFIED FINE, NO CHANGE NEEDED: unmod-warning-modal and unmod-patron-gate-modal (card is width:90%;max-width:480px), ship-manifest-modal (width:96vw;max-width:820px), tutorial-overlay (width:440px;max-width:95vw), miningFullscreenHost (inset:0 by design, z-index 9999 above the chrome deliberately), spSolarStage/spCityStage (H1 false positive: their parent spSystemView/spSurfaceView is position:fixed;inset:0, so the height IS definite), spHUD (300px, under the 320 floor, given max-width:100% anyway).
- THREE TOOL BUGS FOUND BY RUNNING IT, ALL FIXED AND COMMENTED IN PLACE:
  1. `#pnl-root .row` reported as a live 410px overflow. That selector exists ONLY in pnl-simple.css; nothing in any markup or JS renders it. Added a dead-selector filter that cross-references all html+js source.
  2. Five casino games reported as hard pinned. `/width:\d+px/` matches `max-width:520px` as a substring. Only sudoku was real. Regex now anchors on `(?:^|[;{])`. A report that cries wolf is worse than no report because it trains you to skim it.
  3. Phone floor was set at 340px, so `#fm-auth-card{min-width:340px}` was missed for not being GREATER than 340. Floor is now 320 (iPhone SE, older Android), which is the smallest screen that exists rather than a typical one.
- REACHABILITY: 12 of 12 tabs openable from the shell (5 nav destinations + 12 More tiles). No orphans.
- tools/mobile-check.mjs 94 -> 105. Four of the eleven new assertions guard UPSTREAM declarations (the 420px, the 340px, the 369px, the 270px), so if a file is fixed properly later the override here is flagged as stale instead of lingering to fight the new code. REGRESSION TESTED: deleting the audit block from mobile.css fails 7.
- STILL NOT VERIFIABLE HERE: no layout engine. Nothing in either tool can confirm a box renders at the right size. Everything above is read off declarations. Device only.
- Changed: client/assets/mobile.css, client/version.json, tools/mobile-audit.mjs (new), tools/mobile-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.3.3 changes (Galaxy > Cities on mobile; CLIENT, CSS only)
- SYMPTOM: on a phone, Galaxy > Cities showed the header and the district map and nothing else. No scrollbar, no indication the rest existed. Everything below the map was unreachable.
- ROOT CAUSE, three rules that are individually correct: index.html `#gCitiesPane{overflow:hidden;flex:1;min-height:0}`, index.html `#citiesTabInner{height:100%}`, city.js:1313 `SCOPE .cywrap{...height:calc(100% - 42px)}`. Together they say "this pane is exactly viewport tall and manages its own internal scrolling", which is true of the desktop three column layout (rail | map | side, each with its own overflow-y).
- WHAT BREAKS IT: city.js:1317 `@media(max-width:1100px){SCOPE .cywrap,SCOPE .cywrap.railopen{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}}` collapses the three columns into one, so rail/map/side now STACK. The height cap and the overflow:hidden do not collapse with them. Content is three times taller than the box that clips it.
- NOT A SHELL BUG. Reproduces in a 1000px desktop window. The mobile shell only guarantees it, since a phone is always under 1100px. Cities is also the ONLY galaxy pane like this: gFactionsPane / gShippingPane / gMarketsPane / gContractsPane are all `overflow-y:auto;flex:1;min-height:0` and needed nothing.
- FIX, all scoped to body.fm-mobile, CSS only, no JS touched: #gCitiesPane -> overflow-y:auto; #citiesTabInner -> height:auto + min-height:100%; .cywrap (both plain and .railopen) -> height:auto + single column + align-items:start; .cyside -> max-height:none, overflow:visible, and grid-template-columns forced to minmax(0,1fr) because city.js:1318 sets repeat(auto-fit,minmax(280px,1fr)) which can overflow a 320px screen.
- !important IS REQUIRED, NOT LAZY: #gCitiesPane's overflow and #citiesTabInner's height are INLINE styles in index.html. No amount of selector specificity beats an inline declaration.
- NESTED SCROLLERS RELEASED ON PURPOSE: .cyside had `max-height:100%;overflow-y:auto`. A scroll region inside a scroll region cannot be driven with a thumb; you get the inner one or the outer one depending on where your finger lands. The colony rail is the ONE exception left bounded (140px, own overflow-y), because it is a long list and unbounding it pushes the district map completely off the first screen.
- CANVAS RATIO: #cityCv is width=1180 height=720 intrinsic, stretched by city.js:1351 `canvas{width:100%;height:100%}`. With .cywrap no longer height constrained, .cymid has no definite height, so city.js:1350 `.cvwrap{flex:1;min-height:220px}` collapses to 220px and the Voronoi squashes. Replaced on mobile with `flex:0 0 auto; height:auto; min-height:0; aspect-ratio:1180/720`. Hit testing (city.js:1985) already scales per axis via `cv.width/r.width` and `cv.height/r.height`, so taps were never at risk at any ratio; this is purely visual.
- CANVAS GESTURES: city.js:2038-2064 binds touchstart/touchmove/touchend for one finger pan and two finger pinch, with `e.preventDefault()` on a `{passive:false}` touchmove. Inside a newly scrollable pane that races the browser's scroll start. `#cityCv{touch-action:none}` declares it up front, same treatment as #galaxySVG. TRADE-OFF ACCEPTED: the canvas is now a scroll dead zone, so the player scrolls the pane by dragging beside it, not on it.
- FOUR OF THE TEN NEW ASSERTIONS GUARD CODE THE SHELL DOES NOT OWN: #gCitiesPane still overflow:hidden, the other four panes still overflow-y:auto, city.js still collapses .cywrap under 1100px, city.js still height-caps it. These exist so that if someone later fixes Cities properly in index.html or city.js, the suite flags these overrides as stale instead of leaving dead !important rules to fight the new code.
- tools/mobile-check.mjs 84 -> 94. REGRESSION TESTED: deleting the Cities block from mobile.css fails 6.
- STILL NOT VERIFIABLE HERE: jsdom has no layout engine. Nothing in this suite can confirm the pane actually scrolls or that the map renders at the right size. Device only.
- Changed: client/assets/mobile.css, client/version.json, tools/mobile-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.3.2 changes (Sound toggle hidden; viewport assertions; CLIENT)
- SOUND TOGGLE: `#soundToggle{display:none;}` appended to assets/toast.css directly under the existing `#soundToggle` rules. Same specificity, later in the file, so it wins. NOTHING ELSE TOUCHED: index.html:1758 still has the button, sound.js still defines window.toggleSound and window.playSound, every playSound call site is live, and window.toggleSound() still flips _soundOn from the console. Revert = delete that one rule.
- WHY IT WAS SAFE TO HIDE: sound.js:7 `let _soundOn = false`. The default is off and nothing else writes it, so hiding the only control freezes the game in the state it already shipped in. No player loses audio they had.
- SPECIFICITY NOTE: `body.fm-mobile #soundToggle` in mobile.css is higher specificity but sets only bottom/left/font-size/padding/z-index, never `display`, so it cannot resurrect the button. That rule is deliberately KEPT and annotated, so re-enabling gives correct mobile placement with no second edit.
- SCALING, VERIFIED NOT ASSERTED: index.html:9 `width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover`. width=device-width is what makes a phone map CSS px to its logical width (390 iPhone 14, 360 typical Android) instead of rendering at a faked ~980px and scaling down. Shell activates at innerWidth <= 900 so both orientations qualify. Inside it: #fmNav buttons flex:1, .fm-grid3 repeat(3,1fr), panels full width. Only fixed widths are the 238px drawer (74% of a 320px screen) and the 150px #pnl-donut, neither of which can overflow. `.fmt-id{min-width:0}` is the flexbox guard that lets name/cash truncate rather than pushing the EOD clock off the right edge; without it the top bar overflows on any long player name. 100dvh on .wrap with inset:0 as the fallback for engines without dvh. env(safe-area-inset-bottom) on #fmNav paired with viewport-fit=cover. Extra compaction at <=380px, landscape rule at <=500px height.
- NEW ASSERTIONS (8): viewport meta has width=device-width and initial-scale=1; viewport-fit=cover present; #fmNav uses the safe-area inset; .fmt-id has min-width:0; sound toggle hidden; sound toggle markup still present; toggleSound still defined; playSound still defined. The last three exist specifically so a later cleanup pass cannot quietly delete the code this patch promised to keep.
- WHY THE VIEWPORT ASSERTION EARNS ITS PLACE: that meta is one line in a 186KB file and every proportional rule in the shell is measured against it. Edited or dropped, the layout is silently wrong everywhere at once with no error and no obviously broken element. It is the highest ratio of blast radius to visibility in the client.
- tools/mobile-check.mjs 76 -> 84. REGRESSION TESTED: stripping `width=device-width, viewport-fit=cover` from the meta and deleting the hide rule fails 3.
- STILL NOT MEASURABLE HERE: jsdom has no layout engine, so no check in this suite can detect actual horizontal overflow at any width. Every scaling claim above is read off the CSS, not off a rendered box. Real device only.
- Changed: client/assets/toast.css, client/assets/mobile.css (comment only), client/version.json, tools/mobile-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.3.1 changes (drawChart retry loop while hidden; CLIENT)
- ROOT CAUSE, core.js drawChart(): `if (W < 10 || H < 10) { setTimeout(drawChart, 100); return; }`. W and H come from `canvas.clientWidth/clientHeight`, which are 0 for any element inside a `display:none` ancestor. #chart lives inside #marketTab, which is display:none on every other tab. So "not laid out yet" and "tab is hidden" were indistinguishable to this branch.
- WHY IT ACCUMULATED: _pushWave() (core.js ~4013) schedules drawChart through requestAnimationFrame on EVERY price tick, with no visibility test. Each tick that lands on the hidden branch starts an independent 100ms self rescheduling chain. They are separate setTimeout chains, not deduped, and none terminate until the canvas is visible. Time spent on another tab is directly proportional to the number of live chains.
- BACKGROUNDED TABS MAKE IT WORSE: requestAnimationFrame is throttled to zero when the page is hidden, so no NEW chains start, but setTimeout keeps firing, so every chain already started keeps running with the screen off. Each iteration does getContext, two canvas dimension writes and a ctx.scale before bailing.
- PRE-EXISTING, NOT INTRODUCED BY 1.3.0. Desktop hits it the same way. It is listed here because the shell changes the exposure: a phone player is off Market by default.
- FIX: `if (canvas.offsetParent !== null) setTimeout(drawChart, 100);`. offsetParent is null iff an ancestor is display:none (it is also null for position:fixed elements, which #chart is not; do not copy this guard to a fixed element without checking). The genuine "visible but not yet laid out" case still retries, because offsetParent is non-null there.
- COME-BACK PATH: core.js ~4026 already has `new ResizeObserver(() => { if (_waveBuffer.length) drawChart(); }).observe(_chartCanvas)`. That was the ONLY thing covering display:none -> block, which was the open question at the end of 1.3.0. mobile.js render() now calls nudgeChart() on the next animation frame when the visible view is market or board/heat. Belt and braces on purpose: RO firing for a display change is correct in Chrome and Safari but it is a single point of failure for a surface the player looks at first.
- NOT CHANGED: _pushWave still schedules on every tick. An early out there would be a second place that has to know about visibility, and the guarded drawChart already costs almost nothing when hidden. If profiling later says otherwise, the early out belongs in _pushWave, not another guard in drawChart.
- OTHER CANVASES CHECKED, NONE AFFECTED: _drawDonut, _drawBarsAxis and drawEquity all bail with a plain `return` when their element is missing or empty. drawChart was the only one with a timer retry.
- tools/mobile-check.mjs 71 -> 76. Three source assertions (guard present, unguarded form absent, nudgeChart present) and two live ones (entering Market redraws, leaving Market does not). REGRESSION TESTED: restoring the unguarded retry and deleting the nudge fails 3 of the 5, including the live redraw counter with [calls=0].
- HARNESS NOTE: the live assertion works because jsdom has no layout, so window.drawChart is stubbed as a counter. It proves the shell CALLS the redraw; it cannot prove the canvas ends up the right width. That part needs a real device.
- Changed: client/assets/core.js, client/assets/mobile.js, client/version.json, tools/mobile-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.3.0 changes (Mobile shell; CLIENT)
- ROOT CAUSE OF THE CLUTTER, mobile.css 1.2.5: `html,body{overflow:auto;height:auto}` + `.wrap{height:auto;overflow:visible}` + `.grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}`. Three panels stacked into ONE DOCUMENT SCROLL. Every position:fixed and position:absolute child in the client then measures against a viewport that is no longer the container. That is why individual surfaces "format weirdly" and why patching them one at a time never converged.
- SHELL: `.wrap` is position:fixed inset:0 overflow:hidden. `.grid` is position:absolute between `--fm-top` and `--fm-nav` and is THE ONLY SCROLLING ELEMENT. Chrome is #fmTop (46px), #fmCtx (34px, only on views with segments), #fmNav (56px), #fmScrim. All scoped to body.fm-mobile, added only at <=900px.
- NAV BUG THAT WAS SHIPPING SINCE THE 1.2.x MOBILE NAV EXISTED: two independent tab systems. core.js:4224 `$all('.tab').forEach(tab => tab.addEventListener('click', ...))` does the real work (lazyLoad dev-comms/fleshbook/tcg, loadGuildDirectory, drawEquity on pnl, __devlogsSync). market-state.js:631 `function showTab(name)` covers ids ['marketTab','pnlTab','casinoTab','guildTab','storeTab','heatTab','galacticTab','bugsTab','devlogsTab'] and does NO init. mobile.js `mobileNavTo` called showTab(). Result on a phone: P&L never ran drawEquity, guild never loaded the directory, and mining/fleshbook/arena were unroutable. FIX: `clickTab()` dispatches `.click()` on the real `.tab` node, which fires BOTH listeners (market-state also binds a .tab click that calls showTab, so the galaxy.js showTab wrapper still fires too). Nothing else in the client had to change.
- WHY NOT JUST FIX showTab: it is called from core.js:3183/3507, galaxy.js:1567, sound.js:189/283/289 as an internal jump target. Widening it means auditing every caller for double init. Clicking the node is the path the desktop player already takes.
- FIVE DESTINATIONS: market / board / chat / wallet / more. board segments = companies|news|heat, wallet segments = pnl|wire|ranks. Depth 1 destinations (everything under More) set body[data-fmdepth=1] and expose the back chevron.
- NO DOM MOVES, THIS IS LOAD BEARING. index.html is a 186KB monolith with ids referenced across ~50 modules. Views are existing panels shown in place. The drawer IS `#fm-header-user`, repositioned by CSS (position:fixed, flex-direction:column, translateX). funds.js:1203 sets `hdrUser.style.display='flex'` on auth, which the column override cooperates with; before auth the drawer is correctly empty and hidden.
- PANEL VISIBILITY USES .fm-off, NOT display:none. `.fm-off > *:not(#margin-call-banner):not(#dunce-banner){display:none!important}`. Both banners are children of #rightPanel. Hiding that panel to show Market would have buried an active margin call, which is a countdown to auto liquidation. The banners are additionally position:fixed under the top bar. Their `display` stays under their own JS control; the CSS only positions, so it cannot force a dead banner visible.
- LEFT PANEL SPLIT: Companies and News are one panel with no wrapper elements. tagGroups() walks `#fmLeft.children` and assigns `data-fmgrp` = companies before the `<hr>`, news from the `<hr>` onward. Do not add a second `<hr>` to that panel without revisiting this.
- RIGHT PANEL GROUPS: `#chatBox`->chat, `#transferSection`->wire, `#leaderboardCompact`->ranks. The latter two are `.collapsible.collapsed` by default (localStorage keys fm_wire_collapsed / fm_lb_collapsed). As a whole view a collapsed section is a single header, so render() removes `.collapsed` when either is the active segment. It does NOT write localStorage, so the desktop preference is untouched.
- CASINO LOBBY: an 11 item horizontal scroller is not navigable on a phone. #fmCasinoLobby is built from `#casinoTabs .subtab` at init and inserted as the first child of #casinoTab; body[data-fmgame] flips between lobby and game. Tiles copy each subtab's own `data-i18n` key so applyI18n localises them on a language switch. Tapping a tile clicks the real subtab, so core.js CASINO_SCRIPTS lazy loading is untouched.
- GALAXY: body[data-fmfill=1] switches `.grid` to overflow:hidden; `#galacticTab` goes position:absolute inset:0; `#galaxySVG` gets `touch-action:none` so panning does not fight the scroll region. Same fill flag is used by chat.
- MINING: `#miningFullscreenHost` is already position:fixed z-index:9999. Chrome is z-index 9992 to 9995, so a run covers it with no extra handling. Do not raise the chrome above 9999.
- REMOVED: the 1.2.5 `touchend` handler that called `preventDefault()` then `e.target.click()` on every button, tab and subtab. It double fired where a click listener also existed and cancelled any scroll that began on a button. Replaced by `touch-action: manipulation` on body.fm-mobile.
- SOFT KEYBOARD: visualViewport resize listener adds `.fm-kbd` when the viewport loses more than 150px, which sets `--fm-nav:0` and hides the nav so the chat input is not covered on iOS. `#chatInput` is font-size:16px, below which iOS zooms the page on focus.
- TOP BAR MIRRORS RATHER THAN DUPLICATES: a 1s interval copies `#cash`, `#fm-header-name`, `#eod-timer` (text and `.urgent`) and the `#fm-header-portrait` background. Nothing re-implements those updates, so the bar cannot drift from the source of truth. Same loop drives the chat unread total, the fleshbook badge, and hides the Capital Houses tile while `#guildTabBtn` is display:none.
- LORE: `#loreBtn` is mounted by lore-book.js into `#eod-timer-wrap`, which the shell does not surface. The More grid has a Lore tile that clicks `#loreBtn`. If that button ever moves, the tile breaks silently; mobile-check asserts the tile exists but cannot assert the button does, since lore-book.js is not loaded in the harness.
- i18n: 28 new keys (mob.eod, mob.nav.*, mob.seg.*, mob.sect.*, mob.tile.*) in core.js window.I18N, en+zh. They deliberately carry NO emoji, unlike the tab.* equivalents, because the tiles render their own icon and would otherwise show it twice. mob.tile.* zh reuses established terms: 资本商号, 血肉簿, 称号, 库存, 纪事.
- TITLE KEY, NOT TITLE TEXT: state.labelKey stores the i18n key and paintTitle() resolves it through window.t on every mirror tick, so the depth 1 header follows a live language switch. Storing the resolved string here was the first implementation and left the header in English after toggling Jade with noReload.
- LEAVE PATH: crossing back above 900px removes body.fm-mobile, clears every `.fm-off` and `.fm-hide`, and removes all data-fm* attributes. Without the clear, rotating a tablet left panels invisible on the desktop layout. mobile-check asserts zero survivors.
- tools/mobile-check.mjs NEW, 71 assertions, jsdom. It boots the real index.html, evals mobile.js with innerWidth forced to 390, and drives FM_MOBILE. REGRESSION TESTED BOTH WAYS: reverting clickTab to `window.showTab(name)` fails 3 assertions including the two click spies; removing the banner exemption in tagGroups fails the banner survival check with [chat:hide,wallet:hide]. GOTCHA for the next person: the harness supplies only window.showTab, window.t and window.applyI18n. If the shell ever needs a real core.js global, the check is telling you the shell has grown a coupling it should not have.
- CHECK-WRITING GOTCHA HIT DURING THIS PATCH: the first i18n key extractor matched `'mob.tile.'` from the concatenation `state.labelKey = 'mob.tile.' + name`, then reported it as a missing key. Complete keys only, filtered on a trailing dot. And the first market-state assertion used a lazy `[\s\S]{0,400}?\}` window from `function showTab(name){`, which never reached a closing brace inside 400 chars and reported "not found" rather than failing loudly. It now cuts the `var ids = [...]` line directly.
- NOT FIXED BY THIS PATCH, ON PURPOSE: the shell gives every surface a clean full width box; it does not reflow the contents. The 11 casino games and `#gColonyDetail` are still laid out for a ~700px pane. That is a per surface pass and should be measured against real use before it is done wholesale.
- Changed: client/assets/mobile.css, client/assets/mobile.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/mobile-check.mjs (new), docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.2.5 changes (Hotfix: lore editor unreachable on an empty book; CLIENT)
- ROOT CAUSE, lore-book.js render(): `if (!pages.length) { right.innerHTML = empty-message } else { renderPage(right) }`. renderPage is the ONLY path that delegates to renderEditor. On an empty book the editor branch was unreachable, so NEW PAGE set `editing` and render() overwrote the editor with the empty-book message. Silent, no console error.
- SCOPE: empty book only. Any book with >=1 page worked, which is why manual testing after seeding a page would not reproduce it. Every fresh install starts empty.
- FIX: `if (editing) { ...renderEditor } else if (!pages.length) {...} else {...}`. The editing branch also renders the index so the page list stays visible while writing.
- WHY THE 38 EXISTING CHECKS MISSED IT: all static regex over source. They can prove a route is gated or a string is escaped; they cannot prove a click sequence produces a result. tools/lore-check.mjs 38 -> 48 with a jsdom block that boots the real module, mounts, opens, clicks NEW PAGE on an EMPTY page list, and asserts the editor exists and the empty message is gone; repeats with a page present including a full type-and-save round trip asserting the POST body carries the token and the typed title; and asserts a non-dev gets no controls at all.
- JSDOM GOTCHA: document.readyState is 'loading' when the module is eval'd, so it registers DOMContentLoaded and mounts a beat later. The check polls for #loreBtn (waitFor helper) rather than asserting on the next tick.
- A CHECK THAT WAS PASSING FOR THE WRONG REASON: 'no innerHTML is fed a raw page field' used /innerHTML...p.(title|body)(?![^;]*esc)/ i.e. it looked for esc AFTER the field. Escaping is esc(p.title), i.e. BEFORE. It reported clean regardless of the code. Replaced with a scan that checks the 5 characters preceding every p./e. field use for `esc(`. REGRESSION TESTED: unescaping one title makes it fail with the offset.
- Changed: client/assets/lore-book.js, client/version.json, tools/lore-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.2.4 changes (Lore Events book; dev-authored world record; SERVER + CLIENT)
- SCHEMA: new table lore_pages (id, title, body, author, author_name, created_at, updated_at, sort, published) + idx_lore_sort, created in db_city.js initCityDb. Additive; rollback leaves it unread.
- SERVER: 4 routes. GET /api/lore (open; returns dev flag and, for devs only, unpublished pages). POST /api/lore, PUT /api/lore/:id, DELETE /api/lore/:id (all 401 unauthenticated, 403 non-dev). All writes broadcast {type:'lore_update'}.
- SANITISING: loreLabel() is a NEW module-scope helper. The city handlers' cleanLabel is declared inside the ws message closure at server.js:~9747 and is NOT reachable from an express route; using it returned 500 on every write. isTextClean IS module scope and hoists, so bodies use it directly. LORE_TITLE_MAX 90, LORE_BODY_MAX 12000. updateLorePage takes LORE_COLS allowlist {title, body, sort, published}.
- CLIENT: assets/lore-book.js + lore-book.css, both linked from index.html. Button mounts into #eod-timer-wrap (retries every 400ms until it exists, since the module loads before that markup in some paths). Modal renders index left / entry right.
- ANIMATION: Book_Sheet.png is 1024x1024 = 4x4 grid of 256px cells, 15 frames (cell 15 empty). Stepped by background-position at 40ms/frame. One request instead of fifteen.
- TOKEN GOTCHA: the canonical global is window.__fmToken (set in core.js:21). Initial implementation guessed window.MY_TOKEN / window._token / window.ME.token, none of which exist; every write would have been unauthenticated. Live-refresh listens on document 'fm_ws_msg' (core.js re-emits every socket frame there), NOT a custom event.
- XSS: body is esc()'d then only \n -> <br>; titles, authors and editor field values all esc()'d. A stored '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>' title is accepted by the API and rendered as text. Escaping at render is the correct layer; lore-check asserts every page field that reaches innerHTML goes through esc().
- FONT, THE REAL TRAP: Anti_kvak.ttf is upm 16, 774 codepoints, Latin + Cyrillic + a handful of stray CJK-block codepoints. VERIFIED MISSING: 血 (0x8840), 玉 (0x7389), 市 (0x5e02). The game's own name is not in the font. --lore-font therefore stacks 'Antikvak', 'Noto Serif SC', 'Songti SC', 'SimSun', serif. Without the Han fallback the entire book is tofu in zh. lore-check asserts the stack leads with Antikvak AND contains a Han face AND ends in a generic.
- ASSETS ADDED: client/assets/fonts/Anti_kvak.ttf; client/assets/ui/book/{Book_Sheet,Book_Open,Book_Close,book_icon}.png. book_icon.png is the closed-book frame cropped to its bbox and downscaled to 64x64 (nearest) for the header.
- i18n: 20 new keys, en+zh. lore-check independently verifies every T()/TF() key used in the module exists AND has a non-empty Han zh value.
- tools/lore-check.mjs NEW, 38 checks, no dependencies. GOTCHA for the next person: route slices must be cut at the NEXT app.<verb> call, not by a fixed character window; a 1800-char window from the GET route ran into the POST route and read its 403 as the GET's.
- LIVE VERIFICATION against a running server: player GET 200 / POST 403; no token 401; dev POST/PUT/DELETE 200; empty title 400; unknown field 400; unpublished page visible to dev (2 pages) and not to player (1 page).
- Changed: server/server.js, server/db_city.js, client/index.html, client/assets/lore-book.js (new), client/assets/lore-book.css (new), client/assets/core.js, client/assets/fonts/Anti_kvak.ttf (new), client/assets/ui/book/*.png (new), client/version.json, tools/lore-check.mjs (new), docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.2.3 changes (Circuit hulls on the passage; scan is sensor-range; passage state persisted; SERVER)
- HULL FAMILY: poolForLane used `isJadeWorld(from) && isJadeWorld(to)` -> JADE_POOL. The passage has ONE jade end, so it fell through to MERCHANT_POOL and the gate was flown by Coalition hulls. Changed to `||`. A run with either end in the Circuit is Circuit freight.
- SECTOR TAG: shipGalaxy returned 'jade' or 'coalition'; a crossing has one end in each and was therefore hidden from one side. New third value 'both', and tagShipGalaxy shows it in either view. New isPassageRun(from,to) = isJadeWorld(from) !== isJadeWorld(to).
- GEOMETRY: the two colonies sit in different views and share an overlapping coordinate space, so a straight line between them would render the ship crossing empty map. New passageEndpoints(from,to) substitutes portalConfig() coords for the far end, so each side draws anchor-to-gate. Applied in spawnServerShip before the angle is computed.
- TRAFFIC WEIGHT: new PASSAGE_TRAFFIC_WEIGHT (env-tunable, default 16) against ~127 total weight across the other 63 lanes = 11.2%, ~1 spawn in 8.9. MEASURED BEFORE THE CHANGE: 113 fleet samples produced 0 crossings at ordinary weight. MEASURED AFTER, live: crossings present while open, and 0 NEW crossings spawned while sealed (one already-in-flight crossing completed, which is correct and is distinguished by startTs in the probe).
- SCAN RULE REFRAMED. Was: Circuit hulls file with the Circuit, so no manifest exists (jurisdiction). Now: Flesh Station is in the Coalition cluster and its sensors reach to the gate (range). Consequence: a Changzheng hull on this side of the passage IS readable; only a run wholly inside the Circuit refuses. The predicate is unchanged in code (shipGalaxy(...)==='jade'), but 'both' now falls outside it, which is what makes crossings scannable. i18n galx.scanCircuit reworded en+zh: 'beyond the passage, outside station sensor range'.
- PERSISTENCE: WORMHOLE_KEY='wormhole_open' in city_kv. setCityKV on every /api/dev/wormhole call; restored at boot AFTER db.js has run initCityDb (db.js:422), read at server.js:~2533. Absent = never set = open. Boot log now prints 'passage OPEN|SEALED'.
- tools/lane-check.mjs 38 -> 52. Weight-band assertion computes the share from the real lane table rather than asserting a number. Plus hull family, both-tagging, anchor-to-gate, scan predicate, refusal wording, and 4 persistence assertions.
- tools/fleet-check.mjs 55 -> 56, one assertion CORRECTED not added: 'the refusal names the Circuit, not an unregistered hull' encoded the old jurisdiction fiction and failed on this build. Replaced with sensor-range + names-the-passage. GOTCHA for the next person: the lane-check slice for the scan block must target `window.openShipManifest = function`, not the first occurrence of `window.openShipManifest`, which is a call site ~3500 lines earlier.
- TUTORIAL: Circuit slide extended en+zh with the hull family arriving at Cascade Station and the sensor-range explanation of why an internal Circuit run refuses the scan.
- Changed: server/server.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/tutorial.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/lane-check.mjs, tools/fleet-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.2.2 changes (Passage becomes a real lane; cross-border trade; SERVER)
- MODEL CORRECTION. 1.2.1 assumed the border was absolute and gated Circuit markets on WORMHOLE_OPEN. That was half right: the seal was correct, but with 0 cross-border lanes there was no crossing to seal, so cargo could NEVER move between sectors in either state. Intended design is a single gated lane.
- LANE ADDED, both tables: {from:'cascade_station', to:'mozi_array', vol:'medium', type:'passage', passage:true}. LANES_SERVER 63 -> 64, client LANES 63 -> 64, parity maintained (lane-check enforces).
- GATE AT THE CHOKE POINT. findLane() returns undefined for a passage lane while !WORMHOLE_OPEN. Every consumer (findRoute direct hit, cargo quote, smuggling_start, shipping_start, share_buy, share_swap, blockade_fund, getLaneVol) inherits the gate without its own check. findRoute's BFS adjacency and npcPickLane's weighted table each skip the edge separately, since both build their own structures from LANES_SERVER.
- NOT PROPERTY: isPassageLane() rejects in share_buy, share_swap and blockade_fund. Counted in the checks (expects exactly 3) because gating 2 of 3 is worse than gating 0.
- CLIENT RENDER. Endpoints are in different galaxies so renderLanes() cannot draw it; it early-returns on laneIsPassage. New renderPassageLink() draws an animated dashed line from PASSAGE_ANCHOR[activeGalaxy] (cascade_station / mozi_array) to portalConfig() coords, stopping short of the gate sprite, in LANE_COLOR.passage #7fe3a0, with a PASSAGE label (i18n galx.passageLane en+zh). Returns early when sealed: absent, not dimmed. Called from renderPortal AND at the end of renderLanes, because renderLanes clears gLanes and the link lives in that layer.
- ALSO EXCLUDED CLIENT-SIDE: pickScoundrelLane and the decorative traffic weighting both skip the passage. A decorative ship tweened between two colonies in different views would appear to fly out of empty space.
- LIVE VERIFICATION, /api/cargo/quote (the real findRoute consumer) across the full cycle: OPEN -> cascade_station->mozi_array 1 hop, new_anchor->yujing 3 hops; SEALED -> both no_lane, while new_anchor->cascade_station and yujing->tiangong still route; REOPENED -> restored. Grid Circuit colony count 16 -> 0 -> 16 in the same run.
- WORMHOLE_OPEN still defaults true at boot (server.js:2437) and is NOT persisted. Sealing does not survive a restart. Left as-is per instruction that it should default open; persisting to city_kv remains a ~10 line change if that ever needs to survive a deploy.
- TUTORIAL, wrong twice, both mine. 1.2.0 omitted the Circuit. 1.2.1 asserted "no cargo is ever hauled between the two galaxies", which wrote my own misreading into the game's explanation of itself. Now states: exactly one lane, Cascade Station and Mozi Array named, opens/closes on the Circuit's word, absent when sealed, not purchasable as a share, not blockadeable. EN + ZH + callouts.
- tools/lane-check.mjs 22 -> 38. INVERTED the two border assertions (was: no lane crosses, Circuit is its own component). Now: exactly one crosser, it is flagged passage, it runs cascade_station|mozi_array, no other crosser, Circuit reachable from Coalition when open, and a passage-free graph rebuild parts the sectors again while leaving the Circuit internally whole. Plus 11 coverage assertions across server and client render paths.
- tools/tutorial-check.mjs 19 -> 22. Cross-checks the slide against the lane table: if a passage lane exists, the tutorial may not claim cargo cannot cross. Catches both prior versions of the slide.
- Changed: server/server.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/tutorial.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/lane-check.mjs, tools/tutorial-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.2.1 changes (Jade passage gates commodities, freight and runs; SERVER)
- ROOT CAUSE: WORMHOLE_OPEN was referenced in only 2 enforcement sites, server.js:7054 (init ticker filter) and :7189 (stock order reject). The commodity layer never consulted it, so /api/commodities-grid listed all 16 Circuit colonies, /api/commodities/:colonyId priced them, and buy/sell filled at them regardless of the gate. Same for smuggling_start and shipping_start.
- FIX: new predicate jadeSealed(colonyId) = !WORMHOLE_OPEN && isJadeColony(colonyId), server.js:2460. Enforcement added at: commodities-grid (filters colonies), /api/commodities/:colonyId (403 passage_sealed), /api/commodities/buy (403), /api/commodities/sell (403), smuggling_start (error), shipping_start (error). 7 references total.
- COVERAGE VERIFIED: isJadeColony reads CITY_COLONIES[id].jade; cross-checked against the 16 faction:'jade' rows in db.js seed. Exact match, no world uncovered.
- SEMANTICS: sealed removes Circuit colonies from the board entirely rather than disabling them. Cargo already parked at a Circuit colony is frozen, not destroyed, and becomes sellable on reopen. In-flight runs complete, matching the COMMODITIES_OPEN precedent.
- CLIENT: window._setWormhole (galaxy.js:11) previously only repainted the map. Now also re-renders gMarketsInner via renderMarketsTab() and gShippingInner via window.renderShippingTab() when visible. NOTE: renderShippingTab is a window property, not an in-scope binding inside the galaxy IIFE; a bare reference throws.

## 1.6.3.0 changes (Corruption skims the bill; siege stores; NPC churn; migration rehearsed; SERVER)
- CORRUPTION -> CIVIC BILL. civicBill split into civicBillBase (unchanged formula) and civicBill = base * (1 + billSkim(d)). billSkim = BILL_SKIM_MAX(0.45) * max(0, s_corruption - BILL_SKIM_FLOOR(20)) / (SCALAR_MAX - FLOOR). Checked monotonic across 0..100 so a mayor can never make the bill cheaper by getting more corrupt. districtSummary exposes billBase and skim; the panel itemises them.
- SIEGE STORES. Three additive REAL columns on city_districts (stock_food/med/tech), guarded ALTER, added to DISTRICT_COLS allowlist. New msg city_stock {colonyId, district, cls, weeks}; mayor only, blocked by arrears, capped at stockCapacity = stockWeekUnits * STOCK_MAX_WK(6), priced at STOCK_UNIT_COST(240k)/unit.
- UNITS, the trap: demand and local in civicPressure are RATES (per week); a stockpile is a QUANTITY. First cut did `local += stockOf(...)`, so 6 weeks of cover read as 6 weeks of production EVERY week and took a besieged unzoned world from pressure +2.50 to -2.50. FIX: `local += min(held, max(0, demand - local))`. Full cover now lands a siege at exactly 0.00.
- SCOPE, the second trap: stockWeekUnits is a DISTRICT's share (colony demand / districtCount) and is what sizes and prices a purchase; cover has to be REPORTED against colonyWeekUnits (whole colony demand). Reporting one in the other's units showed 84 weeks of cover for what was really 6. New export colonyWeekUnits; cityFullData.stock uses perDistrict for weekCost and perColony for weeks.
- DRAWDOWN. drawStock(colonyId, dtWeeks) runs once per COLONY per tick, not per district, because the shortfall cover meets is a colony-level number. factor = (1 - eaten/held) * (1 - spoil) where eaten = min(held, shortfallUnits*dt) and spoil = STOCK_DECAY_WK(0.14)*dt. New export shortfallUnits(colonyId, cls) = max(0, demand - local) ignoring stores. A colony that is not short loses only spoilage. Full stores under permanent siege last ~3 weeks, not 6, because spoilage compounds with consumption.
- NPC CHURN. churnNpcShops(colonyId, d, dtWeeks) called from the tick immediately before ensureNpcShops, so it is a replacement rate not a drain. rate = (CHURN_BASE_WK 0.010 + CHURN_STRESS_WK 0.055 * stress) * dt, stress = unrest*0.6 + crime*0.4 normalised. Fractional expectation resolved by a coin flip so a thin district still turns over occasionally. Oldest first. Floor of 4 firms. Single-tick cap of 25% of the district. MEASURED: 0.51 firms/wk of 40 calm, 2.48 at unrest+crime 95.
- MIGRATION REHEARSAL, first time this has been done. Built a database on the 1.6.0.1 schema (275 districts, 25236 shops), wrote mayors/arrears/invested/works/player-shops into it on the OLD schema, then booted this build. Result: all 5 added district columns present with defaults, city_history/city_petitions/city_kv created, all rows preserved, charter written for the first time, forced 3h settlement clean. 8 simulated days: shop count 25468/25486/25478/25478 -> equilibrium, no drift.
- PERF ON REAL DATA: commodity coupling 13ms per 5-min tick (35 colonies x 3 classes); full city tick 413ms hourly (275 districts, step+churn+stores); resolveDistrictShops across every city 319ms / 42043 rows, and the heavy bucket caps one socket at 3/s.
- ROLLBACK: additive only. Reverting to an older build leaves the extra columns and tables in place, unread. No down-migration needed.
- tools/city-check.mjs 64 -> 84. Two of the new blocks failed on first run and BOTH were real: the stores unit error above, and the cover-units mismatch. The churn check also failed first run and was itself wrong (drained without refounding, so the worse district hit the floor first and looked healthier); it now refounds between weeks as the tick does.
- Changed: server/server.js, server/city.js, server/db_city.js, server/ratelimit.js, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/city-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.
