## 1.6.2.0 changes (City/commodity coupling, legitimacy, petitions, charter, history; SERVER)
- CITY -> COMMODITY COUPLING. server/city.js had zero references to COMMODITIES or getCommodityPrice. COMMODITIES is 120 goods in exactly three classes (40 agri, 40 med, 40 tech); CITY_KINDS is export/food/med/tech. agri<->food, med<->med, tech<->tech, export unmapped by design. New exports civicPressure(colonyId, cls, blk) and civicCommodityNudge(...). Pressure = clamp((demand - local)/demand, -1, 1) * (1 + COMMODITY_BLOCKADE_AMP*blk), signed: positive imports, negative exports. demand = population * {food .35, med .16, tech .10}; local sums favoured districts as (dev*SUPPLY_PER_DEV + worksLv*WORKS_SUPPLY) * outMult * popPerDistrict.
- APPLIED INSIDE tickCommodityPrices, NOT the city tick. Steady state supply is nudge/decay, so applying an hourly nudge to a 5-minute decay would silently resize the effect ~10x. Computed once per colony per class (57 calls / 5 min across 19 city colonies), then applied to each commodity of the matching cls alongside the existing decay line.
- SIZING, SIMULATED FIRST: tools/city-commodity-sim.mjs (new), 200 runs x 4032 ticks. The model is bounded (supplyMod clamps +/-0.4, price clamps [0.5x,1.8x] of target), so the failure mode is FLATTENING not runaway. Measured: DRAW 0.004 -> 22% colony spread (too weak); 0.008 -> 47% spread, 0 clamp; 0.012 -> 79% spread, 0 clamp; 0.020 -> 134% spread but 98% clamp occupancy on 3 of 6 colonies. SHIPPED COMMODITY_DRAW 0.008, COMMODITY_BLOCKADE_AMP 1.5. Peacetime worst-case steady-state supply 0.200 against a 0.400 clamp; a siege reaches 0.500 and clamps, which is deliberate.
- SEAT PRICING READS LEGITIMACY. seatLegitimacyMult(d) = 1 + SEAT_LEGIT_SWING(0.60) * (legit-50)/50, i.e. 0.4x..1.6x. Applied to seatBasePrice ONLY; the invested term still passes through at face value, preserving the invariant that seatCompensation (0.75 * invested) can never exceed what was spent. Checked at legitimacy 5/25/50/75/95.
- PETITIONS. New msg city_petition. Requires: a shop in that district owned by the actor, at least one of those shops older than PETITION_MIN_AGE_MS (6wk, half the ramp), and no filing by that player in that district within PETITION_COOLDOWN_MS (24h, persisted in the new city_petitions table so it survives restart). Hit = PETITION_HIT(6) * min(1, 0.35 + stake*2) where stake is the actor's share of district gross. Writes s_legitimacy directly; stepDistrict's RELAX 0.35/wk then decays it, so a single filing washes out and only sustained filing moves a district. Notifies the mayor via city_petitioned.
- PLAYER DRAW. resolveDistrictShops multiplies the pool by 1 + PLAYER_DRAW_MAX(0.35) * (1 - exp(-distinctOwners / PLAYER_DRAW_K(3.0))). DISTINCT owners, not shop count: keying on count would hand the whole bonus to one player stacking units, which is the monopoly this is meant to discourage. Measured ceiling 1.344x at 12 distinct owners. Note: this does move NPC buyout prices, but only upward and only by leasing real frontage, so it is not a free reversible lever the way cut and favoured were.
- CHARTER. recomputeCharter(colonyId) on every city tick: highest sum of (invested + works*WORKS_WAR_WEIGHT) across districts that player mayors there, must clear CHARTER_MIN_BOOK (F50M). Writes charter_owner and prev_charter via new setCharterOwner. THIS IS THE FIRST CODE THAT HAS EVER WRITTEN THAT COLUMN: server.js:~10654 reads it in the conquest path to spare a city already held by one of the capturing faction's own, and with it permanently null that branch could never fire and every capture always seized everything.
- HISTORY. New city_history table (colony_id, district, ts, kind, actor, detail), trimmed to 40 rows PER PLACE inside pushCityHistory so a busy capital cannot evict a quiet district's record. Logged on: seat taken, unseated, invest, works, petition, charter change. Exposed as cityFullData.history (24 most recent, colony-wide) and rendered in a new client panel.
- cityFullData ALSO NOW CARRIES: charter / charterName / prevCharter, and civic {food, med, tech} as signed pressure so the panel can say imports/exports/balanced. Client renders both in the charter box.
- i18n: 13 new keys, en+zh. i18n-check 6/6. NOTE, gotcha: city.civicNote contains an apostrophe, so both the core.js entry and the city.js fallback use double-quoted strings; single-quoting it fails node --check.
- ratelimit.js: city_petition added to HEAVY_TYPES.
- tools/city-check.mjs: 29 -> 57. THREE NEW CHECKS FAILED ON FIRST RUN, all correctly. Two exposed the fixture seeding 2 districts on a colony whose population implies 14 while popPerDistrict divides by 14 regardless, making every per-district supply figure wrong by 7x. The third was measuring total gross after adding shops and attributing the growth to the player bonus; it now holds shop count fixed and only reassigns owners.
- Changed: server/server.js, server/city.js, server/db_city.js, server/ratelimit.js, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/city-check.mjs, tools/city-commodity-sim.mjs (new), docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.6.1.0 changes (City audit: tick clock, buyout pricing, WS rate limit; SERVER)
- TICK CLOCK. runCityTick used `const dtWeeks = CITY_TICK_MS / CITY_TUNE.WEEK_MS`, a constant, and `runCityTick()` ran at module load with no guard. Every process start credited one full CITY_TICK_MS of creditCityIncome + stepDistrict + stepColonyPopulation regardless of real elapsed time; every outage credited exactly one tick however long it lasted. FIX: new pure export cityTickAdvance(lastTick, now, tickMs) -> {skip, reset, dtWeeks, applyTo, elapsedMs, capped}. Clock persisted in the EXISTING city_kv table under key 'tick_at' (no new table). TICK_MIN_MS 60s below which the tick is a no-op; TICK_CATCHUP_MAX_MS 24h per tick with the remainder carried via applyTo, so a long outage converges over successive ticks instead of one payout. reset covers both an absent clock and a clock in the future (restored older db, system time moved back): resync, pay nothing. setCityKV(applyTo) happens AFTER creditCityIncome returns, so a throw leaves the window unpaid and the next tick re-settles it rather than skipping it.
- BUYOUT PRICING. shopBuyoutCost = shopLeaseCost + max(0, weeklyNet) * NPC_BUYOUT_WEEKS(20), and weeklyNet came from a LIVE resolveDistrictShops. Two of that call's inputs are mayor-writable and take effect on the next read with no cooldown: commerce_cut (CUT_MIN 0.05 to CUT_MAX 0.25) and favoured (FAVOUR_BONUS +0.35 / FAVOUR_PENALTY -0.12). Set cut=MAX and favoured=<other kind>, buy, revert. MEASURED by tools/city-check.mjs against the real pricing path: 25.0% off an unfavoured storefront, 44.4% off one in the favoured trade. FIX: resolveDistrictShops(colonyId, d, blockadeLevel, opts) takes opts.neutral, which forces cut=CUT_DEFAULT and favoured=null. New export quoteShopBuyout(colonyId, d, shopId, blk) is the only sanctioned quote. city_buy_shop calls it; cityFullData now runs TWO resolutions per district (live for displayed gross/net, neutral for the buyout figure) so the panel shows the number the handler will charge.
- LEVER COOLDOWN. CITY_TUNE.LEVER_COOLDOWN_MS 1h. New columns city_districts.cut_at / fav_at via the established try-wrapped ALTER. Belt and braces: pricing no longer reads either lever, this stops the whipsaw itself. A no-op write (same value) does NOT start the clock, or a UI that echoes the current value on open would lock the mayor out of their own lever. vacateDistrict clears both stamps.
- WS RATE LIMIT. server/ratelimit.js (new). Two token buckets per socket, refilled by elapsed time so an idle connection keeps its burst: global RATE 30/s BURST 90; HEAVY_RATE 3/s HEAVY_BURST 12 over HEAVY_TYPES = the four snapshot/request types plus all eleven city mutations (each ends in cityFullData + cityBroadcast, so the cost is not paid by the sender). A heavy message spends from BOTH buckets. EXEMPT_TYPES = {ping}: throttling a keepalive reads as a dead connection to the health check. On refusal the frame is dropped, never the socket, and at most one {type:'rate_limited'} notice per NOTICE_MS 5000 goes back. Client: city.js toasts it, key city.rateLimited en+zh. Context: this database carries 25,236 established businesses, and city_data_request resolves every one in the colony through an 8-pass spill loop.
- ARREARS SPENDING BLOCK. creditCityIncome shortfall path takes min(cash, owed) and banks the rest, so wealth held outside `cash` was untouchable and a rich mayor could coast to the ARREARS_LAPSE_WK(4) lapse, which zeroes arrears. New cityInArrears(pid)/blockedByArrears() gate city_buy_seat, city_invest, city_works, city_lease_shop and city_buy_shop while ANY district that player holds is in arrears. Uses existing state, no schema change.
- CONTESTED GATE. cityContested() existed at server.js:9535 and was used ONLY to populate a display flag in cityFullData; no handler read it. Now blocks city_buy_seat. Deliberately does NOT block commerce: a world under a conquest timer keeps trading, it just does not hand over charters.
- OCCUPATION CONSISTENCY. cityCtx and city_buy_shop refused on st.locked_faction; city_rename_shop and city_close_shop did not. Both now do.
- updateDistrict ALLOWLIST. It built `SET ${keys.map(k=>k+'=?')}` from Object.keys(fields) with no filter, i.e. identifier injection if a key ever came from a message. No call site does. DISTRICT_COLS set added; unknown keys are dropped.
- AUDITED AND FOUND SOUND, recorded so it is not re-audited: seat buy/sell cycling destroys money at every step (SEAT_COMPENSATION 0.75 of invested, seatPrice = base + invested, so a collusion loop loses seatBase + 0.25*invested per cycle); ensureNpcShops computes want as target-players so buying out an NPC does not respawn one, no treadmill; the faction gate on seats is backed by a real 30-day allegiance lock; getPlayer is a fresh row read per message so there is no stale-object TOCTOU across sockets; cityIndex refuses non-integers rather than coercing; setDistrictLevers clamps 0..100; city_set_cut type-checks before clamping; all player text goes through the three-pass isTextClean.
- tools/city-check.mjs NEW, 29 checks, in-memory node:sqlite against the real db_city/city modules, no mocks of anything under test. Run: `node --experimental-sqlite tools/city-check.mjs`.
- Changed: server/server.js, server/city.js, server/db_city.js, server/ratelimit.js (new), client/assets/city.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/city-check.mjs (new), docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.6.0.5 changes (Flesh Station deep-scan refuses Circuit hulls; CLIENT)
- window.openShipManifest now computes fromId/toId/typeKey BEFORE injectManifestModal and early-returns when window._fmFleet.shipGalaxy(fromId,toId)==='jade'. Emits gToast with the jade tone #e8e4d8 and leaves the panel unopened. Modal injection moved below the guard so a refused click does not build DOM it will not use.
- IDENT: the refusal reuses generateManifest(fromId,toId,typeKey,seed).ident with the same seed formula the panel uses, so the vessel number in the refusal matches the one the panel would have shown for that ship at that progress. Class comes from FLEET_HULLS[ship.hullKey].n.
- LANE KEYED, NOT HULL KEYED. shipGalaxy() returns 'jade' if either endpoint is a Circuit world, so a scoundrel on a Circuit lane refuses under the Circuit line and a Changzheng hull on a hypothetical Coalition lane would scan normally. Same predicate as the 1.6.0.4 data-gx tag. Rationale: one border rule beats a nine entry silhouette list, and it means no two identical hulls ever behave differently on click, which was the objection raised against per hull behaviour in 1.6.0.2.
- REVERSES a 1.6.0.2 design point ("Circuit hulls open the transit log like any other merchant"). The objection behind it was that identical silhouettes must not diverge on click; that does not apply here because the predicate is the lane and Changzheng hulls only fly Circuit lanes.
- NOT gated on WORMHOLE_OPEN. Unconditional as asked. Gating is one condition on the branch if the passage opening should confer scan rights.
- I18N: new key galx.scanCircuit, en + zh, tokens {id} and {cls} through tf(). zh uses 玉环 for the Circuit, consistent with core.js FACTION_ZH and the changzheng_yards colony text. i18n-check 6/6.
- HARNESS FIX, worth its own line: fleet-check stubbed window.t as (k,fb)=>fb and had NO window.tf, so every tf() call in galaxy.js fell through to the raw fallback and the refusal assertions passed against a string still containing literal {id} and {cls}. The stub is now a real interpolating tf. It failed the new hull-name assertion on first run, which is the point.
- tools/fleet-check.mjs: 52 -> 55, with one INVERTED. The Circuit end to end block previously asserted 'and it opens the transit log like any other merchant, not a refusal'; that is now the failure condition. Replaced with refused-by-deep-scan, refusal-names-the-Circuit-not-an-unregistered-hull, refusal-names-the-drawn-hull (regex /CZ-\d/), log-stays-shut, plus scan-rule-keys-off-the-lane.
- Changed: client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/fleet-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.6.0.4 changes (Ship layer hidden wholesale on sector swap; CLIENT)
- ROOT CAUSE, galaxy.js swapGalaxy(): `if(ships) ships.style.display = (activeGalaxy==='coalition') ? '' : 'none';`. One line. COLONY_META places both sectors in overlapping map coordinates (jade bbox x110-910 y110-610, non-jade bbox x80-960 y22-870) and every ship from both sectors renders into the single #gShips layer, so the layer could not be filtered by hiding it.
- SYMPTOM PAIR, one cause: Circuit view hid all hulls including Circuit freight ("no ship trade in Jade sector"); Coalition view showed all hulls including Circuit freight, drawn at Circuit coordinates that overlap Coalition space ("Chinese ships populating in coalition"). The line predates Circuit freight and was correct while no ship on the map belonged to the Circuit.
- FIX: shipGalaxy(from,to) returns 'jade' if either endpoint is a Circuit world. tagShipGalaxy(grp,from,to) sets data-gx and the group's initial display, called from all three spawn sites (spawnShip 1972, spawnServerShip 2043, spawnAmbient 2153). applyShipGalaxyFilter() replaces the layer toggle in swapGalaxy: layer.style.display='' always, then per child display by data-gx against activeGalaxy. Hidden groups are still ticked by positionShip so they are in the right place on swap back, and display:none removes them from hit testing so the manifest panel cannot be opened on an off-sector hull.
- SCOPE NOTE: activeGalaxy is declared at galaxy.js:8 in the outer IIFE (5-4579) and the spawn functions live in the nested IIFE (1627-2352), so they read the live binding by closure. No window handoff needed, unlike FLEET_HULLS which crosses into the 4584-5837 IIFE.
- TAG IS DERIVED FROM THE LANE, not the hull art. A Coalition hull on a Circuit lane would still be filed under the Circuit and hidden in Coalition view. Correct: sector membership is a property of where the ship is, not what it looks like.
- NEW EXPORT: window._fmGalaxyView = { get, apply, swap } so checks can drive the sector swap without clicking the portal element.
- ERRATUM against my 1.6.0.3 hand off: I asserted the shipped code could not produce this and supported it with 1200 clean spawns across both lane families. The spawns were clean and irrelevant. Hull selection was never the fault; I exercised the subsystem I had touched and treated a pass there as coverage of the render path, which had not been examined at all.
- tools/fleet-check.mjs: 43 -> 52. New: every-hull-tagged, tag-derived-from-lane-not-art, layer-never-hidden-wholesale (per view), each-view-shows-traffic, each-view-shows-nothing-from-the-other-sector.
- Changed: client/assets/galaxy.js, client/version.json, tools/fleet-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.6.0.3 changes (LANES_SERVER was missing all 26 Circuit lanes; SERVER)
- ROOT CAUSE: server/server.js LANES_SERVER held 37 lanes, exactly the 37 non-Circuit ones. The 26 Circuit lanes were added to client/assets/galaxy.js LANES in 1.5.0.0 and never mirrored. The header comment on LANES_SERVER says "mirror of the client LANES array" and nothing enforced it.
- BLAST RADIUS, all silent: npcPickLane() weights over LANES_SERVER so no NPC freighter could ever be routed into the Circuit (Circuit share of spawn weight was 0.0%); findLane() returns undefined for any Circuit pair so smuggling_start answered 'No lane exists', which reads as a design rule rather than a fault; findRoute()'s BFS builds adjacency from LANES_SERVER so no Circuit world was reachable from anywhere, including other Circuit worlds; the shipping contract board resolves lanes the same way so no contract was ever written against a Circuit spread; the blockade hooks at the bottom of server.js filter LANES_SERVER by colony and matched nothing for Circuit colonies.
- NOT the cause, checked: Circuit colonies are seeded in db.js with faction:'jade' (1.5.0.0), none is in NO_MARKET_COLONIES ({flesh_station, abaddon}), isMarketColony passes for all 16, and tickCommodityPrices loops getAllColonyStates() so all 16 have had commodity_prices rows the whole time. Markets were live. Only transport was missing.
- FIX: 26 lanes copied verbatim from client LANES into LANES_SERVER. Diffed both ways: 26 added, 0 server-only lanes, 0 vol/type drift on the 37 shared. LANES_SERVER now 63, matching client exactly. Circuit share of npcPickLane weight 42.5%.
- CONSEQUENCE, stated: NPC freight, player smuggling runs, multi-hop routing and the contract board all switch on together across 16 colonies whose prices have drifted with no freight flow since 1.5.0.0. Expect volatility for the first hours while NPC flow drags them toward equilibrium. No migration, nothing retroactive.
- ERRATUM against 1.6.0.2: that entry's "42.5% of server NPC freighter traffic flies Circuit lanes" was measured off the CLIENT LANES array and asserted about the server. Server figure was 0%. Its "SERVER UNTOUCHED" claim is also wrong: the pool selection it shipped is inert without this change.
- tools/lane-check.mjs NEW, 13 checks, zero dependencies (parses both tables out of source, no jsdom). Table parity: client-lane-exists-on-server, no-server-only-lane, vol/type-agreement, no-duplicate-under-either-direction. Consequence checks: every-endpoint-has-a-colony-row, no-Circuit-world-in-NO_MARKET_COLONIES, all-16-reachable-from-yujing, no-lane-crosses-the-border, Circuit-is-its-own-component, Circuit-can-win-a-spawn-roll. Regression tested: deleting the 26 lanes again fails it three ways.
- Changed: server/server.js, client/version.json, tools/lane-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.6.0.2 changes (Circuit hull family; Circuit freight; ventral plume; plume scale; CLIENT)
- LANE GRAPH, MEASURED: 63 lanes. 26 have COLONY_META[x].galaxy==='jade' at both ends, 37 have it at neither, 0 have it at one. There is no Circuit/Coalition border crossing and there never was, so the reported "coalition ships in the jade sector" was never a routing fault. Server NPC lane weight puts 42.5% of freighter traffic on Circuit lanes, and all of it was painted from MERCHANT_POOL, which carried no faction data.
- HULL REGISTRY RENAME: the nine role:'verbattan' entries become role:'merchant', faction:'jade', keys sanban / shachuan / fuchuan / guangchuan / caochuan / xingcha / louchuan / changfeng / baochuan, names CZ-1..CZ-9. Hull descriptors moved from warship types (Corvette, Frigate, Destroyer, Cruiser, Carrier) to Circuit merchant descriptors, because a hull that files a manifest should not be labelled a destroyer in the log.
- FILENAMES UNCHANGED. The art pack files keep their original names; hullSrc now resolves FLEET_HULLS[key].f instead of the key. f existed and was dead before this. Zero asset churn, zero re-cut, and fleet-check resolves the same way or it goes looking for sanban_map.png.
- POOL SELECTION: new JADE_POOL {v1:[sanban,shachuan,fuchuan], v2:[guangchuan,caochuan,xingcha], v3:[louchuan,changfeng,baochuan]}, split by mw to line up with the Coalition buckets. poolForLane(from,to,variant) returns JADE_POOL when isJadeWorld holds at both ends, MERCHANT_POOL otherwise. Called from spawnServerShip (seeded on npc.id, so the hull survives the 8s reconcile) and spawnShip. SERVER UNTOUCHED: it still only emits v1/v2/v3 and knows nothing about factions.
- AMBIENT FLEET IS SCOUNDRELS ONLY. Removed VERBATTAN_POOL, VERBATTAN_STRAY, pickVerbattanLane, the verbattan branches in spawnAmbient / ambientIdent / scheduleAmbient, and the verbattan branch of window.openAmbientReadout. The ship.stray flag is gone with it. LORE COST, DELIBERATE: no Circuit navy, no stray sighting over Coalition space, no OBSERVED OUTSIDE CIRCUIT SPACE state. Reinstating it needs a separate hull set, not a subset of these nine, or two identical silhouettes behave differently on click.
- I18N: removed galx.scanVerbattan, now unreferenced. galx.scanScoundrel unchanged. The nine class names render raw from FLEET_HULLS[k].n and have no zh forms; hanzi for whoever wires them: 舢板 沙船 福船 广船 漕船 星槎 楼船 长风 宝船. i18n-check 6/6, i18n-scope-check 0 undeclared.
- VENTRAL PLUME BUG: SHIP_TYPES.v3 declares thrustBot / thrustBotW 18 / thrustBotH 14 / thrustBotOffX 9 / thrustBotOffY 14, tuned for the retired 36x18 v3 sprite. defWithHull overwrote thrustW/thrustH/thrustOffX/thrustOffY and never touched the Bot fields, and positionShip writes them through verbatim. Rendered rect is x 9..27, y 14..28 on all three Class-3 hulls regardless of hull size: cicada 40x14 hangs 14px below, titans_burden 42x17 hangs 11px, titans_fist 38x9 begins 5px clear of the hull entirely. FIX: defWithHull deletes thrustBot and its four geometry fields.
- PLUME SCALE: tw was Math.max(5, round(mh*0.85)). Height-only gave astral_pioneer (26x17) 54% of hull length and scoundrel (20x15) 65%, against consular (46x9) at 17%. FIX: tw = max(5, round(min(mh*0.85, mw*0.35))), th = max(4, min(round(mh*0.72), tw)) so the flame is never taller than it is long. Measured range across all 20 hulls: 17% to 36%.
- FLIP HOOK: FLEET_HULLS entries may carry flip:true; defWithHull copies it to d.flip and positionShip folds it into the existing group scale as (goingLeft ? -1 : 1) * (def.flip ? -1 : 1). The plume mirrors with the hull because the whole group is scaled, so a nose-down source file is a one word fix rather than a re-cut. No hull sets it today. build-fleet-sprites.py rotates every source the same -90, so nose direction is a property of the source file and cannot be fixed globally in code.
- ART: client/assets/space/ships/fleet/titans_fist_detail.png, 36 px of #DCBE0A forming a dashed 18x18 outline at x 70..87 y 1..18, cleared to transparent. It traced a real seam (columns x=70 and x=87 are transparent for rows 5..18, so the pod is genuinely separated) and is therefore deliberate in the pack, but it is the only sprite in 40 with one and gold is the dev/Patreon tone. No pixels invented; the outline simply becomes seam. titans_fist_map.png unaffected, the box is sub-pixel at mh 9. Pack licence permits modification.
- tools/fleet-check.mjs: 31 -> 43. New: per-variant Circuit-lane-draws-only-Changzheng and Coalition-lane-draws-none, pool disjointness across three pools, every-hull-declares-f, nothing-renders-outside-the-hull-band (catches the ventral plume), no-plume-over-40%-of-hull, and an end to end block that spawns a yujing->tiangong v3, asserts the sprite is Circuit art, asserts the click opens the transit log rather than a refusal, and asserts real cargo. Sprite existence and size checks resolve through f. Removed the patrol distribution and VDF ident checks. Requires jsdom.
- NOT CHANGED, ship.sh: the divergence guard (fetch, compare HEAD / origin/main / merge-base, hard stop on behind or diverged) already covers what the plain `git pull --ff-only origin main` was meant to cover and is stronger, and a plain pull would abort regardless because apply.sh mirrors the zip over the tree and leaves it dirty by design.
- Changed: client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/space/ships/fleet/titans_fist_detail.png, client/version.json, tools/fleet-check.mjs, tools/build-fleet-sprites.py, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.6.0.1 changes (Thrust placement; dead ship clicks; gToast never exported; CLIENT)
- THRUST BUG: defWithHull set d.thrustOffX = -(hl.mw * 0.42). positionShip applies thrust x as an ABSOLUTE local coordinate (group origin is the hull's top left after the final translate(-w/2,-h/2)), so the stern is x=0 and the plume must END there. Scaling the offset off WIDTH meant nomad (30 wide) sat at -12.6 with an 8 wide plume, a 4.6px gap, and titans_burden (42 wide) at -17.6, a 9.6px gap. FIX: thrustW/thrustH derived from hull HEIGHT (0.85 / 0.72 of mh, floored at 5/4), thrustOffX = -(thrustW - 1). Verified x=-9 w=10 on a 12px hull, ending at +1.
- CLICK BUG: galaxy.js is three IIFEs (5-4554 with a nested 1627-2333, and 4559-5863). FLEET_HULLS, hullSrc and FLEET_DIR are declared in the ship traffic IIFE; openShipManifest, detailDefForHull and DETAIL_SPRITES are in another. Every FLEET_HULLS reference from the panel threw ReferenceError into openShipManifest's try/catch, which console.errors and returns, so the click was a silent no-op. FIX: window._fmFleet = { HULLS, src, dir }, consumed via window from the panel side. Same pattern as the existing window._spawnServerShip export, which carries a comment saying exactly this about IIFE scope.
- BEHAVIOUR CHANGE: window.openAmbientReadout no longer builds a personnel modal. Verbattan and scoundrel clicks call gToast with a refusal line and leave the panel closed. Merchant traffic is the only thing that opens the transit log.
- gToast WAS NEVER EXPORTED: declared inside the first IIFE at galaxy.js. city.js toast() guards on `typeof gToast === 'function'`, which is false from a separate script, so EVERY city toast since City Charters shipped was discarded silently: city.seatTaken, city.shopOpened, city.policyApplied, city.error and the rest. FIX: window.gToast = gToast at the declaration; city.js toast() now prefers window.gToast and keeps the bare identifier as a fallback.
- I18N: refusal text uses window.tf with {id} and {cls} tokens rather than concatenation, since a concatenated fallback cannot be translated. New keys galx.scanVerbattan, galx.scanScoundrel with zh. Removed galx.holdSealed, now unreferenced after the personnel readout was dropped. i18n-check 6/6.
- TEST HOOKS exported from galaxy.js: window._spawnServerShipForce(npc) builds one ship without requiring gShipActive, window._fmTickOnce() steps positionShip across both lists once. Needed because the harness cannot run the RAF loop.
- tools/fleet-check.mjs: 20 -> 31 checks. Added plume-touches-stern, plume-behind-hull, plume-vertically-centred, plume-scaled-to-hull, merchant-click-opens-log, log-names-the-drawn-hull (asserts membership of the variant pool, not a fixed name, since the hull is chosen from the npc id), log-lists-real-cargo, gToast-exported, and refusal-not-panel for both ambient kinds.
- Changed: client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json, tools/fleet-check.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

Scoundrel branch shows no registry entry and a sealed hold. Route rows become PATROL / TOWARD for Verbattan. ## 1.5.1.1 changes (Dead tab selector broke the OPEN CITY shortcut; CLIENT)
- BUG: window.cityOpen() in client/assets/city.js did `document.querySelector('[data-tab="cities"]')` when the host was off screen. Zero occurrences of data-tab="cities" in client/index.html; the attribute has never existed. Cities is a galaxy SUB tab: #citiesTabInner sits inside #gCitiesPane, switched by [data-gstab="cities"] in galaxy.js initSubTabs. querySelector returned null, no click fired, #gCitiesPane stayed display:none, renderCity() painted into a hidden container. The city_data_request was still sent and still answered, so socket-level inspection showed a working feature.
- FIX: click [data-gstab="cities"], then return. That handler already sets pane display, toggles .fmfull for fullscreen, sets body overflow, and calls cityTabLoad(), which renders and requests using the cOpen assigned at the top of cityOpen. cityTabLoad only assigns cOpen when it is falsy, so the colony from the card survives. Removes a duplicate city_data_request as a side effect.
- ALSO: reopen the galaxy tab first when #gMapPane is off screen. #galacticTab is display:none while another main tab is active, and display:none on an ancestor kills the subtree regardless of #gCitiesPane.fmfull being position:fixed. Reachable only if cityOpen is ever called from outside the galaxy tab, which no current call site does; the guard is cheap and the failure would be identical and equally silent.
- GUARD 1, tools/selector-check.js: sweeps literal [data-tab] and [data-gstab] selectors in client/ against index.html, fails on any that resolve to nothing. Strips block comments and whole-line // comments first (whole-line only, so https:// inside strings survives), otherwise the comment documenting this bug trips the check. 24 checked, 26 in markup, PASS.
- GUARD 2, tools/city-shortcut-check.mjs: jsdom harness over the real index.html. Shims offsetParent from the inline display chain since jsdom has no layout, wires the main-tab and sub-tab handlers the way core.js and galaxy.js do, then asserts pane display, .fmfull, map pane hidden, host on screen, request sent, and correct colonyId. Also covers a second open from inside the view and a cold start with the galaxy tab closed. 20 checks. NEGATIVE TESTED: reverted cityOpen to the 1.5.1.0 body and the harness fails 4 checks while both websocket assertions still pass, which is exactly why the check asserts on visibility rather than traffic. Requires `npm i jsdom`; not a project dependency.

## 1.5.1.0 changes (City panel Chinese; Circuit bonus stated; card layout fix; CLIENT)
- CITY I18N: the city.* namespace did not exist in window.I18N. 157 keys added. Extraction was mechanical: every T('key','literal') call site in client/assets/city.js was parsed and the literal lifted verbatim into the en field, so English is byte identical. A verifier re-parses all 177 call sites and asserts I18N[k].en === the literal; 0 mismatches, 0 unused keys.
- SIX DYNAMIC FALLBACKS converted from string concatenation to window.tf() with named tokens: city.fullyDeveloped {n}, city.confirmOust {who}{where}{price}{comp}, city.confirmSeat {where}{price}, city.confirmWorks {where}{price}, city.oustedMsg {who}{where}{comp}, city.lapsedMsg {where}. Concatenated fallbacks cannot be translated: the key lookup returns one static string and the interpolation is lost.
- THREE KEYS SERVED TWO ENGLISH STRINGS EACH, so adopting them would have changed English output at the second site. city.shopsWord was 'Shops' and 'shops'; city.pop was 'Pop' and 'Citizens'; city.noChanges was 'No changes.' and 'No policy changes.'. Split into city.shopsLower, city.citizens3, city.noPolicyChanges. Found by the parity check, not by reading.
- CONTENT TABLES MIRRORED via Proxy get traps reading window._lang at access time, so a live language toggle takes effect without a re-render path change: KIND_LABEL, FACTION_LABEL, STAGES (n and d), LANDMARK_NAME. All fall through to English on a missing key. Exercised in both languages including an unknown key on each table.
- FACTION_LABEL HAD NO 'jade' ENTRY, so the wrong-faction notice on a Circuit world printed the raw id and FACTION_COL had no jade tint. Both added; jade tint {r:232,g:228,b:216}.
- CIRCUIT CARD: bonusSummary now leads with '+5% on export trade for Circuit storefronts in Circuit cities.' in EN, and the ZH bonus field mirrors it. FACTION_ZH.jade.desc deliberately NOT touched, still awaiting the author's own text.
- LAYOUT FIX in jadeFactionCard: the OPEN/SEALED badge was position:absolute top:8px right:10px while the JOIN button sat at the right of the header flex row, so the two overlapped. Badge moved into a right hand flex group beside the join/aligned control, white-space:nowrap on all three, card no longer needs position:relative. Badge label routed through new keys fac.passageOpen / fac.passageSealed.
- gstab.cities was referenced by data-i18n in client/index.html and undefined in the catalog since the tab shipped. Added with the literal glyph rather than an escaped surrogate pair, because the glyph-loss check compares source text to the DOM and an escape reads as not-the-glyph.
- CHECKS: i18n-check 6/6 PASS (catalog 1200 entries, 1060 literal keys in use), i18n-scope-check PASS, English parity 177/177, offline suites 76, live suite 30.
- Changed: client/assets/core.js, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.5.0.0 changes (Jade Circuit seeded server side; civic works; SERVER + CLIENT)
- JADE COLONIES: 16 entries appended to COLONY_DEFAULTS in db.js with faction 'jade'. The four control_* values are OUTSIDE powers' footholds and sum low deliberately; colonyLeadingFaction() now short circuits on faction==='jade' rather than reading them. Consequence stated in source: Circuit worlds are outside the conquest layer until a control_jade column exists, because funding has nothing to flip them to. That is a separate release with a migration.
- COMMODITIES CAME FREE: tickCommodityPrices() iterates getAllColonyStates(), so seeding was the entire job. 2280 -> 4200 colony x commodity prices. Added COMMODITY_FACTION_MOD.jade {tech:0.86, med:1.02, agri:0.88} and COMMODITY_FACTION_VOL.jade 0.7.
- JADE CITIES: 16 CITY_COLONIES entries with pop + capital + jade flag, 16 COLONY_VISUAL entries, new isJadeColony(). Districts, NPC firms, frontage and seats all generate through the existing path. yujing 760M capital down to chiyou_marches 70M.
- SEAT GATE: 'jade' added to REAL_FACTIONS in city_buy_seat and to the factionGated list in cityFullData. Circuit worlds are Circuit offices.
- DISTRICT NAMES: one 14-name pool served all 148 districts and districtCount maxes at 14, so every city in the galaxy had a Harbor Gate. Split into DISTRICT_NAMES (22) and DISTRICT_NAMES_JADE (22) selected by districtNamePool(colonyId), with a deterministic per colony offset via mulberry32(seedFromId('names:'+colonyId)) so same-culture cities do not share an ordering.
- CIVIC WORKS: new works REAL column on city_districts, added by ALTER in initCityDb (CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS does nothing to an existing table). New colonyWorks(), addDistrictWorks(), worksCost/worksFromSpend/nextWorksCost/worksLevel/worksComplete, city_works WS handler gated on the sitting mayor. WORKS_BASE 2e9, WORKS_GROWTH 1.55, WORKS_MAX 6, so a finished district is F46.79B. First tuning at growth 1.9 to 8 levels cost F375B per district, which is more than the galaxy holds; retuned.
- WORKS RETURNS: stepDistrict subtracts WORKS_UNREST 3.0 per level from the unrest target and adds WORKS_PROSPERITY 3.0 to the prosperity target; supplyOf adds WORKS_SUPPLY 0.05 per level on the favoured trade. No commerce term anywhere, by design.
- WORKS FEED WAR, NOT SALVAGE: cityBook() = colonyInvested + colonyWorks * WORKS_WAR_WEIGHT(1.0). server.js already prices control at WAR_FUND_BASE_PER_PCT + warRate(cityBook), so this lands with no further wiring. stripYield() and maybeStripOccupied() still read colonyInvested only, so monuments survive occupation. MEASURED xuanwu_bastion, 9 districts at devCost(4): book F3.10B -> F120.28B, cost per control point F68.11M -> F912.77M, salvage yield F155.14M unchanged.
- JADE EXPORT BONUS: JADE_EXPORT_BONUS 0.05 applied in resolveDistrictShops to kind==='export' when isJadeColony(colonyId) AND the owner's faction is 'jade'. Faction lookup via a module level resolver installed once with setFactionResolver(getPlayerFaction) rather than threaded through four call sites; per-call memo since one district holds many shops per owner. NPC owners short circuit. Unset resolver means the bonus never applies, which is the safe default for tests and tools. Like the existing favoured-trade multiplier this is a SUBSIDY on top of the pool split, not a redistribution inside it, so a district can pay somewhat above its raw pool; that is the only place the city creates value and it is now documented in source.
- RENDER: SECTORS carries works; stageOf counts works at 0.5 weight; landmarkTier(dev,works) gains tier 4 at works>=3 and tier 5 at works>=5, both unreachable by development alone; landmark scale table extended to [0,1,1.5,2.1,2.9,3.8]; block height multiplied by lift=1+works*0.34 at full weight for tall blocks and 45% for the rest; HEAD_Z 46 -> 78. LANDMARK_NAME extended with tier 4 and 5 names per trade.
- LATENT BUG, PRESENT IN DEVELOPMENT SINCE 1.3.0.0: devFromInvested and worksFromSpend derive a level count by logarithm, and an exactly-paid level reads 1.99926 rather than 2. Math.floor gives the previous level, so nextLevelCost quotes a token amount and the district can be charged again and again for a level it owns. New snapLevel() rounds to the integer within 1e-6. Caught by the works ladder test printing 'W3 cost F0.00M'.
- SUITES: t1 structure 13, t2 economics 14, t3 governance 9, t4 simulation 19, t8 Circuit and works 21, t7 live over WebSocket 30. 106 of 106.
- PERFORMANCE at 35 worlds / 26,271 storefronts: income tick 169ms, simulation tick 73ms.
- OPEN: no control_jade column so the Circuit is outside conquest; the city panel has zero Chinese keys.
- Changed: server/city.js, server/db_city.js, server/db.js, server/server.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.4.4.0 changes (One city per mayor; five faults removed from the city system; SERVER + CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- BALANCE, ONE CITY PER MAYOR: city_buy_seat now calls new mayorColonies(playerId) in db_city.js and refuses a purchase when the player already holds a seat on a different colony. Unlimited seats WITHIN one city are still allowed. Boot pass reconcileOneCityPerMayor() keeps the colony with the highest sum(invested + seat_paid), vacates the rest and refunds seat_paid + invested at face value, flagged city_kv seat_rule='1city' so it cannot run twice. Payload gains myCity; panel shows the blocking city instead of a bare refusal.
- FAULT 1, FATAL, SIMULATION: CITY_TUNE.NPC_BASE was 18. effLever() floors every lever there, and the fixed point of stepDistrict at 0.18 is crime 95 / prosperity 5 / output 5. runCityTick steps EVERY district regardless of mayor, so all 148 districts decayed to prosperity 5 / output 7 / unrest 72 within 12 weeks of boot with no player input, and summed commercialPool fell F5716M -> F265M (-95%). All payback figures in 1.4.1.0-1.4.3.0 were measured at seeded scalars that do not persist. NPC_BASE -> 50, solved as the lowest floor whose equilibrium is crime 45 / unrest 45 / prosperity 44 / output 60. Governed district still ~3x on pool (prosperity/output multipliers 1.36*1.30 vs 0.63*0.86).
- FAULT 2, ECONOMIC LEAK: resolveDistrictShops computed gross = Math.min(kindPool * share, ceiling) per shop and discarded the clipped excess. Measured 22% of all galaxy commerce destroyed, 60% in a well governed new_anchor district (117 firms, F47.4M absorbable against a F96.8M pool). Severity inverted with quality: better governance destroyed more. FIX: per kind iterative spill, up to 8 passes, capping at the ceiling and redistributing the overflow across shops still under it by weight; residue only when every shop in that trade is capped, which is genuine unspent demand. Ceiling is now max(SHOP_CEIL_WK * dev, kindPool * SHOP_CEIL_SHARE 0.35) so it caps ONE shop rather than the district. Verified: new_anchor district absorbs F96.80M of F96.80M.
- FAULT 3, DEAD PROGRESSION: DEV_GROWTH 2.4 against a roughly flat marginal return meant level 5 repaid in 256wk and level 6 in 782wk, while DEV_MAX 14 (absolute) advertised up to 10 purchasable levels on a low baseline district. New CITY_TUNE.DEV_LEVELS_MAX = 4 caps bonus levels above baseline; districtDev() clamps devFromInvested to it; new devComplete(colonyId, d) is the gate used by city_invest. Client gates on (dev - baseline) < tune.devLevels and shows levels built of max. Measured paybacks now 14 / 27 / 57 / 121 weeks.
- FAULT 4, FAKE LEVER: lv_subsidy was written by setDistrictLevers, persisted, shipped in districtSummary.levers and rendered as a range input. Referenced by nothing in stepDistrict, civicBill, commercialPool, supplyOf or resolveDistrictShops. Removed from the writer, the payload and the panel. Column retained in DDL, no migration.
- FAULT 5, FREE DEBT WRITE OFF: creditCityIncome paid perPlayer[mayor] += net AND set arrears = max(0, arrears - net) in the same branch. Now repay = min(arrears, net), keep = net - repay; only keep reaches the player.
- FAULT 6, BOOT ORDER: seedNpcCommerce() ran before migrateCityModelV3(), so the migration counted the 13,517 firms it had just seeded as v2 tenancies and called wipeAllShops(). No cash printed (npc: ids never resolve via getPlayer) but cities were bare until the next hourly tick. Seeder moved after the migration.
- REMOVED AS UNREFERENCED: server payload fields mySeats and myShops; db_city exports countMayorSeats, countPlayerShops, getMayorDistricts, releasePlayerSeats, releasePlayerShops, getPlayerShops; the getMayorDistricts import in server.js.
- SUITES: t1 structure 13, t2 economics 14, t3 governance 9, t4 simulation 19, t7 live over a real WebSocket 17. 72 of 72. Live suite registers accounts over /api/register, grants cash via god_cmd on a dev account, and drives city_buy_seat, city_invest, city_set_levers, city_set_cut and city_data_request against a booted server. NOTE for future harnesses: cityErr replies with type 'city_ack' and ok:false, so the reply TYPE never distinguishes success from refusal; assert on data.ok or every refusal reads as a grant.
- MEASURED PAYBACK, real policy at a tuned rate: frontier_outpost 66wk (heavy services @ 15%, F0.25M/wk), eyejog 59wk (@ 18%, F3.86M/wk), new_anchor 110wk (@ 18%, F12.02M/wk). Default policy repays nowhere.
- PERFORMANCE unchanged: creditCityIncome 97ms, simulation tick 53ms on a fully populated 19 world galaxy.
- OPEN: the city panel has zero Chinese keys and renders entirely through English fallbacks.
- Changed: server/city.js, server/db_city.js, server/server.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json, docs/CHANGELOG.md, docs/MANIFEST.txt.

## 1.4.3.0 changes (Adversarial pass: Laffer curve + strict index parsing; SERVER + CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- FAULT 1, ECONOMIC: commerce_cut was a degenerate lever. mayoralTake was strictly increasing in the rate across 0.05-0.25, so 0.25 was always correct. TAX_FLIGHT alone did not fix it: firms left, but commercialPool was a FIXED pie so survivors took bigger slices and total gross barely moved. Two changes together produce a real curve:
  (a) npcOccupancy now multiplies by (1 - pressure * TAX_FLIGHT 0.62) where pressure = (cut-CUT_MIN)/(CUT_MAX-CUT_MIN). Occupancy responds over TICKS via ensureNpcShops, not instantly, so a mayor who spikes the rate watches the frontage empty.
  (b) resolveDistrictShops scales the pool by activity = shops/slots, so trade depends on how much frontage is open rather than being redistributed among fewer firms. COMMERCE_POP_K raised 1200 -> 2100 to hold typical incomes roughly level given the new activity factor.
  MEASURED, occupancy settled at each rate: new_anchor 5%/196 firms/F1.77M, 10%/166/F3.06M, 15%/135/F3.72M, 20%/105/F3.66M, 25%/74/F3.56M -> optimum 15%. frontier_outpost optimum 20%. Interior and district-dependent, so the lever is a real decision.
  NOTE: read INSTANTLY the take is still monotone, which is correct (raising the rate pays more until firms leave). Any test of this must settle occupancy first; the first version of the check did not and reported a false failure.
- FAULT 2, INPUT: every district-scoped handler used Math.round(Number(msg.district)). Number() coerces null, '', [], true and '0' to 0, and rounds 0.5 to 1. Fuzzing 17 hostile values x 7 handlers produced 61 ACCEPTED actions, each landing on a real district. No value could be obtained for free, but a crafted or buggy message could buy a seat or lease frontage in a district the player never chose. New cityIndex(v) requires typeof number, Number.isInteger and >= 0; -0 is normalised to 0 (it IS zero, not hostile) rather than rejected. Applied to district and shopId; city_set_cut now requires a real number type too.
- NEW SUITES: adv1 (economics) 8 checks -- pool conservation across all 148 districts, tax+net==gross to 1e-11, ouster payout < outlay at four investment levels, blockade buyout discount, interior optimum on the rate. adv2 (fuzz) 9 checks -- malformed district on 7 handlers, malformed shop ids on 3, hostile colony ids, balance untouched by rejected actions, five-way double-spend on one buyout granting exactly once and charging once, rate clamping under hostile values. laffer 4 checks -- interior optimum on two colonies.
- WHAT HELD: no district pays out more than its pool (max 100.0%); gross splits exactly; ouster is loss-making at every level; siege discount only 4%; concurrent buyout races resolve to one grant one charge; no injection or unknown colony accepted; no message went unanswered; cash finite and non-negative throughout.
- Removed the map hint line under the canvas.
- Regression: struct 12, layout 14, css 7, frame 11, render 14, vocabulary 6, fullscreen 9, perf 5, economics 8, laffer 4, city E2E 28, npc+buyout 17, filter 7, fuzz 9, oust+lapse 11, ui 18.
- Changed: server/city.js, server/server.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json.

## 1.4.2.0 changes (District native trade restores colour; fullscreen city view; SERVER + CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- COLOUR BUG, introduced by 1.4.1.0 and diagnosed rather than guessed: ensureNpcShops drew NPC firm kinds from commercialSplit(), the CITY-WIDE demand split, which weights food 0.40. Result: food was the modal kind in 148 of 148 districts, so districtTrade() returned 'food' everywhere and every district on every world rendered green. The 1.4.0.0 vocabulary was correct and being fed a uniform input. Confirmed by dumping modal kind per district before touching anything.
- FIX: new districtNativeTrade(colonyId, idx). Builds a bag of trades proportional to SHOP_KIND_WEIGHT sized to the district count, shuffles it with mulberry32(seedFromId('native:'+colonyId)), deals one per district, caches per colony. NPC seeding now weights 13:2:2:2 toward the district's native trade instead of using the city split. new_anchor deals 3 export / 5 food / 2 med / 2 tech; eyejog 2 export / 3 food / 1 med / 1 tech. Verified on canvas: three colour families painted in a single frame (green, warm, violet).
- FULLSCREEN: selecting the cities gstab adds .fmfull to #gCitiesPane (position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:9000) and sets body overflow hidden; deselecting removes both via window.cityLeaveFull(). EXIT control in the city header calls window.cityExit() which clicks the map gstab. Deliberately CSS-only: no element is moved in the DOM, because markup surgery on this page has broken the layout twice (1.3.1.2, 1.3.3.1). struct guard passes unchanged. fit() is re-run 30ms after entry so the canvas re-measures the new box.
- CSS SCOPE GUARD tightened, not relaxed. It previously asserted every selector contains '.fmcity', which the legitimate '#gCitiesPane.fmfull' rule does not. It now encodes the actual invariant: reject a BARE host id (the shape left behind by the non-distributing selector-list prefix that drew a line across the page in 1.3.1.1), accept '.fmcity ...' or '#host.class'. Negative-tested by re-injecting the original leak; still reports '[BARE HOST TARGETED]'.
- NEW HARNESS (full.mjs): 9 checks covering trade spread across districts, no single trade dominating, at least three colour families actually painted on canvas in a frame, fullscreen entering without reparenting, position:fixed + inset:0 present, exit controls exposed, class cleared on leave, EXIT button rendered. Note the canvas assertion needs the wrapper's clientWidth/Height stubbed, since fit() bails at zero layout in jsdom and draw() would never run.
- ui4/full harnesses made port-configurable via process.env.PORT.
- Regression (each isolation-sensitive suite on its own fresh DB): struct 12/12, layout 14/14, css 7/7, frame 11/11, render 14/14, vocabulary 6/6, npc+buyout 17/17, perf 5/5, city E2E 28/28, filter 7/7, ui 18/18, fullscreen 9/9.
- Changed: server/city.js, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/version.json.

## 1.4.1.0 changes (NPC commerce + buyouts; tick perf; copy pass; SERVER + CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- NPC BUSINESSES seeded into every district on boot and topped up each tick. Count = shopCapacity x npcOccupancy(), where occupancy = NPC_OCC_BASE 0.14 + log2(popPerDistrict)/7 x 0.46 + capital x 0.10 + (dev-baseline)/5 x 0.20, capped 0.78. Result: ~17,300 firms galaxy-wide; new_anchor 152/district at 64% occupancy, frontier_outpost 33/district at 34%. Player shops count AGAINST the target so players displace NPC firms rather than adding to them; surplus NPC firms are retired when a district shrinks or fills.
- IDENTITY: NPC firms carry owner='npc:<ts36>:<seq>', a DISTINCT id each, so the 1/sqrt(rank) per-owner decay in resolveDistrictShops treats them as separate firms rather than one conglomerate. getPlayer() never resolves them so they are never paid. No schema change. Generic names composed from NPC_PREFIX + district name + NPC_NOUN[kind], e.g. "Inner Tide Stair Depot", "New Tide Stair Granary".
- WHY THIS MATTERS MECHANICALLY: before it, mayoralTake was 0 until players opened shops, so every seat-payback figure in 1.3.0.0 was theoretical. A seat now earns on day one (frontier_outpost district take F452k/wk with zero players present).
- SHOP RAMP, new: a storefront opens at SHOP_RAMP_FLOOR 0.18 of full trade and climbs linearly to 1.0 over SHOP_RAMP_WK 12. Applied as a multiplier on the share weight inside the pool, so it redistributes rather than inflating. NPC firms are seeded with leased_at backdated 1.4x the ramp window, i.e. fully established. adoptShop() deliberately does NOT reset leased_at, which is the entire point of buying a going concern.
- BUYOUT: city_buy_shop handler. Price = shopLeaseCost + NPC_BUYOUT_WEEKS(20) x current weekly net, computed live off resolveDistrictShops so a thriving district costs more. Guards: NPC-only (player shops rejected), colony not occupied, funds, name >=3 chars and isTextClean. On new_anchor: lease F13.5M earning F37k/wk vs buy F17.7M earning F209k/wk, i.e. 5.6x income for 1.3x price. Vacancy remains at 36% of frontage on the capital so leasing is never closed off.
- PERFORMANCE, the significant fix: creditCityIncome called addShopPaid per shop per tick. At 17,311 shops that is 17,311 separate UPDATEs = 14,722ms of blocking work per tick, which would have stalled the whole process hourly in production. NPC firms are now excluded from the payout write path entirely (only their tax reaches the mayor), and the remaining writes are wrapped in a single transaction via new beginBatch/endBatch helpers (depth-counted, rollback on throw). 14,722ms -> 56ms. NOTE: an earlier attempt at the NPC exclusion silently failed to apply because the replace had no assert; the perf regression is what surfaced it. New /tmp perf guard asserts resolve <600ms, income tick <1.5s, npc top-up <600ms, total <2s. 5/5.
- BEHAVIOUR CHANGE worth knowing: a DEVELOPED district now covers its own civic bill from established commerce, so it will not lapse to arrears just because its mayor is broke. The oust/lapse E2E was updated to target an undeveloped district on the minimum commerce rate; the old setup was asserting behaviour that is no longer correct.
- CLIENT: frontage list marks independent firms (dashed border, "independent", no owner name) with an inline BUY button showing the live price; buyout prompts for a trading name. New note explains the ramp. COPY PASS: 22 panel strings rewritten from prose to plain description of function and meaning.
- MAP: removed the faint disc in the sky. Intended as a moon, read as an unexplained empty circle.
- VERIFIED: npc/buyout E2E 17/17, perf 5/5, oust+lapse 11/11, city E2E 28/28, filter 7/7, vocabulary 6/6, struct 12/12, layout 14/14, css 7/7, frame 11/11, render 14/14, ui 18/18.
- Changed: server/city.js, server/db_city.js, server/server.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json.

## 1.4.0.0 changes (Trade-driven architecture + investment landmarks; CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- PROBLEM: every block was one primitive at varying height, which reads as texture not architecture. Fixed with a vocabulary, not more detail.
- FORMS, 14 new building types across four kits, drawn 72/18/10 from [common, common, occasional, rare] so a district is mostly its own trade with a minority of others:
  export  formTankFarm (drums on a shared pad + walkway bands), formFoundry (sawtooth roof with molten light bleeding through the risers), formCoolingTower (waisted profile from 7 stacked prismN rings), formChimney (banded stack, flared crown, burning tip)
  food    formTerraceFarm, formGlasshouse, formSilo (unchanged, they already read)
  med     formMedBlock, formCleanTower (unchanged)
  tech    formLowTech, formStackedArc, formHoloTower (vast lit ad panel down one face), formMegaBlock (spans 2.3x its own footprint at ~2.9x height, 4 setbacks, near-black mass with horizontal light bands)
- PLUS: formPipeRack threaded every 4th block in export districts (the connective tissue that makes a plant read as one works), and drawTraffic stringing spinner lanes between the tall tech structures via a __tall marker the forms leave behind. New prismN(cx,cy,r,z0,z1,sides) primitive for drums/domes/pyramids.
- LANDMARKS: landmarkTier(dev) -> 0/1/2/3 at D6/D10/D13. drawLandmark branches on trade: food = stepped dome, med = pale drum with a lit cross on a mast, tech = 6-step pyramid dwarfing the district, export = sawtooth hall + rank of flare stacks + gantry. Landmark clears a radius of 5.5*[0,1,1.5,2.1][tier] so blocks never bury it.
- TRADE SOURCE (the bit that makes this informative rather than decorative), districtTrade(s) in priority order: (1) d.favoured, the mayor's nominated trade; (2) modal kind across the district's storefronts; (3) stable per-district hash of seedFromId('trade:'+colony+':'+idx) so a colonial-administered city still reads varied on day one. A district therefore arrives with character and visibly morphs as players specialise it.
- Building colour now comes from TRADE_COL (export amber / food green / med pale cyan / tech violet); district outline and map labels use it too. District panel gains "Builds in" (marked "zoned" when it is the mayor's explicit choice) and "Landmark".
- PORTED PRIMITIVE: the forms call box(l, z0, z1, c, o); the game only had boxAt(l, h, c, o). Missing it meant every form threw inside the render catch and drew NOTHING while the "renders without throwing" assertion still passed. Caught only because the harness asserts PAINT VOLUME: 87k ops before, 3,784,883 after. Worth keeping that assertion shape.
- PROCESS: the look was developed and signed off in a standalone harness (six directions, live dials, real exported geometry) before any of it entered the game, because whether something looks good is the one thing the headless suites cannot check. Ported at the approved settings; the harness dials become fixed constants GLOW=1, LIT=0.45.
- VERIFIED: 6/6 across all 6 colonies x 14 dev levels x 4 forced trades, plus shop-derived and no-data paths. Regression: struct 12/12, layout 14/14, css 7/7, frame 11/11, render 14/14, ui 18/18, server city E2E 28/28, boot clean.
- Changed: client/assets/city.js, client/version.json.
