## 1.3.3.0 changes (Cities -> galaxy sub-tab; map made dominant; CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- NAVIGATION: removed the top-level [data-tab="cities"] button and the #citiesTab pane. Cities is now data-gstab="cities" in #galaxySubTabs beside Factions, with #gCitiesPane as a sibling of gMapPane/gFactionsPane/gShippingPane/gContractsPane/gMarketsPane. initSubTabs() in galaxy.js toggles it and calls window.cityTabLoad() on select. Removed the now-dead top-level wiring from core.js. 1.3.2.0 had it in BOTH places, which was a duplicate entry point AND the wrong row (that row is for whole sections, not galaxy views).
- MAP DOMINANCE, three parts:
  (1) LAYOUT: .cywrap grid was 210px rail / 1fr map / 320px detail, leaving the map ~1/3 of the width. Now 0px / 1fr / 300px by default, with .cywrap.railopen switching the first column to 210px. The rail is opened by a SWITCH COLONY control in the header (window.cityRailToggle, persisted to localStorage fm_city_rail, default CLOSED); window.cityPick() closes it after choosing so the map returns immediately. Grid is height:calc(100% - 42px); .cymid is a flex column with .cvwrap flex:1 so the canvas claims all remaining vertical space.
  (2) CANVAS: fit() now measures the WRAPPER's clientWidth AND clientHeight and sets CW/CH to that box (falling back to 0.62 aspect only when the container reports no height). Canvas device size is box*DPR; base transform is DPR rather than cv.width/CW, so viewScale() = DPR * view.z.
  (3) PROJECTION: S/HZ/OX/OY were four constants tuned for one canvas size. New autoFrame() derives them: projects the plate corners into unscaled iso space (u=x-y, v=(x+y)/2), computes the largest S fitting both axes given HEAD_Z=46 world-z of tower headroom at HZ=S*1.147, then centres. Called from fit(), so the map fits any box.
  MEASURED across four container shapes with live geometry: plate spans 81-96% of width, >55% of total canvas area, and stays fully inside the canvas at every shape.
- NEW HARNESSES: frame.mjs drives fit() at 1100x620 / 760x520 / 1400x760 / 520x420 against live server geometry, asserting containment and fill (11 checks; needs live data since the canvas only exists once cData lands). layout.mjs asserts no top-level cities tab, cities present in the .galaxy-stab row, #gCitiesPane sibling of #gContractsPane, old #citiesTab gone, rail column 0px by default, railopen 210px, detail <=300px, .cvwrap flex:1, canvas height:100% (14 checks). New debug hook window.__cityFrame() returns {S,HZ,OX,OY,CW,CH,minX,maxX,minY,maxY} so fill can be measured rather than eyeballed.
- SCROLLING ARCHITECTURE CHANGED with the move: #gCitiesPane uses overflow:hidden + flex:1 + min-height:0 like every other sub-pane, so the map cannot scroll away; .cyrail and .cyside scroll internally at max-height:100%. The two ui-suite assertions that checked #citiesTab for overflow-y:auto were updated to assert the new shape (pane bounded, columns scroll), not deleted.
- Regression: ui 18/18, css scope 7/7, frame 11/11, layout 14/14, cold open renders, server city E2E 28/28.
- Changed: client/index.html, client/assets/city.js, client/assets/galaxy.js, client/assets/core.js, client/version.json.

## 1.3.2.0 changes (Cities tab: map zoom/pan, scrolling, collapsible panels; CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- ZOOM/PAN on the city canvas, mirroring galaxy.js's interaction rather than inventing a second one. New view={z,tx,ty} with tx/ty in DEVICE pixels; draw() composes ctx.setTransform(k,0,0,k,view.tx,view.ty) where k=(cv.width/CW)*view.z. toWorld() is the exact inverse and the click hit-test now goes through it. Wheel zooms toward the cursor (hold the world point under the pointer fixed), drag pans, touch does 1-finger pan / 2-finger pinch. Clamp VIEW_MIN 0.55 to VIEW_MAX 6. window.cityZoom(f) / window.cityZoomReset() exposed for the +/-/RESET buttons. drawStars() is wrapped in save/setTransform(base)/restore so the backdrop does NOT pan with the plate. _dragMoved suppresses the click that follows a pan, so dragging across a district no longer selects it.
- SCROLLING: the pane's ancestry is .panel < .grid (overflow:hidden, min-height:0) < .wrap (height:100vh, overflow:hidden), so a pane taller than the column was clipped with no scrollbar. #citiesTab now carries height:100%; overflow-y:auto; box-sizing:border-box. .cyrail and .cyside each get max-height:calc(100vh - 120px) with their own overflow-y:auto, so the rail, the map column and the detail column scroll independently.
- COLLAPSIBLE PANELS reusing the EXISTING mechanism (style.css .collapsible/.collapse-toggle/.collapse-chevron/.collapse-body, same as Wire Credits and Leaderboard). cyp() now splits the leading <p class="cyt"> off each panel and re-emits it as the toggle with a chevron, wrapping the remainder in .collapse-body. State lives in cFold, persisted to localStorage key fm_city_folds, driven by window.cityFold(id). It cannot use the global initialiser in index.html because that runs once at DOMContentLoaded and the detail column is rebuilt wholesale on every selection change and data update; verified the fold survives a rebuild.
- Zoom control row (.cyzoom) added between the map and the district strip, with the interaction hint.
- VERIFIED: new 17-check harness against live server payloads covering all three areas: pane/rail/side scroll rules present, 7 collapsible panels each with toggle+body+chevron, fold/unfold/persist-through-rebuild, zoom raises the max canvas transform in a frame, zoom-back returns to base, zoom clamps at 6x, reset returns exactly to base. NOTE the first version of the zoom assertion read the LAST setTransform of the frame, which is the deliberately-unzoomed star backdrop, and reported a false failure; it now reads the max across the frame.
- Regression: render 14/14, css scope 7/7, placement 8/8, cold open renders, server city E2E 28/28.
- Changed: client/assets/city.js, client/index.html, client/version.json.

## 1.3.1.2 changes (Cities tab rendered blank: pane was outside the layout; CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- BUG: clicking Cities showed an empty centre column. Not a JS fault. #citiesTab was inserted in 1.3.1.0 anchored on the end of a <script> block, landing at BODY level next to #godPanel instead of inside the centre column holding #marketTab / #galacticTab / #miningTab. The tab handler correctly hid the other panes (empty column) and displayed #citiesTab at the foot of the document, outside the layout, off screen. The renderer was working the whole time: cold-open harness confirms it emits header + 'Charter registry loading...' and dispatches city_summaries_request + city_data_request.
- WHY IT PASSED REVIEW: verification was `grep -c 'id="citiesTab"'` plus a jsdom harness that supplied its OWN markup. Both check presence, neither checks placement, which was the only broken thing.
- FIX: pane moved to sit immediately after #miningTab closes, before #miningFullscreenHost, same parent and same nesting depth as every other pane. #citiesTabInner also carries class="fmcity" in markup now rather than only being set at runtime.
- NEW TEST (place.mjs pattern): parses the REAL client/index.html via jsdom, collects marketTab/galacticTab/miningTab/casinoTab, asserts they share exactly one parent, then asserts citiesTab shares that parent, is not a direct child of body, and matches nesting depth. 8/8 on the fix; verified it reports 3 failures against a reconstruction of the shipped layout ('cities parent is <body>', depth 0 vs 3).
- Regression: full tab render 14/14, css scope guard 7/7, cold-open path renders, server city suite 28/28.
- Changed: client/index.html, client/version.json.

## 1.3.1.1 changes (CSS scope leak drew a line across the page; CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- BUG: opening a city rendered a thin green bar across the full viewport instead of the city. Root cause is CSS selector-list semantics. city.js built its stylesheet from rule strings containing a SCOPE token, substituted at injection. With two hosts (tab + planet-card overlay) the token expanded to the list '#cityOverlay, #citiesTab', so 'SCOPE .obar{height:3px;...}' emitted '#cityOverlay, #citiesTab .obar{height:3px;...}'. A selector list does NOT distribute a prefix: that is '#cityOverlay' AND '#citiesTab .obar'. All ~50 rules were therefore applying their declarations to #cityOverlay itself; .obar's height:3px on a position:fixed inset:0 element collapsed it to a 3px full-width bar. Reproduced in isolation before fixing.
- FIX: scope is now a single class .fmcity carried by the host element, which cannot mis-parse. Stylesheet moved into document.head (id fmCityStyles), injected once via ensureStyles().
- SIMPLIFICATION, and the actual cause of the cause: the modal overlay is DELETED. The Cities tab is the only home. window.cityOpen() now switches to the tab (clicks [data-tab="cities"]) and selects the colony instead of showing a modal over the galaxy map; window.cityClose() is retained as a jump back to Galaxy for older markup that calls it. ensureOverlay(), the #cityOverlay element and the modal header close button are gone. Two chromes sharing one renderer is what forced a two-host selector in the first place.
- NAV: Cities entry added to the galaxy sub-tab row beside Factions. Deliberately NOT a data-gstab pane (no pane exists); it is an onclick jump to the top-level tab, placed where someone reading colony panels reaches for it.
- NEW TEST /tmp harness css.mjs style: parses the generated stylesheet and asserts (a) every selector contains .fmcity, (b) no selector is a bare host id, (c) no host receives a fixed 3px height, (d) no overlay element is created, (e) the host carries the scope class, (f) the sheet is in <head>. 7/7. This is the specific bug shape and the check would have caught it pre-ship. Full tab render harness unchanged at 14/14.
- Changed: client/assets/city.js, client/index.html, client/version.json.

## 1.3.1.0 changes (Cities tab, UI redesign, skyline renderer; CLIENT + SERVER, TEST BUILD)
- OWN TAB: new data-tab="cities" button beside Galaxy, #citiesTab / #citiesTabInner container, wired in core.js alongside the other tabs (display toggle + window.cityTabLoad() on select). city.js is already loaded eagerly so no lazyLoad needed. The planet-card overlay still works: renderCity() targets cHost, which is #citiesTabInner when the tab is visible and #cityPanel otherwise. cityOpen() detects which by offsetParent. All CSS rules re-scoped from '#cityOverlay .x' to '#cityOverlay, #citiesTab .x' via a SCOPE token substituted at injection.
- NAVIGATION REDESIGN: 19-button ctab wall replaced by a left colony RAIL (.cyrail/.cyrow) sorted by population, each row showing class, OPEN SEATS (gold, the number a buyer actually shops for) and shop count. Districts moved from a vertical .slist to a horizontal chip strip (.cystrip/.cychip) under the map so a 12-district capital fits one line. Grid is 210px rail / fluid map / 320px detail, collapsing to two columns under 1240px and one under 820px.
- EMPTY PANEL BUG FIXED: every section now goes through cyp(id, html) which returns '' when the html is empty, instead of always emitting a bordered .cyp wrapper. Previously renderMayorBox() returned '' for non-mayors and left a blank box mid-column (visible in the 1.3.0.0 screenshot). refreshPanels() now rebuilds the whole side column via sideHtml() rather than patching panels by id, because per-id setHtml silently dropped any panel that did not exist at first render (e.g. the mayoral office appearing after you buy a seat).
- FRONTAGE: the interior canvas (icv/ictx/drawInterior/iHit/ICW/ICH) is deleted. Storefronts now render as an HTML list (.cyshops/.cyshop) sorted by net, showing name, trade, owner and weekly income, description on title hover, own shops highlighted. HTML because the point of player-named shops is reading the names, and it survives a panel rebuild with no canvas rebinding.
- SKYLINE RENDERER: boxAt reduced to a plain box; new tower() does the work. Setbacks (1/2/3 steps by height) are the main silhouette win. Roof furniture: lit mast above h>9, water tank on ~55% of h>3.2. windows() lights a grid up both visible faces instead of scattering dots; density tracks the district's shop occupancy so a trading quarter glows. Contact shadow under each footprint. Street lines drawn between built blocks before the massing. One block per district promoted to a landmark at 1.75x height. All variation from blockRng(l), an FNV hash of the block's world position, so it is stable per frame and identical across clients.
- SERVER FIX, contested worlds: 5 of 19 colonies carry colony_state.faction='contested', a state not a party. city_buy_seat compared it directly, so those worlds had permanently unbuyable seats and the error read "Only contested members may hold office on this world." Now gated only when faction is one of coalition/syndicate/void/guild; contested worlds are open to anyone. New factionGated flag on the payload so the client can say so.
- Stale lot-era copy removed from the map hint ("as their lots are built ... tier threshold").
- VERIFIED: new jsdom render harness runs the tab against live server payloads, 14/14 (rail lists 19, strip matches district count, zero blank .cyp, no 'contested members' string, no stale lot copy, no undefined/NaN, selection tracked). Server suites unchanged: 28/28 city, 7/7 filter, 10/10 ouster+lapse. Boot log clean.
- Changed: client/index.html, client/assets/core.js, client/assets/city.js, server/server.js, client/version.json.

## 1.3.0.0 changes (Cities permanent, players buy mayoral office; SERVER + CLIENT, TEST BUILD)
- TEST BUILD. CONTENT FILTERING now covers every player-authored field: city_shops.name/descr, city_districts.name, funds.name/description (create AND edit paths, both previously unfiltered), players.name (register + rename). New isTextClean() in server.js runs THREE passes because each alone had a hole: (1) isNameClean substring match over BANNED_WORDS, (2) the same list over normalizeLeet(text), (3) containsSlur from chat-filter.js. Pass 2 is the new one and it is load-bearing: containsSlur anchors on (?<![a-z])...(?![a-z]) so 'n1gg3rking' defeated it, and BANNED_WORDS has no leet coverage so it defeated that too. Caught by E2E, not by reading. normalizeLeet is now exported from chat-filter.js. Verified 7/7 over live WS: slur handle rejected at registration, dirty shop name blocked, dirty DESCRIPTION blocked behind a clean name, dirty district rename blocked, and 'Kebab Shop' / 'Little Anatolia' / all 148 generated district names still pass.
- NEW server/audit_names.mjs: walks players, funds, city_shops, city_districts and REPORTS pre-filter rows that fail, changing nothing (false positives on real names are common; silent renaming is worse than the problem). --csv for a spreadsheet. Exits 1 when anything is flagged so it can gate a deploy. Verified: clean DB scans 152 fields and flags 0; seeded with 'retard emporium' and 'F4g Quarter' it flags exactly those 2 and passes 'Kebab Shop'. Its BANNED_WORDS copy is synced from server.js by hand, noted in the file header.
- LIMIT ORDER BOOT ERROR fixed, and the earlier diagnosis was WRONG. A duplicate restore block sat above the const limitOrders / ORDER_EXPIRY_MS declarations and threw a TDZ ReferenceError on every boot; it was caught and swallowed, and the REAL restore block (correctly placed after the declarations, with expiry refunds) did the work. Orders were never being lost on restart. Proved by seeding an order into limit_orders, rebooting, and observing '[Limit Orders] Restored 1, expired 0'. Dead block deleted; boot log now clean (grep for restore error / cannot access returns 0).
- SHIP.SH: the local divergence guard was ALREADY present and is better than the 'git pull --ff-only' previously recommended, because apply.sh mirrors the zip over the tree so a plain pull would abort on a dirty tree. Verified all four branches against a scratch repo: in-sync continues, BEHIND stops before committing, DIVERGED stops with the merge -s ours hint, ahead-only continues. Added the missing server-side half: the remote line now refuses to deploy if /root/Flesh-Market has local modifications (prints git status and both fixes), and uses git pull --ff-only so prod cannot grow a merge commit. Still one remote one-liner, since anything pasted before ssh connects is eaten by the login banner.
- CITY_TICK_MS is env-overridable for testing (CITY_TICK_MS=8000) so the arrears-lapse path can be exercised without waiting an hour.
- WHY: v1.2.6.0/v1.2.7.0 sold ground. 4,788 lots galaxy-wide vs an 8,000 concurrency target meant the mechanic sold out permanently instead of getting expensive, and razed player ground left permanently dead parcels. v3 sells OFFICE instead: cities are permanent world objects, players buy the mayoral seat of a district.
- SCHEMA: new city_districts (colony_id, idx, name, zone, mayor, seat_paid, took_office, invested, commerce_cut, favoured, arrears, 5 levers, 6 scalars, last_tick) + idx_districts_mayor. city_shops rebuilt to (id, colony_id, district, owner, kind, name, descr, leased_at, paid_in) with player-authored name/descr. city_lots RETAINED but unused, read once by the v3 migration then wiped.
- DISTRICTS: count = clamp(round(3 + sqrt(popM)/3.2), 3, 14). new_anchor 12, the_hollow 11, frontier_outpost 5. 148 seats galaxy-wide. Geometry is the same Voronoi machinery, now generating districtCount cells instead of a flat 11; blocks inside a cell are pure render furniture carrying no ownership or economy. baselineDev = clamp(round(1.4*log2(pop per district)), 1, 11): new_anchor D9, frontier_outpost D4. Districts arrive ALREADY DEVELOPED under NPC administration with randomised working-city scalars.
- SEATS: price = SEAT_BASE(500k) * 2.4^baselineDev + invested. TWO DIFFERENT SHAPES ON PURPOSE. The baseline term is exponential (frontier F16.6M, dust_basin F39.8M, vein_cluster F229M, new_anchor F1.32B) and is pure sink; the invested term passes through at face value. An earlier pass priced the seat off TOTAL dev, so F2.2B invested on a D9 district pushed the seat to F1.68T and paid out over a trillion in compensation. Harness caught it pre-client. Compensation = 0.75 x INVESTED only, so an ousted mayor can never recover more than they spent (verified: F137.4M invested -> F103.0M paid). SEAT_MIN_HOLD_MS 7d grace on a new mayor. Faction-gated to the colony's owning faction.
- LAPSE: mayor pays civicBill = popPerDistrict x BILL_PER_M(90k) x (0.35+0.65*leverAvg) out of their commerce take; shortfall accrues arrears; arrears > 4 weeks of bill vacates the seat to NPC administration. Verified end-to-end incl. that the district's buildings REMAIN after lapse. This is the peacetime turnover valve, so planets no war reaches still cycle.
- PERKS: levers are PER DISTRICT now (12 mayors on one planet can pull 12 directions). commerce_cut player-set within CUT_MIN 0.05 to CUT_MAX 0.25, clamped server-side (verified: 0.99 clamps to 0.25). favoured trade gives +35%/-12% and is also what feeds supplyOf, so civic production is now a mayoral choice rather than a lot type. District rename, 40 chars, mayor only.
- ECONOMY, fully retuned; the first pass was wrong by ~1000x (a units bug multiplied the pool by 0.001 with population already in millions). commercialPool = popPerDistrict x 1000 x COMMERCE_POP_K(1200) x (dev/baseline)^1.6 x prosperity x output. The dev term is RELATIVE TO BASELINE, replacing a flat per-level bonus that added storefront capacity without adding customers, so development used to only dilute existing tenants (F2.2B moved the pool F2.9M/wk). Now: new_anchor +1 level costs F15M and pays back 7wk, +5 costs F842M and pays back 74wk; frontier +1 is 28wk, +5 is 257wk. Seat paybacks at baseline: frontier 21wk, dust_basin 28wk, vein_cluster 68wk, new_anchor 216wk.
- SHOPS: capacity = dev x 34 slots. Lease = SHOP_LEASE_BASE(3M) x dev x 0.5. Per-shop ceiling 45k x dev. Share weight 1/sqrt(nth shop that player holds in that district), applied INSIDE the pool so it conserves. Uncapped per player. Player-authored name (3-40 chars, required) and descr (200 max), stripped of control characters; category is the only field the economy reads.
- WAR/OCCUPATION: onColonyCaptured vacates every seat and clears every storefront; districts survive. maybeStripOccupied strips only MAYORAL INVESTMENT one level at a time, never the population baseline, so a sacked city is poor rather than erased. Strip 1/20 behind a 7d hold and the 4.42 x book^0.75 funding surcharge both carry over, now measured against mayoral investment.
- MIGRATION v3 (one way, flagged city_model=3): refunds every v2 lot at full invested value plus F12M per v2 storefront lease, wipes city_lots and city_shops, pushes a headline. Cannot be undone by reverting the build.
- TESTING: CITY_TICK_MS is now env-overridable so the lapse path can be exercised without waiting an hour. 28/28 E2E on seats/perks/development/named shops/bounds; 10/10 on ouster + lapse.
- Changed: server/server.js, server/city.js, server/db_city.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json.

## 1.2.7.0 changes (Storefronts: tenancy layer opens the closed city; SERVER + CLIENT)
- WHY: 4,788 lots exist in the entire game (new_anchor 253) against an 8,000 concurrency target, and the one-lot cap made that a hard ceiling, not a price. The 254th player wanting the capital was locked out permanently. Fix is vertical, not horizontal: ground stays finite (the fraction gates that drive the stage ladder need a finite denominator, and war needs a defined target), floor space does not.
- NEW TABLE city_shops (id, colony_id, q, r, owner, kind, leased_at, paid_in) + 3 indexes. Additive, no migration. Shops address the host lot by the same (sector, lotIndex) pair as city_lots.
- CAPACITY: shopSlots(tier)=round(tier^1.35) -> T1 1, T3 4, T5 9, T7 14, T9 19, T11 25. A developed new_anchor (253 lots at T5) exposes 2,277 slots. Leasing transfers NO title, so the one-lot cap and the community-arcology principle survive: a whale can fund every storefront in a sector and own none of it. Verified E2E: T1 building full at 1 shop, upgrading to T2 opens more, uncapped shops per player while a second LOT is still refused.
- DEMAND: commercialPool = book x COMMERCE_PCT_WK(0.0022) x prosperity/70 x output/70, so the pie scales with development instead of being fixed and split (this was the correction to the first design pass, where pop-scaled demand barely grew: POP_BOOK_K moves new_anchor 820M->882M across a F52B build, under 8%). Pool at a developed capital ~F139M/wk vs F208M/wk landlord layer. commercialSplit weights export .25 / food .40 / med .20 / tech .15, each multiplied by (1 + 1.6 x shortfall) from supplyOf and normalized, so the city pays most for whatever it lacks. NPC market is ALWAYS a counterparty; scarcity is in the appetite, never in finding a buyer, and it moves over weeks not ticks.
- THREE BRAKES, all harness-verified: (1) per-shop ceiling shopCeiling = 60k x tier x costMult, without which the first storefronts paid back in 3wk (best investment in the game by ~50x); with it, low occupancy pays 27wk. (2) Above ~200 shops the pool binds: 400 shops -> 46wk payback, 800 -> 91wk, 1500 -> 171wk, so occupancy self-regulates. (3) Per-player decay: share weight = lotTier / sqrt(nth shop that player holds in that city), applied as a WEIGHT INSIDE the pool so the pool conserves. At 400 occupancy a player's #1 earns F269k/wk, #12 earns F47k; a newcomer's FIRST out-earns a veteran's twelfth 5.7:1. No anti-hoarding rule needed.
- RENT: SHOP_RENT_CUT 0.25 of tenant gross to the lot owner. A fully-let T5 holding earns ~F800k/wk rent on top of its F1.37M yield: the first mechanic that pays for building TALL rather than merely building. Rent on an unowned/seized lot is not paid. City-wide at 400 shops: F34.7M/wk to landlords, F104.2M/wk to tenants, sums to the pool.
- SIEGE verified over 6 weekly ticks at full blockade of a developed capital: pool F148.9M -> F23.5M (-84%), general retail -93%, grocers -81% (scarcity tilt cushions essentials), output 84->19, unrest 20->89. Buyer never disappears, appetite collapses. LIFECYCLE: maybeStripOccupied clears shops on lots knocked to tier 0; onColonyCaptured clears the whole commercial layer (clearColonyShops); layout v2 migration also calls wipeAllShops.
- WS: city_lease_shop (validates lot exists, tier>0, free slot, funds), city_close_shop (owner-checked). cityFullData gains commerce{pool,split,slots,occupied,lease,rentCut}, shops[] with per-shop resolved gross/net, myShops, and per-lot slots/shops counts. creditShopIncome runs on the city tick between creditLotIncome and creditMayoralCuts; occupied cities pay nobody.
- CLIENT: new COMMERCE panel (consumer spend, storefronts let of total, vacancy, per-trade demand bars = the "what the city wants" strategic read), YOUR STOREFRONTS panel with per-shop net/wk and close buttons, lease buttons per trade inside the selected holding when slots are free. 18/18 E2E on the tenancy layer, 27/27 regression on the 1.2.6.0 city suite.
- FLAGGED: new money faucet, a developed city prints up to F139M/wk of consumer spend (~67% on top of its landlord layer); wants checking against mining/trading income as cities mature. NOT INCLUDED: landlord-set rents (needs a vacancy market), shop upgrades/specialisation (flat instrument today), storefronts on the plan-view canvas, city index fund.
- Changed: server/server.js, server/city.js, server/db_city.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json.

## 1.2.6.0 changes (Cities rebuilt on polygonal sectors; SERVER + CLIENT)
- GEOMETRY: the 14x10 grid is retired. Every colony generates 11 VORONOI sectors by half-plane clipping against a 230x170 plate, seeded mulberry32 per colony ('sector:'+id, 'ang:'+id+':'+i, 'lots:'+id+':'+i). Six seed distributions (radial/spine/grid/archipelago/terraced/organic) x six terrains (tether/station/ice/rift/veins/dust), hand-assigned per colony in COLONY_VISUAL; first six match the design mockup. Cells inset 0.14 (0.26 archipelago) so districts read as separate; lots on a per-sector ROTATED grid, step 6.4+rng*1.6; ice worlds skip the melt channel; sectors under 180 area or 4 lots dropped. Verified all 19 colonies: exactly 11 sectors each, 158-276 lots, lots/sector p5 12 / median 21 / p95 39. NO CLIENT MIRROR: server caches geometry and SENDS it in city_data (~17KB for new_anchor); client renders received truth only, so no cross-engine float divergence. Lots addressed (sector, lotIndex) into the existing city_lots q/r columns, so NO schema change to city_lots.
- STAGES (client-derived, no server state): VACANT/SETTLEMENT/DISTRICT/PLATFORM/CONURBATION/ARCOLOGY on FRACTION gates [1,.25] [3,.50] [5,.60] [7,.70] [9,.80], not averages, so a lone whale at T11 cannot advance a sector. Combined with the one-lot cap this makes an arcology structurally a multi-owner artifact.
- ONE LOT PER PLAYER PER CITY (CITY_TUNE.LOT_CAP_PER_CITY=1), enforced server-side, E2E verified: second claim by the same player rejected, different player in the same city accepted. KNOWN GAP: no lot resale or transfer, so a bad lot choice is a stuck position.
- COSTS/INCOME: tierCost(t)=sum 1.8M*2.4^i, tier max 11, T11 = F19.56B cumulative before costMult (spec target ~19.6B, verified). Claim builds T1. Income is now a FRACTION OF BUILT VALUE (EXPORT_PCT_WK 0.0055 x output mult, ~0.1%/day well run), replacing the old EXPORT_BASE x tier^1.35 curve; civic kinds pay 0.25x with the subsidy uplift mayor-funded. Payback ~172wk at output 84, flat across tiers by construction. classifyCity thresholds rescaled for ~100x books (metropolis >=F100B, city >=F20B, town >=F3B); POP_BOOK_K 9.5 -> 0.27 for the same reason.
- WAR MATH corrected to spec. STRIP_RATE 0.10 -> 0.05: at 1/10 the harness showed profitable vandalism above ~F193B book (F1.84T hive: take F104.7B, strip F184B, +F79.3B net for destroying F1.84T of others' work). STRIP_HOLD_MS 14d -> 7d. NEW war funding surcharge in /api/galaxy/fund: per-control-point cost = F10M base + warRate(book) where warRate = 4.42 x book^0.75, applied only at isCityColony colonies; pooled-pending math and the 96% ceiling unchanged, pctToNext auto-corrects. Verified table reproduces the design chat exactly and raider net is NEGATIVE at F25B/F112B/F330B/F1.84T (-2.9B/-7.2B/-12.4B/-12.7B).
- CLIENT REWRITE (client/assets/city.js, 1122 lines): isometric prism renderer over server polygons. Projection S 2.18 / HZ 2.5, per-sector terrace z-base, stage-specific draw paths, three arcology forms by zone (industrial stacks + flare beacons, residential terraces, commercial spire), owner colour bands up the superstructure, skyways between sectors within 58 units, faction tint with dashed outline when contested, per-sector name + stage labels. INTERIOR CANVAS: plan view of individual holdings below PLATFORM (owner-coloured, T# labels, click to select), vertical SECTION at CONURBATION+ showing the same owners as stacked floor bands with L-range addresses. Owner colours hashed from player id into a 12-entry palette. Colony tabs across all 19 cities (layout/terrain sublabels now carried on citySummary). Highlight-holder traces one holder's lots colony-wide. War panel reads live server numbers (d.war), not client math. Mockup panel chrome scoped under #cityOverlay so nothing leaks into the game stylesheet.
- MIGRATION (one way, new city_kv table): on first boot every grid-era lot is refunded at FULL invested value to built_by and city_lots is cleared; charters (colony-level) survive. Flagged layout_ver=2, verified idempotent across two boots (cash F1,000 -> F88,947,078 on boot 1 from a F88,946,078 book, unchanged on boot 2), credits the original builder even for lots that had been seized to null owner. Pushes a headline. CANNOT be undone by reverting the build.
- E2E over live WS, 27/27: geometry payload shape, charter purchase, claim on (s,i), one-lot-cap rejection, cross-player claim accepted, out-of-range sector/lot/negative rejected, upgrade + invested accumulation, non-owner upgrade rejected, retool with downtime, mayor lever set, non-mayor lever rejected, 19 summaries with layout/terrain.
- BALANCE, PROVISIONAL: SUPPLY_PER_F 1.0e-8 retuned for the new book scale, blockade endpoints land at 59% output fall unfarmed vs 15% farmed+subsidised over 3 weeks (design target was ~60% vs <10%, so the farmed side is a touch soft). Sector zones are cosmetic, not coupled to lot kind. Full build of new_anchor is F10.19T at costMult 2.2.
- Changed: server/server.js, server/city.js, server/db_city.js, client/assets/city.js, client/version.json.

## 1.2.5.25 changes (City Charters prototype: player-owned cities on colony planets; SERVER + CLIENT)
- 19 colonies (all seeded except flesh_station and abaddon; Jade still outside colony_state per the 1.2.5.24 note) each carry a city. CHARTER: one mayor per city, faction-gated to the colony's owning faction, priced off population (frontier ~F130M, New Anchor ~F514M), collects 12% of export GDP, pays the population-scaled civic bill. LOTS: deterministic 14x10 district map (seeded mulberry32 per colony, solitaire mirror pattern, server/city.js block copied verbatim into client/assets/city.js, cross-checked identical on all 19), claim ~F20M+, exponential upgrades to tier 10 (~F1B cumulative mid-size), four uses: export pays the owner, agri/med/tech feed the city, 48h retool downtime so farms cannot appear mid-blockade. SIM: six coupled scalars vs five levers with an NPC floor; coefficients solved against the design-harness endpoints (police state: crime 10 / unrest 92 / output 40; neglect: crime 92 / output 7; services: unrest 21 / output 84 / prosperity 92, pays most). Supply is pop-scaled food/med/tech demand, imports cover shortfall unless lanes are blockaded: unfarmed capital loses ~58% output in 3 weeks under siege, farmed+subsidised one ~6%. MONEY: weekly figures paid in hourly tick slices; mayor bankruptcy guard (pays what they have, remainder degrades the city, floored); charter/claims/upgrades are pure sinks; investment grows population which raises all later costs. WAR: conquest seizes the city unless the mayor is conquering-faction; 14d hold then one tier stripped per day, 10% salvage to war chest, until levelled and the charter reopens; retake before maturity restores lots to builders and the charter to its holder; unrest>70 nudges colony tension a few times daily. Server-authoritative throughout (casino_play trust model), E2E verified over live WS incl. wrong-faction/double-claim/non-owner/non-mayor rejections; boot-tick payout verified against hand-computed slice. BALANCE FIRST PASS, knobs in CITY_TUNE: services overpays the mayor (civic curve should bite harder), tier-5 payback ~16wk vs ~66wk tier-10 so wide beats tall until the 60-93 finite lots run out, no per-player lot cap (capture risk is the counterweight). Changed: server/server.js, server/db.js, client/index.html, client/assets/galaxy.js. New: server/city.js, server/db_city.js, client/assets/city.js.

## 1.2.5.24 changes (God Panel world gates: Jade passage + commodity trading; SERVER + CLIENT)
- New World Gates block in the God Panel CONTROL tab (world-state tab: market freeze, volatility, transfer tax). JADE PASSAGE: /api/dev/wormhole already existed and had no UI; now has Open/Seal buttons, seal behind a confirm since it fires at every connected client. Delists Jade tickers and blocks Jade trades; open positions NOT liquidated. COMMODITY HALT (new): COMMODITIES_OPEN gates /api/commodities/buy and /sell, which reject 423. SCOPE LIMITS, both deliberate and commented in source: does NOT touch in-transit cargo or launched runs (halting mid-flight would destroy player cargo), and the flag is in-memory so it resets to open on restart (a halt is a live intervention, the world should not boot halted after a 3am crash; persistence belongs in a settings table, not a module-level let). New /api/dev/gates returns both positions; panel reads it on tab open rather than tracking its own clicks, since a second dev or a restart can move them. PLAYER-FACING: halted buy/sell previously read as generic 'Buy failed' (looks like a bug); now says trading is halted, EN + CN. ALSO FIXED: sell error chain still had a raw English no_ship from an earlier pass, only reachable when selling without a ship, which is why no screenshot caught it. NOT INCLUDED (prerequisite for cities on Jade planets): Jade colonies still client-side map data, not in colony_state, no control_jade column. Cities attach to colonies, so seeding is the first move of the city pass, not a tack-on to a UI release, since it is a schema migration against a live DB. Files: server/server.js, client/index.html, god-panel.js, core.js, galaxy.js.

Rationale: live patching was structurally partial. applyI18n reaches data-i18n nodes and re-render hooks reach panels that have them, but any panel already built by a lazily loaded module keeps its original strings until rebuilt, so how much of the screen translated depended on which tabs the player had opened. index.html already reads fm_jade_theme before first paint, so a reload makes language a boot-time fact. Checker walks outward from each alias call, accepts a declaration in any enclosing scope or at module level. Negative-tested by deleting the FZ declaration. # FleshMarket v1.1.7.2 - MANIFEST

## v1.1.7.2 changes (Capital House trade integrity: tape impact + buy rate limit, SERVER + DB)

```
server/
  server.js               + module helpers impactSlip(notionalC, sideSign) and applyTapeMove(c, lnDelta) (single source of truth for large-order slip + tape push)
                          + const HOUSE_BUY_COOLDOWN_MS (default 30 min, env-tunable)
                          + import getLastFundTradeTs from db.js
                          executeFundTrade(): buy gated by per-house 30-min cooldown (player funds only; flsh/patreon exempt; covers direct + all proposal paths; rate-limited proposal -> failed_exec)
                                              buys fill at price·(1+slip/2) & push tape up; sells fill at price·(1−slip/2) & push tape down; logs/headlines use fill price
                          processFundProposals() (legacy guild): same impact, special stocks held to slip=0 so FLSH pin is untouched
                          personal order handler: inline slip formula + tape block replaced by calls to the shared helpers (behavior-identical)
  db.js                   + getLastFundTradeTs(fundId, type='trade_buy'): MAX(ts) of a fund's activity rows of that type (restart-safe cooldown source, NO schema change)
client/
  version.json            1.1.7.1 -> 1.1.7.2
```

No DB schema migration (getLastFundTradeTs reads the existing fund_activity table) and no asset changes. `pm2 restart fleshmarket` required (server logic). No client cache concern.

## Version History Summary

See `CHANGELOG.md` for full details.

| Version | Key Change |
|---|---|
| v1.1.7.0 | Corpo-Cards in-game card game (packs, collection, deck-builder, PvE matches, 7 factions, shiny variants, server-authoritative pack economy); Ƒbay player card market (list/buy/cancel, atomic card+cash transfer, listed cards gate deck play); all card UI folded into one Corpo-Cards tab (formerly Arena); casino blackjack/poker pixel-art cards; single-strand tab strip |
| v1.1.6.0 | Market dynamics rework + large-order slippage/impact; news drives instant price gaps; predictability and wildness cut |
| v1.1.3.5 | Commodity round-trip exploit fix: fills price off post-impact price (buy/sell eat their own slippage) |
| v1.0.2.4 | Capital Houses guild redesign: rename, passive faucets removed, per-share performance P&L, four-pane house view, and governance/voting (Executive/Vote/Council, share-weighted, owner-set voting window) |
| v1.0.2.3 | Fund disband pays NAV share value (not deposit), drone enemies spawn outside aggro range, SWT hard-locked flat at Ƒ4500, Patreon webhook email matching hardened |
| v1.0.1.9 | Discord button in header, dividend eligibility requires 7 trading-day hold |
| v1.0.1.8 | FLSH permanently pinned at Ƒ1B, removed stock split + chat broadcast |
| v1.0.1.7 | SWT anchored at Ƒ4500, BRNC normal ticker, fix sawtooth from anti-runaway gravity |
| v1.0.1.6 | FLSH 5:1 stock split at Ƒ5B, short cover P&L only (no share allocation), Private Army blockade break, short DB persistence fix |
| v1.0.1.5 | Eyejog system view re-enabled |
| v1.0.1.4 | Wall-clock aligned day-trade reset and passive income (:00/:30 boundaries) |
| v1.0.1.3 | Short-sell money duplication exploit fix, Eyejog detail panel fix |
| v1.0.1.2 | Watchlist, price alerts, portfolio metrics, company detail, news filter, mobile responsive, tutorial rewrite |
| v1.0.1.1 | Title rework, store tab restructure, Patreon button glow |
| v1.0.1.0 | Beta market model, news feed rewrite, heatmap fix |
| v1.0.0.4 | Faction persistence fix, server-authoritative day-trade enforcement |
| v1.0.0.2 | Market stabilization, Wire Credits, leaderboard EOD freeze, SWT/BRNC normalized, smuggling 15min cooldown |
| v0.9.0 | Client modularised — 18 JS modules, 13 CSS files, lazy loading |
| v0.8.x | Cyberpunk augmentation update — implants, pixel art, set bonuses |
| Build H | UI/casino overhaul — blackjack AI, roulette rewrite, EOD timer |
| v5.0 | Full Flesh — limit orders, shorts, earnings, IPOs, dividends, quests, heatmap |
| Patch G | Quest system removed, activity-based XP introduced |
| Patch F | Casino stability fixes |
| v3.0/E | SQLite player persistence (requires Node 22.5+) |
| v2.1/A-D | Foundation — health endpoint, autosave, token auth, unique usernames |
